# 课程信息 Course Information

•  课程性质：本科生必修课；Undergraduate Major Core Course;

•  学时：3学分，48学时； 48 hours, 3 credits

•  课程评分标准：期末考试 50%，期中考试20%，平时作业20%，出勤 5% ，课堂表现5% 。

Evaluation Criteria: Final Exam 50%; Mid-term Exam 20%; Assignments 20%; Attendance 5%; Class performance 5%.

•  目的：理解固体废物处理处置与资源化的重要性，全面系统地掌握资源循环利用的基础理论知识；掌握常用的固体废弃物处置技术、方法和工艺流程。

Understand the importance of solid waste treatment, disposal and resource utilization, comprehensively and systematically master the basic theoretical knowledge of resource recycling; master commonly used solid waste disposal technologies, methods and processes.

lch Southern University of Science and Technology

# 固体废物定义 Definition of Solid Waste

定义：在生产、生活和其他活动中产生的丧失原有利用价值或虽未丧失利用价值但被抛弃或者放弃的固态、半固态和置于容器中的气态物品、物质以及法律、行政法规规定纳入固体废物管理的物品、物质。

Definition: Solid waste refers to solid, semi-solid, and gaseous items or substances placed in containers that are generated during production, daily life, and other activities, which have lost their original utility value or, although not having lost their utility value, have been discarded or abandoned, as well as items and substances that are stipulated by laws and administrative regulations to be managed as solid waste.

lchSouthern University

of Science and

-15-                                       Technology

---

固体废物定义 Definition of Solid Waste

# 1. 无主性

被丢弃后，不再属于谁，找不到具体负责者，特别是城市固体废物。

"Ownerless: Once discarded, it no longer belongs to anyone, and no specific responsible party can be identified, especially in the case of municipal solid waste.

# 2. 分散性

丢弃、分散在各处，需要收集。

Dispersed: Scattered and discarded in various places, requiring collection.

# 3. 危害性

给人们的生产和生活带来不便，危害人体健康。

Hazardous: Causes inconvenience to people's production and daily life, and poses risks to human health.

# 4. 错位性

一个时空领域的废物在另一个时空领域是宝贵的资源，又被称为“在时空上错位的资源”。

Misplaced: Waste in one temporal and spatial context can be a valuable resource in another, also referred to as 'resources misplaced in time and space'.

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

---

# 固体废物危害 Hazards Posed by Solid Waste

✓ 固体废物对环境的危害与所涉及的固体废物性质和数量有关

The environmental hazards posed by solid waste are related to the nature and quantity of the solid waste involved

✓ 处理依据，主要是当地的环境污染控制标准

The basis for treatment primarily depends on local environmental pollution control standards

✓ 对环境污染的控制程度，与经济发展和民众生活水平有密切关系

The degree of control over environmental pollution is closely linked to economic development and the living standard of the population

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology



---

# 固体废物危害 Hazards Posed by Solid Waste

✓ 固体废物中的重金属 、难降解有机污染物通过水 、气传播，也可能产生病原菌等；Heavy metals and persistent organic pollutants in solid waste can spread through water and air, and may also generate pathogens

✓ 重金属 、 难降解有机污染物 、 恶臭 、 病原菌是主要污染物和控制对象 。 Heavy metals, persistent organic pollutants, foul odors, and pathogens are the main pollutants and targets for control

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

固体废物的性质 Properties of Solid Waste

# 1. 物理性质 Physical properties

- 包括物理组成、色、臭、温度、含水率、空隙率、渗透性、粒度、密度、磁性、电性、光电性、摩擦性、弹性等。Including physical composition, color, odor, temperature, moisture content, porosity, permeability, particle size, density, magnetic properties, electrical properties, photoelectric properties, frictional properties, elasticity, etc.
- 生活垃圾的压实、破碎、分选等处理方法主要与其物理性质有关，其中色、臭等感官特性可以通过视觉或嗅觉直接加以判断。The treatment methods for domestic waste, such as compaction, crushing, and sorting, are primarily related to its physical properties. Sensory characteristics like color and odor can be directly assessed through vision or smell.

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

---


# 固体废物的性质 Properties of Solid Waste

# (2) 化学性质 Chemical properties

✓ 包括元素组成、重金属含量、pH值、植物营养元素（N\P\K)、污染有机物含量、碳氮比（C/N）、生化需氧量与化学需氧量之比值（BOD₅/COD）、垃圾中生物呼吸所需的耗氧量、热值、灰分熔点、闪点与燃点、挥发分、灰分和固定碳、表面润湿性等；

Elemental composition, heavy metal content, pH value, nutrients (N\P\K), organic pollutants, carbon-to-nitrogen ratio (C/N), (BOD₅/COD), the oxygen demand required for biological, calorific value, ash fusion point, flash point and fire point, volatile matter, ash and fixed carbon, surface wettability, etc.

lch Southern University of Science and Technology

---

固体废物的性质 Properties of Solid Waste

# (2) 化学性质 Chemical properties

✓ 固体废物的堆肥、发酵、焚烧、热解、浮选等处理方法主要与其化学性质有关。The treatment methods for solid waste, such as composting, fermentation, incineration, pyrolysis, and flotation, are mainly related to its chemical properties.

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

-21-

---

固体废物的性质 Properties of Solid Waste

# (3) 生物化学性质 Biochemical properties

- 包括病毒、细菌、原生及后生动物、寄生虫卵等生物性污染物质的组成、有机组分的生物可降解性等；
Including the composition of biological pollutants such as viruses, bacteria, protozoa and metazoa, parasite eggs, etc., and the biodegradability of organic components.
- 固体废物的堆肥、发酵、填埋等生化处理方法主要与其生物化学性质有关。
The biochemical treatment methods for solid waste, such as composting, fermentation, and landfilling, are mainly related to its biochemical properties.

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

固体废物的分类

# Classification of solid waste

- 按化学成分， 可分为有机废物和无机废物，也可分为有机固废、无机固废、电子废物、危险废物；
By chemical composition, it can be classified into organic waste and inorganic waste, and also categorized as organic solid waste, inorganic solid waste, electronic waste, and hazardous waste
- 按热值，可分为高热值废物和低热值废物；
By calorific value, it can be divided into high-calorific waste and low-calorific waste
- 按处理处置方法，可分为可资源化废物、可堆肥废物、可燃废物和无机废物等；
By treatment and disposal methods, it can be classified into recyclable waste, compostable waste, combustible waste, and inorganic waste

lchSouthern University

of Science and Technology

-23-

---


# 固体废物的分类

按来源，可分为城市生活垃圾和工农业生产中所产生的废弃物；By source, it can be categorized into municipal solid waste and waste generated from industrial and agricultural production

按危害特性，可分为有毒有害固体废物和无毒无害固体废物两类；By hazard characteristics, it can be divided into toxic and hazardous solid waste and non-toxic and harmless solid waste

方便起见，一般把生活垃圾简称为垃圾，其它固体废物则以全称描述之。For convenience, domestic waste is generally referred to as garbage, while other solid wastes are described by their full names.

lchSouthern University

of Science and

固体废物－污染控制-处理-资源化-末端处置
# Pollution control and resources recovery of solid wastes

1. 生活垃圾 Refuse, municipal solid wastes
2. 危险废物和废液 Hazardous wastes
3. 建筑垃圾 Demolition wastes, huge quantity
4. 电子废物 Waste electric and electronic equipment (WEEE)
5. 大宗工业废物 Bulk industrial solid wastes, huge quantity
6. 污水厂污泥 Sewage sludge with 80% moisture
7. 污染土壤、河泥、湖泥 Polluted soil and sediments of rivers
8. 农林废弃物（生物质） Agricultural and forest wastes, huge quantity
9. 放射性废物 Radioactive wastes

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

固体废物产量 （2020）Quantity of solid waste

✓ 我国人口多，地域广，固体废物产量也多且复杂；China has a large population and vast territory, resulting in a substantial and complex output of solid waste

✓ 2020年，全国共有196个大、中城市向社会发布了2019年固体废物污染环境防治信息；In 2020, a total of 196 large and medium-sized cities across the country released information on the prevention and control of environmental pollution by solid waste for the year 2019.

✓ 大、中城市一般工业固体废物产生量为13.8亿吨，工业危险废物产生量为4498.9万吨，医疗废物产生量为84.3万吨，城市生活垃圾产生量为23560.2万吨 。The general industrial solid waste generated in these large and medium-sized cities amounted to 1.38 billion tons, industrial hazardous waste to 44.989 million tons, medical waste to 843,000 tons, and municipal solid waste to 235.602 million tons.

中华人民共和国生态环境部 二0二0年十二月 lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

# 固体废物产量（2023）Quantity of solid waste

# 1. 一般工业固体废物

初步核算，2023年，全国一般工业固体废物产生量为42.8亿吨，综合利用量为25.7亿吨，处置量为8.7亿吨。

According to preliminary estimates, in 2023, the national generation of general industrial solid waste amounted to 4.28 billion tons, with a comprehensive utilization volume of 2.57 billion tons and a disposal volume of 870 million tons.

# 2. 城市生活垃圾

初步核算，2023年，全国城市生活垃圾无害化处理能力为115.2万吨/日，无害化处理量为2.59亿吨，无害化处理率为99.9%。

According to preliminary estimates, in 2023, the national capacity for the harmless treatment of municipal solid waste reached 1.152 million tons per day, with a harmless treatment volume of 259 million tons and a harmless treatment rate of 99.9%.



---


# 3.危险废物

初步核算，2023年，全国约有7.7万家单位，危险废物年产生量在10吨及以上，申报产生约1.1亿吨危险废物。

截至2023年底，全国约有3600家危险废物集中利用处置单位，集中利用处置能力约2.1亿吨/年。

# 4.农业固体废物

2023年，畜禽粪污综合利用率超过78%，秸秆综合利用率稳定在86%以上，农膜回收率稳定在80%以上。



---

固体废物（2024）solid waste

# 九、 固体废物与化学品  专栏

# 深化“无废城市”建设

# (一) 固体废物

2024年，生态环境部高标准推进“无废城市”建设，印发《2024年度“无废城市”建设工作推进计划》，推动“113+8”个城市和地区加快落实“无废城市”建设工作任务和工程项目，各地累计开展工程项目3900项，投入资金8800亿元，累计建设“无废细胞”2.5万余个。支持中石化、国能集团、中国宝武、中国中化等4家大型央企开展“无废集团”建设试点。有序推进浙江等20个省份全域“无废城市”建设，京津冀、长三角、川渝等地区区域“无废城市”共建。制定《“无废城市”建设进展评价办法（试行）》，开展“无废指数”研究。推进“无废城市”投融资工作，推动北京银行发布首个“无废贷”产品。推动天津、浙江、河南、广东等8个省份、71个地级及以上城市和地区办好“国际无废日”主题活动，让“无废”理念更深入人心。

2024年，生态环境部持续加强新污染物治理，召开新污染物治理部际协调小组第二次会议，全年共部署完成127项重点工作任务。推动将化学物质环境风险管理法规制定纳入国务院2024年度立法工作计划预备类。推动将新污染物治理要求纳入环评、监测、执法等现行环境管理制度。印发《化学物质环境风险评估与管控技术标准体系框架（2024年版）》，加强标准预研究引导。开展全国化学物质环境信息统计调查，完成高关注、高生产使用量化学物质环境风险筛查，有序开展新污染物环境风险评估。聚焦2023年淘汰的8种类重点管控新污染物，加强监测溯源、现场核查、进出口管控等，对120余家企业开展现场帮扶，巩固淘汰成果。印发《关干&#x3C;新污染物治理试点工作方案>的通知》，聚焦长江、黄河等重点流域，全氟化合物、抗生素等重点领域，印染、涂料等重点行业，启动两批17省份共计23个新污染物治理试点项目。

固体废物处理与资源化方法
# Methods for Solid Waste Treatment and Resource Recovery

- 无废（最高境界，但不可能）No waste；
- 少废（勤俭节约、清洁生产）Less waste；
- 源头分类（生活垃圾、建筑垃圾为主，工业生产亦然）Source sorting；
- 再用、再生 Reuse and regeneration；
- 加工处理 ， 生产合格下游原料或产品 Processing for raw materials or products；
- 焚烧获取能源（供热和发电）Incineration for heat and power；
- 卫生填埋（生活垃圾）或安全填埋（危险废物）Sanitary landfill for MSW or dedicated landfill for hazardous waste；

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

---

固体废物处理与资源化方法
# Methods for Solid Waste Treatment and Resource Recovery

- 按照处理对象展开；By treatment object: expand based on the type of waste being treated
- 按照处理技术呼应；By treatment technology: correspond to the specific technologies used for treatment
- 按照处理对象 + 处理技术协同论述； By treatment object + technology synergy: discuss the combined application of treatment objects and technologies.
- 法律法规内容简单，只涉及一些排放标准或鉴别标准。Laws and regulations: the content is straightforward, primarily involving some emission standards or identification criteria.

lch Southern University of Science and Technology

固体废物处理与资源化技术源头
# Best practices for Solid Waste Management

# 师法自然 Learning from Nature

- 堆场过渡到卫生填埋 - 可持续填埋 - 安全填埋，及其相应的污染控制；

The transition from open dumps to sanitary landfills, sustainable landfills, and secure landfills, along with their corresponding pollution control measures
- 一把火燃烧过渡到烟气治理的焚烧、热量回收的焚烧-飞灰无害化处理的焚烧；

The evolution from simple burning to incineration with flue gas treatment, incineration with heat recovery, and incineration with fly ash management
- 自然通风的有机废物好氧堆肥；

Aerobic composting of organic waste with natural ventilation
- 隔绝空气的有机废物厌氧消化；

Anaerobic digestion of organic waste in an oxygen-free environment

# Best practices for Solid Waste Management

# 人为干预

- 混乱杂乱到分类分选的熵减过程，难度增大 The process of reducing entropy from chaos and disorder to sorting and classification becomes increasingly challenging;
- 从混杂体系中回收有价物资的熵减过程，难度增大 The process of recovering valuable materials from mixed systems, which also involves reducing entropy, becomes increasingly difficult

固体废物处置难题
# Main challenges for solid wastes management

- 生活垃圾分类及其分类后各种废物循环利用 Source classification of domestic solid waste
- 高混杂（干垃圾）生活垃圾机械分选和机器人分选 Mechanical and Robot separation for mixed refuse
- 有害垃圾处理与资源化利用 Treatment and recycling of the hazardous in domestic solid waste
- 可降解有机垃圾聚合转化为有用之材料 Conversion of the organic fraction into useful materials
- 固体废物处理与资源化的碳达峰 、 碳中和 Carbon peaking and carbon neutralization in solid waste treatment and recycling

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

固体废物处置难题
# Main challenges for solid wastes management

- 有机污泥不调理的深度脱水或高效调理 Deep dewatering of sludge without conditioning using chemical agents；
- 有机垃圾厌氧发酵中碳的高效转化 Extreme conversion of carbon in organic waste anaerobic digestion process；
- 工业废盐与污泥处理与资源化 Treatment of industrial salts and sludge；
- 重金属工业固体废物中有毒有害元素的分离 Separation of heavy metals from industrial wastes；
- 含As、Cr、Cd、Pb、Cu、Hg危险废物利用 Recycling of As、Cr、Cd、Pb、Cu、Hg in hazardous wastes；

# Main challenges for solid wastes management

- ✓ 危险废物智能化在线监测与管控 Intelligent online monitoring and control of hazardous waste；
- ✓ 大宗磷石膏、脱硫石膏、粉煤灰处理与资源化 Treatment and recycling of bulk phosphogypsum, desulfurization gypsum and coal fly ash；
- ✓ 固废焚烧烟气中二氧化硫 、 氮氧化物 、 氯化氢 、 二噁英高效分离富集 Separation and enrichment of SO₂, NOx, HCl, Dioxins from flue gas；
- ✓ 生活垃圾焚烧飞灰的高温熔融处理与利用 Melt of refuse incineration fly ash；
- ✓ 废机电废家电废汽车的再生再用与处理 Recycling and reuse and treatment of used machinery, electrical appliances and automobiles；

lchSouthern University of Science and Technology

《固废处置及资源化》
# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 10.1 Industrial Waste Overview

工业固体废物概述

Yang Lei

2026-05-11

---

工业固体废物定义与分类
# Definition and classification of industrial solid waste

# 定义 Definition：

• 工业固体废物，是指在工业原料生产 、产品加工、以及燃料燃烧、 矿物开采 、交通运输、环境治理过程中所 废弃的固体和半固体物料（非危险废物）。Industrial solid waste refers to solid and semi-solid materials discarded during the production of industrial raw materials, product processing, as well as processes such as fuel combustion, mineral extraction, transportation, and environmental management (non-hazardous waste).



---

工业固体废物定义与分类
# Definition and classification of industrial solid waste

# 按行业分类 Classification：

- 煤系固体废物： 产生于燃煤发电，如煤矸石、粉煤灰；
Coal-based solid waste: generated from coal-fired power generation, such as coal gangue and fly ash;
- 冶金工业固体废物：产生于金属冶炼过程，如高炉矿渣、钢渣；
Metallurgical industry solid waste: generated from metal smelting processes, such as blast furnace slag and steel slag;
- 石油化工固体废物：产生于石油加工和化工生产；
Petrochemical solid waste: generated from oil processing and chemical production;
- 矿业固体废物：产生于采矿、选矿过程，如废石、尾矿；
Mining solid waste: generated from mining and mineral processing processes, such as waste rock and tailings;
- 轻工业固体废物：食品、造纸印刷 、纺织印染 、皮革等排出的下脚料 、污泥、槽渣等；
Light industry solid waste: waste materials, sludge, slag, etc. discharged from food, paper printing, textile printing and dyeing, leather, etc.;
- 其他：产生于机械加工 ， 如金属碎屑 、 电镀污泥 ， 废轮胎；
Others: generated from mechanical processing, such as metal scraps, electroplating sludge, and waste tires;

-4-

---

工业固体废物对生态环境的危害

# The hazards of industrial solid waste to the ecological environment

- 露天堆积，侵占土地，阻塞河道，造成水灾。
Open-air accumulation, land occupation, river blockage, and flooding.
- 自燃产生大量有害气体，如 CO, CO₂, SO₂, H₂S, NOₓ 等，诱发火灾。
Spontaneous combustion produces a large amount of harmful gases, such as CO, CO₂, SO₂, H₂S, NOₓ, etc., which induce fires.
- 酸性淋溶水损伤土壤、农作物及地下地表水环境。
Acidic leaching water damages soil, crops, and underground and surface water environments.
- 细颗粒随风飘散，造成降尘污染。
Fine particles are blown away by the wind, causing dust pollution.
- 其中的天然放射性元素对人体与环境产生危害。
The natural radioactive elements in it are harmful to the human body and the environment.
- 崩塌时，危及人畜安全。
When collapsing, it endangers the safety of humans and animals.

-5-

---

# 工业固体废物处理原则

# Principles of Industrial Solid Waste Treatment

早期世界各国都注重末端治理，提出了资源化、减量化和无害化的“三化”原则。In the early days, countries around the world focused on end-of-pipe management and proposed the "three transformations" principle of resource utilization, reduction and harmlessness.

- 避免产生(Clean)；Avoid generation (Clean);
- 综合利用(Cycle)；Comprehensive utilization (Cycle);
- 妥善处理(Control) Proper treatment (Control)

---

工业固体废物处理与处置方法
# Industrial solid waste treatment and disposal methods

工业废物的处理是指通过物理、化学和生物手段，将废物中对人体或环境有害的物质分解为无害成分，或转化为毒性较小的物质，使其适于运输、贮存、资源化和最终处置的一种过程。Industrial waste treatment refers to a process of decomposing harmful substances in waste into harmless components or converting them into less toxic substances through physical, chemical and biological means, making them suitable for transportation, storage, resource utilization and final disposal.



---

工业固体废物处理与处置方法

# Industrial solid waste treatment and disposal methods

# ① 化学处理 Chemical treatment

- 酸、碱、重金属废液、氰化物、乳化油；焚烧、溶剂浸出、化学中和、氧化还原
- Acid, alkali, heavy metal waste liquid, cyanide, emulsified oil; incineration, solvent leaching, chemical neutralization, redox

# ② 物理处理 Physical treatment

- 重选、磁选、浮选、拣选、摩擦和弹跳分选
- Gravity separation, magnetic separation, flotation, sorting, friction and bounce sorting

# ③ 生物处理 Biological treatment

- 有机废物堆肥法和厌氧发酵法；提炼铜、铀等金属的细菌冶金法；有机废液活性污泥法；生物修复被污染土壤
- Organic waste composting and anaerobic fermentation; bacterial metallurgy for refining copper, uranium and other metals; organic waste liquid activated sludge method; biological remediation of contaminated soil

-8-

---

工业固体废物资源化途径
# Ways to Recycle Industrial Solid Waste into Resources

# ① 生产建材 Production of building materials

- 优点：耗渣量大、投资少、见效快；能耗低、节省原材料、不产生二次污染；可生的产品种类多，性能好，如用作水泥原料与配料、掺合料、缓凝剂、墙体材料、混凝土的混合料与骨料、加气混凝土、砂浆、砌块、装饰材料、保温材料、矿渣棉、轻质骨料、铸石、微晶玻璃、人造大理石等。
- Advantages: large slag consumption, low investment, quick results, low energy consumption, saving raw materials, no secondary pollution, a wide range of products, good performance; such as cement raw materials and ingredients, admixtures, retarders, wall materials, concrete mixtures and aggregates, aerated concrete, mortar, blocks, decorative materials, thermal insulation materials, slag wool, lightweight aggregates, cast stone, microcrystalline glass, artificial marble, etc.

-9-

---

工业固体废物资源化途径
# Ways to Recycle Industrial Solid Waste into Resources

1. 回收或利用其中有价组分，开发新产品，取代某些工业原料
2. Recycle or utilize valuable components to develop new products and replace certain industrial raw materials

煤矸石沸腾炉发电、洗矸泥炼焦作工业或民用燃料
3. Use coal gangue boiling furnace to generate electricity, wash gangue sludge to make coke as industrial or civilian fuel

钢渣作冶炼溶剂、硫铁矿烧渣炼铁
4. Use steel slag as smelting solvent, use pyrite slag to make iron

新型聚合物基、陶瓷基与金属基的废物复合材料
5. New polymer-based, ceramic-based and metal-based waste composite materials

从烟尘和赤泥中提取镓、钪（kang）等贵金属

-10-

---

工业固体废物资源化途径
# Ways to Recycle Industrial Solid Waste into Resources

# ③ 筑路、筑坝与回填

Road construction, dam construction and backfilling

- 投资少 、 用量大 、 技术成熟 、 易推广 。 Low investment, large amount, mature technology, easy to promote.
- 修筑1km公路可消耗粉煤灰上万吨 。Constructing 1km of highway can consume tens of thousands of tons of fly ash.
- 回填后覆土，可作为耕地、林地或建设用地。After backfilling, cover with soil and use it as arable land, forest land or construction land.
- 游离活性氧化钙、氧化镁是个严重问题，要消除之。Free active calcium oxide and magnesium oxide are a serious problem and must be eliminated.

-11-

---

工业固体废物资源化途径
# Ways to Recycle Industrial Solid Waste into Resources

# ④ 生产农肥和土壤改良

- 含硅、钙、磷以及各种微量元素的工业废物，改性后，可作为农肥使用。

Industrial waste containing silicon, calcium, phosphorus and various trace elements can be used as agricultural fertilizers after modification.
- 利用粉煤灰、炉渣、钢渣、黄磷渣和赤泥及铁合金渣制作硅钙肥、钙镁磷肥。

Use fly ash, slag, steel slag, yellow phosphorus slag, red mud and ferroalloy slag to make silicon calcium fertilizer and calcium magnesium phosphate fertilizer.

-12-

固废处置及资源化
# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 10.2 Typical Industrial Waste Disposal and Utilization

# 典型工业固体废物处理与资源化

Yang Lei

2026-05-11

---

煤矸石 Coal Gangue

•  采煤过程和洗煤过程中排放的固体废物; Solid waste discharged during coal mining and coal washing;

•  成煤过程中与煤层伴生的一种含碳量较低、比煤硬的黑灰色岩石; A type of black-gray rock with a lower carbon content and harder than coal that accompanies coal seams during coal formation;

•  煤矸石产生量约为煤炭生产量的10%-20%左右; The amount of coal gangue produced is about 10% of coal production;

-14-

---

煤矸石产量 Gangue production

2024年，我国煤炭产量为47.6亿吨。煤矸石产生量8.25亿吨，占工业固废的 11.2%。综合利用量，6.07亿吨，综合利用率为73.6%。

In 2024, Coal production in China was 4.76 billion tons. The amount of coal gangue produced was 825 million tons, accounting for 11.2% of industrial solid waste. The comprehensive utilization amount was 607 million tons, and the comprehensive utilization rate was 73.6%.

煤矸石产生量最大的为山西和内蒙古。山西产煤矸石占35%。

Shanxi and Inner Mongolia have the largest amount of coal gangue production. Shanxi produces 35% of coal gangue.

-15-

---


# 煤矸石组成

# Gangue composition

- 典型化学组成（%）Typical chemical composition (%)

| SiO₂  | Al₂O₃ | CaO | MgO | Fe₂O₃ | R₂O   | 烧失量  | Ignition loss |
| ----- | ----- | --- | --- | ----- | ----- | ---- | ------------- |
| 40-65 | 15-35 | 1-7 | 1-4 | 2-9   | 1-2.5 | 2-17 |               |

- 高岭土（68.7%）、蒙脱石、石英砂、硅酸盐矿物、碳酸盐矿物、铁钛矿、炭质等; Kaolin (68.7%), montmorillonite, quartz sand, silicate minerals, carbonate minerals, ferro-titanium ore, carbonaceous materials, etc.;
- 化学元素以Si、Al为主（SiO₂ 40-65%, Al₂O₃ 15-30%); Chemical elements are mainly Si and Al (SiO₂ 40-65%, Al₂O₃ 15-30%);



---

煤矸石性质 Coal Gangue Properties

• 煤矸石和粘土化学成分相似，能够代替粘土提供硅质组成，成为生产水泥原料；Gangue has similar chemical composition to clay, and can replace clay to provide silicon composition and become a raw material for cement production;

• 煤矸石能释放一定热量，可代替部分燃料；Gangue can release a certain amount of heat and can replace part of the fuel;

-17-

---

煤矸石分类

# Gangue classification

# 一类：含碳量≤4%

Category 1: Carbon content ≤4%

- 热值低（ ≤ 2090kJ/kg），可用作路基材料、塌陷区复垦、采空区回填；Low calorific value (≤ 2090kJ/kg), can be used as roadbed material, reclamation of collapsed areas, backfilling of goaf areas;

# 二类：含碳量4-6%

Category 2: Carbon content 4-6%

- 热值低（ ≤ 2090kJ/kg），可用作路基材料、塌陷区复垦、采空区回填；Low calorific value (≤ 2090kJ/kg), can be used as roadbed material, reclamation of collapsed areas, backfilling of goaf areas;

# 三类：含碳量6-20%

Category 3: Carbon content 6-20%

- 热值2090-6270kJ/kg，可用作生产水泥、砖瓦、轻骨料；Calorific value 2090-6270kJ/kg, can be used to produce cement, bricks and tiles, lightweight aggregate;

# 四类：含碳量＞20%

Category 4: Carbon content > 20%

- 热值较高 6270-12550kJ/kg，可用回收煤炭或用作工业用燃料；High calorific value 6270-12550kJ/kg, can be used to recycle coal or used as industrial fuel;

-18-

| 铸造型砂、造型粉 | 硅系产品        | 铝系产品            | 钛白粉         |

---

煤矸石生产烧结砖
# Coal Gangue Production Sintered Brick

煤矸石烧结砖是以煤矸石为原料，替代部分或全部粘土，采用适当的工艺烧制而成；Gangue sintered bricks are made of gangue as raw material, replacing part or all of the clay, and are fired using appropriate technology;

三大呆石

-20-

---


# 粉煤灰及化学成分 Fly ash and its composition

- 粉煤灰是冶炼厂、化工厂和燃煤发电厂排放的非挥发性煤残渣 ，包括飘灰、飞灰和炉底灰。
Fly ash is the non-volatile coal residue emitted by smelters, chemical plants and coal-fired power plants, including dust, fly ash and bottom ash.
- 化学组成类似于粘土的化学组成 ， 包括 SiO₂ 、 Al₂O₃ 、 Fe₂O₃ 、 CaO 和未燃尽的碳；
The chemical composition is similar to that of clay, including SiO₂, Al₂O₃, Fe₂O₃, CaO and unburned carbon;
- 由于煤的品种不同，粉煤灰分为高钙粉煤灰和低钙粉煤灰。
Due to the different types of coal, fly ash is divided into high calcium fly ash and low calcium fly ash.

| SiO₂  | Al₂O₃ | CaO     | MgO     | Fe₂O₃ | SO₃   | K₂O和Na₂O | 烧失量  |
| ----- | ----- | ------- | ------- | ----- | ----- | -------- | ---- |
| 43-56 | 20-32 | 1.5-5.5 | 0.6-2.0 | 4-10  | 0.5-2 | 1.0-2.5  | 3-20 |



---

粉煤灰处理与资源化 Fly ash treatment and resource utilization

• 燃煤电厂将煤磨细成100微米以下的细粉，用预热空气喷入炉内悬浮燃烧，产生高温烟气，

经捕尘装置捕集的粉尘成为粉煤灰，也叫飞灰；Coal-fired power plants grind coal into fine powder less than 100 microns, spray it into the furnace with preheated air for suspended combustion, and produce high-temperature flue gas. The dust captured by the dust collection device becomes fly ash, also called fly ash;

• 粉煤灰的产生量为电厂煤炭用量的25%左右；The amount of fly ash produced is about 25% of the coal used in the power plant;

粉煤灰的产生速度相当于每天填满一个水立方

江西省九江市，九江电厂接灰场中的灰水直接排出，形成一条灰水河

-22-

---


# 粉煤灰矿物组成

# Fly ash mineral composition

分为无定形相和结晶相两大类

It is divided into two categories: amorphous phase and crystalline phase.

- 无定形相主要为玻璃体， 占 50-80%， 是粉煤灰主要矿物成分， 具有良好化学活性；
- The amorphous phase is mainly glass, accounting for 50-80%, which is the main mineral component of fly ash and has good chemical activity;
- 结晶相有石英、长石、云母、磁铁矿等；
- The crystalline phase includes quartz, feldspar, mica, magnetite, etc.;
- 低钙粉煤灰活性主要取决于无定形玻璃相矿物，而不取决于结晶相矿物；
- The activity of low-calcium fly ash mainly depends on the amorphous glass phase minerals, not the crystalline phase minerals;
- 从矿物组成来说，低钙粉煤灰玻璃体含量越高，粉煤灰化学活性越好；
- In terms of mineral composition, the higher the glass content of low-calcium fly ash, the better the chemical activity of fly ash;



---


# 粉煤灰的物理性质

# Physical properties of fly ash

# 细度

# Fineness

- 粉煤灰的粒径范围0.5-300微米 The particle size range of fly ash is 0.5-300 microns
- 我国规定45微米筛余百分数为细度指标 China stipulates that the percentage of 45 micron sieve residue is the fineness index

# 颗粒组成

# Particle composition

- 球形颗粒4-25% Spherical particles 4-25%
- 不规则多孔颗粒约70% Irregular porous particles about 70%

# 需水量比

# Water requirement ratio

- 一般水泥和混凝土中掺入粉煤灰，可降低其用水量。Generally, adding fly ash to cement and concrete can reduce its water consumption.



---

粉煤灰水泥 Fly ash cement

•  硅酸盐类水泥：以石灰石和粘土为主要原料 ，经破碎、配料、磨细制成生料，然后喂入水泥窑中煅烧成熟料 ，再将熟料加适量石膏 （有时还掺加混合材料或外加剂 ）磨细而成 。

Portland cement: limestone and clay are used as the main raw materials, which are crushed, mixed and ground into raw materials, and then fed into cement kilns to be calcined into clinker, and then the clinker is ground with appropriate amount of gypsum (sometimes mixed materials or admixtures).

•  由硅酸盐水泥熟料和粉煤灰，在加入适量石膏磨细制成的水硬性胶凝材料，称为粉煤灰硅酸盐水泥;

The hydraulic cementing material made of Portland cement clinker and fly ash, with appropriate amount of gypsum ground, is called fly ash Portland cement;

•  此种水泥中粉煤灰的掺量按质量分数为20%-40%。

The amount of fly ash in this cement is 20%-40% by mass.

•  早期强度低后期强度增进率大

Low early strength and large later strength increase rate;

•  和易性好，干缩性小

Good workability and low shrinkage;

•  耐腐蚀性好

Good corrosion resistance;

•  水化热低

Good corrosion resistance;

---

粉煤灰应用 Fly ash application

- 用作建筑原料：水泥或混凝土掺料、制砖、空心砌砖 Used as building materials: cement or concrete admixture, brick making, hollow brickwork
- 用作工程填筑：路基、低洼地填充、废矿井或塌陷区填充 Used as engineering filling: roadbed, low-lying land filling, abandoned mine or subsidence area filling
- 用于农业：复合肥、土壤改良剂等 Used in agriculture: compound fertilizer, soil conditioner, etc.
- 用于环境保护：废水处理、脱硫、吸声等 Used in environmental protection: wastewater treatment, desulfurization, sound absorption, etc.
- 生产功能性新型材料：复合混凝剂、沸石分子筛、填料载体等 Production of functional new materials: composite coagulants, zeolite molecular sieves, filler carriers, etc.
- 回收有用物质：稀有金属、工业原料等 Recovery of useful substances: rare metals, industrial raw materials, etc.

---

冶金工业固体废物处理与资源化
# Utilization of solid waste in metallurgical industry

- 高炉渣、钢渣、铁合金渣等。Blast furnace slag, steel slag, ferroalloy slag, etc.
- 高炉渣 是冶炼生铁时从高炉中排出的一种废渣 。Blast furnace slag is a waste slag discharged from the blast furnace when smelting pig iron.
- 高炉冶炼中，从炉顶加入铁矿石 、焦炭以及助熔剂，当炉内温度达到1400-1600 ℃时，物料变成液相，浮在铁水上的熔渣，通过排渣口排出，这就是高炉渣。During blast furnace smelting, iron ore, coke and flux are added from the top of the furnace. When the temperature in the furnace reaches 1400-1600℃, the materials become liquid phase, and the slag floating on the molten iron is discharged through the slag discharge port. This is blast furnace slag.
- 钢渣是炼钢过程中排出的废渣，温度在1500-1700 ℃之间，在高温下呈液体状态，缓慢冷却后呈块状或粉状。Steel slag is waste slag discharged during the steelmaking process. The temperature is between 1500-1700℃. It is in liquid state at high temperature and becomes block or powder after slow cooling.

---

# 高炉渣化学组成

# Chemical composition of blast furnace slag

• 氧化钙 、 氧化铝 、 氧化镁 、 二氧化硅四种占高炉渣 90% 以上 。 Calcium oxide, aluminum oxide, magnesium oxide and silicon dioxide account for more than 90% of blast furnace slag.

| 普通矿渣 | 31-50 | 31-44 | 6-18  | 1-16 | 0.05-2.6 | 0.2-1.5 | 0.2-2   |      |        |
| 锰铁矿渣 | 28-47 | 22-35 | 7-22  | 1-9  | 3-24     | 0.2-1.7 | 0.17-2  |      |        |
| 钒钛矿渣 | 20-31 | 19-32 | 13-17 | 7-9  | 0.3-1.2  | 0.2-1.9 | 0.2-0.9 | 6-31 | 0.06-1 |

# 高炉渣化学组成

# Chemical composition of blast furnace slag

# 高炉渣矿物组成

# Mineral composition of blast furnace slag

- 高炉渣的性质取决于冷却条件。The properties of blast furnace slag depend on the cooling conditions.
- 慢速冷却的高炉渣，具有相对均衡的结晶结构，其主要矿物为钙铝黄长石、镁黄长石、钙黄长石、硫化钙、硅酸二钙等。The blast furnace slag cooled slowly has a relatively balanced crystal structure, and its main minerals are calcite, magnesia, calcite, calcium sulfide, dicalcium silicate, etc.
- 水淬急冷阻止了矿物结晶，形成大量无定形玻璃体结构或网络结构，具有较高的潜在活性。Water quenching and rapid cooling prevents mineral crystallization, forming a large amount of amorphous glass structure or network structure, which has high potential activity.

---


# 高炉渣种类

# Blast furnace slag types

# 水渣/粒化渣

Water slag/granulated slag

- 大量冷却水作用下急冷形成的海绵状浮石类玻璃体物质。
Spongy pumice-like vitreous material formed by rapid cooling under the action of a large amount of cooling water.
- 具有潜在水泥胶凝性能，是优质水泥原料。
It has potential cement gelling properties and is a high-quality cement raw material.

# 气冷渣/重矿渣

Air-cooled slag/heavy slag

- 高温熔渣在空气中自然冷却或少量淋水慢速冷却而形成的致密快渣。
Dense and fast slag formed by natural cooling of high-temperature molten slag in the air or slow cooling with a small amount of water.
- 性质与天然碎石相近，其抗压强度、稳定性、耐磨性等均符合工程要求，可代替碎石用于建筑工程中。
Its properties are similar to natural gravel, with compressive strength, stability, and wear resistance meeting engineering requirements, making it suitable to replace gravel in construction projects.

# 膨珠

Swelling beads

- 在半急冷作用并通过成珠设备击碎、抛甩到空气中，再受空气冷却形成的矿渣。
Slag formed by semi-rapid cooling, crushing through beading equipment, throwing into the air, and then cooling by air.
- 多呈球形，黑色，高孔隙度和低密度。
Mostly spherical, black, high porosity and low density.
- 具有一定的化学活性，适于作建筑用轻骨料和生产水泥。
It has certain chemical activity and is suitable for use as lightweight aggregate in construction and in producing cement.



---

高炉渣应用

# Blast furnace slag application

# 矿渣硅酸盐水泥

•  凡由硅酸盐水泥熟料和粒化高炉矿渣、适量石膏磨细制成的水硬性胶凝材料称为矿渣硅酸盐水泥（简称矿渣水泥）。

Any hydraulic cementing material made by grinding Portland cement clinker, granulated blast furnace slag and appropriate amount of gypsum is called slag Portland cement (referred to as slag cement).

•  水泥中粒化高炉矿渣掺加量按重量百分比计为20%-70%。

The amount of granulated blast furnace slag added to cement is 20%-70% by weight.

# 普通硅酸盐水泥

•  凡由硅酸盐水泥熟料、少量高炉矿渣、3-5%石膏共同研磨制成的水硬性胶凝材料。

Any hydraulic cementing material made by grinding Portland cement clinker, a small amount of blast furnace slag and 3-5% gypsum together.

•  高炉矿渣掺量不超过15%。

The amount of blast furnace slag does not exceed 15%.

# 石膏矿渣水泥

•  将干燥的矿渣、石膏、硅酸盐水泥熟料或石灰按一定比例混合磨细得到的水硬性胶凝材料。

A hydraulic cementing material obtained by mixing and grinding dry slag, gypsum, Portland cement clinker or lime in a certain proportion.

•  高炉渣配入量高达80%。

The amount of blast furnace slag added is as high as 80%.

•  石膏为硫酸盐激发剂。

Gypsum is a sulfate activator.

# 赤泥 Red Mud

- 赤泥是制铝工业提取氧化铝时排出的工业固体废弃物; Red mud is industrial solid waste discharged when the aluminum industry extracts alumina;
- 2019年全球赤泥1.6亿吨; In 2019, the global red mud was 160 million tons;
- 世界总堆存量40亿吨; The world's total stockpile is 4 billion tons;
- 中国2019年产生1.08亿吨; China produced 108 million tons in 2019;
- 4.8亿~8.7亿吨赤泥尚未处理，利用率只有4%; 480 million to 870 million tons of red mud have not yet been processed, and the utilization rate is only 4%;
- 贵州省赤泥堆存量 5000万吨，年新增排放量达 650万吨; The red mud stockpile in Guizhou Province is 50 million tons, and the annual new emissions reach 6.5 million tons;

# 从赤泥中回收铁

Iron recovery from red mud

- 铁是赤泥的主要成分，10%～45%，含量较低；
Iron is the main component of red mud, 10% to 45%, with a relatively low content;
- 预焙烧后入沸腾炉内，700 ～ 800℃还原，Fe₂O₃转变为Fe₃O₄；
After pre-roasting, it is put into a boiling furnace and reduced at 700 to 800℃, and Fe₂O₃ is converted into Fe₃O₄;
- 还原物冷却、粉碎后，湿式或干式磁选机分选，得到含铁63%～81%磁产品，铁回收率为83% ～ 93%；
After the reduced product is cooled and crushed, it is separated by a wet or dry magnetic separator to obtain a magnetic product containing 63% to 81% iron, and the iron recovery rate is 83% to 93%;
- 一种高品位的炼铁精料；
A high-grade ironmaking concentrate;
- 预焙烧，二次污染严重；
Pre-roasting, serious secondary pollution;



---


# 从赤泥中回收其它金属

# Recovery of other metals from red mud

- 回收铝、钛、钒、铬、锰等多种金属
- Recycling of various metals such as aluminum, titanium, vanadium, chromium, manganese, etc.
- 回收稀有金属，但难度很大，成本很高，二次污染严重
- Recycling of rare metals is difficult, costly, and causes serious secondary pollution.
- 必须开发高效的分离方法
- Efficient separation methods must be developed

# The blast furnace: An overview

# Blast furnace reactions

# From iron to steel

| Raw materials                      | Blast furnaces require a steady supply of iron ore and coked coal to keep running. |       |                                        |                                     |                                 |            |                    |           |                |
| ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | -------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------- | ------------------ | --------- | -------------- |
|                                    |                                                                                    |       |                                        | Iron ore                            | Waste gases                     | Coked coal | Waste gases        | Limestone |                |
|                                    |                                                                                    |       | Coked coal                             | Iron ore                            | Limestone                       | CaO/CaC₂   | Removes impurities |           |                |
|                                    |                                                                                    |       |                                        |                                     | Ar                              | Ar-10%H2   | Steelshell         | 250℃      | Reduction zone |
| Refractory (heat resistant) bricks |                                                                                    | 700℃  | C(s) + O₂(g) → CO₂(g)                  | Carbon dioxide                      | molten iron                     |            |                    |           |                |
| Slag forming zone                  |                                                                                    | 900℃  | C(s) + CO₂(g) → 2CO(g)                 | Carbon monoxide                     | Input Gas                       |            |                    |           |                |
|                                    |                                                                                    | 1700℃ | 3Fe₂O₃(s) + CO(g) → 2Fe₃O₄(s) + CO₂(g) | Fe₃O₄(s) + CO(g) → 3FeO(s) + CO₂(g) | FeO(s) + CO(g) → Fe(s) + CO₂(g) |            |                    |           |                |

Limestone removes silica impurities. It decomposes to calcium oxide (CaO) which reacts in the slag forming zone to form calcium silicate (slag, CaSiO). This forms a layer on the molten iron.

CaCO₃(s) → CaO(s) + CO₂(g)

CaO(s) + SiO₂(s/l) → CaSiO₂(s/l) Slag

# The future of blast furnaces

The blast furnace and basic oxygen furnace (BF-BOF) process requires large amounts of energy and emits significant quantities of carbon dioxide.

| 70%        | 21.4             | 2.2 tons         | water in |
| ---------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------- |
| gigajoules | per ton of steel | per ton of steel | hematite |

Alternatively, electric arc furnaces pass electricity through graphite electrodes to melt metal. These furnaces use less energy, can use scrap steel as a raw material, and have lower associated emissions.

# Solid Waste in Chemical Industry

- 化学生产行业多 ， 产品杂 。 There are many chemical production industries and various products.
- 化肥 、 农药 、 橡胶 、 染料 、 无机盐及有机化工原料等众多行业 。 Fertilizers, pesticides, rubber, dyes, inorganic salts and organic chemical raw materials and many other industries.
- 固废来源：废品、副产品、失效催化剂、废添加剂，未反应的原料及原料中夹带的杂质，精制、分离、洗涤时由相应装置排出的工艺废物，净化装置排出的粉尘 ，废水处理产生的污泥 ，化学品容器和工业垃圾。Sources of solid waste: waste, by-products, failed catalysts, waste additives, unreacted raw materials and impurities entrained in raw materials, process waste discharged by corresponding devices during refining, separation and washing, dust discharged by purification devices, sludge generated by wastewater treatment, chemical containers and industrial waste.

# 化工渣资源化

# Chemical slag resource utilization

# 硫铁矿渣 Pyrite slag

- 硫铁矿渣是硫铁矿制酸时在沸腾炉中高温熔烧后的产物。Pyrite slag is the product of high-temperature melting of pyrite in a boiling furnace when making acid.
- 硫酸工业每年约排出一千万吨废渣，除少量供水泥厂作添加剂外，大部分堆存。The sulfuric acid industry discharges about 10 million tons of waste slag every year, except for a small amount used as an additive in cement plants, most of which is stored.

| 硫铁矿      | 沸腾炉氧化焙烧               | smoke        | 净化  | 干燥         | 转化     | 吸收    | 硫酸 |
| -------- | --------------------- | ------------ | --- | ---------- | ------ | ----- | -- |
| Pyrite   | fluidized bed furnace | purification | dry | conversion | absorb | H2SO4 |    |
| 硫铁矿渣（烧渣） | 尘渣 dust               |              |     |            |        |       |    |

# 硫酸生产工艺流程 Sulfuric acid production process

当硫铁矿含硫25～35%时，生产每吨硫酸约产生0.7～1吨硫酸渣！When the sulfur content of pyrite is 25-35%, about 0.7-1 ton of sulfuric acid slag is produced for every ton of sulfuric acid!

硫铁矿渣中有价金属的回收
# Recovery of Valuable Metals from Pyrite Slag

- 含有Fe、Cu、Pb、Zn；Contains Fe, Cu, Pb, and Zn;
- Cu、Pb、Zn 与Fe的同时存在，不能炼铁；Cu, Pb, and Zn cannot be smelted when they coexist with Fe;
- 稀酸直接浸出 、 氯化焙烧综合回收 Cu、Pb、Zn ； Cu, Pb, and Zn can be recovered by direct leaching with dilute acid and chlorination roasting;
- 氯化焙烧：一定温度下，用氯化剂使原料中的目标组分转变为可凝聚相的氯化物 ， 使目标组分 分离富集 ， 分高温 、 中温 、 离析等方法 。 Chlorination roasting: At a certain temperature, a chlorinating agent is used to convert the target component in the raw material into a chloride that can be condensed, so that the target component can be separated and enriched, and the methods include high temperature, medium temperature, and separation.

硫铁矿渣用于生产建筑材料

# Pyrite slag is used to produce building materials

# ① 生产水泥 Cement production

- 利用硫酸渣代替铁矿粉 ， 降低水泥成本 Use sulphuric acid slag instead of iron ore powder to reduce cement costs
- 水泥生料中硫酸渣掺入量约为3～5% The amount of sulphuric acid slag added to cement raw materials is about 3-5%
- 每年用于水泥工业的硫酸渣 ， 大约占硫酸渣年产量的 20 ～ 25 % The sulphuric acid slag used in the cement industry each year accounts for about 20-25% of the annual output of sulphuric acid slag

---

硫铁矿渣用于生产建筑材料

# ② 制砖 Brick making

- 含铁量较低，而硅、铝含量较高的烧渣可代替粘土，掺和适量石灰，经湿碾、加压成型、自然养护制成硫酸渣砖。Slag with low iron content and high silicon and aluminum content can replace clay, mix with appropriate amount of lime, and make sulfuric acid slag bricks through wet rolling, pressure molding and natural curing.
- 生产工艺简单，不需焙烧，也不需蒸压或蒸汽养护，砖的物理性能良好。The production process is simple, no baking, autoclaving or steam curing is required, and the physical properties of the bricks are good.
- 年产1万吨硫酸的废渣，可加工渣砖600万块，减少占地5亩以上，比粘土砖节约标煤600t，成本低于粘土砖。The waste slag from an annual production of 10,000 tons of sulfuric acid can be processed into 6 million slag bricks, reducing the land area by more than 5 mu, saving 600t of standard coal compared to clay bricks, and the cost is lower than clay bricks.

磷石膏 Phosphogypsum

• 磷石膏是以磷矿石和硫酸为原料生产磷酸或其他磷酸盐过程排出的废渣，生产过程主要反应式为：

Phosphogypsum is the waste residue discharged from the process of producing phosphoric acid or other phosphates using phosphate rock and sulfuric acid as raw materials. The main reaction formula of the production process is:

Ca (PO4)2 + 5H2SO4 + 5nH2O → 3H3PO4 + 5CaSO4 ⋅ nH2O ↓ + HF

• 一般地，每生产1吨磷酸约排出4～5吨磷石膏。

Generally, about 4 to 5 tons of phosphogypsum are discharged for every ton of phosphoric acid produced.

| 磷石膏的组成，% | CaO   | SO3  | Fe2O3 | Al2O3 | SiO2 | MgO  | 总F    | 总P2O5 |
| -------- | ----- | ---- | ----- | ----- | ---- | ---- | ----- | ----- |
| 云南磷肥厂    | 29    | 41.5 | 0.07  | 0.105 | 8.5  |      | 0.304 | 2     |
| 鲁北化工厂    | 26.42 | 38.7 | 0.11  | 0.12  | 7.27 | 0.21 |       | 1     |

---

磷石膏概况 Overview of Phosphogypsum

• 磷石膏的年均排放量达7000余万吨，累计堆存量已达3亿吨以上，不仅占用大面积土地等资源，并对水、土壤、生物、大气等环境造成污染。The annual average emission of phosphogypsum reaches more than 70 million tons, and the accumulated stockpile has reached more than 300 million tons, which not only occupies a large area of land and other resources, but also pollutes the water, soil, organisms, atmosphere and other environments.

• 贵州省近年来每年磷石膏产量1700万吨，利用仅650万吨（300万吨用于建材制品、300万吨用于填充地下矿坑、50万吨用于制酸及其他用途），利用率为35%，65%堆放于乌江流域岸边，累计堆存1.2亿吨，每年持续增加。In recent years, Guizhou Province has produced 17 million tons of phosphogypsum each year, but only 6.5 million tons have been used (3 million tons for building materials, 3 million tons for filling underground mines, and 500,000 tons for acid production and other purposes), with a utilization rate of 35%. 65% is piled up on the banks of the Wujiang River Basin, with a cumulative stockpile of 120 million tons, which continues to increase every year.

Building materials industry: production of gypsum board, cement, lime, plastering materials

Chemical industry: production of ammonium sulfate, sulfuric acid, etc.

---

铬渣的利用方法 Utilization of chromium slag

• 将毒性大的Cr⁶⁺还原为毒性小的Cr³⁺，并使其生成不溶性的化合物，从而防止污染。Reduce the highly toxic Cr6+ to less toxic Cr3+ and make it generate insoluble compounds to prevent pollution.

• 将Cr⁶⁺还原为Cr³⁺的同时，进行资源化利用，使其中的铬不易被水溶出，从而避免其污染。While reducing Cr6+ to Cr3+, it is used as a resource to make the chromium in it less likely to be dissolved by water, thus avoiding its pollution.

• 铬渣在高温熔融状态下，加入活性炭粉、锯木屑等，使Cr⁶⁺还原为Cr³⁺，形成含Cr³⁺的熔体→ 冷却→综合利用。Add activated carbon powder, sawdust, etc. to the chromium slag in a high-temperature molten state to reduce Cr6+ to Cr3+, forming a melt containing Cr3+ → cooling → comprehensive utilization.

# 电石渣资源化 Calcium carbide slag resource

# 氯酸钾用途 Potassium chlorate uses：

•  制造火柴 ， 火药 ， 烟火 ， 燃料 ， 医药 ， 农业杀虫剂等工业中。 In the manufacture of matches, gunpowder, fireworks, fuel, medicine, agricultural pesticides and other industries.

# 小知识：

- •  火柴头：氧化剂(KClO₃、MnO₂)、易燃物(如硫等)、粘合剂。Match head: oxidant (KClO3, MnO2), flammable material (such as sulfur, etc.), adhesive.
- •  盒子侧面：红磷、三硫化二锑、粘合剂。Box side: red phosphorus, antimony trisulfide, adhesive.
- •  起火原因：摩擦→发热→KClO₃分解→使红磷发火→引起火柴头上的易燃物(如硫)燃烧。Cause of fire: friction → heat → KClO3 decomposition → ignition of red phosphorus → combustion of flammable material (such as sulfur) on the match head.



---

电解锰阳极渣 Electrolytic manganese anode slag

•  电解金属锰生产，Mn²⁺在阳极放电被氧化形成Mn⁴⁺氧化物（水羟锰矿），Pb-Sb-Sn-Ag四元合金阳极板脱落的铅锡亦被氧化并与锰氧化物一起沉积，形成典型的胶状构造。In the production of electrolytic manganese metal, Mn2+ is oxidized to form Mn4+ oxide (hydrogenated manganese ore) at the anode discharge, and the lead and tin detached from the Pb-Sb-Sn-Ag quaternary alloy anode plate are also oxidized and deposited together with manganese oxide to form a typical colloidal structure.

•  在清理电解槽时，阳极区无法避免会产生大量黑褐色副产物，即电解锰阳极渣平均每生产1吨电解锰，排放0.05~0.15吨电解锰阳极渣，而全国电解锰行业年产量超过10万吨，累积量已达92万吨以上。When cleaning the electrolytic cell, a large amount of black-brown by-products will inevitably be produced in the anode area, that is, for every ton of electrolytic manganese produced, 0.05-0.15 tons of electrolytic manganese anode slag will be discharged, while the annual output of the national electrolytic manganese industry exceeds 100,000 tons, and the cumulative amount has reached more than 920,000 tons.

•  电解锰阳极渣组成和结构复杂，含有Mn、Pb、Sn、Ca、Mg、Se、Fe、Cr等多种元素，难以直接被利用，其中锰含量高达40%~60%，主要以非化学计量比的MnOx（1<x<2）化合物存在。the composition="" and="" structure="" of="" electrolytic="" manganese="" anode="" slag="" are="" complex,="" containing="" multiple elements="" such="" as="" mn,="" pb,="" sn,="" ca,="" mg,="" se,="" fe,="" cr,="" etc.,="" which="" difficult="" to="" be="" directly="" used.="" the="" content="" is="" high="" 40%-60%,="" mainly="" in="" form="" non-stoichiometric="" mnox="" (1&#x3C;x&#x3C;2)="" compounds.&#x3C;="" p="">

-63-
</x<2）化合物存在。the>

电解锰阳极渣 Electrolytic manganese anode slag

我国电解金属锰年均产量约为206万吨，而电解锰渣的储量约以1000万吨/年的速度逐渐增加。The average annual output of electrolytic manganese metal in my country is about 2.06 million tons, while the reserves of electrolytic manganese slag (EMR) are gradually increasing at a rate of about 10 million tons/year.

铜仁地区锰矿资源累计查明的资源储量约 6.862 亿吨，占全国锰矿资源的 50%；The total proven reserves of manganese ore resources in Tongren area are about 686.2 million tons, accounting for 50% of the country's manganese ore resources;

铜仁市松桃县现有锰矿开采企业28家，年开采量约180万吨，电解锰企业10家，年产电解锰15万吨，产生锰渣约130万吨，锰渣库（场）20座，其中松桃河沿岸老旧锰渣库12座，锰渣存量约1390万立方米。There are 28 manganese mining enterprises in Songtao County, Tongren City, with an annual mining output of about 1.8 million tons, 10 electrolytic manganese enterprises, with an annual output of 150,000 tons of electrolytic manganese, and about 1.3 million tons of manganese slag. There are 20 manganese slag depots (fields), including 12 old manganese slag depots along the Songtao River, with a manganese slag stock of about 13.9 million cubic meters.

-64-

---

电解锰阳极渣 Electrolytic manganese anode slag

•  长期以来，电解锰企业大都在山坳处筑坝建设锰渣堆场，筑坝堆存的方式不仅占用大量土地，而且由于渣场工程建设质量、运营管理落后以及年久失修等原因，会造成大量有害物质渗透到土壤、地表水和地下水中，存在严重的环境污染和安全隐患。

For a long time, electrolytic manganese enterprises have mostly built dams in the valleys to build manganese slag dumps. The damming and storage method not only occupies a large amount of land, but also causes a large amount of harmful substances to penetrate into the soil, surface water and groundwater due to the poor quality of slag yard construction, backward operation and management, and disrepair, which poses serious environmental pollution and safety hazards.

•  近些年，因各种问题导致的渣场渗漏污染事件频繁出现，引发沅江上游松桃河跨省断面总锰和氨氮等指标超标，已经严重影响了周边区域的生态环境。

In recent years, slag yard leakage pollution incidents caused by various problems have occurred frequently, causing the total manganese and ammonia nitrogen indicators of the cross-provincial section of Songtao River in the upper reaches of Yuanjiang River to exceed the standard, which has seriously affected the ecological environment of the surrounding areas.

•  资源化利用困境: 建材利用，但重金属无法解决; 回收Mn等金属，但是废物，成本较高; 固化稳定化，做路基材料，但涉及一系列审批。

Dilemma of resource utilization: Utilization of building materials, but heavy metals cannot be solved; Recycling of metals such as Mn, but waste and high cost; Solidification and stabilization, used as roadbed materials, but involving a series of approvals.

-65-

# 有色金属资源循环

• 有色金属提取冶金的任务是从矿石、精矿、二次资源或其他物料中分离出其他伴生元素产出金属或其化合物资源化利用困境。The task of non-ferrous metal extraction metallurgy is to separate other associated elements from ores, concentrates, secondary resources or other materials to produce metals or their compounds for resource utilization.

| 矿石、精矿              | 二次资源            |
| ------------------ | --------------- |
| Ores, concentrates | 提取冶金            |
| Seco               | ndary resources |
| 金属                 | 化合物             |
| Metal              | oxides          |


---

有色金属提取冶金方法

# Metallurgical methods for extracting nonferrous metals

# 火法冶金

利用高温从矿石中提取金属或其化合物的冶金过程。此过程没有水溶液参加，故又称为干法冶金。

Pyrometallurgy: A metallurgical process that uses high temperatures to extract metals or their compounds from ores. This process does not involve aqueous solutions, so it is also called dry metallurgy.

# 湿法冶金

利用某种溶剂，借助化学反应（包括氧化、还原、中和、水解及络合等反应），对原料中的金属进行提取和分离的冶金过程。又称水法冶金。

Hydrometallurgy: A metallurgical process that uses a certain solvent and chemical reactions (including oxidation, reduction, neutralization, hydrolysis, and complexation reactions) to extract and separate metals from raw materials. It is also called hydrometallurgy.

# 电冶金

应用电能从矿石或其他原料中提取、回收和精炼金属的冶金过程。有电热法和电解法两类。

Electrometallurgy: A metallurgical process that uses electrical energy to extract, recover and refine metals from ores or other raw materials. There are two types: electrothermal and electrolytic.

电热法：是利用电能转变为热能进行冶炼的方法。

Electrothermal: A method of smelting that uses electrical energy to convert into thermal energy.

电解法：利用电化学反应，使金属从含金属盐类的溶液或熔体中析出。

Electrolytic: Uses electrochemical reactions to precipitate metals from solutions or melts containing metal salts.

-78-

---

火法冶金 Pyrometallurgy

火法冶金一般是在高温条件下进行，包括焙烧、熔炼、还原、吹炼、火法精炼、真空精炼等主要过程，同时也包括某些辅助作业如熔铸和收尘等。

Pyrometallurgy is generally carried out under high temperature conditions, including roasting, smelting, reduction, blowing, fire refining, vacuum refining and other main processes, and also includes some auxiliary operations such as melting and dust collection.

| 矿石、精矿 | 二次资源 |
| ----- | ---- |

-79-

---

湿法冶金 Hydrometallurgy

湿法冶金是在水溶液中进行的冶金过程，包括浸出、液固分离、溶液净化和溶液中金属提取等主要过程。Hydrometallurgy is a metallurgical process carried out in aqueous solution, including major processes such as leaching, liquid-solid separation, solution purification and metal extraction from solution.

矿石、精矿

二次资源

-80-

---

电冶金 Electrometallurgy

电冶金是一种利用电热或电化学反应进行的冶金过程，分为电热冶金和电化冶金两类。

Electrometallurgy is a metallurgical process that utilizes electric heat or electrochemical reactions. It is divided into two categories: electrothermal metallurgy and electrochemical metallurgy.

电化冶金按电解质分为水溶液电解和熔盐电解，按电解目的分为电解提取和电解精炼。

Electrochemical metallurgy is divided into aqueous solution electrolysis and molten salt electrolysis according to the electrolyte, and into electrolytic extraction and electrolytic refining according to the purpose of electrolysis.

| 矿石、精矿 | 二次资源 | 耐火材料熔炼电弧炉 | 电单 |
| ----- | ---- | --------- | -- |

1.炭块

2.侧爪

3.铝导杆

4.阳极母线

5.阴极炭块组

6.槽罩

铝电解槽是练铝的主要设备，本幅绘示的是预培阳极电解槽的级剖面示意图，(1)阳极装置。一般有1020个阳极炭块组，每个炭块组包括发块、钢爪和铝导杆，(2)别极装置和电解槽内村。阴极装置就是阴极炭块组。

(3)槽壳、槽罩。

(4)导电系统。

(5)电解时的化学反应。

-81-

---

有色金属冶炼 Nonferrous metal smelting

• 有色金属的提取冶金常根据原料的不同和对产品的要求，采用两种或三种方法相互配合组成的提取流程。The extraction and metallurgy of non-ferrous metals often adopts an extraction process composed of two or three methods according to the different raw materials and the requirements for the products.

• 大多数的重金属如铜、铅、锡，镍、钴等多采用以火法冶金方法制取粗金属，以电化冶金方法制取纯金属的提取流程。Most heavy metals such as copper, lead, tin, nickel, cobalt, etc. are mostly extracted by pyrometallurgy to produce crude metals and electrochemical metallurgy to produce pure metals.

• 轻金属多采用以湿法冶金方法制取金属纯化合物，以电冶金方法制取粗金属和纯金属的提取流程。Light metals are mostly extracted by hydrometallurgy to produce pure metal compounds and electrometallurgy to produce crude metals and pure metals.

-82-

---

有色金属冶炼 Nonferrous metal smelting

• 大多数稀有金属采用以湿法冶金方法制取纯金属化合物，以火法冶金方法或电冶金方法制取纯金属的提取流程。 Most rare metals are extracted by hydrometallurgy to produce pure metal compounds and pyrometallurgy or electrometallurgy to produce pure metals.

• 贵金属中的金、银多采用以湿法冶金方法富集化合物，以火法冶金方法制取金属和以电冶金产出纯金属的提取流程。Gold and silver in precious metals are mostly extracted by hydrometallurgy to enrich compounds, pyrometallurgy to produce metals and electrometallurgy to produce pure metals.

-83-

---

提取冶金工艺过程 Extractive Metallurgical Processes

有色金属种类繁多，一般常需经过三个主要步骤 There are many types of non-ferrous metals, which generally go through three main steps:

| 步骤 1 step   | 步骤 2 step             | 步骤 3 step           |
| ----------- | --------------------- | ------------------- |
| 矿物 Minerals | 金属制取 Metal production | 金属精炼 Metal Refining |

1. Mineral decomposition and compound preparation

2. Metal production

3. Metal Refining

-85-

---

有色金属冶炼 Nonferrous metal smelting

# 1. 矿物分解和化合物制取 Mineral decomposition and compound preparation

- 破坏矿物稳定结构，并使其中欲提取的金属与主要伴生元素分离的过程 The process of destroying the stable structure of the mineral and separating the metal to be extracted from the main associated elements.
- 焙烧、造锍熔炼或浸出等方法 The process involves roasting, matte smelting or leaching.
- 经过挥发和冷凝 （或经进一步处理 ）使之或转入锍相或转入水溶液而达到与主要伴生元素分离的目的 After volatilization and condensation (or further treatment), it is either transferred to the matte phase or to an aqueous solution to achieve separation from the main associated elements.

| Off-gas Hood | Feed              | Slinger |
| ------------ | ----------------- | ------- |
| Gaspo        | Puncher           | Tuyeres |
| Matte Ladle  | Slag Ladle Hauler |         |



---

有色金属冶炼 Nonferrous metal smelting

# ② 金属制取 Metal production

- 将精矿或金属化合物还原产出粗金属的过程；The process of reducing concentrate or metal compounds to produce crude metal;
- 还原熔炼以及金属热还原法、碳还原法、氢还原法等；Reduction smelting and metal thermal reduction, carbon reduction, hydrogen reduction, etc.;
- 在高温下将以精矿或金属化合物形式存在的金属还原产出粗金属，而与进入渣中的伴生元素分离；Reducing metal in the form of concentrate or metal compound at high temperature to produce crude metal, and separating it from the associated elements entering the slag;
- 通过熔盐电解、水溶液电解或置换等方法将金属化合物还原产出金属。

# AR/OXYGENANOCEL/NATURALGA

Oil or natural gas can be injected down the lance with combustion air. Air can be enriched with oxygen.

# OFFGAS AND FUME

The stationary furnace design allows efficient offgas collection and maximises offgas strength while minimising solids carryover.

# AGGLOMERATED PATENTED LANCE DESIGN

The submerged lance design promotes formation of a frozen slag layer on the lance tip.

# FROZEN SLAG COATING

The slag coating the lance tip protects it from wear by the molten bath.

# REFRACTORY LINED FURNACE

Simple furnace design reduces capital cost and permits easier refractory installation.

# VIGOROUSLY STIRRED BATH

The submerged lance creates a highly agitated bath ensuring rapid chemical reactions and good mixing.

# TAPHOLE

One or more water-cooled tapholes can be used for molten product removal.

-87-

---

有色金属冶炼 Nonferrous metal smelting

# ③ 金属精炼 Metal Refining

- 脱除粗金属中杂质，产出纯金属的过程 The process of removing impurities from crude metals to produce pure metals
- 火法精炼和电解精炼 Fire refining and electrolytic refining
- 前者是在精炼炉内，大多于物料熔融的高温条件下进行 The former is carried out in a refining furnace, mostly under high temperature conditions where the material is molten
- 后者是在电解槽内，于电解质中，通直流电使粗金属阳极溶解而在阴极析出精金属的过程 The latter is a process in an electrolytic cell, in an electrolyte, where direct current is passed to dissolve the crude metal at the anode and precipitate the refined metal at the cathode

-88-

---

工业固废处置及资源化存在的问题和发展方向

# Problems and development direction of industrial solid waste disposal and resource utilization

- 工业固废和电子废物，长期采用湿法冶金，但效果不佳。
Industrial solid waste and electronic waste have long been treated with hydrometallurgy, but the effect is not good.
- 火法冶金正在重新受到重视。高温处理过程中，重金属和有价元素挥发、冷却后收集，高温残渣作为原料生产水泥，流程短，如果烟气能够利用，就非常理想了。
Pyrometallurgy is regaining attention. During the high-temperature treatment process, heavy metals and valuable elements are volatilized, cooled and collected, and the high-temperature residue is used as a raw material to produce cement. The process is short, and if the flue gas can be used, it would be very ideal.
- 大宗工业固废，把有害元素分离后，生产建筑材料，是个方向，但成本、质量、附加值都需要综合考虑。
For bulk industrial solid waste, separating harmful elements and producing building materials is a direction, but cost, quality and added value need to be considered comprehensively.
- 分离技术与设备创新，是关键。废物，都是混合物，需要从复合体分离为单体，才有利用价值。
Separation technology and equipment innovation are the key. Waste is a mixture, and it needs to be separated from the complex into monomers before it has utilization value.

-89-

- 2019全球5360万吨 53.6 million tons of e-waste in the world in 2019

传统处理二次污染严重

Traditional treatment leads to serious secondary pollution

Groundwater, air, and soil pollution

hazards of POPs and heavy metals

POPs are concentrated in crops, fish, and breast milk

Carrying out resource utilization and harmless treatment and disposal of electronic waste is an inevitable choice for China to achieve sustainable development and a major national resource strategic demand.

国家废弃电器电子产品处理目录（2014年版）
# National Catalogue of Waste Electrical and Electronic Products (2014 Edition)

| 电冰箱 fridge                 | 冷藏冷冻箱（柜）、冷冻箱（柜）、冷藏箱（柜）及其他具有制冷系统，消耗能量以获取冷量的隔热箱体（容积≤800升）                                                       |
| -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 空气调节器 air conditioner      | 整体式空调器（窗式、穿墙式等）、分体式空调器（挂壁式、落地式等）、一拖多空调器等制冷量在14000W及以下（一拖多空调时，按室外机制冷量计算）的房间空气调节器具                              |
| 吸油烟机 range hood            | 深型吸排油烟机、欧式塔型吸排油烟机、侧吸式吸排油烟机和其他安装在炉灶上部，用于收集、处理被污染空气的电动器具                                                        |
| 洗衣机 washing machine        | 波轮式洗衣机、滚筒式洗衣机、搅拌式洗衣机、脱水机及其他依靠机械作用洗涤衣物（含兼有干衣功能）的器具（干衣量≤10公斤）                                                   |
| 电热水器 Electric water heater | 储水式电热水器、快热式电热水器和其他将电能转换为热能，并将热能传递给水，使水产生一定温度的器具（容量≤500升）                                                      |
| 燃气热水器 Gas water heater     | 以燃气作为燃料，通过燃烧加热方式将热量传递到流经热交换器的冷水中以达到制备热水目的的一种燃气用具（热负荷≤70kw）                                                    |
| 打印机 Printer                | 激光打印机、喷墨打印机、针式打印机、热敏打印机和其他与计算机联机工作或利用云打印平台，将数字信息转换成文字和图像并以硬拷贝形式输出的设备，包括以打印功能为主，兼有其他功能设备（印刷幅面\<A2，印刷速度≤80张/分钟） |
| 复印机 copier                 | 静电复印机、喷墨复印机和其他用各种不同成像过程产生原稿复印品的设备，包括以复印功能为主，兼有其他功能的设备（印刷幅面\<A2，印刷速度≤80张/分钟）                                   |
| 传真机 fax machine            | 利用扫描和光电变换技术，把文字、图表、相片等静止图像变换成电信号发送出去，接收时以记录形式获取复制稿的通信终端设备，包括以传真功能为主，兼有其他功能的设备                                 |
| 电视机 TV                     | 阴极射线管（黑白、彩色）电视机、等离子电视机、液晶电视机、OLED电视机、背投电视机、移动电视接收终端及其他含有电视调谐器（高频头）的用于接收信号并还原出图像及伴音的终端设备                       |
| 监视器 monitor                | 阴极射线管（黑白、彩色）监视器、液晶监视器等由显示器件为核心组成的图像输出设备（不含高频头）                                                                |
| 微型计算机 laptop               | 台式微型计算机（含一体机）和便携式微型计算机（含平板电脑、掌上电脑）等信息事务处理实体                                                                   |
| 移动通信手持机 mobile phone       | GSM手持机、CDMA手持机、SCDMA手持机、3G手持机、4G手持机、小灵通等手持式的，通过蜂窝网络的电磁波发送或接收两地讲话或其他声音、图像、数据的设备                                |
| 电话单机 telephone             | PSTN普通电话机、网络电话机（IP电话机）、特种电话机和其他通信中实现声能与电能相互转换的用户设备                                                            |

基金补贴滞后，而对于企业来说，如果没有基金补贴，则处于亏损状态。Fund subsidies are delayed, and for enterprises, if there are no fund subsidies, they will be in a loss-making state.

2012年-2019年，基金测算的回收拆解量是1.64亿台，而实际回收拆解总量接近5.2亿台。From 2012 to 2019, the fund estimated the recycling and dismantling volume to be 164 million units, while the actual total recycling and dismantling volume was close to 520 million units.

# Hazardous substances in electronic waste

| 铍：                           | 存在于电路板基板、接头                                               |
| ---------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| Beryllium:                   | Found in circuit board substrates and connectors          |
| 水银：                          | 存在于电子元器件                                                  |
| Mercury:                     | present in electronic components                          |
| 铅：                           | 存在于电路板焊接处                                                 |
| Lead:                        | Found in solder joints on circuit boards                  |
| 铬：                           | 存在于电子元器件防腐                                                |
| Chromium:                    | present in electronic components for corrosion protection |
| 镉：                           | 存在于薄片电阻                                                   |
| Cadmium:                     | present in sheet resistors                                |
| 溴化阻燃剂：                       | 存在于电路板                                                    |
| Brominated flame retardants: | found in circuit boards                                   |

# 大量使用的溴系阻燃剂

# Brominated flame retardants are widely used

| 电脑  | 电冰箱  |
| --- | ---- |
| 3kg | 5kg  |
| 电视机 | 手机   |
| 4kg | 100g |

• 大量使用，全球总用量250~300kt/a Used in large quantities, with a global total of 250~300kt/a

• 难降解，毒性大 ，存在于拆解、分选各环节 Difficult to degrade, highly toxic, present in all stages of disassembly and sorting

500,000 tons

# 环境健康风险

# Electronic waste collection, treatment and disposal processes

环境健康风险！



---


# 新型光电材料使用的有机物

# Organic substances used in new optoelectronic materials

- 多环芳香化合物并五苯 ， 蒽等
- Polycyclic aromatic compounds such as pentacene and anthracene
- 对神经系统血液系统及孕妇胎儿等具有致畸甚至致死作用
- Has teratogenic and even lethal effects on the nervous system, blood system, pregnant women and fetuses
- 蒽对皮肤、粘膜有刺激性；易引起光感性皮炎
- Anthracene is irritating to the skin and mucous membranes; it is easy to cause photosensitivity dermatitis

# 高3-己基噻吩，高P-次苯基次亚乙烯基及聚乙炔和它们的派生物 （如聚吡咯和聚苯胺）

# High 3-hexylthiophene, high P-phenylene vinylene and polyacetylene and their derivatives (such as polypyrrole and polyaniline)

- 高 3-己基噻吩易燃 ， 刺激眼睛 、 呼吸系统和皮肤
- High 3-hexylthiophene is flammable and irritating to the eyes, respiratory system and skin
- 苯胺毒性较大属致癌物质
- Aniline is highly toxic and a carcinogen

# 分选 Sorting

- 涡流分选：利用涡电流力分离金属和非金属的方法，特别适用于轻金属材料与比重相近的塑料材料之间的分离。
Eddy current separation: A method of separating metals and non-metals using eddy current force, especially suitable for separating light metal materials from plastic materials with similar specific gravity.
- 静电分选：分离有色金属和塑料方法；金属回收效率较高，90%以上。
Electrostatic separation: A method of separating non-ferrous metals and plastics; the metal recovery efficiency is relatively high, more than 90%.

废弃印刷电路板概述

# Overview of Waste Printed Circuit Boards

- 线路板是电子电器产品的核心部件，废线路板属于危险废物，在国家危险废物名录中代码为 900-045-49 。

Circuit boards are the core components of electronic and electrical products. Waste circuit boards are classified as hazardous wastes, and the code in the national hazardous waste list is 900-045-49.
- 线路板类型复杂，种类多样，组成线路板的各种构件和物质的含量相差较大，但大致可归纳为三大类。

Circuit boards are complex and diverse in types. The contents of various components and materials that make up circuit boards vary greatly, but they can be roughly classified into three categories.
- 金属材料：90%以上为Cu，含有少量Ag、Pd、Au等贵金属；
- 有机高聚物：含卤素的环氧树脂等；
- 陶瓷和玻璃纤维：主要成分为硅及铝氧化物。



---

废弃印刷电路板概述
# Overview of Waste Printed Circuit Boards

• 未有效地回收处理，会对环境造成二次污染。线路板中的金属种类相当于普通矿物中金属种类的几十倍至上百倍，金属的含量高达40%以上，最多的是铜，此外还有金、银、钯、锡、镍、铅等金属，其中，稀有贵金属远高于自然界中的富矿金属含量，故有“城市矿山”之称。

If not effectively recycled, it will cause secondary pollution to the environment. The types of metals in circuit boards are dozens to hundreds of times the types of metals in ordinary minerals, and the metal content is as high as more than 40%, with copper being the most abundant. In addition, there are gold, silver, palladium, tin, nickel, lead and other metals. Among them, the content of rare and precious metals is much higher than that of rich ore metals in nature, so it is called "urban mine".

-39-

---

国内废弃印刷电路板的来源

# Sources of domestic waste printed circuit boards

# ① 电子电器产品的报废

Disposal of electronic and electrical products

- 报废的电子电器产品是废线路板的主要来源。自20世纪50年代以来，线路板被逐步应用于各类电子产品中，随着电子电器产品报废或更新，大量的线路板被淘汰。
Scrapped electronic and electrical products are the main source of waste circuit boards. Since the 1950s, circuit boards have been gradually used in various electronic products. As electronic and electrical products are scrapped or updated, a large number of circuit boards have been eliminated.
- 联合国环境规划署统计资料显示，2018年，全球共有4850万吨废弃的电子电器产品，然而其中仅有 15%～20% 得到了回收利用，而且电子电器废弃物的产生量会以年8%的速度增长。
According to statistics from the United Nations Environment Programme, in 2018, there were 48.5 million tons of discarded electronic and electrical products in the world, but only 15% to 20% of them were recycled, and the amount of electronic and electrical waste generated will increase at an annual rate of 8%.
- 2018年，中国电子电器废弃物的产生量就接近800万吨，增长速度为14%，远高于世界平均水平。不同的电子电器产品中，线路板占比2%～20%不等。以占比5%估计，全球每年产生约200万吨废旧线路板，中国则每年产生40万吨废旧线路板。
In 2018, the amount of electronic and electrical waste generated in China was close to 8 million tons, with a growth rate of 14%, far higher than the world average. Among different electronic and electrical products, circuit boards account for 2% to 20%. Based on an estimated proportion of 5%, the world produces about 2 million tons of waste circuit boards each year, and China produces 400,000 tons of waste circuit boards each year.

-40-

---


# 国内废弃印刷电路板的来源

# Sources of domestic waste printed circuit boards

# ② 线路板生产过程中产生的副产品及边角料

By-products and scraps generated during the production of circuit boards

- 线路板生产过程中的副产品及边角料，是废旧线路板的重要组成部分。
- Byproducts and scraps in the production process of circuit boards are an important part of waste circuit boards.
- 我国已经成为全球最大的线路板生产基地，PCB市场产值居全球第一。据预测，到2023年，我国PCB产值占全球份额将达54.3%。
- China has become the world's largest circuit board production base, and the PCB market output value ranks first in the world. It is predicted that by 2023, my country's PCB output value will account for 54.3% of the global share.
- 生产过程损耗率接近10%，我国线路板生产企业每年产生的废旧线路板及边角料在70万～100万吨。
- The loss rate in the production process is close to 10%, and my country's circuit board manufacturers generate 700,000 to 1 million tons of waste circuit boards and scraps each year.



---

国内废弃印刷电路板的来源

# Sources of domestic waste printed circuit boards

# ③ 国外入境的废线路板

Waste circuit boards imported from abroad were once part of my country's waste circuit boards.

废弃电子电器产品具有资源属性，一直被作为矿产资源进行国际贸易。由于发达国家环保法规要求严格、人力成本高，长期以来，国际上一直存在发达国家向发展中国家输出电子废弃物的现象。

Waste electronic and electrical products have resource attributes and have always been traded internationally as mineral resources. Due to the strict environmental protection regulations and high labor costs in developed countries, there has long been a phenomenon of developed countries exporting electronic waste to developing countries.

-42-

---

国内废弃印刷电路板的来源

# Sources of domestic waste printed circuit boards

# ③ 国外入境的废线路板

- 中国一直是电子废弃物的最大进口国，中国环境会固废分会报道资料显示，2011～2016年，全球70%的电子废弃物最终都流入我国。
China has always been the largest importer of electronic waste. According to the report of the Solid Waste Branch of the China Environmental Association, 70% of the world's electronic waste eventually flowed into my country from 2011 to 2016.
- 2017年，国务院印发了《关于禁止洋废弃物入境推进固体废物进口管理制度改革实施方案》，方案实施以来，我国对电子废弃物加大了进口检查力度，严厉打击非法电子废弃物的走私行为，并对各地的电子废弃物处置企业进行清理整顿。
In 2017, the State Council issued the "Implementation Plan on Prohibiting the Entry of Foreign Waste and Promoting the Reform of the Solid Waste Import Management System". Since the implementation of the plan, my country has increased the inspection of electronic waste imports, cracked down on the smuggling of illegal electronic waste, and cleaned up and rectified electronic waste disposal companies in various places.

-43-

---

国内废弃印刷电路板处理现状
# Current status of domestic waste printed circuit board treatment

废线路板通常会经过破碎、分选或者焚烧、高温热解等预处理过程，将铜等金属物质与废塑料、废树脂等分离，然后再对金属物质进行火法冶炼或湿法冶炼，回收铜、贵金属等。

Waste circuit boards usually undergo pre-treatment processes such as crushing, sorting or incineration, high-temperature pyrolysis, etc. to separate metal materials such as copper from waste plastics and waste resins, and then the metal materials are subjected to pyrometallurgy or hydrometallurgy to recover copper, precious metals, etc.

| 印刷线路板粉碎料 | Comminuted PCB                 |
| -------- | ------------------------------ |
| 印刷线路板    | Printed Circuit Boards (PCB）   |
| 金属富集体    | Separated Metal Components     |
| 非金属富集体   | Separated Non-metal Components |

-44-

---

废弃印刷电路板预处理
# Pretreatment of waste printed circuit boards

# ① 物理机械法 Physical and mechanical method

- 物理机械法的主要环节是机械破碎，在实际应用中，破碎级数从 2～4 级不等。由于废电路板有一定硬度，金属有延展性，所以破碎设备需同时带有剪切功能。
The main part of the physical and mechanical method is mechanical crushing. In practical applications, the crushing levels range from 2 to 4. Since waste circuit boards have a certain hardness and metals are ductile, the crushing equipment must also have a shearing function.
- 常用的破碎设备有切碎机、剪切式破碎机、锤式粉碎机、磨碎机等，经过一级破碎，板子尺寸可降到1～10mm；经过两级破碎，可降低到1mm以下，金属与非金属的解离度高达90%。
Commonly used crushing equipment includes shredders, shear crushers, hammer crushers, grinders, etc. After one-stage crushing, the board size can be reduced to 1-10 mm; after two-stage crushing, it can be reduced to less than 1 mm, and the degree of dissociation between metals and non-metals is as high as 90%.
- 经过破碎后，再使用分选的办法将金属与非金属分离。分选是根据破碎颗粒的物理差异，使用摇床、气流、静电等方式，分选出来的铜等金属颗粒作为铜冶炼的原料，非金属颗粒常采用填埋的方式进行处理。
After crushing, the metal and non-metal are separated by sorting. Sorting is based on the physical differences of the crushed particles, using shaking tables, airflow, static electricity, etc., and the copper and other metal particles are sorted out as raw materials for copper smelting, and non-metal particles are often treated by landfill.

-45-

---

国内废弃印刷电路板处理现状
# Current status of domestic waste printed circuit board treatment

# ② 焚烧处理 Incineration treatment

- 焚烧处理法是目前应用较为广泛的废线路板预处理方法。Incineration treatment is currently the most widely used pretreatment method for waste circuit boards.
- 焚烧法处理是指废弃物中的可燃物在焚烧炉中与氧进行燃烧的过程，处理流程是先将废弃物经机械破碎至2～5cm后，送入一次焚化炉中焚烧，将其所含约40%的树脂分解、破坏，使有机物与固体物分离，剩余残渣即为裸露的金属及玻璃纤维，经粉碎后即可送往金属冶炼厂进行金属回收，有机气体则送入二次焚化炉进一步燃烧处理。Incineration treatment refers to the process in which the combustibles in the waste are burned with oxygen in an incinerator. The treatment process is to first mechanically crush the waste to 2-5 cm, and then send it to a primary incinerator for incineration, decompose and destroy the resin containing about 40% of it, separate the organic matter from the solid matter, and the remaining residue is the exposed metal and glass fiber. After crushing, it can be sent to a metal smelter for metal recovery, and the organic gas is sent to a secondary incinerator for further combustion treatment.

-46-

---

国内废弃印刷电路板处理现状

# Current status of domestic waste printed circuit board treatment

# ② 焚烧处理 Incineration treatment

焚烧法主要是用来回收废线路板中的金属，其优点是工艺简单、耗时短，能够实现废线路板的减容减量，但是焚烧过程中环境风险较高，废线路板中的有机物不能被充分利用。

The incineration method is mainly used to recover metals from waste circuit boards. Its advantages are simple process, short time consumption, and can achieve volume reduction and reduction of waste circuit boards. However, the environmental risk during the incineration process is high, and the organic matter in the waste circuit boards cannot be fully utilized.

-47-

---

# 国内废弃印刷电路板处理现状

# Current status of domestic waste printed circuit board treatment

# ③ 高温热解 High-temperature pyrolysis

- 热解法处理是对焚烧法处理的一种有效改进，利用热解将废线路板中的有机物热裂解，
可回收可燃油气及金属物质。The pyrolysis treatment is an effective improvement on the
incineration treatment. The organic matter in the waste circuit board is thermally cracked by
pyrolysis, and the recyclable oil, gas and metal substances can be recovered.
- 与焚烧处理不同，真空热解过程是在无氧的条件下进行的，因此可以抑制二噁英、呋喃
的产生，废气产量少，对环境污染小。Unlike incineration treatment, the vacuum pyrolysis
process is carried out under oxygen-free conditions, so the production of dioxins and furans can
be suppressed, the waste gas output is small, and the environmental pollution is small.
- 热解法为在封闭反应器中进行废线路板的热解反应，使用惰性气体作为保护气体，使用
恒定流速通过热解产生的热解气体，经冷凝系统进入管道并冷凝，以获得热解油。The
pyrolysis method is to carry out the pyrolysis reaction of the waste circuit board in a closed reactor,
using inert gas as the protective gas, and the pyrolysis gas generated by pyrolysis at a constant
flow rate enters the pipeline through the condensation system and condenses to obtain pyrolysis
oil.

---

废弃印刷电路板的冶炼

# Smelting of waste printed circuit boards

# ① 湿法冶炼

- 利用强酸或强碱将PCB板中铜、金、银等金属浸出，然后对浸出溶液进行电解或加入沉淀剂，将有价金属分次回收。
Use strong acid or alkali to leach copper, gold, silver and other metals from PCB boards, then electrolyze the leached solution or add a precipitant to recover the valuable metals in batches.
- 湿法冶炼技术回收成本高，腐蚀性和毒性较大，工艺复杂，化学试剂消耗量大，后续处理难，对设备要求高，易造成二次污染，若处理不当还会对水资源造成严重的污染。因此，在废线路板处理过程中主要是贵金属的分离和提纯。
Wet smelting technology has high recovery costs, high corrosiveness and toxicity, complex processes, large consumption of chemical reagents, difficult subsequent treatment, high requirements for equipment, and is prone to secondary pollution. If improperly handled, it will also cause serious pollution to water resources. Therefore, the main focus of waste circuit board processing is the separation and purification of precious metals.

-49-

---

废弃印刷电路板的冶炼

# Smelting of waste printed circuit boards

# ① 湿法冶炼

印刷线路板上的电路由厚膜工艺制作，厚膜金基浆料中的金、银、钯、铂等是以微粒的形式悬浮于有机载体中，浆料中还含有无机黏结剂，通常是硼硅酸盐玻璃及Al₂O₃、CuO、CdO、ZnO、TiO₂、NiO 等氧化物。

The circuits on the printed circuit board are made by thick film technology. The gold, silver, palladium, platinum, etc. in the thick film gold-based paste are suspended in the form of particles in an organic carrier. The paste also contains inorganic binders, usually borosilicate glass and oxides such as Al2O3, CuO, CdO, ZnO, TiO2, and NiO.

将电子废料在高温400℃预热，可使有机物分解除去。再用硝酸溶解金属氧化物，过滤，可得含银及其他有色金属的硝酸盐溶液 ， 电解回收银。

Preheating electronic waste at a high temperature of 400°C can decompose and remove organic matter. Then dissolve the metal oxides with nitric acid and filter them to obtain a nitrate solution containing silver and other non-ferrous metals, and recover silver by electrolysis.

金、铂不溶于硝酸，仍在线路板上，可用王水溶解、过滤，滤液蒸发、用水稀释，然后用无水亚硫酸钠还原沉淀金，溶液中的钯、铂则用萃取剂萃取回收。

Gold and platinum are insoluble in nitric acid and are still on the circuit board. They can be dissolved and filtered with aqua regia, the filtrate evaporated, diluted with water, and then the precipitated gold is reduced with anhydrous sodium sulfite, and the palladium and platinum in the solution are extracted and recovered with an extractant.

-50-

---

废弃印刷电路板的冶炼

# Smelting of waste printed circuit boards

# 火法冶炼： 处理废线路板的主要方法

Pyrometallurgy: The main method for treating waste circuit boards includes two processes:

- 一种是废电路板焚烧，然后再加入转炉或阳极炉进行精炼；废电路板在焚烧炉内 800℃燃烧，有机物几乎全部分解，金属和玻璃纤维不熔化。One is to incinerate the waste circuit boards and then add them to the converter or anode furnace for refining; the waste circuit boards are burned at 800℃ in the incinerator, and the organic matter is almost completely decomposed, and the metal and glass fiber are not melted.
- 烟气在经过一个二次燃烧室，温度达到1200℃以上，使二噁英彻底分解，然后高温烟气在几秒钟内骤冷到 400℃ 以下，避免二噁英再次合成。The flue gas passes through a secondary combustion chamber, and the temperature reaches above 1200℃, which completely decomposes the dioxins. Then the high-temperature flue gas is quenched to below 400℃ in a few seconds to prevent the dioxins from being synthesized again.

-51-

---

废弃印刷电路板的冶炼

# Smelting of waste printed circuit boards

# 火法冶炼： 处理废线路板的主要方法

Pyrometallurgy: The main method for treating waste circuit boards includes two processes:

焚烧后的玻璃纤维和金属再进入转炉或阳极炉中进行精炼，最终得到金属铜。The incinerated glass fiber and metal are then refined in the converter or anode furnace to finally obtain metallic copper.

该工艺有机物处理彻底，有效解决了二噁英的问题；焚烧后的产物既可以自己精炼，也可以销售给铜冶炼厂作为配料，铜回收率高达98%以上，焚烧过程可以实现自热。This process thoroughly treats organic matter and effectively solves the problem of dioxins; the incinerated products can be refined by themselves or sold to copper smelters as ingredients. The copper recovery rate is as high as more than 98%, and the incineration process can achieve self-heating.

但是，二次燃烧需提供额外热源，而且，焚烧和熔炼不在同一个系统内进行，不利于能量的综合利用。However, secondary combustion requires an additional heat source, and incineration and smelting are not carried out in the same system, which is not conducive to the comprehensive utilization of energy.

-52-

---

废弃印刷电路板的冶炼

# 1. 火法冶炼

直接冶炼，包括鼓风炉、卡尔多炉、顶吹炉等；废线路板经过脱锡直接进入鼓风炉，加入适量焦炭做还原剂、石灰等造渣剂；在1200～1300℃条件下进行还原气氛熔炼，最后产出黑铜或粗铜，含铜量80%～90%，稀贵金属进入粗铜，大部分锡在还原气氛下也进入粗铜，铅则进入渣和烟气中。

Direct smelting, including blast furnace, Kaldo furnace, top-blown furnace, etc.; waste circuit boards are directly put into blast furnace after detinning, and appropriate amount of coke is added as reducing agent, lime and other slag-forming agents; smelting is carried out under reducing atmosphere at 1200-1300℃, and finally black copper or crude copper is produced, with a copper content of 80%-90%, rare and precious metals enter crude copper, most of tin also enters crude copper under reducing atmosphere, and lead enters slag and flue gas.

粗铜再经过火法精炼、电解精炼，可得到铜含量99.95%以上的阴极铜，而电解阳极泥富集了金银等贵金属，然后使用火法+湿法工艺从阳极泥里综合回收贵金属。

Crude copper is then refined by pyrometallurgy and electrolytic refining to obtain cathode copper with a copper content of more than 99.95%, while electrolytic anode mud is enriched with precious metals such as gold and silver, and then precious metals are comprehensively recovered from anode mud using pyrometallurgy + hydrometallurgy process.

熔炼渣后经过水淬，可作为水泥原料。

After smelting slag, it is quenched in water and can be used as cement raw material.

-53-

---

废弃印刷电路板的冶炼

# ③ 废线路板与其他物料的协同处置

废线路板与废杂铜协同冶炼：废线路板和废杂铜中的金属赋存状态类似，为协同处置提供了条件。从热平衡上来说，废线路板中含有多种塑料及可燃物，热值较高，单独处理废线路板热量过剩，通过与废杂铜协同处置，可达到热平衡。

Co-smelting of waste circuit boards and scrap copper: The metal occurrence states in waste circuit boards and scrap copper are similar, which provides conditions for co-disposal. From the perspective of thermal balance, waste circuit boards contain a variety of plastics and combustibles with high calorific value. The heat of waste circuit boards alone is excessive, and thermal balance can be achieved through co-disposal with scrap copper.

废线路板与电镀污泥协同处置：电镀污泥属于危险固废，必须妥善处置。电镀污泥的特点是水分含量高，同时含有铜、镍等有价金属；废线路板与电镀污泥协同处置可有效利用过剩的热量，在弱还原气氛下，产出粗铜。电镀污泥得到无害化处置，同时还回收了铜、镍等有价金属。

Co-disposal of waste circuit boards and electroplating sludge: Electroplating sludge is a hazardous solid waste and must be properly disposed of. Electroplating sludge is characterized by high moisture content and contains valuable metals such as copper and nickel; the co-disposal of waste circuit boards and electroplating sludge can effectively utilize excess heat and produce crude copper in a weak reducing atmosphere. Electroplating sludge is harmlessly disposed of, and valuable metals such as copper and nickel are also recovered.

-54-

废线路板“全组分-短流程-绿色资源化”关键技术

# Key technologies of "full components - short process - green resource utilization" for waste circuit boards

# 滚筒卧式微生物浸出反应器

对元器件与废线路板基板内有色金属进行浸出，有色金属的浸出率达99%以上，浸出液可循环利用。

Drum horizontal microbial leaching reactor: leaching non-ferrous metals in components and waste circuit board substrates, the leaching rate of non-ferrous metals is more than 99%, and the leaching solution can be recycled.

# 纳米催化技术提升嗜酸性硫杆菌的浸出效率

氮掺杂碳纳米管(N-CNTs)、石墨烯等可提升嗜酸性硫杆菌浸出废线路板中有色金属的浸出速率达20%以上。

Nanocatalytic technology improves the leaching efficiency of acidophilic thiobacillus: nitrogen-doped carbon nanotubes (N-CNTs), graphene, etc. can increase the leaching rate of non-ferrous metals in waste circuit boards leached by acidophilic thiobacillus by more than 20%.

# 混合浸出

微生物浸出液多金属直接分步电沉积调控工艺技术：基于旋流电沉积可实现锌、铜直接电沉积效率90%以上，并通过调整溶液pH值，进一步实现镍的分步电沉积，避免废水排放。

Microbial leaching solution multi-metal direct step-by-step electrodeposition control process technology: based on cyclonic electrodeposition, the direct electrodeposition efficiency of zinc and copper can be achieved above 90%, and by adjusting the pH value of the solution, the step-by-step electrodeposition of nickel can be further achieved to avoid wastewater discharge.

# 旋流电沉积技术

消除浓差极化，提高效率实现传统电积中的边界层

低浓度条件下的多金属（铜、镍等）分步提取。

# Cyclone electrodeposition technology

eliminates concentration polarization, improves efficiency and realizes step-by-step extraction of multiple metals (copper, nickel, etc.) under low concentration conditions.
