# Key technologies of "full components - short process - green resource utilization" for waste circuit boards

# 贵金属短流程高值资源化

# Short-process high-value resource conversion of precious metals

废线路板基板微生物浸出后的滤渣 ，电化学溶解法选择性浸出其中的金、钯，浸出率均达99.0%以上。

The filter residue after microbial leaching of waste circuit board substrates is selectively leached with gold and palladium by electrochemical dissolution, with leaching rates exceeding 99.0%.

引入选择性过滤膜，经分离浓缩、调节pH值后，浸出液可直接用于制备贵金属盐、催化剂前驱体或催化剂（如三苯基膦氯化金催化剂、四氟硼酸银等）。

After the selective filtration membrane is introduced, the leached liquid can be directly used to prepare precious metal salts, catalyst precursors or catalysts (such as triphenylphosphine gold chloride catalyst, silver tetrafluoroborate, etc.) after separation and concentration and pH adjustment.

# 非贵金属短流程高值资源化

# Short-process high-value resource conversion of non-precious metals

• 开发了无机酸-有机溶剂溶出工艺，分别回收环氧树脂与玻璃纤维，实现环氧树脂和玻璃纤维的彻底分离和回收，回收率达99%，环氧树脂可再次交联使用。

An inorganic acid-organic solvent dissolution process has been developed to recover epoxy resin and glass fiber separately, achieving complete separation and recovery of epoxy resin and glass fiber with a recovery rate of 99%. The epoxy resin can be cross-linked and reused.

固废处置及资源化
# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 11.4 Recycling of waste lithium batteries and other resources

废旧锂电池等资源循环

Yang Lei

2026-05-25

---

锂电池 Lithium battery

- 锂离子电池具有能量密度高、寿命长、体积小、重量轻、自放电率低、无记忆效应、应用温度范围广和环境友好等特点；Lithium-ion batteries have the characteristics of high energy density, long life, small size, light weight, low self-discharge rate, no memory effect, wide application temperature range and environmental friendliness;
- 锂离子电池通常由正极、负极、电解液、隔膜和外壳；Lithium-ion batteries usually consist of a positive electrode, a negative electrode, an electrolyte, a separator, and a casing;
- 锂离子电池的正极材料主要是含锂的过渡金属氧化物，主要有LiCoO₂、LiNiO₂、LiMn₂O₄、LiFePO₄和LiNiₓCoyMn₁ − x − yO₂等；The positive electrode material of lithium-ion batteries is mainly lithium-containing transition metal oxides, mainly LiCoO₂, LiNiO₂, LiMn₂O₄, LiFePO₄ and LiNiₓCoyMn1−x−yO₂, etc.;
- 锂离子电池的电解液通常包括溶剂和溶质，所用的溶质通常为LiClO₄、LiPF₆、LiBF₄等，有机溶剂为碳酸丙烯酯（PC）和碳酸二乙酯（DEC）等，通常为其中的一种或几种的混合物；The electrolyte of lithium-ion batteries usually includes solvents and solutes. The solutes used are usually LiClO₄, LiPF₆, LiBF₄, etc., and the organic solvents are propylene carbonate (PC) and diethyl carbonate (DEC), etc., usually one or a mixture of several of them;
- 锂离子的隔膜通常采用单层或多层的聚乙烯（PE）或聚丙烯（PP）微孔隔膜。The lithium-ion diaphragm usually adopts a single-layer or multi-layer polyethylene (PE) or polypropylene (PP) microporous diaphragm.

-73-

---

# 废旧锂离子电池的危害

# Hazardous substances in used lithium-ion batteries

- 锂离子电池中使用的一些材料不仅含有Cu、Co和Ni等重金属，还含有电解液和隔膜等有机化学品；Some materials used in lithium-ion batteries contain not only heavy metals such as Cu, Co and Ni, but also organic chemicals such as electrolytes and diaphragms;
- 废锂离子电池当作一般固体废弃物烧毁，会产生大量的有毒气体（如HF气体）污染大气；Waste lithium-ion batteries burned as general solid waste will produce a large amount of toxic gases (such as HF gas) to pollute the atmosphere;

| Component   | Materials                                                                                     | Hazardous                                                                                                              |
| ----------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Cathode     | LiNiO₂, LiMn2O4, LiCoO2, LiFePO4, LiNiCo, Mn1−xyO2                                            | Heavymetals such as Ni and Co pose a threat to the environment and to human health                                     |
| Electrolyte | LiClO, LiPF, LiBF, dimethylsulfoxide (DMSO), propylene carbonate (PC), diethylcarbonate (DEC) | Corrosive, produces hazardous gas such as HF, chlorine (Cl) carbon dioxide (CO₂), and carbon monoxide (CO) when burned |
| Binder      | Polyvinylidene fluoride (PVDF), polytetrafluoroethylene (PTFE)                                | Produces HF when heated                                                                                                |


---

废锂离子电池的回收现状
# Current recycling status of waste lithium-ion batteries

• 由于经济利益的驱使，目前废旧锂离子电池的回收工艺主要针对正极材料中Co、Li以及Ni等价值较高的金属的回收。对于负极材料和电解液的回收鲜有报道。

Driven by economic interests, the current recycling process of waste lithium-ion batteries mainly focuses on the recovery of high-value metals such as Co, Li and Ni in positive electrode materials. There are few reports on the recovery of negative electrode materials and electrolytes.



---

废锂离子电池的回收现状

# Current recycling status of waste lithium-ion batteries

依据废锂离子电池回收过程中的处理步骤，将整个回收过程分为三个过程，即预处理过程、金属提取过程以及产品制备过程。其中金属提取过程是整个回收过程的核心。

According to the processing steps in the recycling process of waste lithium-ion batteries, the entire recycling process is divided into three processes, namely the pretreatment process, the metal extraction process and the product preparation process. Among them, the metal extraction process is the core of the entire recycling process.

| Spent LIBs               | Pretreatment                         | Metal-extraction | Product preparation |             |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------ | ---------------- | ------------------- | ----------- |
| Plastics, C, Al, Fe, Cu  | (Discharging/dismantling/separation) |                  |                     |             |
| Power supply, reductants | Reagents, water                      | Slag             | Waste gas           | Waste water |

Separation and recovery of different metals

Synthesis of cathode materials

-76-

---


# 预处理过程 Preprocessing

- 防止在处理过程中废锂离子电池短路或自燃，废旧锂离子电池需要进行放电处理，通常采用盐水浸泡将废电池放电。To prevent short circuit or spontaneous combustion of waste lithium-ion batteries during the treatment process, waste lithium-ion batteries need to be discharged, usually by soaking in salt water.
- 人工拆解废旧锂电池过程是首先将电池的塑料外壳去除，然后将其浸入到液氮中以使其中的有害物质失活。之后将废电池固定在车床上，用锯子将电池外壳的端部切除后，将电池纵向开口并去除外壳。最后分离正极、负极以及隔膜并在60℃条件下干燥24 h，得到的正极片和负极片进一步分离进入金属提取过程。The process of manually disassembling waste lithium batteries is to first remove the plastic shell of the battery, and then immerse it in liquid nitrogen to inactivate the harmful substances in it. After that, the waste battery is fixed on a lathe, and the end of the battery shell is cut off with a saw, and the battery is opened longitudinally and the shell is removed. Finally, the positive electrode, negative electrode and diaphragm are separated and dried at 60°C for 24 hours. The obtained positive and negative electrode sheets are further separated and enter the metal extraction process.



---


# 预处理过程 Preprocessing

• 正极材料一般通过黏结剂[聚偏二氟乙烯（PVDF）或聚四氟乙烯（PTFE）黏附在铝箔上，

由于其较强的黏附力，一般很难将正极活性物质从基体上分离。为了将正极活性物质从集流体铝箔上分离，通常会采用溶剂溶解法、NaOH碱溶法、超声辅助分离、热处理法以及机械分选法等方法分离正极活性物质与集流体。

The positive electrode material is generally adhered to the aluminum foil through a binder [polyvinylidene fluoride (PVDF) or polytetrafluoroethylene (PTFE). Due to its strong adhesion, it is generally difficult to separate the positive electrode active material from the substrate. In order to separate the positive electrode active material from the current collector aluminum foil, solvent dissolution, NaOH alkaline dissolution, ultrasonic assisted separation, heat treatment and mechanical sorting are usually used to separate the positive electrode active material and the current collector.



---


# 预处理过程

# Preprocessing

# ① 溶剂溶解法

# Solvent dissolution method

- 溶剂溶解法是采用对有机黏结剂有较好溶解性的有机溶剂处理正极片，使正极材料从集流体铝箔上脱落，从而排除正极材料回收过程中铝箔集流体的干扰。The solvent dissolution method is to use an organic solvent with good solubility in the organic binder to treat the positive electrode sheet, so that the positive electrode material falls off the current collector aluminum foil, thereby eliminating the interference of the aluminum foil current collector during the recovery of the positive electrode material.
- 溶剂溶解法的关键是选择有效的有机溶剂溶解黏结剂；The key to the solvent dissolution method is to select an effective organic solvent to dissolve the binder;
- 采用溶剂溶解法可以有效分离正极活性物质和集流体，实现正极材料的富集。但目前的回收方法主要是针对PVDF黏结剂，对于PTFE黏结剂的正极片的研究较少。另外，分离过程所采用的溶剂成本较高，具有一定的毒性，易对环境和人体健康造成威胁。The solvent dissolution method can effectively separate the positive electrode active material and the current collector to achieve the enrichment of the positive electrode material. However, the current recycling method is mainly for PVDF binders, and there is little research on positive electrode sheets with PTFE binders. In addition, the solvent used in the separation process is relatively expensive and has certain toxicity, which can easily pose a threat to the environment and human health.



---


# 预处理过程 Preprocessing

# ② NaOH 碱溶解法 Alkali dissolution method

- 碱溶法是利用单质Al的两性性质，使用NaOH碱溶液将铝箔集流体溶解，而正极活性物质LiCoO₂能够稳定存在，从而实现正极材料和铝箔集流体的选择性分离。The alkaline dissolution method uses the amphoteric properties of elemental Al to dissolve the aluminum foil current collector using NaOH alkaline solution, while the positive electrode active material LiCoO₂ can exist stably, thereby achieving selective separation of the positive electrode material and the aluminum foil current collector.
- 当NaOH溶解锂离子电池正极片中的铝箔集流体时 ，一般有两种物质溶解，即包覆在集流体表面的Al₂O₃保护层的溶解以及单质铝的溶解。When NaOH dissolves the aluminum foil current collector in the positive electrode sheet of a lithium-ion battery, generally two substances are dissolved, namely the dissolution of the Al2O3 protective layer coated on the surface of the current collector and the dissolution of elemental aluminum.
- 操作简单、效果较好，但铝以离子形式进入溶液中不利于铝的回收，另外，强碱溶液（NaOH溶液）会对环境造成一定的危害。The operation is simple and the effect is good, but aluminum enters the solution in the form of ions, which is not conducive to the recovery of aluminum. In addition, strong alkaline solutions (NaOH solutions) will cause certain harm to the environment.

Al2O3 + 2NaOH + 3H2O → 2Na[Al(OH)4]

2Al + 2NaOH + 6H2O → 2Na[Al(OH)4] + 3H2



---


# 预处理过程 Preprocessing

# ③ 超声辅助分离 Ultrasonic assisted separation

由于黏结剂（PVDF）具有较强的黏结力，废锂离子电池回收过程中很难将正极材料从铝箔集流体上分离下来。因而，超声波处理被认为是一种有效的分离正极活性物质和铝箔集流体的方法。Due to the strong adhesive force of the binder (PVDF), it is difficult to separate the positive electrode material from the aluminum foil current collector during the recycling of waste lithium-ion batteries. Therefore, ultrasonic treatment is considered to be an effective method for separating the positive electrode active material and the aluminum foil current collector.

机械搅拌分离时，大部分电极材料仍黏附于集流体表面。当仅用超声波清洗时，也只有部分正极材料分离。但当同时使用二者时，几乎所有的正极材料都能从铝箔集流体上分离。这可能因为超声波的空化效应使不溶物破坏并分散在水中，而机械搅拌的冲刷效果进一步促进正极材料从铝箔上分离。During mechanical stirring separation, most of the electrode materials still adhere to the surface of the current collector. When only ultrasonic cleaning is used, only part of the positive electrode material is separated. But when both are used at the same time, almost all of the positive electrode material can be separated from the aluminum foil current collector. This may be because the cavitation effect of ultrasound destroys the insoluble matter and disperses it in water, while the flushing effect of mechanical stirring further promotes the separation of the positive electrode material from the aluminum foil.



---


# 预处理过程 Preprocessing

# ④ 热处理法 Heat treatment method

- 热处理法采用高温分解黏结剂，降低活性材料颗粒之间的黏结力，使活性物质层与铝箔之间的黏结力变弱，进而实现正极活性物质和铝箔集流体的分离。The heat treatment method uses high temperature to decompose the binder, reduce the bonding force between the active material particles, weaken the bonding force between the active material layer and the aluminum foil, and then separate the positive electrode active material and the aluminum foil current collector.
- PVDF黏结剂一般在350℃以上开始分解，其他组分（乙炔黑、导电碳等）一般在600℃以上开始分解。PVDF binder generally begins to decompose above 350°C, and other components (acetylene black, conductive carbon, etc.) generally begin to decompose above 600°C.
- 热处理法操作简单，易于大规模生产，但缺点是热处理过程黏结剂和添加剂会生成有害气体，需要添加废气处理装置，处理过程能耗较大。The heat treatment method is simple to operate and easy to mass produce, but the disadvantage is that the binder and additives in the heat treatment process will generate harmful gases, and waste gas treatment equipment needs to be added, and the treatment process consumes a lot of energy.



---


# 预处理过程 Preprocessing

# ⑤ 机械分选法 Mechanical sorting method

- 机械分选法被认为是废锂离子电池预处理的一种有效的方法，机械分选法包括筛分、破碎、磁选等方式。Mechanical sorting method is considered to be an effective method for pretreatment of waste lithium-ion batteries. Mechanical sorting method includes screening, crushing, magnetic separation and other methods.
- 废锂离子电池具有较好的选择性破碎性质，其破碎产物可分为三个部分：Al富集组分（>2 mm）、Cu和Al富集组分（0.25～2 mm）以及Co和石墨富集组分（&#x3C;0.25 mm）。Waste lithium-ion batteries have good selective crushing properties, and their crushing products can be divided into three parts: Al-rich components (>2 mm), Cu and Al-rich components (0.25-2 mm) and Co and graphite-rich components (&#x3C;0.25 mm).
- 机械过程虽然能够一定程度上分离废锂离子电池的组分，但是它不能够使废离子电池中的所有组分完全分离，另外在破碎过程中电解质LiPF₆的分解以及有机物的分解也会对环境造成一定的危害。Although the mechanical process can separate the components of waste lithium-ion batteries to a certain extent, it cannot completely separate all the components in the waste ion batteries. In addition, the decomposition of electrolyte LiPF6 and the decomposition of organic matter during the crushing process will also cause certain harm to the environment.



---


# 预处理过程优缺点比较

# Comparison of advantages and disadvantages of pretreatment process

尽管目前已经开发了很多废锂离子电池预处理的方法，但废锂离子电池的预处理过程仍然面临着巨大的挑战。

Although many methods for pretreatment of waste lithium-ion batteries have been developed, the pretreatment process of waste lithium-ion batteries still faces huge challenges.

| Technology                     | Advantages                                   | Disadvantages                                                                            |
| ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Solvent dissolution            | High separation efficiency                   | High cost of solvent, environmental hazards                                              |
| NaOH dissolution               | Simple operation, high separation efficiency | Difficulty in aluminum recovery, alkali wastewater emission                              |
| Ultrasonic-assisted separation | Simple operation, almost no exhaust emission | Noise pollution, high device investment                                                  |
| Thermal treatment              | Simple operation, high throughput            | High energy consumption, high device investment, poisonous gas emission                  |
| Mechanical methods             | Simple and convenient operation              | Poisonous gas emission, cannot separate all kinds of components in spent LIBs completely |



---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process
# 金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

•  金属提取过程是整个回收工艺的核心，其目的是将废锂离子电池正极材料中的金属转化为合金形式或者溶液状态，以利于后续金属组分的分离回收。

The metal extraction process is the core of the entire recycling process. Its purpose is to convert the metal in the positive electrode material of waste lithium-ion batteries into an alloy form or a solution state, so as to facilitate the subsequent separation and recovery of metal components.

•  主要采用的方法有火法冶金法、湿法冶金法和生物冶金法等，其中湿法冶金方法由于回收率高、得到的产品纯度高等优点，已经成为目前废锂离子电池回收的主要技术。

The main methods used are pyrometallurgy, hydrometallurgy and biometallurgy. Among them, hydrometallurgy has become the main technology for recycling waste lithium-ion batteries due to its high recovery rate and high purity of the product.

-85-

---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

# ① 火法冶金 Pyrometallurgy

- 典型的火法冶金工艺回收废锂离子电池有价金属是通过高温还原熔炼的方法将有价金属还原并以合金的形式回收。The typical pyrometallurgical process for recycling valuable metals from waste lithium-ion batteries is to reduce the valuable metals by high-temperature reduction smelting and recover them in the form of alloys.
- 比利时优美科（Umicore）工艺。将废电池直接投入熔炼炉中还原熔炼，省去了预处理工艺，电池中的塑料、有机溶剂和石墨燃烧提供热量，而金属组分则被还原熔炼，得到含有 Al、Si、Ca、Mn 和 Fe 的炉渣及含有少量 Cu、Co、Ni 和少量 Fe 的合金。得到的合金采用硫酸浸出和溶剂萃取的技术制备钴的氧化物和 Ni(OH)₂。尽管该工艺不需要进行机械预处理，但 Li 的损失严重。Umicore process in Belgium. The waste batteries are directly put into the smelting furnace for reduction smelting, eliminating the pretreatment process. The plastic, organic solvent and graphite in the battery are burned to provide heat, while the metal components are reduced and smelted to obtain slag containing Al, Si, Ca, Mn and Fe and alloys containing a small amount of Cu, Co, Ni and a small amount of Fe. The alloy obtained is prepared by sulfuric acid leaching and solvent extraction technology to produce cobalt oxide and Ni(OH)₂. Although this process does not require mechanical pretreatment, the loss of Li is serious.

-86-

---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

# ① 火法冶金 Pyrometallurgy

- 火法冶金工艺处理废锂离子电池工艺简单，但回收过程中能耗高、二次污染严重，另外回收过程中锂的损失也是火法冶金工艺需要解决的一个重要问题。

The pyrometallurgical process for treating waste lithium-ion batteries is simple, but the energy consumption is high and the secondary pollution is serious during the recycling process. In addition, the loss of lithium during the recycling process is also an important problem that needs to be solved by the pyrometallurgical process.

-87-

---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

# ② 湿法冶金 Hydrometallurgy

湿法冶金方法回收废锂离子电池中有价金属主要采用化学浸出的方法，其主要目的是实现固态正极活性物质中的金属组分转移至溶液中，以便后续的分离和回收，浸出过程中主要采用的浸出剂有无机酸、有机酸以及氨-铵盐体系。

The hydrometallurgical method for recovering valuable metals from waste lithium-ion batteries mainly adopts the chemical leaching method. Its main purpose is to transfer the metal components in the solid positive electrode active material to the solution for subsequent separation and recovery. The main leaching agents used in the leaching process are inorganic acids, organic acids and ammonia-ammonium salt systems.

-88-

---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

# ② 湿法冶金 Hydrometallurgy

- 废锂离子电池中有价金属的浸出多采用盐酸、硫酸、硝酸以及磷酸等无机酸作为浸出剂，配合使用双氧水、亚硫酸氢钠或葡萄糖等作为还原剂，将固相中的高价态的Co或Mn还原为易溶解的 Co²⁺或 Mn²⁺。 The leaching of valuable metals in waste lithium-ion batteries mostly uses inorganic acids such as hydrochloric acid, sulfuric acid, nitric acid and phosphoric acid as leaching agents, and uses hydrogen peroxide, sodium bisulfite or glucose as reducing agents to reduce the high-valent Co or Mn in the solid phase to easily soluble Co²⁺ or Mn²⁺.
- 影响金属浸出的主要影响因素有温度、时间、浸出剂浓度、固液比和还原剂浓度等。The main factors affecting metal leaching are temperature, time, leaching agent concentration, solid-liquid ratio and reducing agent concentration.

2LiCoO2 + 8HCl → 2LiCl + 2CoCl2 + 4H2O + Cl2

-89-

---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

# ② 湿法冶金 Hydrometallurgy

- 在采用无机酸浸出时，能够实现金属的高效浸出但其在回收过程中会产生含酸废水，以及
Cl₂ 、SO₂ 和NOₓ等有害气体 ，会造成环境污染。When inorganic acid leaching is used,
efficient leaching of metals can be achieved, but acidic wastewater and harmful gases
such as Cl₂, SO₂ and NOₓ will be produced during the recovery process, which will cause
environmental pollution.
- 环境友好型的有机酸，如抗坏血酸、柠檬酸、草酸、甲酸、乙酸、琥珀酸、酒石酸等，作
为浸出剂回收锂离子电池正极材料，受到欢迎。Environmentally friendly organic acids,
such as ascorbic acid, citric acid, oxalic acid, formic acid, acetic acid, succinic acid,
tartaric acid, etc., are popular as leaching agents for recycling lithium-ion battery positive
electrode materials.

2LiCoO₂ + 4C6H8O6 → C2H6O6Li2 + 2C2H6O6Co + C6HO6 + 4H2O

-90-

---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

# ② 湿法冶金 Hydrometallurgy

目前回收主要的研究对象是锂离子电池经人工拆解后得到正极材料或锂离子电池生产废料，这部分材料一般金属组分单一，采用酸浸后溶液中的杂质离子较少。工业上回收的废锂离子电池一般成分比较复杂，预处理得到的正极材料中通常含有复杂金属组分，酸浸正极材料工艺的选择性效果不好，通常浸出液中会含有大量的杂质离子，增加了后续分离纯化步骤。

At present, the main research objects of recycling are positive electrode materials obtained after manual disassembly of lithium-ion batteries or waste materials from lithium-ion battery production. These materials generally have a single metal component, and there are fewer impurity ions in the solution after acid leaching. The waste lithium-ion batteries recycled in industry generally have complex components. The positive electrode materials obtained by pretreatment usually contain complex metal components. The selectivity of the acid leaching positive electrode material process is not good, and the leachate usually contains a large amount of impurity ions, which increases the subsequent separation and purification steps.

-91-

---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

# ③ 生物冶金 Biometallurgy

- 生物浸出工艺具有回收效率高、处理成本低、所需设备少等优势，被认为是一种最有望取代传统化学浸出的技术。The bioleaching process has the advantages of high recovery efficiency, low processing cost, and less equipment required, and is considered to be the most promising technology to replace traditional chemical leaching.
- 生物浸出的原理是通过微生物代谢产物的无机酸或有机酸实现废锂离子电池中金属组分的溶解。The principle of bioleaching is to dissolve the metal components in waste lithium-ion batteries through inorganic or organic acids produced by microbial metabolites.
- 生物浸出工艺具有成本低、操作方便等优势，但其缺点是浸出所需细菌难以培养、浸出时间长、浸出效率低等。The bioleaching process has the advantages of low cost and easy operation, but its disadvantages are that the bacteria required for leaching are difficult to culture, the leaching time is long, and the leaching efficiency is low.

-92-

---

金属提取过程 Metal Extraction Process

# ④ 其它方法 Other methods

- 目前实际应用过程中，回收废锂离子电池主要采用的技术是火法冶金技术和湿法冶金技术，
但是火法冶金技术能耗高、金属损失率高、环境污染严重。湿法冶金技术回收流程长，
回收过程中需要消耗大量的化学试剂，具有一定的环境污染。
In the current practical application process, the main technologies used to recycle waste lithium-ion batteries are pyrometallurgy and hydrometallurgy. However, pyrometallurgy has high energy consumption, high metal loss rate, and serious environmental pollution. Hydrometallurgy has a long recycling process, and a large amount of chemical reagents are consumed during the recycling process, which has certain environmental pollution.
- 采用机械化学法回收废锂离子电池中有价金属，在球磨过程中，由于机械能的作用使反应
物的结构和物化性能发生变化，从而促进化学反应的进行。
Mechanochemical method is used to recover valuable metals in waste lithium-ion batteries. During the ball milling process, the structure and physicochemical properties of the reactants change due to the action of mechanical energy, thereby promoting the chemical reaction.

-93-

---


# 金属提取过程优缺点比较

# Comparison of advantages and disadvantages of metal extraction processes

火法冶金工艺过程较为简单、处理容量大，有利于实现工业化应用。但是回收过程较为粗放，回收产品附加值低、金属损失率高、能耗高等问题限制了其工业应用。另外，火法回收过程中电池中的有毒电解液和有机物的热解造成了有害气体的释放。因此，在实际应用过程中还需要配备废气处理装置。

The pyrometallurgical process is relatively simple and has a large processing capacity, which is conducive to industrial application. However, the recycling process is relatively extensive, and the low added value of the recycled products, high metal loss rate, and high energy consumption limit its industrial application. In addition, the thermal decomposition of toxic electrolytes and organic matter in the battery during the pyrometallurgical recycling process causes the release of harmful gases. Therefore, in the actual application process, it is also necessary to equip the exhaust gas treatment device.

| Process         | Advantages                                                                  | Disadvantages                                                      | Environmental hazards |
| --------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------- |
| Pyrometallurgy  | Great capacity, simple operation                                            | High temperature, high energy consumption, low metal recovery rate | Waste gas, dust       |
| Hydrometallurgy | Low energy consumption, high metal recovery rate, high product purity       | Long recovery process, high chemical reagents consumption          | Wastewater            |
| Biometallurgy   | Low energy consumption, mild operating conditions, high metal recovery rate | Long reaction period, bacteria are difficult to cultivate          | Wastewater            |



---

金属提取过程优缺点比较
# Comparison of advantages and disadvantages of metal extraction processes

- 生物冶金技术采用微生物代谢过程中产生的将废锂离子电池正极材料溶解，得到含有金属组分的溶液。虽然生物冶金技术所需能耗低、处理成本低，但其所需菌种难培养、浸出时间长且处理过程易受污染。

Biometallurgy uses the waste lithium-ion battery cathode material produced during microbial metabolism to dissolve it to obtain a solution containing metal components. Although biometallurgy requires low energy consumption and low processing costs, the required strains are difficult to culture, the leaching time is long, and the processing process is susceptible to contamination.
- 湿法冶金工艺湿法具有金属回收率高、得到的产物纯度高等优点，成为目前废锂离子电池回收处理研究的热点。但是湿法冶金过程中采用酸浸提取有价金属，其选择性造成了浸出液中金属组分复杂，使得回收过程复杂、处理成本增加，另外，回收过程中产生的含酸或含碱废水也不容忽视。

Hydrometallurgical process has the advantages of high metal recovery rate and high purity of the obtained products, and has become a hot spot in the current research on the recycling and treatment of waste lithium-ion batteries. However, acid leaching is used to extract valuable metals in the hydrometallurgical process. Its selectivity causes the metal components in the leachate to be complex, making the recovery process complicated and the processing cost increased. In addition, the acidic or alkaline wastewater generated during the recovery process cannot be ignored.

-95-

---


# 产品制备过程

- 产品制备过程很大程度上取决于对净化溶液的进一步处理；The product preparation process depends largely on the further treatment of the purified solution;
- 如果将溶液中镍钴等元素分步分离，产品可为单一金属盐；If the elements such as nickel and cobalt in the solution are separated step by step, the product can be a single metal salt;
- 若对净化后溶液成分进行调节，可进一步制备相对附加值高的正极材料前驱体；If the composition of the purified solution is adjusted, a positive electrode material precursor with relatively high added value can be further prepared;



---


# 产品制备过程 Product preparation process

# ① 浸出液中金属的分离回收 Separation and recovery of metals in leachate

• 正极材料经过酸浸后得到的溶液中通常含有多种金属离子，为实现浸出液中金属盐的制备，通常需要采用溶剂萃取、化学沉淀、结晶等组合工艺以实现金属组分的分离与回收。The solution obtained after acid leaching of the positive electrode material usually contains a variety of metal ions. In order to prepare the metal salt in the leachate, it is usually necessary to use a combination of solvent extraction, chemical precipitation, crystallization and other processes to separate and recover the metal components.



---


# 产品制备过程 Product preparation process

# ① 浸出液中金属的分离回收 Separation and recovery of metals in leachate

由于废锂离子电池金属组分复杂，浸出液中通常含有多种金属离子，虽然采用化学沉淀、溶剂萃取等组合工艺能够实现浸出液中金属组分的分离回收，但是回收过程中需要消耗大量的化学试剂，回收的产品纯度受杂质和回收工艺的影响较为明显，对工艺参数控制要求较高。

另外，分离回收金属组分也存在回收工艺流程复杂、金属组分回收率低、处理成本高和环境风险高等缺点。

Due to the complex metal components of waste lithium-ion batteries, the leachate usually contains a variety of metal ions. Although the combination of chemical precipitation, solvent extraction and other processes can achieve the separation and recovery of metal components in the leachate, a large amount of chemical reagents are consumed in the recovery process. The purity of the recovered product is significantly affected by impurities and the recovery process, and the control requirements for process parameters are high. In addition, the separation and recovery of metal components also have the disadvantages of complex recovery process, low recovery rate of metal components, high processing cost and high environmental risk.



---


# 产品制备过程 Product preparation process

# ② 正极材料的制备 Preparation of positive electrode materials

- 由于溶液中Co、Ni和Mn等过渡金属离子的性质相似、不易分离，因此考虑直接将多种离子重新制备前驱体，再进一步合成电极材料，避免了分离的复杂步骤。Since the properties of transition metal ions such as Co, Ni and Mn in the solution are similar and difficult to separate, it is considered to directly prepare precursors from multiple ions and then further synthesize electrode materials to avoid the complex separation steps.
- 目前 ，用于前驱体制备的方法主要有 共沉淀法 和 溶胶凝胶法 。 At present, the methods used for precursor preparation mainly include coprecipitation and sol-gel methods.



---


# 产品制备过程 Product preparation process

# ② 正极材料的制备 Preparation of positive electrode materials

- 共沉淀法是目前合成三元正极材料最常用的方法，该方法的优点是工艺设备简单，有利于工业化生产，但沉淀过程的影响因素较多，容易产生杂质共沉淀。Coprecipitation is the most commonly used method for synthesizing ternary positive electrode materials. The advantage of this method is that the process equipment is simple and it is conducive to industrial production, but there are many factors affecting the precipitation process, and impurity coprecipitation is easy to occur.
- 溶胶凝胶法是另一种常用的合成电极材料的方法，该方法首先是将原料分散在溶剂中，加入适量络合剂使之发生水解和聚合等过程形成溶胶，进而生成具有一定空间结构的凝胶，最后经过热处理得到产品。Sol-gel method is another commonly used method for synthesizing electrode materials. This method first disperses the raw materials in a solvent, adds an appropriate amount of complexing agent to cause hydrolysis and polymerization to form a sol, and then generates a gel with a certain spatial structure, and finally obtains the product after heat treatment.

# 废锂电池中钴和锂的精制回收

# Refining and recovery of cobalt and lithium from waste lithium batteries

# 钴离子与锂离子的溶解浸出

# Dissolution and leaching of cobalt ions and lithium ions:

采用硫酸+双氧水体系对经分离所得的锂电池正极活性物质黑渣进行钴元素与锂元素的溶解浸出。The cobalt and lithium elements are dissolved and leached from the separated black slag of the positive electrode active material of the lithium battery using a sulfuric acid + hydrogen peroxide system.

废锂离子电池回收处理过程中面临的问题与展望
# Problems and prospects in the recycling of waste lithium-ion batteries

预处理过程一方面是为了减小废锂离子电池的体积、降低回收过程中的污染问题，另一方面是为了实现有价金属组分的富集。在预处理阶段，如何实现废锂离子电池的安全高效自动拆解成为目前阻碍废锂离子电池产业化回收的重要因素。另外，在预处理过程中会产生大量的污染物，如HF、PF5等有害气体，这势必会对环境造成二次污染。

The pretreatment process is to reduce the volume of waste lithium-ion batteries and reduce pollution during the recycling process, and to achieve the enrichment of valuable metal components. In the pretreatment stage, how to achieve safe, efficient and automatic disassembly of waste lithium-ion batteries has become an important factor hindering the industrial recycling of waste lithium-ion batteries. In addition, a large amount of pollutants, such as HF, PF5 and other harmful gases, will be generated during the pretreatment process, which will inevitably cause secondary pollution to the environment.

-109-

---

废锂离子电池回收处理过程中面临的问题与展望
# Problems and prospects in the recycling of waste lithium-ion batteries

目前的回收过程主要关注在价值较高的金属组分的回收，对于经济价值低、回收成本高的组分的回收关注较低，尤其是对负极碳材料的回收和电解液的回收处理研究较少，因此，如何系统地对废锂离子电池中各组分进行资源化回收再利用还需要进一步的深入研究。

The current recycling process mainly focuses on the recovery of high-value metal components, and pays less attention to the recovery of components with low economic value and high recycling costs, especially the recovery of negative electrode carbon materials and the recovery and treatment of electrolytes. Therefore, how to systematically recycle and reuse the components in waste lithium-ion batteries requires further in-depth research.



---

废锂离子电池回收处理过程中面临的问题与展望
# Problems and prospects in the recycling of waste lithium-ion batteries

- 金属提取过程作为整个回收过程的核心，在实际应用过程中主要采用火法冶金技术和湿法冶金技术，但这些技术存在着废水、废渣以及废气排放等问题，对环境和人体健康存在着威胁。因此，如何减少或避免这些二次污染也是回收需要关注的主要问题。
The metal extraction process is the core of the entire recycling process. In actual application, pyrometallurgy and hydrometallurgy are mainly used. However, these technologies have problems such as wastewater, waste residue and waste gas emissions, which pose a threat to the environment and human health. Therefore, how to reduce or avoid these secondary pollution is also a major issue that needs to be paid attention to in recycling.
- 由于废锂离子电池金属组分复杂，经过金属提取过程之后得到的溶液中金属组分复杂，分离回收金属组分需要采用化学沉淀、溶剂萃取等技术的组合，使回收处理变得复杂；直接合成电池正极材料则需要考虑再制备材料的性能和回收过程中的成本问题。
Due to the complex metal components of waste lithium-ion batteries, the metal components in the solution obtained after the metal extraction process are complex. The separation and recovery of metal components requires the use of a combination of chemical precipitation, solvent extraction and other technologies, which makes the recycling process complicated; direct synthesis of battery positive electrode materials requires consideration of the performance of the re-prepared materials and the cost issues in the recycling process.

-111-

---

---

电子废物处置及资源化存在的问题和发展方向

# Problems and development direction of electronic waste disposal and resource utilization

- 电子废物含有价金属，但含量低，各种物质交错，很难分离，处理成本很高 Electronic waste contains valuable metals, but the content is low. The processing cost is very high.
- 需要政府补贴，负担很重，急需开发高效低成本技术 Government subsidies are needed, which is a heavy burden. It is urgent to develop efficient and low-cost technologies.
- 真空冶炼，成本也高，安全性差 Vacuum smelting is also costly and unsafe.
- 高效分离 ， 特别是物理方法分离 ， 是发展方向 Efficient separation, especially physical separation, is the direction of development.
- 单一技术很难，应该要多种技术耦合 Single technology is difficult, and multiple technologies should be coupled.
- 生产时，就应该考虑处理问题 When producing, the processing problem should be considered.

# Some facts on global waste management

全球每年产生约20亿吨固体废物；预计到2050年，全球垃圾产生量将增长至34亿吨；高收入国家（占全球人口的16%）产生了全球34%的垃圾。

Approximately 2 billion tons of solid waste are generated globally each year; By 2050, global waste generation is projected to increase to 3.4 billion tons; High-income countries (16% of the global population) generate 34% of the world's waste.

Source

垃圾回收率最高的国家包括德国（67%）、韩国（59%）和斯洛文尼亚（58%）。

Countries with the highest recycling rates include Germany (67%), South Korea (59%), and Slovenia (58%).

Source

美国每年产生约2.5亿吨垃圾，人均垃圾产生量为2.1公斤/天；垃圾回收率为32%，填埋仍然是主要的处理方式。

The U.S. generates approximately 250 million tons of waste annually, with per capita waste generation at 2.1 kg/day. The recycling rate is 32%, with landfilling remaining the primary disposal method.

Source

# Some facts on global waste management

- 印度每年产生约1.6亿吨垃圾，人均垃圾产生量为0.5公斤/天。垃圾处理设施不足，约70%的垃圾被露天堆放或焚烧。

India generates approximately 160 million tons of waste annually, with per capita waste generation at 0.5 kg/day. Waste treatment facilities are insufficient, with around 70% of waste being openly dumped or burned.
https://cpcb.nic.in/
- 欧盟国家每年产生约2.5亿吨垃圾，人均垃圾产生量为1.3公斤/天。垃圾回收率平均为48%，部分国家（如德国、奥地利）超过60%。

EU countries generate approximately 250 million tons of waste annually, with per capita waste generation at 1.3 kg/day. The average recycling rate is 48%, with some countries (e.g., Germany, Austria) exceeding 60%.
https://www.eea.europa.eu/
- 中国每年产生约2.2亿吨城市生活垃圾。垃圾分类试点城市（如上海、北京）的回收率显著提高，但全国平均回收率仍低于30%。

China generates approximately 220 million tons of municipal solid waste annually. Recycling rates in pilot cities for waste classification (e.g., Shanghai, Beijing) have significantly improved, but the national average recycling rate remains below 30%.
https://www.mee.gov.cn/

2016-12-27实施 STechSouthern University of Science and 监督局 发布 Technology

---

Solid Waste Management in China

中国每年产生约 2.2亿吨城市生活垃圾，且随着城市化进程加快，垃圾产生量持续增长。

China generates approximately 220 million tons of municipal solid waste annually, and this figure continues to rise due to rapid urbanization.

人均生活垃圾产生量约为 0.8-1.2公斤/天，大城市（如北京、上海）的人均产生量更高。

The per capita waste generation is about 0.8-1.2 kg/day, with higher rates in major cities like Beijing and Shanghai.

自2019年《生活垃圾分类制度实施方案》实施以来，全国46个重点城市已基本建成垃圾分类系统。上海、北京等城市垃圾分类覆盖率超过90%，但全国平均回收率仍低于30%。可回收物（如塑料、纸张、金属）的回收率逐步提高，但低值可回收物（如玻璃、复合包装）的回收仍面临困难。挑战：居民分类意识不足，分类收运和处理设施不完善。

Since the implementation of the Municipal Solid Waste Sorting System Implementation Plan in 2019, 46 key cities have basically established waste sorting systems. Cities like Shanghai and Beijing have achieved a waste sorting coverage rate of over 90%, but the national average recycling rate remains below 30%. The recycling rate for recyclables (e.g., plastics, paper, metals) has gradually improved, but low-value recyclables (e.g., glass, composite packaging) still face challenges.

2016-12-27实施 STechSouthern University of Science and Technology

https://www.mee.gov.cn/ -7- 监督局 发布

---

Solid Waste Management in China

中国生活垃圾的末端处理主要包括填埋、焚烧和资源化利用三种方式。近年来，处理结构逐步优化，焚烧和资源化利用的比例显著提升。截至2023年，填埋处理比例已降至40%以下，焚烧处理比例已超过60%，焚烧发电厂数量超过600座。 The primary methods for municipal solid waste treatment in China include landfilling, incineration, and resource recovery. In recent years, the treatment structure has been optimized, with a significant increase in the proportion of incineration and resource recovery. As of 2023, the proportion of waste treated by landfilling has dropped to below 40%. The proportion of waste treated by incineration exceeds 60%, with over 600 waste-to-energy plants in operation.

“十四五”规划明确提出推进垃圾分类，推动“无废城市”建设，实现垃圾减量化和资源化。The 14th Five-Year Plan explicitly promotes waste sorting, build "zero-waste cities" to achieve waste reduction and resource recovery.

# 挑战

- 分类体系不完善；
- 资源化利用水平低；
- 区域发展不平衡；
- 公众参与度不足。

# Incomplete Sorting System:

Some cities lack adequate sorting, collection, and treatment facilities.

# Low Resource Recovery Rate:

The utilization rates for low-value recyclables and kitchen waste need improvement.

# Regional Imbalances:

Eastern coastal regions have stronger treatment capacities, while central and western regions lag behind.

# Low Public Participation:

Public awareness and habits regarding waste sorting need further cultivation.

2016-12-27实施 STechSouthern University of Science and Technology

https://www.mee.gov.cn/

-8- 监督局 发布

# Composition of domestic waste

- 含水率 Moisture content 50-65%；
- 塑料 Plastics 12-18%（dry weight 干重），1200-1800万吨/年 12-18 million tons per year；
- 纸张 Paper 8-15%（干重），800-1500 万吨/年 （脏！dirty ） 8-15 million tons per year；
- 纤维 Textiles 20-30%（干重），1200-1800万吨/年（脏！）；
- 食品废物 Food Waste 60-70%（wet weight 湿重）、20-30%（干重），2000-3000万吨/年（脏！）20-30 million tons per year；
- 建筑垃圾 Construction waste 10-20%（干重）。

城市生活垃圾资源利用要求
# Requirements for Resource Utilization of Municipal Solid Waste

- ✓ 塑料橡胶类废物：冶炼石油，但要求废物中分流为含氯与无氯塑料，含无机杂物小于0.5-1％；
Plastic and Rubber Waste: Refine petroleum, but the waste must be separated into chlorine-containing and chlorine-free plastics, with inorganic impurities less than 0.5-1%;
- ✓ 纸张类废物：制备纸浆，但要求废物含无机杂物小于0.5-1％；
Paper Waste: Produce pulp, but the waste must contain inorganic impurities less than 0.5-1%;

2016-12-27实施  STech Southern University

监督局 发布 Technology



---

城市生活垃圾资源利用要求
# Requirements for Resource Utilization of Municipal Solid Waste

✓ 纤维废物：冶炼石油，但要求废物含无机杂物小于1％；再生纤维，要求废物深度分类为石油基废物、棉基废物、皮革类废物等，同时无机杂物小于1％；

Textile Waste: Refine petroleum, but the waste must contain inorganic impurities less than 1%; for recycled fibers, the waste must be deeply classified into petroleum-based waste, cotton-based waste, leather waste, etc., with inorganic impurities less than 1%.

STech Southern University of Science and Technology

城市生活垃圾资源利用要求

# Requirements for Resource Utilization of Municipal Solid Waste

- 玻璃类废物：再生玻璃，但要求深度分类为钠玻璃、石英玻璃、有机玻璃、着色玻璃等，清洁干净，杂物含量小于1％；
Glass waste: Recycled glass, but requires deep classification into soda glass, quartz glass, acrylic glass, colored glass, etc. Must be clean and free of contaminants, with impurity content less than 1%.
- 金属制品废物：再冶炼，但要求深度分类为有色、黑色金属；
Metal waste: Re-melted, but requires deep classification into non-ferrous and ferrous metals.

城市生活垃圾资源利用要求

# Requirements for Resource Utilization of Municipal Solid Waste

- 易腐废物：厌氧发酵、气化、压滤脱水焚烧；

Perishable waste: Treated through anaerobic fermentation, gasification, and dewatering by press filtration before incineration.
- 砖瓦等废物：筑路废物，但要求塑料、橡胶、纤维、可降解有机物含量小于1％（灼减率小于2％）。

Brick and tile waste: Used as road construction material, but requires plastic, rubber, fiber, and biodegradable organic content to be less than 1% (loss on ignition less than 2%).

生活垃圾分类发展历史
# History of Municipal Solid Waste Classification

🞑 2000 年 6 月 ， 城建司下发了 《 关于公布生活垃圾分类收集试点城市的通知 》 （ 建城部 〔2000〕12号），确定将北京、上海、广州、深圳、杭州、南京、厦门、桂林等八个城市作为生活垃圾分类收集试点。

In June 2000, the Urban Construction Department issued the "Notice on the Announcement of Pilot Cities for Municipal Solid Waste Classification and Collection". It designated eight pilot cities for municipal solid waste classification and collection.

| 阶段 Stage                                | 试点城市和区域 Pilot cities and regions                                                     | 主要措施 Main Actions                         |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- |
| 酝酿筹备期 Preparation                       | 广州市部分街道（1999年）、上海市曹阳五村第七居委会（1995年）、北京市西城区大乘巷居民区和妇联等单位（1996年）、杭州市花园小区等居民区（1984、1996年） | 在极少数居民区和单位开展垃圾分类收集试点                      |
| 试点示范期 Pilot Demonstration               | 北京、上海、广州、深圳、杭州、南京、厦门、桂林等8个城市                                                         | 在部分居民区、公共场所开展垃圾分类试点                       |
| 全面推广示范期（2010至今）Comprehensive deployment | 北京、上海、广州、深圳、杭州、南京、武汉、宁波等大中城市                                                         | 从居民区、公共场所逐步向企事业单位、学校、农贸市场推广；从单一的点向街镇、区全区域 |

2016-12-27实施 STech Southern University of Science and Technology 监督局 发布

首批垃圾分类示范城市（区）出炉
# The First Batch of Waste Sorting Demonstration Cities (Districts) Announced

• 2015年4月，住建部等五部委发文确定将26个城市（区）作为第一批示范城市 （区）；In April 2015, the Ministry of Housing and Urban-Rural Development and four other ministries issued a document designating 26 cities (districts) as the first batch of demonstration cities (districts).

• 要求到2020年，各示范城市（区）建成区、居民小区和单位的生活垃圾分类收集覆盖率达到90%，人均生活垃圾清运量下降6%，生活垃圾资源化利用率达到60%。The directive required that by 2020: The coverage rate of municipal solid waste classification and collection in built-up areas, residential communities, and institutions of these demonstration cities (districts) should reach 90%. The per capita volume of municipal solid waste collection and transportation should be reduced by 6%. The resource utilization rate of municipal solid waste should reach 60%.

强制分类政策出台

# Mandatory Waste Sorting Policy Introduced

• 2016年6月，国家发改委、住建部发布《垃圾强制分类制度方案》In June 2016, the National Development and Reform Commission and the Ministry of Housing and Urban-Rural Development issued the "Mandatory Waste Sorting System Plan".

# 目标

Goal

| 到2020年底，重点城市生活垃圾得到有效分类，垃圾分类的法律法规和标准制度体系基本建立，生活垃圾减量化、无害化、资源化和产业化体系基本形成。初步形成可复制、可推广的生活垃圾强制分类典型模式。 | By the end of 2020, municipal solid waste in key cities had been effectively classified. The legal regulations and standard system for waste sorting were essentially established, and systems for waste reduction, harmless disposal, resource utilization, and industrialization had taken shape. A preliminary, replicable, and scalable model for mandatory waste sorting was formed. |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 实施生活垃圾强制分类的城市，生活垃圾回收利用率达到35%以上。                                                                 | In cities implementing mandatory waste sorting, the recycling rate of municipal solid waste reached over 35%.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

2016-12-27实施 STechSouthern University of Science and Technology

---

强制分类政策出台

# Mandatory Waste Sorting Policy Introduced

• 2016年6月，国家发改委、住建部发布《垃圾强制分类制度方案》In June 2016, the National Development and Reform Commission and the Ministry of Housing and Urban-Rural Development issued the "Mandatory Waste Sorting System Plan".

# 实施范围

Required cities/regions

| • 直辖市、省会城市和计划单列市；                            | • Direct-controlled municipalities, provincial capital cities, and cities with independent planning status;                                         |
| -------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| • 第一批26个生活垃圾分类示范城市；                          | • The first batch of 26 municipal solid waste classification demonstration cities;                                                                  |
| • 鼓励各省、自治区结合本地实际，选择本地区具备条件的其他城市开展生活垃圾强制分类工作。 | • Encourage provinces and autonomous regions to select other cities that meet the conditions in their regions to carry out mandatory waste sorting. |

Based on international definitions, the recycling rate of municipal solid waste in China is defined as follows:

(Recycled amount of recyclable resources in municipal solid waste + separately collected and resource-utilized organic waste, such as kitchen waste, etc.) × 100% / Total municipal solid waste generated.

2016-12-27实施 STech Southern University of Science and Technology

强制分类政策出台 Mandatory Waste Sorting Policy Introduced

# 强制对象    Mandatory Targets

# 1、公共机构。

主要包括党政机关，学校、科研、文化、出版、广播电视等事业单位，协会、学会、联合会等社会团体组织，车站、机场、码头、公共体育场馆、文艺演出场馆等公共场所管理单位。

Public Institutions: Mainly includes party and government agencies, schools, research institutes, cultural, publishing, and broadcasting organizations, associations, academic societies, federations, and other social organizations, as well as management units of public places such as stations, airports, docks, public sports venues, and cultural performance venues.

# 2、相关企业。

主要包括宾馆、饭店、商场、超市、专业市场、农贸市场、农产品批发市场、商铺、商用写字楼管理企业等。

Related Enterprises: Mainly includes hotels, restaurants, shopping malls, supermarkets, specialty markets, farmers' markets, agricultural product wholesale markets, shops, and businesses managing commercial office buildings.

2016-12-27实施  STechSouthern University of Science and Technology

强制分类政策出台

# Mandatory Waste Sorting Policy Introduced

# Mandatory Sorting

# 强制分类类别

- Hazardous waste shall be included as a mandatory sorting category.
- 将有害垃圾作为强制分类的类别。
- Additionally, based on specific circumstances, 1-2 categories, such as perishable waste or recyclable materials, may be selected for mandatory sorting, in accordance with the "Municipal Solid Waste Classification and Evaluation Standards."
- 同时，根据具体情况，参照《生活垃圾分类及其评价标准》，在易腐垃圾、可回收物等几种类别中再选择1-2类进行强制分类。

2016-12-27实施

STech Southern University

监督局 发布

地方经验不断积累—金华模式

# Waste sorting in Jinhua, China

# “两次四分”

农户：分成“会烂的”和“不会烂的”两类。

保洁员再分：村保洁员在分类收集各户垃圾的基础上，进行二次分类。一方面，纠正农户分类中的错误；另一方面，对“不会烂的”垃圾再分“好卖的”“不好卖的”两类

Farmers categorize waste into two types: "perishable" and "non-perishable". Sanitation workers then conduct a secondary sorting: Village sanitation workers perform a secondary classification based on the sorted waste collected from each household. On one hand, they correct any mistakes made by the farmers in their sorting; on the other hand, they further divide the "non-perishable" waste into "sellable" and "non-sellable" categories.

# 阳光堆肥房就地资源化

采取“一村一建”或“多村合建”建设阳光堆肥房

Constructing solar composting houses by adopting the model of 'one village, one facility' or 'multiple villages, one joint facility'

2016-12-27实施

STech Southern University of Science and Technology

长效保障机制

# Long-term Guarantee Measures

# 资金筹集模式

- “财政直补、群众缴费、社会参与”的资金筹集模式
- 建设费用由县级财政负责；Construction costs are covered by the county-level finance
- 运行费用主要由市 、 县两级财政解决 ；Operational expenses are primarily resolved by the municipal and county-level finances;
- 美丽家园共建基金由村民缴纳；The Beautiful Homeland Co-construction Fund is contributed by the villagers.

# 全方位监督考核

- 政府层面 - 实行分类工作分级考核制度 ；At the government level-implement a hierarchical assessment system for classification work
- 村委会和村党员干部层面-实行垃圾分类网格化管理；At the level of village committees and village Party members-implement a grid-based management system for waste classification
- 保洁员层面 - 建立分类评优制度 ；At the level of sanitation workers-establish a classification excellence evaluation system
- 农户层面-建立环境卫生荣辱榜制度；At the level of farmers-establish a system of honor and disgrace for environmental sanitation.

2016-12-27实施

STech Southern University of Science and Technology

地方经验不断积累—上海模式
# Waste sorting in Shanghai，China

# 绿色账户功能 Green Account Function

对居民参与垃圾分类行为发放积分奖励，激励

Residents are awarded points for participating in waste sorting activities, which can be exchanged for a certain amount of money or goods, and they are also given some form of spiritual encouragement.

# 窗口功能 Window Function

通过绿色帐户宣传、帐户开设、激励奖励的兑现等，发挥绿色帐户对市民的窗口宣传作用

Through the promotion of the Green Account, the opening of accounts, and the fulfillment of incentive rewards, the Green Account serves as a window for publicizing information to the citizens.

# 平台功能 Platform Functionality

是市区两级垃圾分类政府主管部门、参与部门、热心公益企业、基层社区组织共同推进生活垃圾分类的工作平台

It is a working platform jointly promoted by municipal and district-level government departments in charge of waste classification, participating departments, public-spirited enterprises, and grassroots community organizations to advance the classification of household waste.

# 载体功能 Carrier Function

依托政府资金、社会募集物资、荣誉称号授予等途径，通过绿色帐户作为载体，有标准、有规则地发放给市民

Relying on government funds, socially raised materials, and the conferral of honorary titles, the Green Account serves as a vehicle to distribute resources to citizens in a standardized and regulated manner.

2016-12-27实施 STechSouthern University of Science and Technology

监督局 发布

生活垃圾源头分类模式的提出
# Proposal of the Household Waste Source Separation Model

# 生活垃圾分类模式确定的原则

# Principles for Determining the Household Waste Classification Model

1. 实现生活垃圾的减量化 、 资源化 、 无害化 Achieving the reduction, resource utilization, and harmless treatment of household waste.
2. 高含水率生活垃圾源头沥水，降低转运和处置成本；Dehydration of high-moisture household waste at the source to reduce transportation and disposal costs.
3. 有价值废物的高效分类与回收；Efficient sorting and recycling of valuable waste.
4. 有机 生活垃圾占比最大 ， 由地方企业进行堆肥等资源化处置 。 Organic household waste, which constitutes the largest proportion, is subjected to resourceful disposal methods such as composting by local enterprises.

2016-12-27实施  STechSouthern University -38- 监督局 发布 Technology

---

生活垃圾源头分类模式的提出
# Proposal of the Household Waste Source Separation Model

# 生活垃圾分类模式确定的原则

# Principles for Determining the Household Waste Classification Model

- 分类模式下各类垃圾的概念清晰易界定 In the classification model, the concepts of various types of waste are clear and easily defined.
- 分类模式可接受程度强，建议实行干、湿分类（物理特征），或有机易腐烂垃圾、不腐烂垃圾分类（可降解性）The classification model has a high degree of acceptability, and it is recommended to implement dry and wet waste separation (based on physical characteristics), or separation of organic perishable waste from non-perishable waste (based on degradability).

2016-12-27实施 STechSouthern University of Science and Technology 监督局 发布

---

生活垃圾源头分类模式的提出
# Proposal of the Household Waste Source Separation Model

# 生活垃圾分类模式确定的原则

# Principles for Determining the Household Waste Classification Model

③ 分类操作简单易行，运行管理成本低 The classification process is simple and easy to implement, with low operational and management costs.

• 生活垃圾分类越多，基础建设要求越高，操作愈繁琐，极易挫伤居民分类积极性，不适用于当前农村地区。 The more categories household waste is sorted into, the higher the infrastructure requirements become, and the more complicated the operations get, which can easily discourage residents' enthusiasm for sorting. This approach is not suitable for current rural areas.

2016-12-27实施 STech Southern University of Science and Technology 监督局 发布

---

确立四种生活垃圾源头分类方案

# Establish four source separation schemes for household waste.

| C₁： | 源头不分类+集中分拣               | No sorting at source + centralized sorting                                             |
| --- | ------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| C₂： | 可回收废物+有毒有害垃圾+不可回收废物      | Recyclable waste + toxic and hazardous waste + non-recyclable waste                    |
| C₃： | 可回收废物+有毒有害垃圾+干垃圾+湿垃圾     | Recyclable waste + toxic and hazardous waste + dry waste + wet waste                   |
| C₄： | 可回收废物+有毒有害垃圾+易腐烂垃圾+不腐烂垃圾 | Recyclable waste + toxic and hazardous waste + perishable waste + non-perishable waste |

方案1与现有模式相同，增加了中转分拣或集中分拣流程

The same as the existing model, with the addition of a transfer sorting or centralized sorting process.

方案2/3/4单独收集有毒有害垃圾，避免收集清运过程中二次污染

Separately collect toxic and hazardous waste to avoid secondary pollution during the collection and transportation process.

方案3/4对有机易腐烂垃圾或湿垃圾分类收集，可降低垃圾清运量

Separate collection of organic perishable waste or wet waste, which can reduce the amount of waste transported.

方案4便于有机易腐烂垃圾分类后的资源化处置

Facilitates the resourceful disposal of sorted organic perishable waste.

2016-12-27实施 STech Southern University -41- 监督局 发布 Technology

生活垃圾源头分类投放技术

# Household Waste Source Separation and Disposal Technology

生活垃圾大致分为四大类：

Household waste is roughly divided into four major categories:

1. 有机易腐类废物（厨余垃圾，植物残体和果皮等）
Organic perishable waste (kitchen waste, plant residues, fruit peels, etc.)
2. 惰性无机废物（灰土煤渣砖块和草木灰等）
Inert inorganic waste (ash, coal cinder, bricks, plant ash, etc.)
3. 可回收废物（废纸，塑料，金属，塑料等）
Recyclable waste (waste paper, plastics, metals, glass, etc.)
4. 有毒有害废物（废电池，农药瓶，日光灯管，油漆桶等）
Toxic and hazardous waste (used batteries, pesticide bottles, fluorescent tubes, paint buckets, etc.)

理想情况：28-38类
Ideal scenario: 28-38 categories.

2016-12-27实施

STech Southern University of Science and Technology

监督局 发布

小家电 vs 大家电 Small appliances vs. Large appliances

生活垃圾处理末端的大件废弃家电占比极少。小件废弃家电易混入生活垃圾中。

The proportion of large discarded household appliances at the final stage of domestic waste treatment is extremely small. Small discarded household appliances are prone to being mixed into domestic waste.

小件废弃家电相关研究较少。

There is relatively little research on small discarded household appliances.

大件废弃家电的回收处理产业链完善。

The recycling and processing industry chain for large discarded household appliances is well-established.

小件家电替换频率高、未达使用寿命即被替换。

Small household appliances are replaced frequently, often being substituted before reaching the end of their service life.

# 小件废弃家用电器

小件废弃家用电器：除“四机一脑”等大件废弃家电外家庭生产生活过程中报废的、需要通过充电或蓄能电池维系运行的家庭用具，尤以因分类体系尚待完善等而易被错误丢弃或归类至干垃圾中、长宽高30cm以下的小件电子废弃物为主，如剃须刀、电子温度计、电动牙刷、钟表、小电扇、小台灯、鼠标、充电式无线耳机、随身听、移动电源、儿童玩具等。

Small discarded household appliances refer to those family tools and devices that are rendered obsolete during the course of domestic production and daily living activities, which rely on charging or energy storage batteries for operation, excluding large discarded household appliances such as the "four machines and one brain" (televisions, refrigerators, washing machines, air conditioners, and computers). These are particularly prone to being mistakenly discarded or classified as dry waste due to the yet-to-be-perfected classification system, and mainly include small electronic waste with dimensions under 30 cm in length, width, and height. Examples include razors, electronic thermometers, electric toothbrushes, clocks, small electric fans, small desk lamps, mice, rechargeable wireless earphones, personal audio players, mobile power supplies, children's toys, and other small electronic devices.

2016-12-27实施

STech Southern University of Science and Technology

监督局 发布

# 小件废弃家用电器分类与高价值组分分离利用示范

# Demonstration of Classification and High-Value Component Separation and Utilization of Small Discarded Household Appliances

有理由相信，如同包含干垃圾、湿垃圾的“四分类”法宣传一样，在持续的宣传教育下，能够实现生活垃圾中小件废弃家电较好的源头分类分流效果，即能够施行更细致化的生活垃圾分类标准。

There is reason to believe that, much like the promotion of the "four-category" method which includes dry and wet waste, through continuous education and awareness campaigns, it is possible to achieve a better source separation and diversion of small discarded household appliances within domestic waste. This implies that a more refined standard for the classification of household waste can be implemented.

示范工程实施结束后被扔入回收桶中的小件废弃家电

After the implementation of the demonstration project, small discarded household appliances are to be thrown into the recycling bin.

STech Southern University of Science and Technology

# 小件废弃家用电器及相关附属品分类分流

# Classification and Diversion of Small Discarded Household Appliances and Related Accessories

生活垃圾源头和末端小件废弃家电含量，按照小区示范工程中筛分数据和老港垃圾综合处理处置中心的末端分离数据，即：

The content of small discarded household appliances at the source and end of domestic waste is determined according to the screening data from the demonstration project in the residential community and the final separation data from the Laogang Integrated Waste Treatment and Disposal Center, namely:

- 小区生活源中的小件废弃家电约占干垃圾质量0.15%-0.30%; Small discarded household appliances in the domestic waste source of the community account for approximately 0.15%-0.30% of the dry waste mass
- 包括了商业源、企业源等来源的进入老港垃圾综合处理处置基地的生活垃圾末端处理中小件废弃家电含量相对更高，约为0.75%; The content of small discarded household appliances in the final treatment of domestic waste entering the Laogang Integrated Waste Treatment and Disposal Base, which includes commercial and enterprise sources, is relatively higher, about 0.75%;
- 本项目以0.15%-0.30%的干垃圾质量占比，作为小件废弃家电含量数据。This project takes the proportion of 0.15%-0.30% of the dry waste mass as the data for the content of small discarded household appliances.

2016-12-27实施  STechSouthern University

监督局 发布  Technology

小件废弃家用电器产量预测
# Forecast of Production Volume of Small Discarded Household Appliances

# 基于Prophet算法预测的上海市2019年至2030年小件废弃家电产量

# Forecast of Small Discarded Household Appliance Production in Shanghai from 2019 to 2030 Based on the Prophet Algorithm

在时间序列分析领域，有一种常见的分析方法叫做时间序列的分解(Decomposition of Time Series)，它把时间序列Yₜ分成几个部分，分别是季节项Sₜ，趋势项Tₜ，剩余项Rₜ。也就是说对所有的t≥0，都有

In the field of time series analysis, there is a common analytical method known as the Decomposition of Time Series, which divides a time series Yt into several components, namely the seasonal component St, the trend component Tt, and the residual component Rt. That is to say, for all t ≥ 0, the following holds:

Yₜ= Sₜ+Tₜ+Rₜ (1-1)

除了加法的形式，还有乘法的形式，即：

In addition to the additive form, there is also a multiplicative form, which is:

Yₜ= Sₜ*Tₜ*Rₜ (1-2)

以上公式等价于

The above formula is equivalent to lnYₜ = lnSₜ + lnTₜ + lnRₜ.

小件废弃家电在实际生活中，除了季节项、趋势项、剩余项之外，通常还有节假日效应，由此(1-2)演变为(1-3):

In real life, small discarded household appliances, apart from the seasonal component, trend component, and residual component, often have a holiday effect as well, which leads to the evolution of

Yₜ= Sₜ+ Tₜ+ Rₜ+ βₜ (1-3)

生活垃圾源头分类意愿与支付意愿调查
# Survey on Willingness for Source Separation and Willingness to Pay for Domestic Waste

# 调查基本情况 Basic Information of the Survey

- 涉及东中西部13省市共518份有效问卷 The survey encompasses 518 valid questionnaires from 13 provinces and cities across the eastern, central, and western regions of the country
- 基于问卷的面对面访谈式调研+以户为单位 conducted through face-to-face, questionnaire-based interviews with households as the unit of analysis
- 与村干部、农户、垃圾收集人员等非正式访谈 includes informal interviews with village officials, farmers, waste collection staff, and others
- 56.8%男，43.2%女 56.8% male, 43.2% female
- 66.5%介于26~60岁 66.5% aged between 26 and 60 years old
- 平均每户常住人口3.91人 with an average of 3.91 permanent residents per household
- 35.5%家庭年收入高于4万元 and 35.5% of families have an annual income exceeding 40,000 yuan.

# Survey on Willingness for Source Separation and Willingness to Pay for Domestic Waste

# 平均支付意愿

- 每个家庭平均支付意愿2.2-10元/月，即每年26.4-110元；The average willingness to pay per household is 2.2-10 yuan per month, which amounts to 26.4-110 yuan annually
- 与已有研究比较，结果相对较低；Compared to existing studies, the results are relatively low

应优先着眼于欠发达（相对）地区生活垃圾管理系统的建立，而非发达地区生活垃圾管理水平的提升。Priority should be given to the establishment of a domestic waste management system in less developed (relatively) regions, rather than the enhancement of waste management standards in developed areas.

# 基于有价废品收购的生活垃圾管理机制

# 背景：

- 有价废品是农村生活垃圾的典型组成部分，可占到5%~20% Valuable recyclables are a typical component of rural domestic waste, accounting for 5% to 20%
- 废品收购从业人员的重要角色 The significant role of professionals in the recyclables purchasing industry

示范区居民垃圾分类意识及行为调查
# Survey on Residents' Awareness and Behavior Regarding Waste Classification in the Demonstration Area

# 调研方式: 实地走访、入户访谈、发放问卷

# Research Methods: Field Visits, Household Interviews, and Questionnaire Distribution

# 结果分析

# Results

- 生活垃圾收集设施落后(37.1%)、垃圾清运频次低(67.7%)是导致居民对环境不满意的主要原因
- The primary reasons for residents' dissatisfaction with the environment are the outdated domestic waste collection facilities (37.1%) and the low frequency of waste removal (67.7%);
- 56.8%的居民表示愿意参与垃圾分类，积极性较高；
- 56.8% of residents expressed willingness to participate in waste sorting, showing a relatively high level of enthusiasm;
- 83.4%的居民会将日常产生的可回收废物单独分类，等待回收人员上门收购（45.2%）或直接卖给废品站（33.8%）
- 83.4% of residents separate recyclable waste generated daily, either waiting for collectors to pick it up (45.2%) or selling it directly to recycling stations (33.8%).

已有分类意识及行为基础，分类积极性高，但基础设施亟待完善。

There is already a foundation of awareness and behavior regarding waste sorting, with a high level of enthusiasm for classification, but the infrastructure is in urgent need of improvement.

2016-12-27实施  STechSouthern University of Science and Technology  监督局   发布

生活垃圾分类收集示范工程建设与运行
# Construction and Operation of Demonstration Projects for Classified Collection of Domestic Waste

# ③ 完善示范区生活垃圾基础设施: 布点

Improve the domestic waste infrastructure in the demonstration area: Principles for Site Allocation

生活垃圾收集设施的布点主要围绕道路、街道、居民住宅区进行

The placement of domestic waste collection facilities primarily revolves around roads, streets, and residential areas. The specific guidelines are as follows:

- a) 收集点间距：居民住宅区约为50~100m，商业性街道约为50~100m，主干路、次干路等交通道路，约为100~200m，并考虑对居民区、农田、河流等设置缓冲距离；

Spacing of Collection Points: Residential areas: Approximately 50–100 meters between collection points. Commercial streets: Approximately 50–100 meters between collection points. Main roads, secondary roads, and other traffic routes: Approximately 100–200 meters between collection points. Buffer zones should be established near residential areas, farmland, and rivers to minimize environmental impact.

2016-12-27实施 STechSouthern University of Science and Technology 监督局 发布

生活垃圾分类收集示范工程建设与运行
# Construction and Operation of Demonstration Projects for Classified Collection of Domestic Waste

# ③ 完善示范区生活垃圾基础设施: 布点

Improve the domestic waste infrastructure in the demonstration area: Principles for Site Allocation

生活垃圾收集设施的布点主要围绕道路、街道、居民住宅区进行

The placement of domestic waste collection facilities primarily revolves around roads, streets, and residential areas. The specific guidelines are as follows:

- 分类设施选择：主要交通道路、道路交叉口、村内主干道、功能性街道（村委会、村政府等所在街道）、商业性街道（餐饮业、菜市场）、居民小区，考虑优先布置分类垃圾房，道路情况不允许时，布置分类垃圾桶并就近配置分类垃圾房，住宅小区楼层处布置分类垃圾桶；
- Priority should be given to installing sorted waste rooms along main traffic roads, road intersections, village main roads, functional streets (e.g., streets with village committees or government offices), commercial streets (e.g., areas with restaurants and markets), and residential communities. Where road conditions do not allow for waste rooms, sorted waste bins should be placed, accompanied by nearby sorted waste rooms. In residential buildings, sorted waste bins should be placed on each floor.

2016-12-27实施

STech Southern University of Science and Technology

监督局 发布

# Construction and Operation of Demonstration Projects for Classified Collection of Domestic Waste

# ③ 完善示范区生活垃圾基础设施: 布点

Improve the domestic waste infrastructure in the demonstration area: Principles for Site Allocation

生活垃圾收集设施的布点主要围绕道路、街道、居民住宅区进行

The placement of domestic waste collection facilities primarily revolves around roads, streets, and residential areas. The specific guidelines are as follows:

- 分类垃圾桶匹配比：根据区域特征合理调整垃圾桶配置比例，如餐饮业、菜市场区域可增设双层沥水垃圾桶，建筑施工区域增设一般垃圾桶，祜水小学附近额外配置可回收分类垃圾桶等。
- Matching Ratio of Sorted Waste Bins: Adjust the ratio of waste bins based on regional characteristics. For example: Double-layer drainage bins can be added in areas with restaurants and markets. General waste bins can be added in construction zones. Additional recyclable waste bins can be placed near Hushui Primary School.

These measures aim to optimize waste collection efficiency, promote waste sorting, and reduce environmental impact while catering to the specific needs of different areas.

2016-12-27实施

STech Southern University of Science and Technology

b) 分类垃圾袋

Sorted Waste Bags: 以家庭或户为单位领取，不腐烂/其他垃圾袋为每月1卷；有毒有害垃圾袋每两月1卷；有机易腐烂垃圾袋每年6~10月可免费领取2卷，其他月份为1卷。

Distributed to households, non-perishable/other waste bags are provided at a rate of 1 roll per month; hazardous waste bags are provided at a rate of 1 roll every two months; organic perishable waste bags are available for free at a rate of 2 rolls per month from June to October, and 1 roll per month during other months.

# 生活垃圾分类组织方式

# Organizational Methods for Domestic Waste Classification

---

社会化与专业化分类

# 社会化分类

Socialized Classification: 系指全社会（从居民家庭到企事业等单位，各行各业的不同年龄、性别、文化程度、工作岗位的所有人）都参与的垃圾分类活动。目前，全国范围内推行的垃圾分类本质上属于社会化分类，且大多数是按其形状/理化特征分类。

This refers to waste sorting activities that involve the entire society, encompassing all individuals from residential households to enterprises and institutions, across various industries, ages, genders, educational backgrounds, and job positions. Currently, the waste sorting initiatives being promoted nationwide are essentially socialized classification, with most categorizations based on the shape or physicochemical characteristics of the waste.

# 专业化分类

Professional Classification: 专指少数人利用技术/技能及设施设备进行垃圾分类。专业化分类的特点是实行垃圾分类的方式与过程不在于公众参与及社会化而是专业化，即专有技术、专用设备、专业人员（简称“三专”）。

This specifically denotes waste sorting conducted by a minority utilizing specialized technology/skills and facilities/equipment. The hallmark of professional classification is that the method and process of waste sorting do not rely on public participation and socialization but rather on specialization, which includes proprietary technology, dedicated equipment, and professional personnel (collectively referred to as the "three specializations").

2016-12-27实施 STechSouthern University 监督局 发布 Technology

---


# 企业形式 Business model

- 成立xxx市生活垃圾分类有限公司 Establish xxx City Domestic Waste Classification Co., Ltd.
- 每个小区分派2-3人，负责现场指导分类、自己分类、分类废品存放、垃圾桶维修（加盖、防雨水、消毒）Assign 2-3 personnel to each residential community, responsible for on-site sorting guidance, self-sorting, storage of sorted recyclables, and maintenance of waste bins (covering, rainproofing, disinfection);
- 公司负责各类垃圾收运、销售 The company is responsible for the collection, transportation, and sale of various types of waste;
- 不能销售的垃圾，由公司运至焚烧厂或填埋场，政府付费 For waste that cannot be sold, the company will transport it to incineration plants or landfills, with the government covering the costs.

2016-12-27实施 STech Southern University of Science and Technology 监督局 发布

- 由焚烧厂负责生活垃圾分类 The incineration plant is responsible for the classification of domestic waste
- 每个小区分派2-3人，负责现场指导分类、自己分类、分类废品存放、垃圾桶维修（加盖、防雨水、消毒）Assign 2-3 personnel to each residential community, responsible for on-site sorting guidance, self-sorting, storage of sorted recyclables, and maintenance of waste bins (covering, rainproofing, disinfection)
- 公司负责各类垃圾收运、销售 The company is responsible for the collection, transportation, and sale of various types of waste;
- 不能销售的垃圾，由公司运至焚烧厂，政府不再付费 For waste that cannot be sold, the company will transport it to the incineration plant, and the government will no longer cover the costs.

垃圾收运

Yang Lei

2025-03-02

垃圾收集质量要求

# Quality Requirements for Waste Collection

1. 垃圾收集容器应定位设置，摆放整齐。设置点及周围2～3m内应整洁，无散落、存留垃圾和污水。

Waste collection containers should be positioned and arranged neatly. The setup point and the surrounding area of 2–3 meters should be clean, with no scattered or leftover waste or sewage.
2. 垃圾收集容器应无残缺、破损，封闭性好，外体干净。构筑物内外墙面不得有明显积灰、污物。

Waste collection containers should be intact, undamaged, well-sealed, and clean on the exterior. The interior and exterior walls of the structures should not have visible dust or dirt.
3. 特种垃圾的收集，必须用设有明显标志、能防止污染扩散的密封容器。

Special waste must be collected in sealed containers with clear markings to prevent pollution from spreading.
4. 蝇、蚊孳生季节，垃圾收集站(点)，应定时喷洒消毒、灭蚊蝇药物。在可视范围内，苍蝇应少于3只/次。

During seasons when flies and mosquitoes breed, waste collection stations (points) should be regularly disinfected and sprayed with insect repellent. Within the visible range, there should be fewer than 3 flies per inspection.

-100-

---

垃圾收集质量要求

# Quality Requirements for Waste Collection

⑤ 楼房垃圾管道的底层垃圾间应整洁，无散落垃圾和积留污水 ，无恶臭，基本无蝇。

The ground-floor waste rooms of building garbage chutes should be clean, with no scattered waste or accumulated sewage, no foul odor, and essentially no flies.

⑥ 生活垃圾应全部实行容器收集 ，有条件的地区，可实行分类收集。

Domestic waste should be entirely collected using containers. In areas with the necessary conditions, waste should be collected separately.

⑦ 居民应按规定将生活垃圾倒入垃圾收集容器内。实行分类、袋装收集的地区，居民应将垃圾分类、袋装封闭后 ，定时投入收集容器内或放置于指定的收集点。

Residents should dispose of domestic waste into waste collection containers as required. In areas where waste is sorted and bagged, residents should sort and bag their waste, seal it, and dispose of it into collection containers or designated collection points at specified times.

⑧ 企 、 事业等单位应将所产生的生活垃圾投放于自设的收集容器内 ， 不得裸露堆放。

Enterprises, institutions, and other units should dispose of their domestic waste into their own collection containers and must not leave it exposed or piled up.

-101-

---

垃圾收集质量要求

# Quality Requirements for Waste Collection

⑨ 特种垃圾、工业垃圾和建筑垃圾 ，必须与生活垃圾分开存放 ，分别收集。

Special waste, industrial waste, and construction waste must be stored and collected separately from domestic waste.

⑩ 居民的生活垃圾应每日清除，无堆积；单位的生活垃圾应按时清除，无积压，不腐烂发臭；废旧家具、家用电器等粗大垃圾应按指定地点存放，定期清除。

Residents' domestic waste should be cleared daily, with no accumulation; units' domestic waste should be cleared on time, with no backlog, and should not rot or emit foul odors; bulky waste such as old furniture and household appliances should be stored at designated locations and cleared regularly.

⑪ 收集作业完成后，应及时清理场地，将可移动式垃圾收集容器复位，车走地净。垃圾应直接送至指定的转运站或处置场。

After collection operations are completed, the site should be promptly cleaned, and movable waste collection containers should be reset. The area should be left clean, and waste should be directly transported to designated transfer stations or disposal sites.

-102-

---

垃圾收集质量要求 Quality Requirements for Waste Collection

⑫ 废物箱内的垃圾应及时清除、无满溢和散落，并定时清洗箱体。 Waste in litter bins should be cleared promptly, with no overflow or scattering, and the bins should be cleaned regularly.

⑬ 地面(含天桥、地道)清扫的垃圾应及时收集和运输，不遗漏，不得堆放在路边。 Waste from ground (including overpasses and underpasses) sweeping should be collected and transported promptly, with no omissions, and must not be piled on the roadside.

⑭ 公交车、长途汽车、飞机、火车上的垃圾，应由营运单位自行袋装收集，按规定处置，严禁任意排放。Waste from buses, long-distance coaches, airplanes, and trains should be bagged and collected by the operating units and disposed of according to regulations. Random disposal is strictly prohibited.

-103-

---

垃圾收集质量要求 Quality Requirements for Waste Collection

⑮ 水域沿岸的码头 、 趸船 、 单位所产生的垃圾应定时收集 。 严禁向水域倾倒或抛撒垃圾 。

Waste generated by docks, pontoons, and units along waterfront areas should be collected regularly. Dumping or scattering waste into water bodies is strictly prohibited.

⑯ 行驶、停泊在市区水域的各类船舶产生的生活垃圾，应按规定分类袋装，定时收集，统一处置；扫舱垃圾及特种垃圾应分类存放 ， 按规定处置 ， 严禁任意排放 。

Domestic waste generated by vessels navigating or docked in urban waters should be sorted, bagged, and collected regularly for unified disposal. Cabin sweepings and special waste should be stored separately and disposed of according to regulations. Random disposal is strictly prohibited.

⑰ 市内水域漂浮垃圾应有专人负责打捞，及时清除；主要河段、湖区应每天清除，在可视范围内水面不得有单个面积在 0.5m² 以上的漂浮垃圾和动物尸体 。

Floating waste in urban waters should be cleared promptly by designated personnel; major river sections and lake areas should be cleared daily, and no floating waste or animal carcasses larger than 0.5 square meters should be visible on the water surface.

-104-

---


# 收集设备与设施

# Collection equipment and facilities

- 收集点：接收居民投放垃圾的地点（没有房子或简易房子），布置密度：城市70米，乡镇100米，村庄200米；
Collection Point: A location where residents can drop off waste (without a house or simple structure). Placement density: 70 meters in urban areas, 100 meters in towns, and 200 meters in villages.
- 收集站：接收附近居民投放垃圾的设施（有房子）；
Collection Station: A facility that receives waste from nearby residents (with a building).
- 服务半径：人力0.4 km；小型机动车2 km；
Service radius: 0.4 km for manual collection; 2 km for small motorized vehicles.

# 垃圾转运质量要求

# Waste transfer quality requirements

1. 转运站应有防尘、防污染扩散及污水处置等设施。
The transfer station should have facilities such as dust prevention, pollution prevention and sewage disposal.
2. 转运站内外场地应整洁，无撒落垃圾和堆积杂物，无积留污水。
The site inside and outside the transfer station should be clean, without scattered garbage and piled up debris, and no accumulated sewage.
3. 室内通风应良好，无恶臭，墙壁、窗户应无积尘、蛛网。
The indoor ventilation should be good, without bad smell, and the walls and windows should be free of dust and cobwebs.
4. 进入站内的垃圾应当日转运，有贮存设施的，应加盖封闭，定时转运。
The garbage entering the station should be transferred on the same day. If there are storage facilities, they should be covered and closed, and transferred regularly.
5. 站内垃圾装运容器应整洁，无积垢，无吊挂垃圾。
The garbage shipping containers in the station should be clean, without dirt, and no hanging garbage.



---

垃圾转运质量要求 Waste transfer quality requirements

⑥ 蚊蝇孳生季节，应每天喷药灭蚊蝇。在可视范围内，站内苍蝇应少于3只/次 In the mosquito and fly breeding season, mosquitoes and flies should be sprayed every day. Within the visible range, there should be less than 3 flies/time in the station.

⑦ 除急用，有条件的地区应建设密闭转运站，不宜长期采用露天临时转运点转运垃圾。 Except for emergency use, areas with conditions should build closed transfer stations, and it is not appropriate to use open-air temporary transfer points to transfer garbage for a long time.

⑧ 垃圾临时转运点距离居民住地不得小于300m。场地周围应设置不低于2.5m的防护围栏和污水排放渠道。 The temporary transfer point of garbage shall not be less than 300m away from residential areas. A protective fence and sewage discharge channel of not less than 2.5m should be set up around the site.

⑨ 装卸垃圾应有降尘措施，地面应无散落垃圾和污水。 Dust reduction measures should be taken when loading and unloading garbage, and there should be no scattered garbage and sewage on the ground.

-110-

---


# 垃圾转运质量要求 Waste transfer quality requirements

1. 垃圾应及时转运，蚊蝇孳生季节应定时喷药灭蚊蝇，在可视范围内苍蝇应少于6只/次，无恶臭。

Garbage should be transferred in time, mosquito and fly breeding season should be regularly sprayed to kill mosquitoes and flies, flies should be less than 6/time within the visible range, and there should be no bad smell （The temporary transfer point）.
2. 场地应有专人管理，工具、物品置放应有序整洁。

The site should be managed by a dedicated person, and tools and items should be placed in an orderly and tidy manner.
3. 通过码头转运垃圾时，应逐步采用密闭方式集装，除特殊情况外，不得在转运码头堆放垃圾。

When transferring garbage through the dock, it should be gradually packed in a closed manner. Except for special circumstances, garbage should not be piled up at the transfer dock.



---


# 垃圾转运质量要求

# Waste transfer quality requirements

⑬ 垃圾转运码头应设置防散落、防飞扬和降尘设施，垃圾不得散落于水体。作业场地应有污水收集管道或收集池，有条件的，应把污水处理后排入城市污水管网。

Garbage transfer docks should be equipped with anti-scattering, anti-flying and dust reduction facilities, and garbage should not be scattered in the water. The operation site should have sewage collection pipes or collection pools. If conditions permit, sewage should be treated and discharged into the urban sewage network.

⑭ 转运码头及周围环境应整洁，装卸作业完毕，应及时清扫场地。

The transfer dock and the surrounding environment should be clean, and the site should be cleaned in time after loading and unloading operations.

⑮ 蝇蚊孳生季节应定时喷洒药物，在可视范围内，转运码头的苍蝇应少于6只/次，无恶臭。

During the mosquito breeding season, drugs should be sprayed regularly. Flies should be less than 6/time within the visible range at the transfer dock, and there should be no bad smell.



---


# 生活垃圾运输 Waste Transfer

# 直运模式选择 Direct transfer mode

• 垃圾收运的基本原则是尽量减少中间环节。因此具备直运条件的城市或区域应优先选择直运模式。 The basic principle of garbage collection and transportation is to minimize the intermediate links. Therefore, cities or regions with direct transportation conditions should give priority to the direct transportation mode.

# 直运条件 Requirements for direct transfer

- • 垃圾运输车可直接靠近垃圾收集点 Garbage trucks can approach garbage collection points directly
- • 垃圾处理场（厂）距城市的距离较近（在15km以内） Garbage disposal sites (plants) are close to cities (within 15 km)
- • 垃圾运输车与垃圾收集点的垃圾容器配套 Garbage trucks are equipped with garbage containers at garbage collection points



---

生活垃圾运输 Waste Transfer

# 转运模式选择 Indirect transfer model

当无法采用直运时，或者运输距离过大直运成本过高时采用转运模式 When direct shipping is not possible, or the transportation distance is too long and the direct shipping cost is too high, the indirect transfer model mode is used.

# 转运条件 Requirements for direct transfer

- 垃圾运输车无法靠近垃圾收集点 ，只能靠人力车或小型机（电）动车将垃圾收集点的垃圾运出。
Garbage trucks cannot get close to garbage collection points, and can only rely on rickshaws or small motor (electric) vehicles to transport garbage from garbage collection points.
- 垃圾处理场（厂）距城市过远（平均运距15km以上,可建小转运站，20km以上的可建中大型转运站）。
The garbage treatment site (plant) is too far away from the city (the average transportation distance is more than 15km, a small transfer station can be built, and more than 20km can be built. Medium-sized and large transfer stations).
- 直接运输成本过大，通过转运站将小车（压缩或非压缩）换成大型垃圾运输车运输（一般是压缩后运输），可使垃圾总运输成本节省。
The direct transportation cost is too high. By replacing small vehicles (compressed or non-compressed) with large garbage trucks for transportation (generally compressed transportation) through transfer stations, the total transportation cost of garbage can be saved.

-114-

---


# 生活垃圾运输

# Waste Transfer

# 转运模式选择

# Choice of transfer model

| 小型转运站压缩转运                                | 小型转运站压非压缩转运                                  | 大型转运站压水平压缩转运                                           |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| Compression-based small transfer station | Non-compression-based small transfer station | Large transfer station horizontal compression transfer |



---


# 水平压缩转运 Horizontal compression transfer

# ① 预压缩式 Pre compression:

垃圾进入压缩机后，在压缩机的预压仓内先进行压缩，从而最终形成一个密实的垃圾包， 然后被一次性推入垃圾集装箱中，适合于处理规模较大的垃圾转运站采用。After the garbage enters the compressor, it is first compressed in the pre-compression chamber of the compressor, thus eventually forming a dense garbage bag, which is then pushed into the garbage container at one time. It is suitable for use in garbage transfer stations with larger processing scales.

# ② 直接压缩式 Direct compression:

压缩机将进入其中的垃圾直接推入垃圾集装箱内，边装边压实，对于处理量1200吨/天以下的垃圾转运站较为适宜。The compressor pushes the incoming garbage directly into the garbage container, compacting it while loading. It is more suitable for garbage transfer stations with a processing capacity of less than 1200 tons/day.

垃圾收运过程的环境卫生风险防控

Yang Lei

2025-03-09
