# 垃圾分类过程伴随环境卫生风险

Waste sorting is accompanied by environmental hygiene risks.

- 全过程分类，增加了新增容器、破袋、二次纠偏分拣、人工分拣分选等环境风险暴露环节；

The entire process of waste categorization introduces additional environmental risk exposure points, such as the introduction of new containers, the breaking of bags, the secondary correction and sorting, and manual sorting and selection.
- 敏感人群与垃圾接触环节与频率增加，二次污染物和病原微生物对暴露人群产生环境卫生风险；

The increased interaction and frequency of contact between sensitive populations and waste increase the environmental health risks posed by secondary pollutants and pathogenic microorganisms to those exposed.
- 应加强环境风险防控工作；

It is imperative to enhance the efforts in environmental risk prevention and control.

# 传染病种类 Types of infectious

# 按照法定管理规定 Classified based on law:

- ✓ 甲类传染病：鼠疫、霍乱。Class A Infectious Diseases: Plague, Cholera.
- ✓ 乙类传染病：如艾滋病、病毒性肝炎、麻疹、狂犬病、登革热、肺结核等。Class B Infectious Diseases: Such as AIDS, viral hepatitis, measles, rabies, dengue fever, tuberculosis, etc.
- ✓ 丙类传染病：如流行性感冒、流行性腮腺炎、风疹、急性出血性结膜炎等。Class C Infectious Diseases: Such as influenza, mumps, rubella, acute hemorrhagic conjunctivitis, etc.

# 常见病菌与寄生虫死亡温度

# Lethal Temperatures for Common Bacteria and Parasites

| 名称 Name                           | 死亡情况 Lethal Conditions                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 沙门氏伤寒菌 Salmonella typhi           | 55\~60 ℃，30 min内死亡 dies within 30 minutes at 55\~60 °C.                                                                                                      |
| 沙门氏菌属 Salmonella                  | 56 ℃，1 h内死亡；60 ℃，15\~20 min死亡 dies within 20 minutes at 60 °C.                                                                                               |
| 志贺氏杆菌 Shigella                    | 55 ℃，1 h内死亡 dies within 60 minutes at 55 °C.                                                                                                                 |
| 大肠杆菌 Escherichia Coli             | 绝大部分：55 ℃，1 h内死亡；60 ℃，15\~20 min内死亡 dies within 60 minutes at 55 °C.                                                                                         |
| 阿米巴属 Amoeba                       | 68 ℃死亡 dies at 68 °C.                                                                                                                                        |
| 无钩涤虫 Unhooked polyester worm      | 71 ℃，5 min内死亡 dies within 5 minutes at 71 °C.                                                                                                                |
| 美洲钩虫 American hookworm            | 45 ℃，50 min内死亡 dies within 50 minutes at 45 °C.                                                                                                              |
| 流产布鲁士菌 Brucella abortus           | 61 ℃，3 min内死亡 dies within 3 minutes at 61 °C.                                                                                                                |
| 化脓性细球菌 Mlicrococcus pyogenes      | 50 ℃，10 min内死亡 dies within 10 minutes at 50 °C.                                                                                                              |
| 酿浓链球菌 Streptococcus denosa        | 54 ℃，10 min内死亡 dies within 10 minutes at 54 °C.                                                                                                              |
| 结核分枝杆菌 Mycobacterium tuberculosis | 66 ℃，15\~20 min内死亡，有时在67 ℃死亡 dies within 20 minutes at 67 °C.                                                                                                |
| 牛结核杆菌 Mycobacterium bovis         | 55 ℃，45 min内死亡 dies within 45 minutes at 55 °C.                                                                                                              |
| 新型冠状病毒 novel coronavirus          | 56 ℃ 30 min可灭活；75%乙醇，含氯消毒剂可消毒 dies within 30 minutes at 56 °C.                                                                                               |
| 疯牛病病毒（朊病毒）Mad cow disease         | 高温高压强碱，300 ℃\~400 ℃左右持续3-5小时即可消灭 High temperature, high pressure, virus (Prion) and strong alkali can eliminate it at around 300 °C to 400 °C for 3-5 hours. |

# 消毒方法比较

# Comparison of disinfection methods

| 消毒水平  | 作用范围                              | 常见消毒方法                             |
| ----- | --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
| 高水平消毒 | 杀灭一切细菌繁殖体包括分枝杆菌、病毒、真菌及其孢子和致病性细菌芽孢 | 含氯消毒剂、含溴消毒剂、二氧化氯、过氧乙酸、过氧化氢、臭氧、紫外线等 |
| 中水平消毒 | 杀灭细菌繁殖体、分枝杆菌、真菌和病毒                | 碘类消毒剂、醇类及醇类复方消毒剂、酚类消毒剂             |
| 低水平消毒 | 杀灭一般细菌繁殖体和亲脂病毒                    | 季铵盐类消毒剂、胍类消毒剂、碱类消毒剂                |

# 常用消毒方法优缺点

# Draws and Merits of typical disinfection methods

| 消毒方法  | 有效成分                              | 应用范围                                          | 优点                     | 缺点                                                    |
| ----- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ---------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| 乙醇    | 乙醇含量为 70%-80%                     | 手、皮肤、较小物体表面                                   | 中效、速效；无毒；无残留；对金属无腐蚀性   | 易挥发、易燃；对皮肤粘膜有刺激；受有机物影响大；不宜大面积使用和用于脂溶性物体表面的消毒，不可用于空气消毒 |
| 含氯消毒剂 | 漂白粉 ≥20%，二氯异氰尿酸钠 ≥55%，84消毒液 2%-5% | 物体表面、织物等污染物品；水、果蔬和食具等；室内空气、二次供水设备设施表面、手、皮肤和黏膜 | 杀菌广谱；高效、速效；低毒；价格廉；使用方便 | 对金属有腐蚀性；对织物有漂白、褪色作用；受有机物影响大；不稳定，有效氯成分易丧失；有刺激性气味       |

| -------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------ | ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |

微酸性次氯酸水发生器消毒除臭设备工作原理
# Working principle of slightly acidic hypochlorous acid water generator

# disinfection and deodorization equipment

• 利用无隔膜式电解槽将盐酸水溶液电解，生成以次氯酸为主要杀菌成分的酸性水溶液的装置。通过电解，在阳极生成氯气和H⁺，H⁺溶于水使水呈酸性，pH值为5.0-6.5，氯气与水反应生成盐酸和次氯酸，阴极只生成氢气。其反应方程式如下：

A device that uses a non-diaphragm electrolytic cell to electrolyze hydrochloric acid aqueous solution to generate an acidic aqueous solution with hypochlorous acid as the main bactericidal component. Through electrolysis, chlorine and H+ are generated at the anode. H+ dissolves in water to make the water acidic with a pH value of 5.0-6.5. Chlorine reacts with water to generate hydrochloric acid and hypochlorous acid, and only hydrogen is generated at the cathode. The reaction equation is as follows:

Cl- → 1/2Cl2 + e-
Cl2 + H2O ⇋ HClO + H+ + Cl-
H+ + e- → 1/2H2

-131-

---

微酸性次氯酸水发生器消毒除臭设备工作原理
# Working principle of slightly acidic hypochlorous acid water generator

# disinfection and deodorization equipment

次氯酸可作为强氧化性消毒剂，与硫化氢、氨气发生化学反应，生成臭味较轻或无臭味的物质，有效控制垃圾恶臭。其反应方程式如下：

Hypochlorous acid can be used as a strong oxidizing disinfectant to react chemically with hydrogen sulfide and ammonia to produce substances with less or no odor, effectively controlling the foul smell of garbage. The reaction equation is as follows:

| H₂S + 4HClO | == | 4HCl + H₂SO₄       |
| ----------- | -- | ------------------ |
| 3NH₃ + HClO | == | N₂H₄ + NH₄Cl + H₂O |

-132-

---

微酸性次氯酸水发生器消毒除臭设备

• 杀菌基础是次氯酸（HClO），能损害细胞膜，致使细胞内的蛋白酶、RNA和DNA无法发挥正常的生化活性，导致微生物死亡，且与微生物及有机物接触后立即还原为水。与有机物反应方程式如下：

The sterilization basis is hypochlorous acid (HClO), which can damage the cell membrane, causing the protease, RNA and DNA in the cell to be unable to perform normal biochemical activities, resulting in the death of microorganisms, and immediately reducing to water after contact with microorganisms and organic matter. The reaction equation with organic matter is as follows:

[ HOCl + protein of pathogens or organisms]
H-O-Cl + R-NH₂ → R-NH-Cl + H₂O
H-O-Cl + R-SH → R-S-Cl + H₂O

-133-

---

微酸性次氯酸水发生器消毒除臭设备

# Demonstration of disinfection and deodorization of domestic garbage with slightly acidic hypochlorous acid water generator

- 在上海市多个高层、老公房、别墅小区安装了微酸性次氯酸水发生器；Slightly acidic hypochlorous acid water generators have been installed in many high-rise buildings, old public housing, and villa communities in Shanghai;
- 该设备采用电解法生成次氯酸水对垃圾房进行喷雾消毒除臭处理，有效氯含量为40-80 mg/L、pH值5.0-6.5、残留氯离子&#x3C;1000 mg/L、生成量≥1000 mL/min；其特点是低浓度、易分解、残留量小、安全性较高。The equipment uses electrolysis to generate hypochlorous acid water for spray disinfection and deodorization of garbage rooms, with an effective chlorine content of 40-80 mg/L, a pH value of 5.0-6.5, residual chloride ions &#x3C;1000 mg/L, and a generation volume of ≥1000 mL/min; its characteristics are low concentration, easy decomposition, small residue, and high safety.

破碎—颚式破碎机

# Crushing-Jaw Crusher

- 颚式破碎机主要工作件是两个颚板，即动颚和固定颚，两者成一夹角；The jaw crusher primarily consists of two jaw plates, namely the movable jaw and the fixed jaw, which are positioned at an angle to each other.
- 颚式破碎机分为简摆和复摆式两类；Jaw crushers are categorized into two types: the simple pendulum and the compound pendulum styles.

# 颚式破碎机主要类型与结构原理示意图

| (a) | 1 | 2 | 3 |
| --- | - | - | - |
| (b) | 1 | 2 | 4 |
| 5   | 4 | 3 |   |
| 6   | 6 |   | 7 |
|     | 6 |   | 7 |

(a)简摆式; (b)复摆式；1-固定颚；2-动颚；3-悬挂轴；4-偏心轴；5-连杆；6-推力板；7-排料口间隙调整装置。

(a) Simple Pendulum Type; (b) Compound Pendulum Type; 1-Fixed Jaw; 2-Movable Jaw; 3-Suspension Shaft; 4-Eccentric Shaft; 5-Connecting Rod; 6-Thrust Plate; 7-Discharge Opening Adjustment Device.

-3-

---

破碎—圆锥破碎机 Crushing-Cone Crusher

圆锥破碎机主要工作件是两个正截锥体，定锥1和动锥2，两锥轴线成一夹角。偏心轴套6在传动齿轮5的驱动下，使主轴4做偏心旋转运动，因物料的摩擦作用，动锥沿定锥内表面产生自转，致其表面磨损趋于均匀。The main working parts of the cone crusher are two right truncated cones, the fixed cone 1 and the moving cone 2, and the axes of the two cones form an angle. The eccentric sleeve 6, driven by the transmission gear 5, makes the main shaft 4 do eccentric rotation. Due to the friction of the material, the moving cone rotates along the inner surface of the fixed cone, causing its surface wear to tend to be uniform.

圆锥破碎机分为旋回破碎机（粗碎）和菌形圆锥破碎机（中/细碎）两类。

---

破碎—锤式破碎机 Crushing-Hammer Crusher

• 锤式破碎机主要工作件是带有锤头2的转子3，通过高速旋转，使锤头打击物料并使之与冲击板1撞击进行破碎，大于篦条筛4篦缝尺寸的物料阻留在篦条上，继续受到锤子的冲击和磨剥作用直至粒度小于篦缝排出。The main working part of the hammer crusher is the rotor 3 with a hammer head 2. Through high-speed rotation, the hammer head strikes the material and causes it to collide with the strike plate 1 for crushing. The material larger than the grate size of the grate screen 4 is retained on the grate bar and continues to be impacted and abraded by the hammer until the particle size is smaller than the grate gap and discharged.

• 锤式破碎机种类较多 ，按转子数目，分为单转子和双转子两类。There are many types of hammer crushers, which are divided into single-rotor and double-rotor types according to the number of rotors.

---

破碎—锤式破碎机

# Crushing-Hammer Crusher

锤式破碎机生产能力大，破碎比高，单位能耗较低，且结构简单、紧凑。但粉碎坚硬物料时锤头和篦条磨损较大，粉碎粘湿性物料时处理能力降低。

Hammer crusher has large production capacity, high crushing ratio, low unit energy consumption, simple and compact structure. However, the hammer head and grate bars are more worn when crushing hard materials, and the processing capacity is reduced when crushing sticky materials.

| 单锤破碎机  | Single hammer crusher |
| ------ | --------------------- |
| 双转子破碎机 | Double rotor crusher  |

粉磨—球磨机 Grinding₋Ball Mill

• 主要由轴承与支座1、筒体5、衬板2、研磨体3、隔仓板4，及进出料装置和传动装置等组成； It is mainly composed of bearings and supports 1, cylinder 5, lining plate 2, grinding body 3, partition plate 4, feeding and discharging device and transmission device;

• 研磨体通常为钢球、钢段或钢棒等，也有用陶瓷、砾石以控制研磨体磨损掺杂污染。当筒体转动时，研磨体和物料随筒体上升至一定高度后，呈抛落冲击状态或呈泻落下滑状态，周而复始循环工作； The grinding body is usually steel ball, steel segment or steel rod, etc., and ceramics and gravel are also used to control the wear and contamination of the grinding body. When the cylinder rotates, the grinding body and the material rise to a certain height with the cylinder, and then they are in a state of falling impact or falling down, and the cycle is repeated;

# 球磨机的主要工作参数

# Main working parameters of ball mill

① 工作转速 Working speed

nc=42.4/√D₀  ; nₜ=32.2/√D₀

式中nc、nₜ—分别为磨机的临界转速和理论适宜转速(r/min) ；D₀—筒体的有效直径，即筒体内径减去2倍的衬板厚度(m)；

In the formula, nc and nₜ are the critical speed and theoretical suitable speed of the mill respectively (r/min); D₀ is the effective diameter of the cylinder, that is, the inner diameter of the cylinder minus 2 times the thickness of the liner (m);

球磨机实际工作转速的确定需要考虑磨机的规格 、衬板形状、磨介种类、生产方
式 、 填充率 、 物料性质和粒度等因素 。 一般是临界转速的 65~85% ， 较多的是
65~78%。

The determination of the actual working speed of the mill needs to consider factors such as the specifications of the mill, the shape of the liner, the type of grinding media, the production method, the filling rate, the material properties and the particle size. Generally, it is 65~85% of the critical speed, and more often it is 65~78%.



---


# 球磨机的主要工作参数

# Main working parameters of ball mill

# ② 磨介填充率

# Grinding medium filling rate

ϕ=Vₜ/Vₘ×100%

Vₜ is the total volume of the grinding medium; Vₘ is the effective volume of the grinding equipment.

磨介填充率一般在25~45%之间。对干法粉磨取值较低，在28~35%之间。棒磨机钢棒填充率，湿法时取35~40%，干法时取35%。国内水泥工业磨介填充率的取值：闭路流程球磨：40%；闭路流程中长磨：30~32%；开路流程多仓磨：25~28%；粉磨兼烘干式磨机，一般填充率均小于25~28%。

The filling rate of grinding media is generally between 25 and 45%. For dry grinding, the value is lower, between 28 and 35%. The filling rate of steel rods in rod mills is 35 to 40% in wet process and 35% in dry process. The values of the filling rate of grinding media in the domestic cement industry are: closed-circuit process ball mill: 40%; closed-circuit process medium and long mill: 30 to 32%; open-circuit process multi-bin mill: 25 to 28%; grinding and drying mills, the filling rate is generally less than 25 to 28%.



---

# 粉磨作业流程 Grinding process

# 开路系统 Open-circuit system

•  开路系统无分级设备，物料从磨机中出来后即为产品 。流程简单，设备少，操作简便，基
建投资少 。 其缺点是容易产生过粉磨 ， 粉磨效率低 ， 电耗高 ， 产量低 。 The open-circuit
system has no grading equipment, and the material is the product after it comes out of the mill. The
process is simple, the equipment is few, the operation is simple, and the capital investment is small.
Its disadvantages are that it is easy to produce over-grinding, low grinding efficiency, high power
consumption, and low output.

# 闭路系统 Closed-circuit system

•  闭路系统有分级设备，出磨物料须经分级设备分选，合格细粉为产品，粗粉返回磨内重磨。
The closed-circuit system has grading equipment, and the material out of the mill must be sorted by
the grading equipment. The qualified fine powder is the product, and the coarse powder is returned
to the mill for re-grinding.

•  闭路系统可以消除过粉磨现象 ，可调控产品粒度，且能提高粉磨效率和产量 。其缺点是流
程复杂，设备多，基建投资大，操作管理复杂。The closed-circuit system can eliminate the
phenomenon of over-grinding, can adjust the product particle size, and can improve the grinding
efficiency and output. Its  disadvantages are complex processes, many equipment, large capital
investment, and complex operation and management.

---


# 粉磨作业流程 Grinding process

在闭路系统中，分级机的回料量T与成品量Q之比，以百分数计称为循环负荷率K，循环负荷率一般在50%～300%。In a closed-loop system, the ratio of the return material volume T of the classifier to the finished product volume Q is called the circulation load rate K in percentage. The circulation load rate is generally between 50% and 300%.



---


# 辊压机 Roller Press

辊压机通过两个辊子对物料施以巨大的挤压力（50～500MPa），使物料粉碎并压成所谓“料饼”，颗粒内部产生大量裂纹和应力，使其易于后续粉磨，即通过所谓“预应力粉碎”来显著改善物料的易磨性，起到预粉磨作用。料饼打散后，其中有较多的细颗粒已达粗磨和中磨的粒度若与分级机配套，可组成闭路终粉磨系统，故辊压机也称为辊压磨或挤压磨。

The roller press applies a huge squeezing force (50-500MPa) to the material through two rollers, crushing the material and pressing it into the so-called "material cake". A large number of cracks and stresses are generated inside the particles, making them easy to grind later. That is, the so-called "pre-stressed crushing" significantly improves the grindability of the material and plays a pre-grinding role. After the material cake is broken up, there are more fine particles that have reached the particle size of coarse grinding and medium grinding. If it is equipped with a classifier, it can form a closed-circuit final grinding system, so the roller press is also called a roller mill or extrusion mill.

辊筒磨 Roller mill

# 辊筒磨 Roller mill

辊筒磨，又称HORO磨，是由法国公司研发。

主要工作原理(与辊压机的区别 )：不仅在于辊压机用两个辊子的外表面挤压物料，辊筒磨是用圆筒的内表面与磨辊的外表面挤压物料，而且辊筒磨的筒体转动时，使物料在离心力作用下沿筒体内壁铺展成薄料层，并带动磨辊在同向回转过程中，以中、高粉磨压力，对物料进行循环挤压粉磨。

Roller mill, also known as HORO mill, was developed by a French company. The main working principle (the difference from roller press): not only does the roller press use the outer surface of two rollers to squeeze the material, but the roller mill uses the inner surface of the cylinder and the outer surface of the grinding roller to squeeze the material. When the roller mill rotates, the material spreads along the inner wall of the cylinder under the action of centrifugal force to form a thin layer, and drives the grinding roller to rotate in the same direction, with medium and high grinding pressure, to circulate and extrude the material.

超细粉碎 Ultrafine grinding

# 超细球磨机 Ultrafine ball mill

- 球磨机用作超细粉碎时，通常需要与超细分级机构成干法闭路粉碎系统，或者采用间歇式粉磨作业，以控制产品粒径达到超细尺寸要求；When the ball mill is used for ultrafine grinding, it is usually necessary to form a dry closed-circuit grinding system with an ultrafine classifier, or use intermittent grinding operations to control the product particle size to meet the ultrafine size requirements;
- 采用球磨机进行超细粉碎，处理量大、球形度好、可靠耐用，对物料适应性强。但粉碎效率低，系统较复杂，噪音较大；Using a ball mill for ultrafine grinding has a large processing capacity, good sphericity, reliability and durability, and strong adaptability to materials. However, the grinding efficiency is low, the system is more complex, and the noise is relatively high;

# 超细粉碎 Ultrafine grinding

# 行星球磨 Planetary ball mill

✓ 行星球磨简称行星磨 ，每个磨筒在绕主轴偏心旋转 （公转）的同时，还绕自轴旋转（自转）。由于研磨体与物料做复合运动 ，公转和自转使离心力形成矢量和 ，即大小和方向随回转位置而变化，导致研磨体对物料的挤压和磨剥作用及搅动显著增强，从而强化了粉碎作用，达到微粉碎或超微粉碎的效果。

Planetary ball mill is short for planetary mill. Each grinding cylinder rotates around its own axis (rotation) while rotating eccentrically around the main axis (revolution). Because the grinding body and the material make a composite motion, the revolution and rotation make the centrifugal force form a vector sum, that is, the size and direction change with the rotation position, resulting in a significant increase in the extrusion, grinding and stirring of the material by the grinding body, thereby strengthening the crushing effect and achieving the effect of micro-crushing or ultra-micro-crushing.

# 细胞磨 Cell milling

• 是一种集重力和流化两种技术于一体的新型研磨机，利用多级合金搅拌盘的旋转动能，使研磨腔中的介质和浆料混合物产生运动，介质和物料形成涡流，并使物料颗粒相互碰撞和接触，进而产生颗粒之间的剪切和挤压力，形成细磨磨矿过程。It is a new type of grinding machine that integrates gravity and fluidization technologies. It uses the rotational kinetic energy of the multi-stage alloy stirring disk to make the medium and slurry mixture move in the grinding chamber, the medium and the material form a vortex, and the material particles collide and contact with each other, thereby generating shear and extrusion forces between the particles, forming a fine grinding process.

• 流化技术意味着搅拌速度合适，足以将各种密度的磨矿介质分布整个浆料之中，使颗粒和磨矿介质发生全方位的接触，物料得到了有效的研磨而获得 ≤ 1μm 甚至 100 纳米以下的颗粒粒径范围。Fluidization technology means that the stirring speed is appropriate, which is enough to distribute the grinding media of various densities throughout the slurry, so that the particles and the grinding media have full contact, and the material is effectively ground to obtain a particle size range of ≤1μm or even below 100 nanometers.

气力输送系统 Pneumatic conveying system

# 机械式气力输送 Mechanical pneumatic conveying

给料 • 吹扫式回转供料器的核心部件是一个带有多个叶片的转子，转子在圆筒形机壳内旋转。物料从上部料斗进入，填充在叶片间的空格内，随着转子的旋转被输送至下部卸料口。在输送过程中，设备通过压缩空气对物料进行吹扫，防止物料堵塞或粘附在设备内部，同时确保物料的连续流动。

The core component of the purge rotary feeder is a rotor with multiple blades that rotates in a cylindrical casing. The material enters from the upper hopper, fills the spaces between the blades, and is transported to the lower discharge port as the rotor rotates. During the conveying process, the equipment uses compressed air to purge the material to prevent the material from clogging or adhering to the inside of the equipment, while ensuring the continuous flow of the material.

气力输送系统 Pneumatic conveying system

# 机械式气力输送 Mechanical pneumatic conveying

- 喷射泵:
- 喷嘴：将工作流体的压力能转化为动能，形成高速射流。

Nozzle: Converts the pressure energy of the working fluid into kinetic energy to form a high-speed jet.
- 吸入室：被输送流体通过吸入室进入泵内。

Suction chamber: The transported fluid enters the pump through the suction chamber.
- 混合室：工作流体与被输送流体在此混合。

Mixing chamber: The working fluid and the transported fluid are mixed here.
- 扩散器：将混合流体的动能转化为压力能，提高出口压力。

Diffuser: Converts the kinetic energy of the mixed fluid into pressure energy to increase the outlet pressure.
- 连接管道：用于输送工作流体和排出混合流体。

Connecting pipes: used to transport working fluid and discharge mixed fluid.

# 机械式气力输送 Mechanical pneumatic conveying

# 给料

•  螺旋输送泵的工作原理基于螺旋叶片的旋转运动，将流体从泵的吸入端推向排出端。具体过程如下：

The working principle of the screw conveying pump is based on the rotational movement of the spiral blade, which pushes the fluid from the suction end of the pump to the discharge end. The specific process is as follows:

# 止回阀 Check valve

# •  螺旋旋转

电机驱动螺旋叶片（螺杆）旋转。

Spiral rotation: The motor drives the spiral blade (screw) to rotate.

# •  流体吸入

螺旋叶片的旋转在吸入端形成低压区，流体被吸入泵内。

Fluid suction: The rotation of the spiral blade forms a low-pressure area at the suction end, and the fluid is sucked into the pump.

# 混合腔

# •  流体输送

螺旋叶片将流体沿着泵壳内壁推向排出端。

Fluid delivery: The spiral blade pushes the fluid along the inner wall of the pump casing to the discharge end.

# •  流体排出

流体在排出端被压出，完成输送过程。

Fluid discharge: The fluid is pressed out at the discharge end to complete the delivery process.

# 高压气力输送 High pressure pneumatic conveying

# 单仓泵

仓式泵为充气罐式给料装置，其形式有上部压送出料和底部引射出料等多种。

Single-chamber pump: The chamber pump is an air-filled tank feeding device, which has multiple forms such as upper pressure discharge and bottom injection discharge.

# 双仓泵

将两个单仓泵组合起来协同工作，即可形成连续高压气力输送系统。两个单仓泵的组合方式有串联和并联两种。

Double-chamber pump: Combining two single-chamber pumps to work together can form a continuous high-pressure pneumatic conveying system. There are two ways to combine two single-chamber pumps: series connection and parallel connection.

# 栓流式气力输送

• 也称为脉冲气力输送，属于密相、高压、低速气力输送系统。该系统可以降低气力输送的能耗、减少管道的磨损和物料的破碎。Also known as pulse pneumatic conveying, belongs to the dense phase, high pressure, low speed pneumatic conveying system. This system can reduce the energy consumption of pneumatic conveying, reduce pipeline wear and material breakage.

空气输送斜槽 Air conveying chute

•  空气输送斜槽采用低压空气，使干燥的粉料流态化，并借助于重力移动，属于密相输送。空气输送斜槽结构简单、本身无运动部件，故磨损少，易维护检修；运转中无噪音，易改变输送方向；适用于多点喂料及多点卸料。

The air conveying chute uses low-pressure air to fluidize the dry powder and move it by gravity, which belongs to dense phase conveying. The air conveying chute has a simple structure and no moving parts, so it has less wear and tear and is easy to maintain and repair; there is no noise during operation and it is easy to change the conveying direction; it is suitable for multi-point feeding and multi-point unloading.

| 给料 | 1 |                   |
| -- | - | ----------------- |
| 排气 | 2 | 1-上壳体；2-物料；3-透气层； |
| 进气 | 4 | 3-透气层；            |
| 出  | 4 | 4-下壳体             |

连续输送机械 Continuous conveying machinery

•  连续输送机械是指沿给定线路连续输送散粒料或成件物品的机械装置。

Continuous conveying machinery refers to a mechanical device that continuously conveys bulk materials or finished items along a given route.

•  常见的输送散粒料的连续输送机有：胶带输送机、刮板输送机、斗式提升机、螺旋输送机、振动输送机等。

Common continuous conveyors for conveying bulk materials include: belt conveyors, scraper conveyors, bucket elevators, screw conveyors, vibrating conveyors, etc.

-26-

---

胶带输送机 Belt conveyor

利用运动的胶带承载物料进行连续输送的装置称为胶带输送机。结构简单，运行安全可靠，维修方便，使
用寿命长，能耗低，经济性好。尤其输送能力大，输送距离长。Belt conveyor: A device that uses a moving
belt to carry materials for continuous transportation. It has a simple structure, safe and reliable operation, convenient
maintenance, long service life, low energy consumption, and good economy. In particular, it has a large conveying
capacity and a long conveying distance.

# 螺旋输送机 Screw Conveyor

利用旋转的螺旋面对粉粒体散状物料的推动作用，使物料在机槽内沿轴向输送的装置称为螺旋输送机。结构简单、紧凑，造价低廉，可以水平、倾斜或垂直布置，机槽密闭，防尘和防污染性好，零部件标准化程度高。输送距离一般不超过100 m。Screw conveyor: A device that uses the rotating spiral surface to push the powder or granular bulk materials to transport the materials axially in the machine slot. It has a simple and compact structure and low cost. It can be arranged horizontally, inclined or vertically. The machine slot is closed, dust-proof and anti-pollution, and the parts are highly standardized. The conveying distance is generally not more than 100 m.

斗式提升机 Bucket Elevator

• 利用上下循环运动的链条或胶带上均匀设置的料斗来承载物料，进行垂直提升（可部分水平）的装置称为斗式提升机。输送量大，提升高度高，运行平稳，零部件标准化程度高，但其检修不易。

• Bucket elevator: A device that uses a chain or belt that circulates up and down to carry materials and lifts them vertically (partially horizontally). It has a large conveying capacity, a high lifting height, a stable operation, and a high degree of standardized parts, but it is not easy to repair.

# 链式输送机 Chain conveyor

• 利用运动链条上的翼板或刮板推动物料，或由链条上安装的板条、金属网带、辊道等承载物料的输送装置称为链式输送机。 Chain conveyors are conveyors that use the wing plates or scrapers on the moving chain to push materials, or that use slats, metal mesh belts, rollers, etc. installed on the chain to carry materials.

• 根据链条上安装的承载件的不同，链式输送机可分：链条式、链板式、链网式、板条式、链斗式、台车式等类型。刮板输送机常用来输送流动性好、非磨蚀性的粒状或块状物料。送能力大（可达4000t/h），输送距离较长（可达400m以上），使用寿命长。但空载功率消耗较大，易发生掉链、跳链故障，消耗钢材较多。According to the different load-bearing parts installed on the chain, chain conveyors can be divided into: chain type, chain plate type, chain mesh type, slat type, chain bucket type, trolley type, etc. Scraper conveyors are often used to convey granular or block materials with good fluidity and non-abrasiveness. They have large conveying capacity (up to 4000t/h), long conveying distance (up to more than 400m), and long service life. However, the no-load power consumption is large, and chain drop and chain jump failures are prone to occur, and more steel is consumed.

# 振动输送机 Vibrating conveyor

• 利用激振装置使固定在弹性谐振支撑体上的槽体沿某一倾斜方向产生振动，带动物料做抛掷或滑移运动来进行物料的输送。The excitation device is used to make the trough fixed on the elastic resonant support vibrate in a certain inclined direction, driving the material to do throwing or sliding motion to transport the material.

• 振动输送机的基本结构包括：输送槽体、弹性谐振支承体和激振器，其速度可通过改变振动的振幅或频率来调节。The basic structure of the vibrating conveyor includes: conveying trough, elastic resonant support and exciter, and its speed can be adjusted by changing the amplitude or frequency of the vibration.

# 1 2 3

# 强制机械驱动振动输送机 Forced mechanical drive vibration conveyor.

| ------------------- | ---------------------------- | -------------------------------- |
| 1-Conveying trough; | 2-Elastic resonance support; | 3-Crank connecting rod mechanism |



---

振动输送机 Vibrating conveyor

•  振动输送机结构简单，功率消耗较低，且对物料的适应性较强，可以输送各种性质的物料，如各种粒度、形状、温度、密度、腐蚀性、软硬性和磨蚀性物料等；可以采用封闭槽体，输送扬尘性、毒性或易爆燃的物料等。The vibrating conveyor has a simple structure, low power consumption, and strong adaptability to materials. It can transport materials of various properties, such as various particle sizes, shapes, temperatures, densities, corrosive, soft and hard and abrasive materials; closed troughs can be used to transport dusty, toxic or explosive materials.

•  振动输送机单机输送长度和输送能力受到限制，一般不超过100m，不能输送粘性大，或流动性特别强的物料，不能大角度向上倾斜输送（显著降低输送能力），会使某些物料碎裂。The conveying length and conveying capacity of a single vibrating conveyor are limited, generally not exceeding 100m. It cannot transport materials with high viscosity or particularly strong fluidity, and cannot be transported at a large angle upward (significantly reducing the conveying capacity), which will cause some materials to break.

•  对环境有噪音和振动影响。There are noise and vibration effects on the environment.

干燥与脱水 Drying &#x26; Dehydration

# 物料与水分的结合方式 The combination way of materials and water

- 化学结合水：包括水分与物料的离子结合和结晶型分子结合。化学结合水的脱除不属于干燥的范围。

Chemically bound water: includes the ionic combination of water and materials and the combination of crystalline molecules. The removal of chemically bound water does not fall within the scope of drying.
- 物理化学结合水 ：包括吸附水、渗透水、微孔毛细管水和结构水。

Physical chemically bound water: includes adsorbed water, permeated water, microporous capillary water and structural water (H bonding).
- 机械结合水：包括巨孔毛细管水、空隙水和润湿水。

Mechanically bound water: includes macroporous capillary water, interstitial water and wetting water.

-35-

---

干燥与脱水 Drying &#x26; Dehydration

# 去湿的方法有三种 Three methods for dehumidification：

- ✓ 机械去湿法：通过浓缩和过滤等机械方法除去物料中的湿分。
Mechanical dehumidification: remove moisture from materials through mechanical methods such as concentration and filtration.
- ✓ 化学去湿法：利用吸湿剂，如浓硫酸、无水氯化钙、分子筛等除去气体、液体或固体物料中少量水分。
Chemical dehumidification: use desiccant, such as concentrated sulfuric acid, anhydrous calcium chloride, molecular sieve, etc. to remove a small amount of water from gas, liquid or solid materials.
- ✓ 加热（或冷冻）去湿法：利用热能加热物料，使物料湿分蒸发而干燥，或利用冷冻法使湿分冷冻后由固态直接升华为气态而除去湿分。
Heating (or freezing) dehumidification: use heat energy to heat the material to evaporate the moisture in the material and dry it, or use freezing method to freeze the moisture and then directly sublimate from solid to gas to remove moisture.

-36-

---


# 物料与结合水分的平衡关系

# Balance between material and bound water

✓ 平衡水分：当一种物料与一定温度及湿度的空气接触时，物料势必会放出水分或吸收水分，物料的水分将趋于一定值。

Equilibrium moisture: When a material comes into contact with air of a certain temperature and humidity, the material will inevitably release or absorb moisture, and the moisture content of the material will tend to a certain value.

✓ 自由水分：在干燥过程中能够脱除的水分，即是物料中超出平衡水分的部分，这部分水分称为自由水分，或称可脱除水分。

Free moisture: The moisture that can be removed during the drying process is the part of the material that exceeds the equilibrium moisture content. This part of moisture is called free moisture, or removable moisture.

与平衡水分相关的水分

# Drum dryer: co-current type and counter-current type with reverse motion

| 原料 | 传动齿轮    | 支撑滚圈 | 废气 |
| -- | ------- | ---- | -- |
| 热气 |         |      |    |

# 转筒干燥器结构原理示意图

# Schematic diagram of the structure and principle of rotary drum dryer

---

转筒干燥器 Drum dryer

# 1. 顺流式

热气流与物料同向运动。其特点是物料脱水强烈，干燥速率较大，物料与介质的终了温度都相对较低。其缺点是干燥速率不均匀，物料终水分受介质终湿度的限制，介质中含尘量较高。适合于初水分高，并允许强烈脱水及对高温敏感或易燃物料的干燥。如：粘土、矿渣、煤等。水泥厂大多采用这种类型的烘干机。

Co-current type: The hot air flow moves in the same direction as the material. Its characteristics are strong dehydration of the material, high drying rate, and relatively low final temperature of the material and the medium. Its disadvantages are uneven drying rate, the final moisture content of the material is limited by the final humidity of the medium, and the dust content in the medium is high. It is suitable for drying materials with high initial moisture content, strong dehydration, high temperature sensitive or flammable materials. Such as: clay, slag, coal, etc. Most cement plants use this type of dryer.

# 2. 逆流式

热气流与物料逆向运动。其特点是干燥速率较均匀，物料的终水分低，热效率高，适合于终水分要求很低而又不能强烈脱水的物料，或对高温不敏感的物料的干燥。如：砂子、石灰石。

Counter-current type: The hot air flow moves in the opposite direction of the material. Its characteristics are relatively uniform drying rate, low final moisture content of the material, high thermal efficiency, and suitable for materials with very low final moisture requirements but cannot be strongly dehydrated, or materials that are not sensitive to high temperatures. Such as: sand, limestone.

-39-

---

流化床干燥器 Fluidized bed dryer

# 流化床干燥器 Fluidized bed dryer

热风通过铺在具有格孔的气体分布器上的物料层，在一定的风速下，物料被流态化，使气固间产生剧烈的热交换，湿分被蒸发而干燥。主要用于连续干燥颗粒状物料，如粘土、矿渣、砂子等。

The hot air passes through the material layer on the gas distributor with grid holes. At a certain wind speed, the material is fluidized, causing intense heat exchange between gas and solid, and the moisture is evaporated and dried. It is mainly used for continuous drying of granular materials, such as clay, slag, sand, etc.

# 振动流化床干燥器结构原理示意图

| 1-fan             | 2-heater  | 3-elastic support   |
| 4-bed grate plate | 5-exciter | 6-cyclone separator |

喷雾干燥器 Spray Dryer

喷雾干燥是采用雾化器将原料液分散为雾滴，利用小液滴较平面液体的饱和蒸气压值高，以及小液滴比表面积大与热气流能充分接触的干燥原理，使原料液干燥。Spray drying uses an atomizer to disperse the raw material liquid into droplets, and uses the drying principle that the saturated vapor pressure of small droplets is higher than that of flat liquid, and the large specific surface area of small droplets can fully contact with the hot air flow to dry the raw material liquid.

| 浆料    | 热气   |
| ----- | ---- |
| 1-压力泵 | 2-喷嘴 |
| 排空    |      |
| 产物    |      |

# 气流干燥器 Air flow dryer

•  气流干燥器是将物料通过适当的加料方式加入干燥管内，被分散在高速流动的热气流中，物料在气流输送过程中水分被蒸发，得到粉粒状的干燥产品。 The air flow dryer adds the material into the drying tube through an appropriate feeding methode, and the material is dispersed in the high-speed hot air flow. The moisture of the material is evaporated during the air flow transportation process to obtain a dry product in powdery or granular form.

| - | ------------- |

箱式干燥器 Box dryer
箱式干燥器外形为箱体结构，壁壳有绝热保温层，由风机和加热器产生的循环流动热风，吹到逐层存放于托盘中的物料表面进行干燥。The box dryer has a box structure with a heat-insulating layer on the wall. The circulating hot air generated by the fan and heater is blown onto the surface of the materials stored in the tray layer by layer for drying.

# 隧道干燥器 Tunnel dryer

- 隧道干燥机是连续式烘干设备，可持续不间断地烘烤，提高产品生产效率。双边配有链条传动，解决运输过程中跑偏的现象。The tunnel dryer is a continuous drying equipment that can continuously and uninterruptedly bake and improve product production efficiency. Both sides are equipped with chain drive to solve the problem of deviation during transportation.
- 烘箱分段式加热，独立电箱控制、操作方便。The oven is heated in sections, controlled by an independent electric box, and easy to operate.
- 结构主要由输送机系统与烘干炉两大部分组成。多段独立PID温度控制，炉内温度均匀。输送速度变频调速，调节自如，运行平稳，生产效率高。The structure mainly consists of two parts: the conveyor system and the drying oven. Multi-stage independent PID temperature control ensures uniform temperature in the oven. The conveying speed is variable frequency and adjustable, with smooth operation and high production efficiency.

滚筒筛性能及参数调节

# Drum screen performance and parameter adjustment

1. 筛分过程中4个作用力：离心力、重力、静摩擦力和滑动摩擦力。
Four forces in the screening process: centrifugal force, gravity, static friction and sliding friction.
2. 筛孔直径越小，L/D应越大
The smaller the sieve hole diameter, the larger the L/D should be.
3. 当筛孔直径为120 mm时，L/D应为2.5～3；
When the sieve hole diameter is 120 mm, the L/D should be 2.5-3;
4. 当筛孔直径为80 mm时，L/D应大于3.3；
When the sieve hole diameter is 80 mm, the L/D should be greater than 3.3;
5. 当筛孔直径为40 mm时，L/D值应远高于3.3的值；
When the sieve hole diameter is 40 mm, the L/D value should be much higher than 3.3;

分选机 Sorting Machine

分选机的工作原理主要基于物料的不同物理或化学特性，通过机械、气流、磁场、电场、光学等手段实现分离。The working principle of the sorting machine is mainly based on the different physical or chemical properties of the materials, and separation is achieved through mechanical, airflow, magnetic field, electric field, optical and other means.

1. 风力分选机 Wind separator
2. 重力分选机 Gravity separator
3. 磁选机 Magnetic separator
4. 电选机 Electric separator

-59-

风力分选设备
# Wind separation machine

# ① 卧式风力分选机

# Horizontal wind separator

✓ 水平气流速度为5m/s，在回收的轻质组分中废纸约占90%；重质组分中黑色金属占100%；中重组分主要是木块、硬塑料等；The horizontal air flow velocity is 5m/s. Waste paper accounts for about 90% of the light components recycled; ferrous metals account for 100% of the heavy components; medium and heavy components are mainly wood blocks, hard plastics, etc.

✓ 卧式风力分选机的最佳风速为20m/s The optimal wind speed of the horizontal wind separator is 20m/s.

风力分选机结构三视图
# Three views of wind separator structure

# ① 卧式风力分选机

# Horizontal wind separator

- 侧面送风 Side air supply;
- 垃圾经破碎机破碎和圆筒筛筛分使其粒度均匀后，定量给入机内 After the garbage is crushed by the crusher and screened by the drum screen to make its particle size uniform, it is quantitatively fed into the machine;
- 当废物在机内下落时，被鼓风机鼓入的水平气流吹散 When the waste falls in the machine, it is blown away by the horizontal airflow blown by the blower;
- 垃圾中各种组分沿着不同运动轨迹分别落入重质组分、中重组分和轻质组分收集槽中 The various components in the garbage fall into the heavy component, medium and heavy component and light component collection tanks along different movement trajectories.

---

风力分选设备

# Wind separation machine

# ② 立式风力分选机 Vertical wind separator

利用垂直方向的气流，物料从上部进入分选机，在高速上升气流的作用下，轻质物料被气流带向上方，重质物料则因重力作用沉降到底部。

Using vertical airflow, materials enter the separator from the top. Under the action of high-speed rising airflow, light materials are carried upward by the airflow, while heavy materials settle to the bottom due to gravity.

# 1—给料；2—排出物；3—提取物；4—旋流器；5—风机；6—空气

1 - feed; 2 - discharge; 3 - extract; 4 - cyclone; 5 - fan; 6 - air

通过调节气流速度和出口位置，可以实现不同密度和粒度的物料分离。

By adjusting the airflow speed and outlet position, materials of different densities and particle sizes can be separated.

静电辊筒式分选机 Electrostatic roller separator

# 分离塑料和金属粉末 Separation of plastic and metal powders

1. # 高压电场生成

设备内部的高压发生装置产生高压静电，对接地旋转辊筒放电，形成高压电场 High-voltage electric field generation: The high-voltage generator inside the equipment generates high-voltage static electricity, which discharges to the grounded rotating roller to form a high-voltage electric field.
2. # 物料带电

待分选物料通过下料装置均匀进入旋转辊筒表面，在电晕电极作用下带负电 Material is charged: the material to be sorted enters the surface of the rotating roller evenly through the feeding device and is charged under the action of the corona electrode.
3. # 分离过程

导体物料，由于导电性好，电荷迅速通过接地辊筒释放，并在离心力作用下被抛入导体接料斗；非导体物料，导电性差，电荷无法释放，吸附在辊筒表面，随辊筒旋转至后方被毛刷扫入非导体接料斗。Separation process: Conductive materials, due to their good conductivity, release their charges quickly through the grounded roller and are thrown into the conductor receiving hopper under the action of centrifugal force; non-conductive materials, due to their poor conductivity, cannot release their charges and are adsorbed on the roller surface. As the roller rotates to the rear, they are swept into the non-conductive receiving hopper by the brush.

-66-

---


# 人工分选 Manual sorting

• 人工分选平台主要是用于垃圾处理线中不能依靠机械来分选的部位，主要用在滚筒筛前分拣出大的干扰物和滚筒筛上物风选后的资源化利用物品的分拣位置。

The manual sorting platform is mainly used in the parts of the garbage processing line that cannot be sorted by machinery. It is mainly used to sort out large interference objects in front of the drum screen and to sort out resource utilization items after the drum screen is winnowed.

# 废塑料生产木塑材料

# Production of wood plastic materials from waste plastics

- 木塑复合材料是由木质纤维（如木粉、竹粉等）与热塑性塑料（如聚乙烯、聚丙烯、聚氯乙烯等）混合，通过挤出、注塑等工艺制成的复合材料。

Wood-plastic composites are composite materials made by mixing wood fibers (such as wood powder, bamboo powder, etc.) with thermoplastic plastics (such as polyethylene, polypropylene, polyvinyl chloride, etc.) through extrusion, injection molding and other processes.
- 主要成分：木质纤维，提供强度和刚性，常用木粉、竹粉等；热塑性塑料，提供韧性和防水性，常用聚乙烯（PE）、聚丙烯（PP）、聚氯乙烯（PVC）等；添加剂：如偶联剂、稳定剂、润滑剂等，用于改善加工性能和最终产品性能。

Main ingredients: Wood fiber, provides strength and rigidity, commonly used wood powder, bamboo powder, etc.; Thermoplastics, provides toughness and waterproofness, commonly used polyethylene (PE), polypropylene (PP), polyvinyl chloride (PVC), etc. Additives, such as coupling agents, stabilizers, lubricants, etc., used to improve processing performance and final product performance.
- 性能特点与木材相比：材质均一、耐水性好、易直接成型、加工方便

Performance characteristics compared with wood: uniform material, good water resistance, easy to shape directly, convenient processing. 与塑料相比：热稳定性好、成本较低

Compared with plastic: good thermal stability, lower cost.

# 干生活垃圾处理生活污水技术

# Dry domestic waste treatment technology for domestic sewage

- 组合填料：织物+动物骨头+发泡混凝土 Combined filler: fabric + animal bones + foamed concrete
- 运行方式：上进水+自然通风+配水时间16 h/d Operation mode: water inlet + natural ventilation + water distribution time 16 h/d
- 造价：8.12万元，吨水建设成本2.46万元 Cost: 81,200 yuan, construction cost per ton of water 24,600 yuan
- 目标污染物： COD 和NH₃-N Target pollutants: COD and NH₃-N

# 连续运行时进出水氨氮和COD去除效果

# 干垃圾生物滴流床处理垃圾渗滤液

# Treatment of landfill leachate by dry garbage biological trickle bed

# 1. 反应床构建

Reactor construction

# 容器箱体

Container body: The container has been damaged during the garbage transfer process in the solid waste base.

# 填料组成

Filler composition: From bottom to top, it is pebbles, mineralized garbage, animal bones, waste fabrics, foam concrete.

# 处理对象

Treatment objects: water in the regulating pool, leachate from the transfer station.

# 运行方式

Operation mode: The leachate is lifted by the pump and sprayed from the top to the filler layer by the water distribution device, and then it slowly flows through the filler layer by gravity and is treated by microorganisms. The leachate gathers at the bottom and is discharged through the drain pipe.

# 配水方式

Water distribution method: The water distribution volume is carried out in a stable increasing manner. The water distribution method is to distribute water for 10 minutes, with an interval of 50 minutes, and continuously for 24 hours.

# 干垃圾生物滴流床处理中转站垃圾渗滤液 Treatment of leachate from transfer station using biological trickle bed of dry garbage

# ③ 渗滤液反应床构建 Reactor bed construction for leachate treatment

- 原液水箱: 原液水箱用于储存来自中转站的渗滤液，污水泵用于将渗滤液提升至下一设备中。

Raw liquid water tank: The raw liquid water tank is used to store the leachate from the transfer station, and the sewage pump is used to lift the leachate to the next device.
- 河砂过滤反应器: 填料为河砂，采用穿孔管的大阻力配水方式进行配水，经过河沙的过滤作用来对渗滤液进行处理。

River sand filter reactor: The filler is river sand, and the water is distributed by the large resistance water distribution method of the perforated pipe. The leachate is treated by the filtering effect of the river sand.
- 由下到上分别为: 卵石、矿化垃圾，动物骨头、废旧织物、泡沫混凝土。

From bottom to top, they are: pebbles, mineralized garbage, animal bones, waste fabrics, and foam concrete.



---


# 干垃圾生物滴流床处理中转站垃圾渗滤液 Treatment of leachate from transfer station using biological trickle bed of dry garbage

# ③ 渗滤液反应床构建 Reactor bed construction for leachate treatment

◼ 干垃圾生物滴流器: 将过滤液提升至中间水箱，再通过重力自流作用进入生物反应器中进行反应。主体尺寸为：∅×H=3.19m×7.16m，过滤液从反应器顶端进水，采用穿孔管的大阻力配水方式进行配水

Dry garbage biological dripper: The filtrate is lifted to the intermediate water tank, and then enters the bioreactor for reaction by gravity. The main body size is: ∅×H=3.19m×7.16m, the filtrate enters the top of the reactor, and the water is distributed by the large resistance water distribution method of the perforated pipe.

干垃圾生物滴流床处理中转站垃圾渗滤液 Treatment of leachate from transfer station using biological trickle bed of dry garbage

# 1. COD浓度变化

正常运行6天后砂滤池出水COD浓度已经明显低于原样的COD，在进行进一步的生物滴流床处理后，出水COD基本稳定在1000 mg/L左右，COD去除率在94.4-96%之间。

The COD concentration of the effluent from the sand filter tank during the 6-day normal operation was significantly lower than the original COD. After further treatment with the biological trickling bed, the effluent COD was basically stable at around 1000 mg/L, and the COD removal rate was between 94.4-96%.

# 2. TN浓度变化

在经过生物滴流床后，TN不降反升，可能由于初始生物滴流床污染物的溶出，导致TN浓度升高；同时，由于滴流床处于厌氧环境，无法硝化，最终导致TN以NH₃-N形式累积。

After passing through the biological trickling bed, TN did not decrease but increased. This may be due to the dissolution of pollutants in the initial biological trickling bed, which led to an increase in TN concentration. At the same time, since the trickling bed is in an anaerobic environment and cannot nitrify, TN eventually accumulates in the form of NH3-N.

# 3. TP浓度变化

经砂滤和生物床处理后，TP可以降至40mg/L左右，最终的去除效率可达88.6-91.6%。

After sand filtration and biological bed treatment, TP can be reduced to around 40 mg/L, and the final removal efficiency can reach 88.6-91.6%.

干垃圾生物滴流床耦合反渗透处理工艺
# Dry garbage biological trickle bed coupled with reverse osmosis treatment process

工艺流程：中转站渗滤液或冲洗水经河砂过滤反应器粗过滤后，进入干垃圾生物滴流床生物降解处理，出水经过反渗透膜处理，达到脱氮和强化去除大分子有机物质的效果，最终可使出水水质达《污水排入城镇下水道水质标准》

Process flow: After the leachate or flushing water from the transfer station is coarsely filtered through the river sand filter reactor, it enters the dry garbage biological trickling bed for biodegradation treatment. The effluent is treated by reverse osmosis membrane to achieve the effect of denitrification and enhanced removal of large molecular organic matter. Ultimately, the effluent water quality can meet the “Water Quality Standard for Sewage Discharge into Urban Sewers”.

After reverse osmosis membrane treatment, the average COD of the effluent is 50 mg/L, the average TN is 28 mg/L, the average NH₃-N is 20 mg/L, and the average TP is 2 mg/L. The effluent quality meets the discharge standard.

垃圾预处理总结
# Summary on waste

| 脱水          | 破碎       | 输送        | 筛分         | 干燥     | 清洗       |

- 滚筒筛的堵塞难于解决，特别是高含水率废物；分选出来的组分，单一性很差。开孔率、废物含水率、粒径、转速、长径比等，均会影响分选效果。The clogging of the drum screen is difficult to solve, especially for waste with high moisture content; the components separated are of poor uniformity. The opening rate, waste moisture content, particle size, rotation speed, aspect ratio, etc. will affect the sorting effect.
- 风力分选，机制非常复杂，进料与风力进风方向的角度、风力速度、进料速度、进料粒径、出料口间距等等有关，没有任何设计院掌握相关技术，设备制造商也很难掌握。The mechanism of wind sorting is very complicated. The feed is related to the angle of the wind inlet direction, wind speed, feed speed, feed particle size, discharge port spacing, etc. No design institute has mastered the relevant technology, and it is difficult for equipment manufacturers to master it.
- 滚筒筛和风力分选联合，要恰到好处，很难掌握，一个滚筒筛，可以分选出若干粒径不同废物，每种废物再用风力分选进一步细分，实现最大化，很难，目前基本上靠经验。高含水率废物，无法分选。It is difficult to master the combination of drum screen and wind sorting. A drum screen can sort out several wastes with different particle sizes. Each waste is further subdivided by wind sorting to achieve maximization. It is difficult and currently basically relies on experience. Waste with high moisture content cannot be sorted.

中国有机固废现状

✓ 我国有机固废产量世界第一、存量大，环境问题突出；全国每年有机固废60-100亿吨，存量200亿吨。

China ranks first in the world in terms of organic solid waste production, with a large stock and prominent environmental problems; the country produces 6-10 billion tons of organic solid waste each year and a stock of 20 billion tons.

✓ 根据来源可分为生活源、农业源、工业源。

According to the source, it can be divided into domestic sources, agricultural sources, and industrial sources.

✓ 生活源4亿吨，主要包括2-3亿吨生活垃圾、1亿吨厨余垃圾、城镇污泥6500吨（80%含水量）、医疗废物240万吨。

Domestic sources are 400 million tons, mainly including 200-300 million tons of domestic waste, 100 million tons of kitchen waste, 6,500 tons of urban sludge (80% water content), and 2.4 million tons of medical waste.

✓ 农业源40亿吨，主要包括农林秸秆10亿吨，畜禽粪污30-60亿吨。

Agricultural sources are 4 billion tons, mainly including 1 billion tons of agricultural and forestry straw and 3-6 billion tons of livestock and poultry manure.

✓ 工业源20亿吨，主要包括废旧塑料6300万吨，轮胎700万吨；菌渣3300万吨、含油污泥500万吨。

Industrial sources are 2 billion tons, mainly including 63 million tons of waste plastics and 7 million tons of tires; 33 million tons of fungus residue and 5 million tons of oily sludge.

# 厌氧发酵定义

# Anaerobic fermentation definition

• 在无外加氧化剂条件下，被分解的有机物作为还原剂被氧化，而另一部分有机物作为氧化剂被还原的生物学过程。

In the absence of an external oxidant, the decomposed organic matter is oxidized as a reducing agent, while another part of the organic matter is reduced as an oxidant.

• 有机物的厌氧发酵过程主要经过液化（水解）、酸化（包括酸化前阶段、酸化后阶段）及气化三个阶段。

The anaerobic fermentation process of organic matter mainly goes through three stages: liquefaction (hydrolysis), acidification (including the pre-acidification stage and the post-acidification stage) and gasification.

-7-



---


# 厌氧发酵过程中的微生物群落

# Microbial communities during anaerobic fermentation

- 数量最多 、 作用最大的微生物是细菌 ， 18 属 、 51 种； The most numerous and most effective microorganisms are bacteria, with 18 genera and 51 species;
- 真菌（丝状真菌和酵母）虽也能存活，但数量很少； Fungi (filamentous fungi and yeasts) can also survive, but in small numbers;
- 藻类和原生动物偶有发现，但数量也较少； Algae and protozoa are occasionally found, but in small numbers;
- 细菌以厌氧菌和兼性厌氧菌为主； Bacteria are mainly anaerobic and facultative anaerobes;
- 参与有机物逐级厌氧发酵降解的细菌主要有三大类群，依次为水解发酵细菌、产氢产乙酸细菌、产甲烷细菌。 There are three main groups of bacteria involved in the step-by-step anaerobic fermentation and degradation of organic matter, namely hydrolytic and fermentative bacteria, hydrogen-producing and acetic acid-producing bacteria, and methanogenic bacteria.



---


# 影响发酵细菌功能的环境条件

# Environmental conditions that affect the function of fermentation bacteria

# ① 温度因素

# Temperature

常温发酵（自然发酵）、中温发酵（28～38℃）和高温发酵（48～60℃）

Normal temperature fermentation (natural fermentation), medium temperature fermentation (28-38°C) and high temperature fermentation (48-60°C)

| 100 | Thermophilic bacteria  |
| --- | ---------------------- |
| 80  | Mesophilic bacteria    |
| 60  | 嗜温细菌                   |
| 40  | Psychrophilic bacteria |
| 20  | 嗜寒细菌                   |
| 0   |                        |

Effect of temperature on the growth of anaerobic methanogens

温度对厌氧发酵甲烷菌生长的影响



---


# 影响发酵细菌功能的环境条件

# Environmental conditions that affect the function of fermentation bacteria

# 发酵细菌营养及营养物比例

# Fermentation bacteria nutrition and nutrient ratio

- 厌氧发酵适宜C/N比为（30～20）:1
- The suitable C/N ratio for anaerobic fermentation is (30-20):1
- 厌氧发酵对磷（以磷酸盐的形式）需求量大约为氮的1/5;
- The phosphorus (in the form of phosphate) required for anaerobic fermentation is about 1/5 of nitrogen;



---


# 影响发酵细菌功能的环境条件

# Environmental conditions that affect the function of fermentation bacteria

# ③ 混合均匀程度

- 搅拌的目的是使发酵原料分布均匀，增加微生物与发酵基质的接触，也使发酵产物及时分离，从而提高产气量，加速反应；

The purpose of stirring is to make the fermentation raw materials evenly distributed, increase the contact between microorganisms and fermentation substrates, and separate the fermentation products in time, thereby increasing gas production and accelerating the reaction;
- 液态发酵用泵喷水搅拌法；对于固态或半固态用发酵气循环搅拌法和机械混合搅拌法等；

For liquid fermentation, use pump spray stirring method; for solid or semi-solid fermentation, use fermentation gas circulation stirring method and mechanical mixing stirring method;



---


# 影响发酵细菌功能的环境条件

# ④ 添加剂

- 在发酵液中添加少量化学物质，有助于促进厌氧发酵，提高产气量和原料利用率；Adding a small amount of chemical substances to the fermentation liquid can help promote anaerobic fermentation, increase gas production and raw material utilization;
- 添加过磷酸钙，能促进纤维素分解，提高产气量；Adding superphosphate can promote cellulose decomposition and increase gas production;
- 添加少量钾、钠、钙、镁、锌、磷等元素能促进厌氧反应；Adding a small amount of potassium, sodium, calcium, magnesium, zinc, phosphorus and other elements can promote anaerobic reaction;



---


# 影响发酵细菌功能的环境条件

# Environmental conditions that affect the function of fermentation bacteria

# ⑤ 酸碱度、pH值和发酵液缓冲作用

- 消化液酸度与脂肪酸含量有关；The acidity of digestive liquid is related to the fatty acid content;
- 脂肪酸含量较多的，有乙酸、丙酸、丁酸，其次为甲酸、己酸、戊酸、乳酸；The fatty acids with higher content are acetic acid, propionic acid, butyric acid, followed by formic acid, caproic acid, valeric acid and lactic acid;
- 丙酸积累是造成酸抑制基本原因，脂肪酸含量大于2000~3000 mg/L会使发酵过程受阻；The accumulation of propionic acid is the basic cause of acid inhibition. A fatty acid content greater than 2000~3000 mg/L will hinder the fermentation process;
- 消化液碱度，通常由其中的氨氮量决定，它能中和酸而使发酵液保持适宜pH值；The alkalinity of digestate is usually determined by the amount of ammonia nitrogen in it, which can neutralize the acid and keep the fermentation medium at a suitable pH value;
- 氨有一定毒性，一般应不超过1000 mg/L。Ammonia has a certain toxicity and should generally not exceed 1000 mg/L.



---


# 影响发酵细菌功能的环境条件

# Environmental conditions that affect the function of fermentation bacteria

# ⑤ 酸碱度、pH值和发酵液缓冲作用

# Acidity, pH and buffering capacity

- 水解与发酵菌及产氢产乙酸菌对pH值的适应范围大致为5~6.5，而甲烷菌对pH值的适应范围为6.6~7.5；pH&#x3C;4.8时，体系过度酸化，厌氧发酵停止；

The pH range of hydrolysis and fermentation bacteria and hydrogen-producing and acetogenic bacteria is roughly 5~6.5, while the pH range of methanogens is 6.6~7.5; When pH&#x3C;4.8, the system is over-acidified and anaerobic fermentation stops;
- 在发酵系统中，如果水解发酵阶段与产酸阶段的反应速率，超过产甲烷阶段，则pH值会降低，影响甲烷菌生活环境。

In the fermentation system, if the reaction rate of the hydrolysis and fermentation stage and the acid production stage exceeds that of the methanogen stage, the pH value will decrease, affecting the living environment of methanogens.
- 氨与二氧化碳反应生成的碳酸氢氨，使得发酵液具有一定的缓冲能力，在一定范围内，可以避免发生过度酸化。

Ammonium bicarbonate generated by the reaction of ammonia and carbon dioxide gives the fermentation liquid a certain buffering capacity, which can avoid over-acidification within a certain range.
- 应保持碱度在2000 mg/L 以上，使其有足够的缓冲能力，可有效防止系统 pH 值下降。

The alkalinity should be kept above 2000 mg/L to ensure sufficient buffering capacity, which can effectively prevent the pH value of the system from decreasing.



---


# 影响发酵细菌功能的环境条件

# Environmental conditions that affect the function of fermentation bacteria

# ⑥ 生物固体停留时间 （污泥龄）与负荷

# Biosolids retention time (sludge age) and loading

• 发酵罐容积负荷和发酵时间之间一般呈反比例关系，厌氧发酵好坏与污泥龄有直接关系; There is generally an inverse proportional relationship between the volume load of the fermentation tank and the fermentation time, and the quality of anaerobic fermentation is directly related to the sludge age;

| X·V | SLR= Q·S0    |
| --- | ------------ |
| SRT | Qw·Xw+Qe ·Xe |
| X.V |              |

- X：反应器中污泥浓度（MLSS, g/L） Sludge concentration in the reactor
- V：反应器体积（L）Reactor volume
- Qw：污泥排放流量（L/d）Sludge discharge flow rate
- Xw：排放污泥浓度（g/L）Discharge sludge concentration
- Qₑ：出水流量（L/d）Effluent flow rate
- Xₑ：出水污泥浓度（g/L）Effluent sludge concentration
- Q：进水流量（L/d）Influent flow rate
- S₀：进水有机物浓度（g/L） Influent organic concentration

现代大型工业化沼气发酵设备

- 各种形状的污泥和有机废物发酵罐

# 发酵罐的混合方式 Mixing ways in fermentation tank

- 动力泵：反应污泥利用外部的动力泵实现循环。使用于经典型与欧洲平底型。此外，为了防止在发酵罐底部形成沉淀，需要安装刮泥器。

Power pump: The reaction sludge is circulated by an external power pump. It is used in classic and European flat bottom types. In addition, a scraper is required to prevent precipitation at the bottom of the fermenter.
- 混合搅拌装置：螺旋桨机械搅拌混合器主要包括升液管、加速器、混合器、循环折流板和驱动泵。

Mixing and stirring device: The propeller mechanical stirring mixer mainly includes a riser, an accelerator, a mixer, a circulation baffle and a driving pump.
- 加气设备：气体在空气压缩泵的作用下进入发酵罐的底部并形成气泡，气泡在上升过程中带动污泥向上运动形成循环，从而达到混合目的。

Aeration equipment: The gas enters the bottom of the fermenter under the action of the air compression pump and forms bubbles. The bubbles drive the sludge upward during the rising process to form a cycle, thereby achieving the purpose of mixing.



---

沼气发酵工艺

# Biogas fermentation process

# (1) 按温度分类: 高温发酵；中温发酵；常温发酵

# Classification by temperature:

- 高温发酵：发酵温度50~60℃之间，微生物特别活跃，有机物分解消化快，产气率高[一般在2.0 m³/（m³料液·d）以上]，滞留期短。
- High temperature fermentation: The fermentation temperature is between 50~60℃, the microorganisms are particularly active, the organic matter is decomposed and digested quickly, the gas production rate is high [generally above 2.0 m³/(m³ liquid·d)], and the retention period is short.
- 中温发酵：发酵温度30~35℃，有机物消化速度较快，产气率较高[一般在1 m³/（m³料液·d）以上]。实际应用较多。
- Medium temperature fermentation: The fermentation temperature is 30~35℃, the organic matter is digested quickly, and the gas production rate is high [generally above 1 m³/(m³ liquid·d)]. There are many practical applications.

-22-

---


# 沼气发酵工艺

# （1）按温度分类:

- 高温发酵
- 中温发酵
- 常温发酵

# 常温发酵：

自然温度下进行的沼气发酵。发酵温度不受人为控制，基本上是随气温变化而不断变化，通常夏季产气率较高，冬季产气率较低。这种工艺的优点，是沼气池结构相对简单，造价较低。一般固体废物处理很少采用常温厌氧发酵。

Room temperature fermentation: Biogas fermentation at natural temperature. The fermentation temperature is not controlled by humans, and basically changes with the temperature. Usually the gas production rate is higher in summer and lower in winter. The advantage of this process is that the biogas tank structure is relatively simple and the cost is low. Normal temperature anaerobic fermentation is rarely used in general solid waste treatment.



---

沼气发酵工艺

# Biogas fermentation process

- 从现场工程运行温度来看，中温厌氧35～45℃，高温厌氧50～55℃，均可维持厌氧系统稳定运行。

From the perspective of the on-site engineering operating temperature, medium temperature anaerobic 35-45℃ and high temperature anaerobic 50-55℃ can maintain the stable operation of the anaerobic system.
- 从沼气产量来看，当厌氧停留时间超过30天，中温和高温厌氧产气相差不大。

From the perspective of biogas production, when the anaerobic residence time exceeds 30 days, the gas production of medium temperature and high temperature anaerobic is not much different.
- 结合前端预处理后物料温度，尽量少通过外源升温或降温措施控制厌氧温度，避免能源浪费。

Combined with the material temperature after front-end pretreatment, try to control the anaerobic temperature as little as possible through external heating or cooling measures to avoid energy waste.

-24-

---

沼气发酵工艺

# （2）按发酵级数分类

相互连通沼气池数量多少，分为单级、两级和多级发酵。

Classification by fermentation stage: The number of interconnected biogas tanks is divided into single-stage, two-stage and multi-stage fermentation.

# ① 单级发酵

混合发酵只有一个沼气池（或发酵装置），其沼气发酵过程只在一个发酵池内进行。设备简单，但条件控制较困难。

Single-stage fermentation: There is only one biogas tank (or fermentation device) for mixed fermentation, and the biogas fermentation process is carried out in only one fermentation tank. The equipment is simple, but the conditions are difficult to control.

-25-

---

沼气发酵工艺

# （2）按发酵级数分类

相互连通沼气池数量多少，分为单级、两级和多级发酵。

Classification by fermentation stage: The number of interconnected biogas tanks is divided into single-stage, two-stage and multi-stage fermentation.

# ② 两级和多级发酵

二级或多级沼气发酵工艺，发酵在两个或两个以上互相连通的发酵池内进行。原料先在第一个发酵池滞留一定时间进行分解、产气，然后料液从第一个发酵池进入第二个或其余的发酵池继续发酵产气。发酵工艺滞留期长，有机物分解彻底，但投资较高。

Two-stage and multi-stage fermentation: Two-stage or multi-stage biogas fermentation process, fermentation is carried out in two or more interconnected fermentation tanks. The raw material is first retained in the first fermentation tank for a certain period of time to decompose and produce gas, and then the liquid enters the second or other fermentation tanks from the first fermentation tank to continue fermentation and gas production. The fermentation process has a long retention period and the organic matter is thoroughly decomposed, but the investment is relatively high.

-26-

---

沼气发酵工艺

# Biogas fermentation process

# (3) 按投料运转方式分类：

分为连续发酵、半连续发酵、批量发酵、两步发酵。

Classification by feeding operation mode: divided into continuous fermentation, semi-continuous fermentation, batch fermentation, and two-step fermentation.

# ① 连续发酵：

是从投料启动并稳定运行后，按一定负荷量连续进料，此发酵工艺能保持稳定的有机物消化速度和产气率，适于处理来源稳定的城市污水、工业废水和大、中型畜牧场的粪便等。

Continuous fermentation: After starting with feeding and running stably, feeding continuously at a certain load. This fermentation process can maintain a stable organic matter digestion rate and gas production rate, and is suitable for treating urban sewage, industrial wastewater, and feces from large and medium-sized livestock farms with stable sources.

-27-

---

沼气发酵工艺

# Biogas fermentation process

# ② 半连续发酵：

启动时一次性投入较多的发酵原料，当产气量趋于下降时，开始定期添加新料和排出旧料，以维持比较稳定的产气率 。我国广大农村由于原料特点和农村用肥集中等原因，主要采用这种发酵工艺。

Semi-continuous fermentation: When starting, a large amount of fermentation raw materials are put into the fermentation process at one time. When the gas production tends to decrease, new materials are added and old materials are discharged regularly to maintain a relatively stable gas production rate. This fermentation process is mainly used in rural areas of China due to the characteristics of raw materials and the concentration of fertilizer use in rural areas.

| Regular or irregular discharge | feed                      | Mixing      | Pile in to        | Water | Fermentation | Material | Fertilizer |
| ------------------------------ | ------------------------- | ----------- | ----------------- | ----- | ------------ | -------- | ---------- |
| Inoculation                    | 一                         | pond        | sealing           | ion   | change       |          |            |
| Activated                      | Regular or irregular feed | Sludge from | fermentation tank |       |              |          |            |

# Semi-continuous fermentation process flow chart

-28-

---

沼气发酵工艺

# ③ 批量发酵

一次投料发酵，运转期中不添加新料，当发酵周期结束后，取出旧料再重新投入新料发酵；这种发酵工艺的产气量在初期上升很快，维持一段时间的产气高峰后，即逐渐下降。该工艺的发酵产气是不均衡的。

Batch fermentation: Fermentation with one feed, no new feed is added during the operation period, and when the fermentation cycle is over, the old feed is taken out and new feed is added for fermentation again; the gas production of this fermentation process increases rapidly in the early stage, maintains a peak gas production for a period of time, and then gradually decreases. The fermentation gas production of this process is unbalanced.

-29-

---

沼气发酵工艺

# Biogas fermentation process

# ④ 两步发酵

在两个池子内进行，第一个水解产酸池，装入高浓度的发酵原料，在此沤制产生浓的挥发酸溶液；第二个产甲烷池，以水解池产生的酸液为原料产气；该工艺可大幅度提高产气率，气体中甲烷含量也有提高，应用较多。

Two-step fermentation: It is carried out in two pools. The first is the hydrolysis and acid production pool, which is loaded with high-concentration fermentation raw materials and fermented to produce concentrated volatile acid solution; the second is the methane production pool, which uses the acid produced in the hydrolysis pool as raw material to produce gas; this process can greatly increase the gas production rate, and the methane content in the gas is also increased, and it is widely used.

| Biogas                | Biogas           | Feed                |
| --------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------- |
| Two-step fermentation | water            | Organic solid waste |
| Volatile              | Retting filtrate | acid                |
| Solid filter bed      |                  |                     |
| Volatile acid         | CH₄ generator    |                     |

-30-

---


# 干式、湿式和半干式厌氧发酵

# Dry, wet and semi-dry anaerobic digestion

- 干式厌氧消化: 罐内含固率在15%以上，对进料重质及轻飘杂质去除率要求较低的厌氧消化工艺。

Dry anaerobic digestion: The solid content in the tank is above 15%, and the anaerobic digestion process has a low requirement for the removal rate of heavy and light impurities in the feed.
- 半干式厌氧消化: 罐内含固率在10-15%，对进料重质或轻飘杂质去除率要求相对较高的厌氧消化工艺。

Semi-dry anaerobic digestion: The solid content in the tank is 10-15%, and the anaerobic digestion process has a relatively high requirement for the removal rate of heavy or light impurities in the feed.
- 厌氧罐内物料含固率在10%以下时，罐内易出现分层现象，工程上需要在预处理环节，将骨头、贝壳等重物质和塑料、纤维等轻飘杂质予以去除。

When the solid content of the material in the anaerobic tank is below 10%, stratification is likely to occur in the tank. In engineering, it is necessary to remove heavy materials such as bones and shells and light impurities such as plastics and fibers in the pretreatment stage.



---


# 干式、湿式和半干式厌氧发酵

# Dry, wet and semi-dry anaerobic digestion

- 当罐内含固率超过15%时，在适当强度搅拌下，厌氧罐内物料分层现象降低，此时无需在预处理环节去除轻飘和重质杂质。When the solid content in the tank exceeds 15%, the stratification of materials in the anaerobic tank is reduced under appropriate stirring intensity, and there is no need to remove light and heavy impurities in the pretreatment stage.
- 结合不同罐内物料含固率下物料分层难易程度，及不同类型厌氧消化罐对前端预处理要求，进行干、湿式厌氧的划分。Combined with the difficulty of material stratification under different solid content of materials in the tank and the requirements of different types of anaerobic digesters for front-end pretreatment, dry and wet anaerobic are divided.
- 罐内含固率低于10%厌氧消化，定义为湿式厌氧消化。Anaerobic digestion with a solid content of less than 10% in the tank is defined as wet anaerobic digestion.
- 罐内含固率高于15%厌氧消化，定义为干式厌氧消化。Anaerobic digestion with a solid content of more than 15% in the tank is defined as dry anaerobic digestion.
- 罐内含固率在两者之间，定义为半干式厌氧消化。The solid content in the tank is between the two, which is defined as semi-dry anaerobic digestion.



---

湿法发酵与干法发酵
# Wet fermentation vs dry fermentation

# 中温湿式发酵工艺

- 固体含量在15%以下，进罐物料含固率控制在8%-10%，粒径在15mm以下，发酵物料呈流动态。The solid content is below 15%, the solid content of the material entering the tank is controlled at 8%-10%, the particle size is below 15mm, and the fermented material is in a fluid state.
- 对成分复杂的厨余垃圾，进行压榨或稀释调质，压榨液进厌氧发酵反应器，压榨渣焚烧或堆肥或卫生填埋。For kitchen waste with complex components, it is squeezed or diluted for conditioning, and the squeezed liquid enters the anaerobic fermentation reactor, and the squeezed residue is incinerated or composted or sanitary landfilled.
- 湿法发酵含固率低，处理设施要求空间加大，沼液产生量大，后续处理困难。The wet fermentation has a low solid content, the treatment facility requires a larger space, the amount of biogas slurry produced is large, and subsequent treatment is difficult.
- 湿法发酵适用处理含水量较高的餐饮垃圾和污泥等，也可用于处理分选后的厨余有机垃圾，但是需要进行压榨或稀释预处理，水耗和能耗均较高。Wet fermentation is suitable for treating catering waste and sludge with high water content, and can also be used to treat sorted kitchen organic waste, but it needs to be squeezed or diluted for pretreatment, and the water consumption and energy consumption are high.

-33-

---

中温干式厌氧发酵系统工艺

# Medium temperature dry anaerobic fermentation system process

# 进 料 出 料 及 罐 内 物 料 运 移 方 式

# Feeding, discharging and material movement in the tank

✓ 柱塞泵进料，每天进料一次，一次进料数小时，从圆柱形罐一侧进入，利用脉冲注入压缩的沼气混合，压缩沼气约 5～8 个大气压，物料绕过罐体中心的隔墙后从罐的另一侧由罐体底部重力排料。

Feeding is done by a plunger pump, once a day for several hours at a time, entering from one side of the cylindrical tank, and mixing with compressed biogas by pulse injection. The biogas is compressed to about 5 to 8 atmospheres. The material bypasses the partition wall in the center of the tank and is discharged from the other side of the tank by gravity from the bottom of the tank.

# 厌氧发酵参数

# Anaerobic fermentation parameters

① Valorga

✓ 进料尺寸：＜6cm，温度：35℃或 55℃，停留时间：约 15 d（55℃）或 30 d（35℃），进料 TS：约 40%，罐内物料平均 TS：25～35%，容积负荷：6～11 kg TS/m³∙d，罐的有效容积：4200 m³。

Feed size: &#x3C;6cm, temperature: 35℃ or 55℃, residence time: about 15 d (55℃) or 30 d (35℃), feed TS: about 40%, average TS of materials in the tank: 25-35%, volumetric load: 6-11 kg TS/m³∙d, effective volume of the tank: 4200 m³.

-34-

---

中温干式厌氧发酵系统工艺
# Medium temperature dry anaerobic fermentation system process

# ② Kompogas Process

# 进料出料及罐内物料运移方式

Feeding, discharging and material movement in the tank

- 通过转子泵连续进料；罐内通过一根长搅拌轴，转速 2～3 rpm，横向搅拌并推动物料，物料以水平柱塞流形式运移；出料为搅拌轴推到出料口，跌落入出料槽，无轴螺杆提升输出。用泵排出消化残余物，约1/3 的出料回流以供接种微生物。
- The material is continuously fed through a rotor pump; a long stirring shaft is passed through the tank, with a speed of 2-3 rpm, stirring and pushing the material horizontally, and the material is moved in the form of horizontal plunger flow; the material is pushed to the discharge port by the stirring shaft, and falls into the discharge trough, and the shaftless screw is lifted and output. The digestion residue is discharged by a pump, and about 1/3 of the discharge is refluxed for inoculation of microorganisms.

# 厌氧发酵参数

Anaerobic fermentation parameters

- 进料尺寸：＜8 cm，温度：55℃，停留时间：14～18 d，罐内物料平均 TS： 25%，容积负荷：6～12 kg TS/m³∙d，罐的容积：1300 m³ 和 1500 m³ 两种类型。
- Feed size: &#x3C;8 cm, temperature: 55℃, residence time: 14-18 d, average TS of material in the tank: 25%, volume load: 6-12 kg TS/m³∙d, tank volume: 1300 m³ and 1500 m³.

-35-

---

中温干式厌氧发酵系统工艺
# Medium temperature dry anaerobic fermentation system process

# 进料出料及罐内物料运移方式

Feeding, discharging and material movement in the tank

✓ 两段式厌氧工艺，在进入厌氧消化罐前先在预发酵槽停留一定时间，然后通过螺旋进料器给消化罐进料，从方形罐的一侧进入，物料是平推流的方式移动，罐内有数个搅拌器，方形消化罐的另一侧采用真空抽吸出料。The two-stage anaerobic process first stays in the pre-fermentation tank for a certain period of time before entering the anaerobic digester, and then feeds the digester through a screw feeder, entering from one side of the square tank. The material moves in a plug flow manner. There are several agitators in the tank, and the other side of the square digester uses vacuum suction to discharge the material.

# 厌氧发酵参数

Anaerobic fermentation parameters

✓ 进料尺寸：＜6 cm，温度：55℃，停留时间：21～29 d，进料 TS：20～ 35%，罐内物料平均 TS：16～27%，出料 TS：16～20%，容积负荷：7～10 kg TS/m³∙d，罐的有效容积：1900m³。Feed size: &#x3C;6 cm, temperature: 55℃, residence time: 21-29 d, feed TS: 20-35%, average TS of materials in the tank: 16-27%, discharge TS: 16-20%, volume load: 7-10 kg TS/m³∙d, effective volume of the tank: 1900 m³.

-36-

---

中温干式厌氧发酵系统工艺
# Medium temperature dry anaerobic fermentation system process

# ④ Dranco Process

# 进料出料及罐内物料运移方式

Feed, discharge and material movement in the tank

• 进料与厌氧罐出料按5:1至8:1比例混合后，用水泥泵将物料运送至圆柱形消化器顶部进料，物料在厌氧发酵器中垂直向下运移，消化罐在底部出料。实际上物料单次在厌氧消化罐中停留时间为约 3d，但由于大物料回流，使其重复在消化罐中移动6～7次，从而使得总停留时间约20d。After the feed and anaerobic tank discharge are mixed in a ratio of 5:1 to 8:1, the material is transported to the top of the cylindrical digester by a cement pump for feeding. The material moves vertically downward in the anaerobic fermenter, and the digester is discharged at the bottom. In fact, the material stays in the anaerobic digester for about 3 days at a time, but due to the reflux of large materials, it is repeatedly moved in the digester 6 to 7 times, making the total residence time about 20 days.

# 控制及安全装置

# 厌氧发酵参数

Anaerobic fermentation parameters

- 进料尺寸：＜4 cm
- 温度：35℃或55℃
- 停留时间：约20 d（55℃）或30d（35℃）
- 进料 TS：约32%
- 罐内物料平均 TS：20～35%
- 容积负荷：5～10 kg TS/m³∙d
- 罐的有效容积：3275 m³

Feed size: &#x3C;4 cm, temperature: 35℃ or 55℃, residence time: about 20 days (55℃) or 30 days (35℃), feed TS: about 32%, average TS of materials in the tank: 20-35%, volume load: 5-10 kg TS/m³∙d, effective volume of the tank: 3275 m³.

-37-

---

中温干式厌氧发酵系统工艺
# Medium temperature dry anaerobic fermentation system process

- 原料总固体含量 20％～35%，物料中不存在可流动的液体，而是呈半固态。
The total solid content of the raw materials is 20% to 35%. There is no flowing liquid in the material, but it is semi-solid.
- 干法发酵工艺含固率较高，占地空间较小，流程简单，能耗低、沼液量少。
The dry fermentation process has a high solid content, occupies a small space, has a simple process, low energy consumption, and a small amount of biogas slurry.
- 易发生氨氮积累，产气率较低。
Ammonia nitrogen accumulation is prone to occur, and the gas production rate is low.
- 高含固率与传质存在矛盾，对罐体结构以及搅拌方式要求非常高。
There is a contradiction between high solid content and mass transfer, and the requirements for tank structure and stirring method are very high.
- 严格控制罐内物料含固率、粘度、进罐惰性物比例。
Strictly control the solid content, viscosity, and proportion of inert materials in the tank.

-38-

---

中温干式厌氧发酵系统工艺
# Medium temperature dry anaerobic fermentation system process

- 搅拌方式，包括气体搅拌、回流搅拌和机械搅拌；前两种搅拌形式，罐内都没有机械搅拌装置。The stirring methods include gas stirring, reflux stirring and mechanical stirring; in the first two stirring methods, there is no mechanical stirring device in the tank.
- 出料口设置四种工艺，都是采用罐体底部出料，但立式罐结构都是采用重力出料，无需再配置动力出料装置；而卧式罐体中瑞典Kompogas工艺，是依靠罐内搅拌轴将物料推送出料，德国 BRV 工艺，是在出料端采用真空抽吸出料的方式。There are four processes for the discharge port, all of which use the bottom of the tank to discharge, but the vertical tank structure uses gravity discharge, and no power discharge device is required; while the Swedish Kompogas process in the horizontal tank relies on the stirring shaft in the tank to push the material out, and the German BRV process uses vacuum suction discharge at the discharge end.

-39-

---

# 中温干式厌氧发酵系统工艺

# Medium temperature dry anaerobic fermentation system process

• 干式厌氧发酵系统不论采用立式结构还是卧式结构，一旦发生相分离，罐内都会发生重质、水相和轻质的三相分离。Whether the dry anaerobic fermentation system adopts a vertical or horizontal structure, once phase separation occurs, three phases of heavy, water and light will separate in the tank.

• 重质的砂石沉降在消化罐底部，轻质的塑料等杂物漂浮在消化罐顶部，而水相居中。Heavy sand and gravel settle at the bottom of the digester, light plastics and other debris float on the top of the digester, and the water phase is in the middle.

• 对于卧式的机械搅拌方式，如德国BRV和瑞典Kompogas工艺，罐体内的沉积物将导致机械搅拌失效无法搅拌，在不清罐的情况下，几乎不可能再使系统恢复运行状态。For horizontal mechanical stirring methods, such as the German BRV and Swedish Kompogas processes, the sediment in the tank will cause the mechanical stirring to fail and it will be almost impossible to restore the system to operation without cleaning the tank.

---

中温干式厌氧发酵系统工艺
# Medium temperature dry anaerobic fermentation system process

- 比利时 Dranco 工艺，由于采用底部锥底出料，一旦发生相分离，沉淀物将堵塞出料口，也需要清罐后再重新启动。The Belgian Dranco process uses a bottom cone discharge. Once phase separation occurs, the sediment will block the discharge port, and the tank needs to be cleaned before restarting.
- 法国 Valorga 立式工艺，采用底部高压沼气搅拌，沉积会降低搅拌效率，但不影响高压沼气搅拌的操作，同时立式罐体出料口设置低位和高位出料口，当沉积物将低位料口堵塞后，可采用高位料口继续出料和回流，再加上高压气体搅拌的正常运行，可在不清罐的状态下恢复罐体运行。The French Valorga vertical process uses bottom high-pressure biogas stirring. Sedimentation will reduce the stirring efficiency, but it does not affect the operation of high-pressure biogas stirring. At the same time, the vertical tank body discharge port is set with low and high discharge ports. When the sediment blocks the low-level discharge port, the high-level discharge port can be used to continue the discharge and reflux. In addition, the normal operation of high-pressure gas stirring can restore the tank body operation without cleaning the tank.

-41-
