# 固废处置及资源化

# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 4.3 Anaerobic System Design

# 厌氧系统设计

Yang Lei

2025-03-23

---

厌氧消化系统

# Anaerobic digestion system

- 厌氧消化可根据项目特点要求合理选择中温或高温厌氧；
Anaerobic digestion can reasonably select medium-temperature or high-temperature anaerobic according to the requirements of the project characteristics;
- 中温厌氧消化温度 35℃ ～ 45℃ 为宜 ， 厌氧反应器温度波动不宜超过 2℃/d ；
The medium-temperature anaerobic digestion temperature is preferably 35℃ ～ 45℃, and the temperature fluctuation of the anaerobic reactor should not exceed 2℃/d;
- 高温厌氧消化温度 50℃ ～ 55℃ 为宜 ， 厌氧反应器温度波动不宜超过 1℃/d ；
The high-temperature anaerobic digestion temperature is preferably 50℃ ～ 55℃, and the temperature fluctuation of the anaerobic reactor should not exceed 1℃/d;
- 湿式厌氧消化罐内含固率宜小于 10%，半干式厌氧消化罐内含固率宜为10%～15%，干式厌氧消化罐内含固率宜为15%～20%左右。
The solid content in the wet anaerobic digester should be less than 10%, the solid content in the semi-dry anaerobic digester should be 10%～15%, and the solid content in the dry anaerobic digester should be about 15%～20%.

-43-

---


# 厌氧消化系统有机负荷

# Organic load of anaerobic digestion system

- 湿式厌氧消化反应器内有机负荷宜在2 kg VS/(m³·d)～3 kg VS/(m³·d)；The organic load in the wet anaerobic digestion reactor should be between 2 kg VS/(m³·d) and 3 kg VS/(m³·d);
- 半干式厌氧消化反应器内有机负荷宜在 3 kg VS/(m³·d) ～4 kg VS/(m³·d) ；The organic load in the semi-dry anaerobic digestion reactor should be between 3 kg VS/(m³·d) and 4 kg VS/(m³·d);
- 干式厌氧消化反应器内有机负荷宜在 4 kg VS/ /(m³·d) ～6 kg VS/(m³·d) ； The organic load in the dry anaerobic digestion reactor should be between 4 kg VS//(m³·d) and 6 kg VS/(m³·d);
- 中温厌氧消化反应器内，氨氮浓度不宜超过 5500 mg/L；高温厌氧消化反应器内，氨氮浓度不宜超过4500 mg/L；In the medium-temperature anaerobic digestion reactor, the ammonia nitrogen concentration should not exceed 5500 mg/L; in the high-temperature anaerobic digestion reactor, the ammonia nitrogen concentration should not exceed 4500 mg/L;
- 高氨氮严重抑制厌氧消化， 最好不超过1000 mg/L ； High ammonia nitrogen seriously inhibits anaerobic digestion, and it is best not to exceed 1000 mg/L;



---

厌氧消化脱水系统

# Anaerobic digestion dehydration system

- 湿式厌氧消化后的沼液脱水宜采用离心脱水；
Centrifugal dehydration is recommended for dehydration of biogas slurry after wet anaerobic digestion;
- 半干式和干式厌氧消化后的沼液脱水宜采用多级组合脱水工艺；
Multi-stage combined dehydration process is recommended for dehydration of biogas slurry after semi-dry and dry anaerobic digestion;
- 挤压脱水沼渣含水率不宜高于60%；
The moisture content of biogas residue after extrusion dehydration should not be higher than 60%;
- 离心脱水沼渣含水率不宜高于80%。
The moisture content of biogas residue after centrifugal dehydration should not be higher than 80%.

-45-

---


# 厌氧消化系统保温

# Anaerobic digestion system insulation

- 厌氧消化反应器应设置加热保温装置
- Anaerobic digestion reactor should be equipped with heating and insulation device
- 总需热量应考虑冬季最不利工况，并可按下式计算:
- The total heat requirement should take into account the most unfavorable working conditions in winter and can be calculated as follows:

| 总需热量， | Q=Q₁+Q₂+Q₃+Q₄                                                                               |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Q—    | Total heat requirement，kJ/h;                                                                |
| Q₁—   | 加热料液到设计温度需要的热量，Heat required to heat the liquid to the design temperature, kJ/h;            |
| Q₂—   | 保持消化器发酵温度需要的热量，Heat required to maintain the fermentation temperature of the digester，kJ/h; |
| Q₃—   | 管道散热量，Pipeline heat dissipation，kJ/h                                                        |
| Q₄—   | 沼气及饱和水蒸气带走的热量，Heat taken away by biogas and saturated water vapor, kJ/h                     |

- 换热装置的总换热面积应根据热平衡计算，并应留有 10% ～ 20% 的余量。
- The total heat exchange area of the heat exchange device should be calculated based on the heat balance, and a margin of 10% to 20% should be left.



---

厌氧消化反应器设计
# Anaerobic digestion reactor design

- 湿垃圾厌氧消化反应器型式选择，应根据来料特性、预处理效果等因素，经技术经济比较后确定；

The type of wet garbage anaerobic digestion reactor should be selected based on the characteristics of the incoming materials, pretreatment effects and other factors, and determined after technical and economic comparison;
- 厌氧消化反应器，应有良好的防渗、防腐、保温、密闭性和安全性，并应具有耐老化、抗强风、雪等恶劣天气的性能；

The anaerobic digestion reactor should have good anti-seepage, anti-corrosion, heat preservation, airtightness and safety, and should be resistant to aging, strong winds, snow and other severe weather conditions;
- 厌氧消化反应器的结构，应有利于物料进出，减少短流和产生滞流死角；

The structure of the anaerobic digestion reactor should be conducive to the entry and exit of materials, reduce short-flow and dead corners of stagnation;
- 应具有良好均匀搅拌功能，防止沉砂和浮渣在反应器中沉积而引起局部酸化；

It should have good uniform stirring function to prevent the deposition of sand and scum in the reactor and cause local acidification;
- 厌氧消化反应器进料方式，可采用连续进料或批次进料方式，根据小时进料量，确定进料系统参数、加热系统功率，以及出料系统参数等。

The feeding method of the anaerobic digestion reactor can be continuous feeding or batch feeding. According to the hourly feeding volume, the feeding system parameters, heating system power, and discharge system parameters are determined.

-47-

---

厌氧消化反应器设计
# Anaerobic digestion reactor design

- 厌氧消化反应器上都应设置正负压保护装置和压力报警装置；
Positive and negative pressure protection devices and pressure alarm devices should be installed on anaerobic digestion reactors;
- 厌氧消化反应器集气管路上，宜设置稳压装置；
A pressure stabilizing device should be installed on the gas collecting pipeline of the anaerobic digestion reactor;
- 采用水封稳压装置时，有效高度，应根据厌氧消化反应器最大工作压力和后端储气压力确定；
When a water seal pressure stabilizing device is used, the effective height should be determined according to the maximum working pressure of the anaerobic digestion reactor and the back-end gas storage pressure;
- 厌氧消化反应器设计，应符合现行国家标准《大中型沼气工程技术规范》GB/T 51063有关规定；
The design of anaerobic digestion reactors should comply with the relevant provisions of the current national standard "Technical Specifications for Large and Medium-sized Biogas Projects" GB/T 51063;

-48-

---

# 厌氧消化罐 Anaerobic digester

- 预制的圆柱形垂直钢筋混凝土罐 ， 内部有一个长度为 2/3 直径的中央垂直隔断墙；The prefabricated cylindrical vertical reinforced concrete tank has a central vertical partition wall with a length of 2/3 of the diameter; Current projects basically use steel plates instead of concrete structures;
- 目前的工程，基本上采用钢板，不再使用混凝土结构；The materials in the digester flow in one direction horizontally;
- 消化罐里的物料，在水平方向上朝着一个方向流动；物料进口和出口，都位于消化罐底部，分别在中央隔断墙的两侧；the material inlet and outlet are both located at the bottom of the digester, on both sides of the central partition wall;
- 中央隔断结构，使得罐内发酵物料围绕它形成圆周路径，泵入消化罐的物料，只有在走完平均为消化罐直径两倍路径后，才会进行出料，保证物料在罐内不会短路。The central partition structure allows the fermented materials in the tank to form a circular path around it. The materials pumped into the digester will only be discharged after they have traveled an average path of twice the diameter of the digester, ensuring that the materials will not short-circuit in the tank.

---

厌氧消化罐 Anaerobic digester

在消化罐里经过30-35天后，物料通过位于消化罐底部的出料口进行出料；After 30-35 days in the digester, the material is discharged through the discharge port at the bottom of the digester;

重力出料，物料靠自身的重量排出消化罐；Gravity discharge, the material is discharged from the digester by its own weight;

消化罐中物料含量，必须遵守内部高度的84%标称值，超过该值的上部空间为气体包，容纳了生成的沼气；The material content in the digester must comply with the nominal value of 84% of the internal height. The upper space exceeding this value is a gas bag, which contains the generated biogas;

沼气由顶部一个DN200管道排往后续沼气系统；The biogas is discharged to the subsequent biogas system through a DN200 pipe at the top;

厌氧消化罐设计计算
# Anaerobic digester design calculation

V有效容积 = K*Q有机*T/（Dave* N）

V总容积 = V有效容积/E

式中：

| VOL | = | Q有机\*DM\*VDM/V有效容积 |
| --- | - | ------------------ |

K，峰值系数，peak coefficient K=1.1；

N，消化罐数量（个），按 3 个设计；number of digesters (pieces), designed as 3;

Q有机，分选后的有机垃圾重量（t/d）；organic, weight of organic waste after sorting (t/d);

D ，罐内消化物平均密度（t/m³），

ave average density of digestate in the tank 0.95-1.05 t/m³

T，停留时间，residence time, T = 32-35 day；

V有效容积，单个消化罐有效容积；effective volume of a single digester;

V总容积，单个消化罐总容积；total volume of a single digester;

E，有效容积比，按0.83计算；effective volume ratio, calculated as 0.83;

VOL，有机容积负荷；organic volume load;

DM，垃圾中干物质百分比；percentage of dry matter in garbage;

VDM，垃圾挥发性干物质百分比；percentage of volatile dry matter in garbage.

-51-

---


# 含水率约65%的有机垃圾300吨

300 tons of organic waste with a moisture content of about 65%

- DM，垃圾中干物质百分比，本项目根据垃圾成分分析取35%；
DM, percentage of dry matter in garbage, this project takes 35% based on garbage composition analysis;
- VDM，垃圾挥发性干物质百分比 ，本项目根据垃圾成分分析取 65%；
VDM, percentage of volatile dry matter in garbage, this project takes 65% based on garbage composition analysis;
- 参数取值，Dave取1t/m³，T取34，K取1.1，由此可计算出单个消化罐的有效容积、总容积和有机负荷。
Parameter values, Dave takes 1t/m³, T takes 34, K takes 1.1, from which the effective volume, total volume and organic load of a single digester can be calculated.

V有效容积= 1.1*300*34/(1*3) = 3735 m³
V总容积=3735/0.83=4500 m³
VOL=300*0.35*0.65*1000/3735/3=6.09 kgVDM/(m³•V有效容积•d)

压榨脱水 Pressing and dehydration

- 固相泥饼和液相压榨滤液；Solid phase mud cake and liquid phase pressing filtrate;

沼气搅拌单元

# Biogas mixing unit

- 高压沼气管网中重要的一个环节，是沼气搅拌罐；

An important link in the high-pressure biogas pipeline network is the biogas mixing tank;
- 搅拌罐采用不锈钢材质，储存容量为24m³，安装在室外，靠近厌氧消化罐；

The mixing tank is made of stainless steel, with a storage capacity of 24m³, and is installed outdoors, close to the anaerobic digester;
- 沼气搅拌罐是高压沼气的存储设备，按照自动程序控制，高压沼气在每次搅拌操作时由沼气搅拌罐释放出来；

The biogas mixing tank is a storage device for high-pressure biogas. According to the automatic program control, high-pressure biogas is released from the biogas mixing tank during each mixing operation;
- 搅拌罐通过沼气压缩机连续不断地提供压缩沼气。

The mixing tank continuously provides compressed biogas through the biogas compressor.

# 厨余垃圾厌氧发酵沼气组分

# Biogas components from anaerobic fermentation of kitchen waste

• 厌氧发酵过程产生的生物气体（沼气）是一种混合气体，主要成分为CH₄、CO₂、H₂S以及水汽；The biogas (biogas) produced during the anaerobic fermentation process is a mixed gas, the main components of which are CH₄, CO₂, H₂S and water vapor.

| 组 分 | 含量（V%）       |
| --- | ------------ |
| CH₄ | 40～65%       |
| CO₂ | 35～60%       |
| H₂S | 800～4800 ppm |
| O₂  | < 0.5%       |
| N₂  | < 3.0%       |
| 湿度  | 饱和           |
| 温度  | 30～45℃       |

# 沼渣和沼液 Digestate and slurry

- 欧洲发达国家湿垃圾厌氧消化后的产物沼渣、沼液较多制成有机肥、液态肥等土地利用产品；In European countries, the products of sludge and liquid biogas after anaerobic digestion of wet garbage are mostly made into land use products such as organic fertilizer and liquid fertilizer;
- 目前国内绝大部分地域或地区沼渣、沼液资源化产品在农业、林业生产中应用仍存在政策性“壁垒”，沼渣出路以外运焚烧处理为主，沼液按照污水进行处理，导致目前湿垃圾处理资源化率较低，处理成本高；At present, there are still policy "barriers" to the application of sludge and liquid biogas resource products in agricultural and forestry production in most regions of China. The sludge is mainly transported and incinerated, and the liquid biogas is treated as sewage, resulting in a low resource utilization rate of wet garbage treatment and high treatment costs;
- 随着国家双碳政策的推进以及资源化技术的发展，湿垃圾厌氧后的沼渣沼液，在未来实现农林业资源化利用或可期待，因此建议预留资源化设施用地。With the advancement of the national dual carbon policy and the development of resource technology, the sludge and liquid biogas after anaerobic digestion of wet garbage may be expected to be used as agricultural and forestry resources in the future, so it is recommended to reserve land for resource facilities.



---


# 沼渣和沼液 Digestate and slurry

- 脱水后的沼渣，通过皮带机输送至沼渣堆肥车间，先进行混料处理；The dehydrated biogas residue is transported to the biogas residue composting facilitates by a belt conveyor and mixed;
- 沼渣与粉碎的秸秆或返混料等，通过混料机进行混合均匀，调节物料水分、C/N值等参数到适宜范围后，通过装载机倒入发酵池内，并堆放1.5-1.8m 高；The biogas residue is mixed evenly with crushed straw or returned mixed materials by a mixer, and after adjusting the material moisture, C/N ratio and other parameters to the appropriate range, it is poured into the fermentation tank by a loader and piled 1.5-1.8 m high;
- 根据堆体的温度，控制翻抛机进行翻抛，并通过鼓风机提供堆体所需的含氧量；According to the temperature of the pile, the turning machine is controlled to turn the pile, and the blower is used to provide the required oxygen content of the pile;
- 目前主要是干燥后，与生活垃圾混合焚烧；也可以直接送生活垃圾焚烧厂焚烧；Currently, it is mainly dried and mixed with domestic waste for incineration; it can also be directly sent to the domestic waste incineration plant for incineration;



---


# 沼渣和沼液利用

# Utilization of digestate and liquid

• 一种优质有机肥，沼液称为沼气水肥，沼渣称为沼气渣肥。A high-quality organic fertilizer, the biogas slurry is called biogas water fertilizer, and the biogas residue is called biogas residue fertilizer.

| 肥料   | 成分    | 有机质   | 腐殖酸       | 全氮        | 全磷       | 全钾 |
| ---- | ----- | ----- | --------- | --------- | -------- | -- |
| 沼气水肥 | ——    | ——    | 0.03～0.08 | 0.02～0.06 | 0.05～0.1 |    |
| 沼气渣肥 | 30～50 | 10～20 | 0.8～1.5   | 0.4～0.6   | 0.6～1.20 |    |
| 人尿粪  | 5～10  | ——    | 0.5～0.8   | 0.2～0.4   | 0.2～0.3  |    |
| 猪粪   | 15    | ——    | 0.56      | 0.4       | 0.44     |    |



---


# 沼渣和沼液利用

# Utilization of digestate and liquid

- 沼渣肥用在水稻上的效果好于旱地作物，沼液用在旱地作物上的效果好于水田；Biogas residue fertilizer is more effective on rice than on dryland crops, and biogas liquid is better on dryland crops than on paddy fields;
- 沼气肥与化肥配合施用，效果好于单用一种的增产效果之和；The combined application of biogas fertilizer and chemical fertilizer is better than the sum of the yield-increasing effects of using either alone;
- 有机肥是迟效肥，而化肥是速效肥，二者配合使用，能相互取长补短，既保证了较快较高的肥效，又能避免连续大量施用化肥对土壤结构的破坏及土壤肥力的降低。Organic fertilizer is a slow-acting fertilizer, while chemical fertilizer is a quick-acting fertilizer. The combined use of the two can complement each other, ensuring faster and higher fertilizer efficiency while avoiding the damage to soil structure and reduction of soil fertility caused by continuous and large-scale application of chemical fertilizers.



---


# 沼渣和沼液利用

# Utilization of digestate and liquid

- 沼渣和沼液施用，可显著改善土壤性质；The application of biogas residue and biogas liquid can significantly improve soil properties;
- 水果等作物，非常需要有机肥；Fruit and other crops are in great need of organic fertilizer;
- 城市有机垃圾厌氧发酵沼渣 ，基本上只能送焚烧厂焚烧，无出路； The biogas residue of urban organic waste anaerobic fermentation can basically only be sent to incineration plants for incineration, with no other options;
- 考虑到沼渣焚烧对焚烧厂后端烟气处理和利用的影响 ，有条件时，宜对沼渣进行干化造粒预处理；Considering the impact of biogas residue incineration on the treatment and utilization of flue gas at the back end of the incineration plant, it is advisable to pre-treat the biogas residue by drying and granulation when conditions permit;
- 沼液采用反渗透浓缩 ， 清水回用 ， 浓液做液体肥； The biogas liquid is concentrated by reverse osmosis, the clean water is reused, and the concentrated liquid is used as liquid fertilizer;
- 厌氧发酵功能 ， 有机污染物的稳定化 ， 同时获取甲烷能源； The anaerobic fermentation function stabilizes organic pollutants and obtains methane energy at the same time;



---


# 沼渣和沼液利用

# Utilization of digestate and liquid

- 沼液沼渣的后处理技术的选择，应以提高沼液沼渣综合利用效益、避免对环境造成二次污染为原则，尽量实现其资源化利用。The selection of post-treatment technology for biogas slurry and biogas residue should be based on the principle of improving the comprehensive utilization benefits of biogas slurry and biogas residue and avoiding secondary pollution to the environment, and try to realize its resource utilization.
- 沼液作为液态肥，应取得相关部门许可，并满足《沼气工程沼液沼渣处理技术规范》（NY/T 2374）的相关要求；As liquid fertilizer, biogas slurry should obtain permission from relevant departments and meet the relevant requirements of the "Technical Specifications for Biogas Slurry and Biogas Residue Treatment in Biogas Projects" (NY/T 2374);



---


# 沼渣和沼液利用

# Utilization of digestate and liquid

- 沼液作为污水处理，宜选用“预处理＋生物处理＋深度处理”组合处理工艺，其中深度处理宜探索非膜处理工艺，以避免浓缩液等二次污染物的产生，预处理、生化处理产生的污泥宜与沼渣一并处理。As sewage treatment, biogas slurry should be treated with a combination of "pretreatment + biological treatment + deep treatment". Among them, deep treatment should explore non-membrane treatment processes to avoid the generation of secondary pollutants such as concentrated liquid. The sludge generated by pretreatment and biochemical treatment should be treated together with biogas residue.
- 沼渣利用前应进行无害化和稳定化处理，宜作为有机肥或其他产品原料。Biogas residue should be harmless and stabilized before use, and should be used as organic fertilizer or other product raw materials.



---


# 沼渣和沼液利用

# Utilization of digestate and liquid

• 当作为有机肥料时，应满足现行国家标准《有机肥料》NY/T 525的要求，并取得相关部门许可；当用作绿化基质土时，应符合现行国家标准《绿化用有机基质》GB/T 33891的要求；当作为土壤调理剂时，应符合现行行业标准《土壤调理剂通用要求》NY/T3034的要求；

When used as organic fertilizer, it should meet the requirements of the current national standard "Organic Fertilizer" NY/T 525 and obtain the permission of relevant departments; when used as greening substrate soil, it should meet the requirements of the current national standard "Organic Substrate for Greening" GB/T 33891; when used as soil conditioner, it should meet the requirements of the current industry standard "General Requirements for Soil Conditioners" NY/T3034;



---


# 沼渣和沼液利用 Utilization of digestate and liquid

- 沼渣干化处理时，干化机内与沼渣接触部件材质不应低于SS304，干化机的蒸发强度不宜高于6kg/m²·h，载气中O₂含量不宜高于6%，载气需经除尘、冷凝脱水后排入除臭系统，冷凝后载气温度不宜高于50℃. When the biogas residue is dried, the material of the parts in the dryer that come into contact with the biogas residue should not be lower than SS304, the evaporation intensity of the dryer should not be higher than 6kg/m²·h, the O₂ content in the carrier gas should not be higher than 6%, and the carrier gas should be discharged into the deodorization system after dust removal, condensation and dehydration. The carrier gas temperature after condensation should not be higher than 50℃.
- 沼渣采用炭化、湿式氧化（裂解）或其他处理工艺时，其尾气或烟气排放应参考相应领域的标准要求；The biogas residue is carbonized and wet oxidized (When using pyrolysis or other treatment processes, the exhaust or flue gas emissions should refer to the standard requirements of the corresponding fields.
- 沼渣资源化产品的储存应根据产品产量、市场需求周期等因素，综合考虑存贮仓库的容量，且不应低于15天的周转；The storage of biogas residue resource products should be based on product output, market demand cycle and other factors, and the capacity of the storage warehouse should be considered comprehensively, and the turnover should not be less than 15 days.



---

沼气的利用
# Biogas utilization

- 用作热源；用作动力源；作为压缩气体利用；与城市燃烧气混合利用 ；
Used as heat source; used as power source; used as compressed gas; mixed with city combustion gas;
- 沼气发热量为37660kJ/m³，当含有CO₂时，其发热量为20920～25100 kJ/m³；
The calorific value of biogas is 37660 kJ/m³, and when it contains CO₂, its calorific value is 20920~25100 kJ/m³;
- 平均1 kg有机物的纯沼气产生量为200～300 L，而1000m³的沼气发热量相当于600m³的天然气；
The average pure biogas production of 1 kg of organic matter is 200~300 L, and the calorific value of 1000 m³ of biogas is equivalent to 600 m³ of natural gas;
- 产热，各种小型燃烧器、锅炉、燃气发电机、汽车发动机等；
Heat generation, various small burners, boilers, gas generators, automobile engines, etc.;
- 生产压缩天然气时， 需要对沼气进行脱硫、 脱水 、 脱二氧化碳 、 脱氮；
When producing compressed natural gas, biogas needs to be desulfurized, dehydrated, decarbonated, and denitrified;

# 餐饮垃圾、厨余垃圾预处理系统

# Catering waste and kitchen waste pretreatment system

餐饮垃圾：餐馆、饭店、单位食堂等的饮食剩余物以及后厨的果蔬、肉食、油脂、面点等的加工过程废弃物。

Catering waste: food leftovers from restaurants, hotels, company canteens, etc., as well as waste from the processing of fruits, vegetables, meat, oils, pastries, etc. in the back kitchen.

厨余垃圾：家庭日常生活中丢弃的果蔬及食物下脚料、剩菜剩饭、瓜果皮等易腐有机垃圾。

Kitchen waste: perishable organic waste such as fruits, vegetables, food scraps, leftovers, melons and fruit peels discarded in daily life.

餐厨垃圾：餐饮垃圾和厨余垃圾的总称。

Catering waste: a general term for catering waste and kitchen waste.

---


# 典型餐厨垃圾厌氧发酵过程

# Typical anaerobic fermentation process of food waste

| 污水处理       | 沼渣及污泥焚烧 |
| ---------- | ------- |
| 分选         | 厨余垃圾    |
| 破袋、制浆/固液分离 | 筛分、磁选   |
| 固渣焚烧       | 液相浆料    |
| 除砂         | 厌氧消化    |
| 沼气发电利用     |         |
| 餐饮垃圾       | 粗分选     |
| 破碎制浆       | 油水分离    |
| 废弃食用油脂     | 过滤+三相分离 |
| 粗油脂利用      |         |
| 杂质焚烧       | 污水处理    |



---


餐饮垃圾处理工艺流程

# 餐饮垃圾处理工艺流程

餐饮垃圾预处理系统主要由接收及滤水、粗分选、精分除杂制浆、除砂、螺旋挤压脱水、除油及杂物装载 7 个工艺子单元组成。

The catering waste pretreatment system mainly consists of seven process sub-units: receiving and filtering, rough sorting, fine separation and impurity removal and pulping, sand removal, screw extrusion dehydration, oil removal and debris loading.

# 1. 接收及滤水

计量后餐饮垃圾被自卸入接收料仓内，料仓具有滤水功能，可将餐饮垃圾中渗滤液滤出并收集至滤液池中。料仓底部的上料无轴螺旋输送机将餐饮垃圾输送进入粗分选机。

Receiving and filtering water: After metering, the catering waste is unloaded into the receiving silo. The silo has a water filtering function, which can filter out the leachate in the catering waste and collect it in the filtrate pool. The shaftless screw conveyor at the bottom of the silo transports the catering waste into the rough sorting machine.

# 2. 粗分选

主要针对进料垃圾中直径≥55mm大块杂质的去除，去除效率≥90%。

Rough sorting: It is mainly aimed at removing large impurities with a diameter of ≥55mm in the feed waste, with a removal efficiency of ≥90%.



---


餐饮垃圾处理工艺流程

# ③ 精分除杂制浆

除杂制浆机是集合锤式破碎与离心拍打筛分的组合工艺设备，餐饮垃圾在该设备内被破碎为粒径8mm浆料，同时粒径≥8mm杂质被筛分去除，对直径在8~55mm杂质去除率≥85%。

Fine separation and impurity removal and pulping: The impurity removal pulping machine is a combined process equipment that combines hammer crushing and centrifugal beating screening. The catering waste is crushed into 8mm slurry in the equipment, and at the same time, impurities with a particle size of ≥8mm are screened and removed. The removal rate of impurities with a diameter of 8~55mm is ≥85%.

# ④ 除砂

除砂系统主要采用平流除砂机，通过重力作用去除砂砾杂质，再经过底部螺旋输送至杂物压缩机。

Sand removal: The sand removal system mainly uses a horizontal flow sand remover to remove sand and gravel impurities through gravity, and then transports them to the debris compressor through the bottom screw.

-74-


---

# 餐饮垃圾处理工艺流程

⑤ 螺旋挤压脱水：螺旋挤压脱水机将杂质先经过入料斗进入螺旋压榨机内部，螺旋轴外面是圆筒形状的筛板，螺旋轴将杂质向出料口方向输送，推挤封堵出口的压椎，从而形成极高的背压，液体和有机垃圾中粘稠状的有机物就会从筛板的滤孔被挤出来，而固相则保留在螺旋压榨机内部，在螺旋轴的推送作用下经由出料口被挤出。

Screw extrusion dehydration: The screw extrusion dehydration machine first passes the impurities through the feed hopper into the screw press. The outside of the screw shaft is a cylindrical sieve plate. The screw shaft transports the impurities toward the discharge port and pushes the pressure cone that blocks the discharge port, thereby forming an extremely high back pressure. The viscous organic matter in the liquid and organic waste will be squeezed out from the filter holes of the sieve plate, while the solid phase is retained inside the screw press and squeezed out through the discharge port under the pushing action of the screw shaft.

---

Catering waste treatment process

# ⑥ 除油

经过除杂制浆、除砂后的餐饮垃圾浆料进入输料管道，通过对管道进行蒸汽加热提升温度，再打入卧式三相离心机进行毛油、固相和水相分离。分离出来的毛油储存在油罐中外售。固相和液相再次混合形成除油后的垃圾浆料，后经换热降温进入厌氧发酵系统。

Oil removal: After the impurity removal and sand removal, the catering waste slurry enters the feed pipeline. The temperature is raised by steam heating the pipeline, and then it is pumped into a horizontal three-phase centrifuge to separate the crude oil, solid phase and water phase. The separated crude oil is stored in an oil tank for sale. The solid phase and liquid phase are mixed again to form the deoiled garbage slurry, which is then cooled by heat exchange and enters the anaerobic fermentation system.

# ⑦ 杂物装载

螺旋挤压脱水分离、粗分选、除砂出来的杂质输送至压缩机进行压缩，压缩后暂存垃圾转运箱，最终运送至焚烧厂进行焚烧处置。

Loading of debris: The impurities removed by spiral extrusion dehydration separation, coarse sorting and sand removal are transported to the compressor for compression, and then temporarily stored in the garbage transfer box before being transported to the incineration plant for incineration disposal.

# 餐饮垃圾处理设备：平流沉降除砂

# Catering waste treatment equipment: advection sedimentation and sand removal

- 去除大颗粒重物质砂粒，如贝壳渣、鸡蛋渣、骨头渣；
Remove large particles of heavy sand, such as shell residue, egg residue, and bone residue;
- 随着浆料的流动和重物质砂砾重力沉降作用，砂砾不断沉降到底部，沉淀的砂砾由一根水平运输螺旋与浆料流动方向相反输送方向，将砂砾推往一根倾斜提砂螺旋。
With the flow of slurry and the gravity sedimentation of heavy sand and gravel, the sand and gravel continue to settle to the bottom. The precipitated sand and gravel are transported by a horizontal conveying screw in the opposite direction of the slurry flow, pushing the sand and gravel to an inclined sand lifting screw.
- 沉砂物质在提升过程中与水分离被运输排出装置；
The sedimented sand is separated from the water during the lifting process and transported to the discharge device;
- 顶部溢出的良浆进入后端工序系统。
The good slurry overflowing from the top enters the back-end process system.

| Horizontal centrifuge structure           |            |   |     |           |
| Horizontal centrifuge physical appearance |            |   |     |           |

三相卧式离心机接收来自于加热罐中的垃圾浆料，采用连续进料、分离和出料的运行方式，在此阶段完成对浆料的固（渣）、液（水）、油的三相分离，分离后得到纯度为95%以上的油脂。

The three-phase horizontal centrifuge receives the garbage slurry from the heating tank and adopts a continuous feeding, separation and discharging operation mode. In this stage, the three-phase separation of the slurry into solid (slag), liquid (water) and oil is completed, and after separation, oil and fat with a purity of more than 95% is obtained.

# 厨余垃圾处理工艺：接收储存

# Food waste treatment process: receiving and storing

- 卸料大厅及垃圾坑设计为高位卸料型式，其中卸料大厅设置在高位地块，垃圾坑设置在低位地块；The unloading hall and garbage pit are designed as high-level unloading types, where the unloading hall is set on a high-level plot and the garbage pit is set on a low-level plot;
- 垃圾池主要起着接收和暂存物料的作用，另外仓底设有滤液收集设施。The garbage pool mainly plays the role of receiving and temporarily storing materials, and there are filtrate collection facilities at the bottom of the warehouse.

| Unloading Hall | Garbage pool |

# kitchen waste treatment process: feeding

# 厨余垃圾处理设备：破碎机

# 双轴破碎

将袋装垃圾划破 ， 破碎成一定尺寸 ， 利于后续处理的稳定性 ， 用双轴破碎机将其破碎成 ≤300mm。

The bagged garbage is cut and broken into a certain size to facilitate the stability of subsequent processing, and is broken into ≤300mm using a double-shaft crusher.



---


# 厨余垃圾处理设备：压榨干湿分离

# Kitchen waste treatment equipment: squeezing dry and wet separation

# 生活垃圾原料

# 第一步：进料阶段

厨余垃圾原料从高压压榨机顶部的接料斗进料，主活塞将物料推送进入有机物提取腔内。在此阶段，设备出口阀门保持关闭。

Step 1: During the feeding stage, the kitchen waste raw materials are fed from the receiving hopper at the top of the high-pressure press, and the main piston pushes the materials into the organic matter extraction chamber. During this stage, the equipment outlet valve remains closed.

# 第二步：压榨阶段

活塞以100-500公斤力/平方厘米压力，将厨余垃圾物料向有机物腔体内进行挤压，有机质以及水分以浆料形式从垃圾体中分离出来成为 “湿基”。湿基浆料通过螺旋输送机出料。

Step 2: During the pressing stage, the piston squeezes the kitchen waste materials into the organic matter chamber at a pressure of 100-500 kgf/cm², and the organic matter and water are separated from the garbage body in the form of slurry to become a "wet base". The wet base slurry is discharged through a screw conveyor.



---


# 厨余垃圾处理设备：压榨干湿分离

# Kitchen waste treatment equipment: squeezing dry and wet separation

# 第三步：出渣阶段

出渣口打开同时主活塞将无机干质物料推送出有机质提取腔外，随后通过带式输送机外输。干基中含水率50%以下。

# Step 3: Slag discharge stage

the slag discharge port opens and the main piston pushes the inorganic dry materials out of the organic matter extraction chamber, and then transports them out through the belt conveyor. The moisture content in the dry basis is less than 50%.



---

厨余垃圾处理设备：压榨干湿分离

# 厨余垃圾处理设备：螺旋挤压脱水

# Kitchen waste treatment equipment: spiral extrusion dehydration

- 入料口宽敞，防止物料在进料段堵塞、架桥。The feed inlet is spacious to prevent the material from clogging and bridging in the feed section.
- 较大的螺旋轴套，使得杂质与挤压腔室的接触面积大，脱水效果好。The larger spiral sleeve makes the contact area between the impurities and the extrusion chamber large, and the dehydration effect is good.
- 圆柱型的滤网设计可以防止纤维物料的堵塞。通过螺旋轴对物料的不断推送与出口端轴向封堵压锥所形成的封闭压力空间，进而形成压榨力。The cylindrical filter screen design can prevent the clogging of fiber materials. The closed pressure space formed by the continuous pushing of the material by the spiral shaft and the axial blocking pressure cone at the outlet end forms the pressing force.



---


# 厨余垃圾处理设备：螺旋挤压脱水

# Kitchen waste treatment equipment: spiral extrusion dehydration

- 压榨机的出口端自始自终都被压锥顶着，出渣含水率平均低。The outlet end of the press is always supported by the pressure cone, and the moisture content of the slag is low on average.
- 压锥的背压压力的调节是通过对背压压力的调节实现的。一方面可以实现处理能力与压榨干度互不影响，另一方面可以确保出料干度的恒定。The back pressure of the pressure cone is adjusted by adjusting the back pressure. On the one hand, it can achieve the goal that the processing capacity and the pressing dryness do not affect each other, and on the other hand, it can ensure the constant dryness of the discharge material.
- 应力循环分解，显著降低损耗和故障率。Stress cycle decomposition, significantly reducing losses and failure rates.



---


# 厨余垃圾处理设备：杂物装载

# Food waste treatment equipment: sundries loading

厨余垃圾经各工艺段分选的杂物经输送机输送至垃圾压缩机，由压缩机将杂物压缩填装入杂物转运箱内，当垃圾填装满转运箱后由自卸式钩臂车垃圾杂物运输至垃圾焚烧发电厂。

The food waste is sorted out in each process section and transported to the garbage compressor via a conveyor. The compressor compresses the debris and fills it into a debris transfer box. When the transfer box is filled with garbage, the garbage and debris are transported to the waste incineration power plant by a dump truck.

# 有机垃圾厌氧发酵面临的挑战

# Challenges of anaerobic fermentation for organic waste

- 挥发性物质转化为沼气的转化率为 70%，出渣率 30%，有机碳转化为沼气的转化率 20-30%，难降解有机物，转化率低；The conversion rate of volatile substances into biogas is 70%, the slag discharge rate is 30%, and the conversion rate of organic carbon into biogas is 20-30%. It is difficult to degrade refractory organics and the conversion rate is low;
- 氨氮是厌氧消化重要抑制因子，其中游离氨被认为是具有生物毒性的抑制因子；Ammonia nitrogen is an important inhibitory factor for anaerobic digestion, among which free ammonia is considered to be an inhibitory factor with biological toxicity;
- 游离氨浓度受温度、pH值影响明显，温度增高和pH升高，都会导致游离氨浓度增加；The concentration of free ammonia is significantly affected by temperature and pH. Increased temperature and pH will lead to increased free ammonia concentration;
- 因此高温厌氧往往比中温厌氧对氨氮更加敏感，但厌氧生化系统复杂，抑制因子很多；Therefore, high-temperature anaerobic digestion is often more sensitive to ammonia nitrogen than medium-temperature anaerobic digestion, but the anaerobic biochemical system is complex and has many inhibitory factors;

---

有机垃圾厌氧发酵面临的挑战
# Challenges of anaerobic fermentation for organic waste

- 沼气发电时，可采用湿法脱硫/生物脱硫+干法脱硫的工艺；生物脱硫需要向沼气中通入适量的空气，提供H₂S氧化所需的O₂，压缩天然气技术指标要求O₂含量≤0.5%，因此沼气提纯时，宜采用湿法脱硫 +干法脱硫的工艺；

When using biogas for power generation, the process of wet desulfurization/biological desulfurization + dry desulfurization can be used; biological desulfurization requires the introduction of an appropriate amount of air into the biogas to provide the O₂ required for H₂S oxidation. The technical indicators of compressed natural gas require that the O₂ content is ≤0.5%. Therefore, when purifying biogas, the process of wet desulfurization + dry desulfurization should be used;
- 湿法脱硫碱液Na₂CO₃，与H₂S中和反应生成NaHCO₃及NaHS，该过程会消耗大量碱液并排放大量废水；脱硫液中添加络合铁，可将HS⁻还原成单质S，同时可实现碱液再生，减少碱液消耗量及废水排放量；

The wet desulfurization alkali solution Na₂CO₃ reacts with H₂S to generate NaHCO₃ and NaHS. This process consumes a large amount of alkali solution and discharges a large amount of wastewater; adding complex iron to the desulfurization solution can reduce HS⁻ to elemental S, and at the same time can achieve alkali solution regeneration, reducing alkali solution consumption and wastewater discharge;

-104-

---

有机垃圾厌氧发酵面临的挑战
# Challenges of anaerobic fermentation for organic waste

- 干法脱硫剂，采用活性炭或氧化铁，活性炭吸附饱和后，废活性炭为危险废物，需委托具有危废处理资质的单位处置；
Dry desulfurization agent uses activated carbon or iron oxide. After the activated carbon is saturated with adsorption, the waste activated carbon is hazardous waste and needs to be entrusted to a unit with hazardous waste treatment qualifications for disposal;
- 氧化铁饱和后为 Fe₂S₃.H₂O 属于一般工业固废，通入空气后可再生循环利用；
After saturated iron oxide is Fe₂S₃.H₂O, it belongs to general industrial solid waste and can be recycled after being ventilated;
- 沼液作为液态肥产品出售时，应取得工商、农委等相关部门许可，并满足相关规范的指标要求；
When biogas slurry is sold as a liquid fertilizer product, it should obtain permission from relevant departments such as the Industry and Commerce Commission and the Agricultural Commission, and meet the index requirements of relevant specifications;

-105-

---

有机垃圾厌氧发酵面临的挑战
# Challenges of anaerobic fermentation for organic waste

- 当前厌氧消化沼液通常采用膜处理作为深度处理工艺，但随之产生的浓缩液形成二次污染问题；
At present, anaerobic digestion of biogas slurry usually uses membrane treatment as a deep treatment process, but the resulting concentrated liquid forms a secondary pollution problem;
- 近年来反硝化滤池、砂滤反硝化等非膜深度处理工艺逐步得到应用，并取得了较好的处理效果；
In recent years, non-membrane deep treatment processes such as denitrification filter and sand filter denitrification have been gradually applied and have achieved good treatment results;
- 沼渣作为有机肥产品出售时，应取得工商、农委等相关部门许可，并满足相关规范的指标要求；当用作绿化基质土时，应符合国家或地方相关标准。
When biogas residue is sold as an organic fertilizer product, it should obtain permission from relevant departments such as the Industry and Commerce Commission and the Agricultural Commission, and meet the index requirements of relevant specifications; when used as greening substrate soil, it should comply with relevant national or local standards.

-106-

---

有机垃圾厌氧发酵面临的挑战
# Challenges of anaerobic fermentation for organic waste

- 湿垃圾是固液混合物，其输送设备选型至关重要，输送过程中的卡堵问题、密闭性、故障率、输送效率等是选型应考虑的主要因素；Wet garbage is a mixture of solid and liquid, and the selection of its conveying equipment is crucial. The blockage problem, airtightness, failure rate, and conveying efficiency during the conveying process are the main factors that should be considered in the selection;
- 目前应用较多的湿垃圾输送设备，通常采用无轴螺旋输送机，其优点是密闭性好，输送效率相对较高，但其缺点无法输送沥水，需单独设置泵送系统；斗式提升机可实现固液同步输送，但物料中杂质较多时容易出现卡堵故障；Currently, the most used wet garbage conveying equipment adopts a shaftless screw conveyor, which has the advantages of good airtightness and relatively high conveying efficiency, but its disadvantage is that it cannot convey drain water and requires a separate pumping system; bucket elevators can realize synchronous conveying of solids and liquids, but when there are more impurities in the material, it is prone to blockage failure;
- 湿垃圾浆液具有含固率高、粘度大、易堵塞等特点，其输送设备的选型应结合工艺需求合理确定。Wet garbage slurry has the characteristics of high solid content, high viscosity, and easy clogging. The selection of its conveying equipment should be reasonably determined in combination with process requirements.

-107-

---

有机垃圾厌氧发酵面临的挑战
# Challenges of anaerobic fermentation for organic waste

- 预处理后进入厌氧系统物料中浆液，无机质含量不易过高，否则易引起后续厌氧系统杂质过高而出现卡堵问题；The inorganic content of the slurry in the material entering the anaerobic system after pretreatment should not be too high, otherwise it may cause the subsequent anaerobic system to have too high impurities and blockage problems;
- 破碎或制浆处理，是湿垃圾预处理重要环节，进料粒径不能太大；Crushing or pulping is an important part of wet garbage pretreatment, and the feed particle size cannot be too large;
- 厌氧消化进料中，长期过高的油脂将引起厌氧系统长链脂肪酸抑制；In the anaerobic digestion feed, long-term excessive oil will cause the long-chain fatty acids in the anaerobic system to inhibit;
- 厌氧消化过程中，C的转化率很低，急需突破性提高C的转化率；In the anaerobic digestion process, the conversion rate of C is very low, and it is urgent to improve the conversion rate of C;



---

厌氧发酵总结 Anaerobic digestion Summary

- 厌氧发酵功能：有机污染物的稳定化，同时获取甲烷能源 Anaerobic fermentation function: stabilization of organic pollutants and acquisition of methane energy
- 干法发酵，搅拌困难，产沼率低，C转化率小 Dry fermentation, difficult to stir, low biogas production rate, low C conversion rate
- 无机物容易卡堵、分层 Inorganic matter is easy to block and stratify
- 湿法发酵，搅拌容易，占地大，C转化率不大（&#x3C;8%） Wet fermentation, easy to stir, large area, low C conversion rate
- 过度酸化难于控制，甲烷浓度偏低（CH₄&#x3C;65%，CO₂>35%） Excessive acidification is difficult to control, and the methane concentration is low
- 城市有机垃圾发酵，沼渣、沼液无出路； Municipal organic waste fermentation, biogas residue and biogas liquid have no way out;

-109-

---

厌氧发酵总结 Anaerobic digestion Summary

- 农业口的畜禽粪便、秸秆厌氧发酵比较普遍，沼渣、沼液进农田；Anaerobic fermentation of livestock and poultry manure and straw is common in agriculture, and biogas residue and liquid biogas enter farmland;
- 发酵过程中 ， 过程控制很困难 ， 发酵罐是个黑箱 ， 无法快速调整参数；During the fermentation process, process control is difficult, the fermentation tank is a black box, and parameters cannot be adjusted quickly;
- 两相系统的发酵负荷和产气率相比单相系统最大提高接近 50％；The fermentation load and gas production rate of the two-phase system are increased by nearly 50% compared with the single-phase system;
- 氨氮回收难度大；Ammonia nitrogen recovery is difficult;
- 预处理很难 ， 颗粒大 ， 废塑料 、 纤维形成浮层 ， 砂砾沉降堵塞管道；Pretreatment is difficult, the particles are large, waste plastics and fibers form a floating layer, and gravel settles and blocks the pipeline;

-110-

---

## 📄 来源文件: Chapter 5. Aerobic composting of organic wastes.md

固废处置及资源化
# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 5. Aerobic composting of organic wastes

# 好氧堆肥技术

Yang Lei

2026-03-23

---

堆肥概述 Composting Overview

• 一种生物转换技术： 微生物新陈代谢作用 ， 分解代谢和合成代谢； A biological conversion technology: microbial metabolism, decomposition and anabolism;

• 自然界的许多微生物具有氧化、分解有机物的能力。利用微生物的这种能力，处理可降解的有机固体废物，达到无害化和资源化，是有机固体废物处理利用的一条重要途径。Many microorganisms in nature have the ability to oxidize and decompose organic matter. Using this ability of microorganisms to treat degradable organic solid waste and achieve harmlessness and resource utilization is an important way to treat and utilize organic solid waste.



---

堆肥概述 Composting Overview

# 微生物类型：Microorganism types

- 原核细胞 Prokaryotic cell：细菌、蓝绿藻；bacteria, blue-green algae;
- 真核细胞 Eukaryotic cell：原生生物；藻类；真菌：霉菌、酵母菌: protozoa; algae; fungi: mold, yeast;

# 堆肥原料：Composting raw materials:

- 禽畜粪便 、 秸秆、绿化废物、湿垃圾 、餐厨垃圾、厌氧发酵残渣等；Livestock manure, straw, greening waste, wet garbage, kitchen waste, anaerobic fermentation residues, etc.;



---

堆肥化 Composting

# 定义 Definition

• 在控制条件下，利用自然界广泛分布的细菌、放线菌、真菌等微生物，使可被生物降解的有机物转化为稳定的腐殖质的生物化学过程。Under controlled conditions, the biochemical process of converting biodegradable organic matter into stable humus using microorganisms such as bacteria, actinomycetes, and fungi that are widely distributed in nature.

# 定义强调 Definition Emphasis

- • 作为堆肥化原料是可生物降解的固体废物；The raw materials for composting are biodegradable solid waste;
- • 堆肥过程是在人工控制条件下进行，不同于卫生填埋、废物自然腐烂与腐化；The composting process is carried out under artificial control conditions, which is different from sanitary landfill and natural decay and decomposition of waste;
- • 堆肥化实质是生物化学过程，堆肥产品对环境无害，即废物达到相对稳定；Composting is essentially a biochemical process, and the compost product is harmless to the environment, that is, the waste is relatively stable.

# 堆肥化产物成为堆肥

- 一种深褐色、质地疏松、有泥土气味的物质，类似于腐殖质土壤，故也称为“腐殖土”； A dark brown, loose substance with an earthy smell, similar to humus soil, so it is also called "humus soil";
- 一种具有一定肥效的土壤改良剂和调节剂； A soil conditioner and regulator with a certain fertilizer effect;

堆肥的产品质量及卫生要求

# Product quality and hygiene requirements for composting

# 无害化卫生要求

Harmless sanitation requirements:

- 堆肥温度（静态堆肥工艺）：＞55℃持续5d以上
Composting temperature (static composting process): >55℃ for more than 5 days
- 蛔虫卵死亡率：95％～100％
Ascaris egg mortality rate
- 粪大肠菌值：10₋₁～10₋2
Fecal coliform value

• 堆肥堆温高，一般在55℃～60℃，极限可达80～90℃，堆制周期短，所以也称为高温快速堆肥。The composting temperature is high, generally between 55℃ and 60℃, and the limit can reach 80~90℃. The composting cycle is short, so it is also called high-temperature rapid composting.

# 堆肥化原理 Composting Principle

# 中温阶段 Medium temperature stage

C₅H₇NO₂ + 5O₂ → 5CO₂ + 2H₂O + NH₃ + heat

堆肥原料来源广泛且组成复杂，但影响其腐殖化过程的主要前驱体物质是相似的，糖类、酚类、醌类、蛋白质、氨基酸、脂肪酸分解产物及微生物代谢产物等，是形成腐殖质的核心物质和必需的反应底物。Compost raw materials come from a wide range of sources and have complex compositions, but the main precursor substances that affect their humification process are similar. Sugars, phenols, quinones, proteins, amino acids, fatty acid decomposition products and microbial metabolites are the core substances and necessary reaction substrates for the formation of humus.

---

堆肥化原理 Composting Principle

# 中温阶段 Medium temperature stage

- 初期，堆层呈中温 （15～45℃），嗜温性微生物活跃 ，温度不断上升。In the early stage, the pile is mesophilic (15-45℃), mesophilic microorganisms are active, and the temperature continues to rise.
- 嗜温性微生物包括：Mesophilic microorganisms include:

-11-

---

堆肥化原理 Composting Principle

# 高温阶段 High-temperature stage

- 堆温升至 45 ℃ 以上 ， 嗜热微生物； When the pile temperature rises above 45℃, thermophilic microorganisms;
- 可溶性有机物质继续分解，复杂有机物如半纤维素、纤维素和蛋白质也开始被强烈分解； Soluble organic matter continues to decompose, and complex organic matter such as hemicellulose, cellulose and protein also begin to be strongly decomposed;
- 温度升到 70℃ 以上时 ，大多数嗜热性微生物已不适应 ， 死亡或进入休眠； When the temperature rises above 70℃, most thermophilic microorganisms are no longer adapted, die or enter dormancy;
- 超过110℃的好氧堆肥，但推广应用案例很少或无； Aerobic composting above 110℃, but there are few or no cases of promotion and application;

-12-

堆肥化原理 Composting Principle

---

堆肥化原理 Composting Principle

# 腐熟阶段 （降温阶段）Maturity stage (cooling stage)

- 内源呼吸后期，较难分解有机物；In the late stage of endogenous respiration, it is more difficult to decompose organic matter;
- 微生物活性下降 ， 发热量减少 ， 温度下降 ， 嗜温性微生物又占优； The activity of microorganisms decreases, the heat generation decreases, the temperature drops, and mesophilic microorganisms become dominant;
- 腐殖质不断增多且稳定化，堆肥进入腐熟阶段，需氧量大大减少，含水率也降低；Humus continues to increase and stabilize, the compost enters the mature stage, the oxygen demand is greatly reduced, and the moisture content is also reduced;

好氧堆肥分类 Classification of aerobic composting

# 1. 按温度范围划分 By temperature range

- 中温堆肥： 15 ~45 ℃ ， 不能有效杀灭病原菌， 目前较少采用；

Medium temperature composting: 15~45℃, cannot effectively kill pathogens, and is rarely used;
- 高温堆肥: 一般在50~65℃，极限可达80~90℃，杀灭病菌，减少臭气产生，采用较多；

High temperature composting: generally 50~65℃, the maximum can reach 80~90℃, kills pathogens, reduces odor, and is widely used;

-15-

---

好氧堆肥分类

# Classification of aerobic composting

# ② 按堆肥中物料运动形式

According to the movement form of materials in composting

- 间歇式堆肥：一批一批地堆制；Intermittent composting: composting in batches;
- 连续式堆肥：机械连续进料、出料，周期短（3~7d），杀灭病原微生物，防异味，成品质量高等特点；Continuous composting: mechanical continuous feeding and discharging, short cycle (3~7d), killing pathogenic microorganisms, preventing odor, high quality of finished products, etc.

-16-

---


# 好氧堆肥分类

# ③ 按堆肥堆制方式

- 露天式堆肥：堆沤 Open-air composting: composting.
- 装置式堆肥：机械化程度高，堆肥时间短，占地面积小，环境条件好，质量可控可调

Device composting: high degree of mechanization, short composting time, small footprint, good environmental conditions, controllable and adjustable quality.



---

好氧堆肥工艺

# Aerobic composting process

- 传统采用厌氧露天堆积法，占地大，时间长；The traditional anaerobic open-air stacking method takes up a large area and takes a long time;
- 现代化堆肥生产一般采用好氧堆肥工艺；Modern composting production generally uses aerobic composting technology;
- 堆肥基本工序包括前处理、主发酵（一次发酵）、后发酵（二次发酵）、后处理、脱臭及贮存等工序组成；The basic composting process includes pre-treatment, main fermentation (primary fermentation), post-fermentation (secondary fermentation), post-treatment, deodorization and storage;

-18-

---

好氧堆肥工艺

# Aerobic composting process

# ① 前处理

• 以城市生活垃圾为堆肥原料时，前处理包括破碎、分选、筛分等工序，除去杂物，调整含水率，增大表面积；When urban domestic waste is used as compost raw material, pre-treatment includes crushing, sorting, screening and other processes to remove debris, adjust moisture content and increase surface area;

• 以家畜粪便、污泥等为堆肥原料时，前处理包括调整水分和碳氮比，或者添加菌种和酶制剂；When livestock manure, sludge and other materials are used as compost raw materials, pre-treatment includes adjusting moisture and carbon-nitrogen ratio, or adding bacteria and enzyme preparations;

-19-

---

好氧堆肥工艺

# ② 主发酵 （一次发酵）

- 在发酵仓内进行，通过翻堆搅拌或强制通风供氧 It is carried out in the fermentation chamber, and oxygen is supplied by turning the pile or forced ventilation.
- 中温阶段→高温阶段，嗜温菌→嗜热菌 Mesophilic stage → high temperature stage, mesophilic bacteria → thermophilic bacteria.
- 大部分有机物被降解 ， 病原菌被杀灭 ， 堆层温度开始下降 Most organic matter is degraded, pathogens are killed, and the temperature of the pile begins to drop.
- 将温度升高到开始降低为止的阶段为主发酵阶段 The stage from raising the temperature to the beginning of the temperature drop is the main fermentation stage.
- 以生活垃圾为主体的城市垃圾好氧堆肥化的主发酵期约为 10 ～ 1 2 d The main fermentation period of aerobic composting of urban garbage with domestic garbage as the main body is about 10 to 12 days.

-20-

---

好氧堆肥工艺

# ③ 后发酵 （二次发酵）Post-fermentation (secondary fermentation)

- 将主发酵尚未分解的有机物进一步分解，使之变成腐殖酸、氨基酸等比较稳定的有机物，得到完全成熟的堆肥制品； Further decompose the organic matter that has not been decomposed in the main fermentation to turn it into relatively stable organic matter such as humic acid and amino acids, and obtain fully mature compost products;
- 把物料堆积到1～2m高的堆层，通过自然通风和间歇性翻堆，进行后发酵，并应防止雨水流入； Pile the materials into a pile layer of 1 to 2 meters high, and carry out post-fermentation through natural ventilation and intermittent turning of the pile, and prevent rainwater from flowing in;
- 时间较长，通常在20～30d；The time is relatively long, usually 20 to 30 days;

-21-

---

好氧堆肥工艺 Aerobic composting process

# ④ 后处理 Post treatment

- 将经二次发酵，变细碎和变形，数量也有所减少（体积减少到50-70％），已成为粗堆肥 After secondary fermentation, it will become fine and deformed, and the quantity will also be reduced (the volume is reduced to 50-70%), and it has become coarse compost;
- 分选工序去除杂物 The sorting process removes impurities;
- 如生产精制堆肥等，则应进行再破碎过程 If refined compost is produced, the re-crushing process should be carried out;
- 也可在散装堆肥中加入N、P、K 添加剂生产复合肥，做成袋装产品 N, P, and K additives can also be added to bulk compost to produce compound fertilizers and make bagged products;
- 有时还需要固化造粒以利贮存 Sometimes solidification and granulation are also required for storage;

-22-

---

好氧堆肥工艺

# 好氧堆肥工艺

# ⑤ 脱臭

- 堆肥过程中，物料局部或某段时间内的厌氧会产生氨、硫化氢、甲基硫醇、胺类等臭气；During the composting process, the anaerobic process of the material in some parts or for a period of time will produce odors such as ammonia, hydrogen sulfide, methyl mercaptan, and amines;
- 除臭方法：化学除臭剂除臭；水、酸、碱溶液吸收法；熟堆肥、活性炭或沸石等吸附剂吸附法；臭氧氧化法；土壤脱臭过滤器 。 Deodorization methods: deodorization with chemical deodorizers; absorption by water, acid, or alkaline solutions; adsorption by adsorbents such as mature compost, activated carbon, or zeolite; ozone oxidation; soil deodorization filter.

-23-

---

好氧堆肥工艺 Aerobic composting process

# 贮存 Store

- 堆肥一般在春秋两季使用，夏冬两季生产的堆肥只能贮存，所以要建立可贮存 6 个月生产量的库房；

Compost is generally used in spring and autumn. Compost produced in summer and winter can only be stored, so a warehouse that can store 6 months of production should be built;
- 可直接堆存在二次发酵仓中 ， 或装入袋中；

It can be directly piled in the secondary fermentation warehouse or packed in bags;
- 贮存在干燥而透气的室内环境 。

Stored in a dry and breathable indoor environment.

筒仓式堆肥发酵仓 Silo composting fermentation chamber

# 1 筒仓式静态发酵仓 Silo-type static fermentation silo

- 呈单层圆筒形，堆积高度4～5米。堆肥物由仓顶经布料机进入仓内，顺序向下移动，由仓底的螺杆出料机出料；It is a single-layer cylindrical silo with a stacking height of 4-5m. The compost enters the silo from the top of the silo through the spreader, moves downward in sequence, and is discharged by the screw discharger at the bottom of the silo;
- 一次发酵时间>10~12天 One fermentation time> 10~12 days

- 呈多层条形，每层堆高不超过 1 米
- Multi-layer strip, each layer is no more than 1 meter high

- There is a closed door on each layer of the bed. While repeatedly cutting off the conveying, the closed door is opened to sequentially convey the raw materials downward

# Positive pressure aerobic composting of organic waste using membrane covering system

- 膜覆盖压力曝气 Membrane covered pressure aeration;
- 通过控制单元有效地鼓风，提高垃圾降解率 The control unit effectively blows air to improve the garbage degradation rate;
- 膜覆盖系统为整个堆体提供了统一的气候条件 The membrane covering system provides uniform climate conditions for the entire pile;
- 降解几乎所有的有机物 This process changes and degrades almost all organic matter;
- 高温灭菌和病毒 High temperature sterilization and virus



---


# 膜覆盖系统有机废物正压好氧堆肥

# Positive pressure aerobic composting of organic waste using membrane covering system

# ◆ 覆盖膜 Covering film

- 由特制的e-PTEF(膨胀聚四氟乙烯)膜组合而成，它被夹持在两层牢固的聚酯膜中间

It is composed of a special e-PTEF (expanded polytetrafluoroethylene) membrane, which is sandwiched between two layers of strong polyester membranes;
- e-PTEF膜上均布0.2μm孔径的微孔，是灰尘、气溶胶和微生物的有效物理屏障，阻止它们向外扩散

The e-PTEF membrane is evenly distributed with micropores with a pore size of 0.2μm, which is an effective physical barrier for dust, aerosols and microorganisms, preventing them from spreading outward;
- 在处理过程中，膜的内表面会生成一层冷凝水膜；尾气中大多数的臭气物质，如氨气、硫化氢、VOC等，都会溶解于水膜中。

During the treatment process, a layer of condensed water film will be generated on the inner surface of the membrane; most of the odorous substances in the exhaust gas, such as ammonia, hydrogen sulfide, VOC, etc., will dissolve in the water film.



---


# 膜覆盖系统有机废物正压好氧堆肥

# Positive pressure aerobic composting of organic waste using membrane covering system

# 通风 ventilation

- 采用中压、高压通风机向底座的通风沟鼓风；
Use medium-pressure and high-pressure fans to blow air into the ventilation grooves of the base;
- 覆盖膜具有增压作用，使布气更均匀，气流穿透力增强，所需通风量减少；
The covering film has a pressurizing effect, making the air distribution more uniform, enhancing the airflow penetration, and reducing the required ventilation volume;
- 一般用一台风机为一个料堆通风供氧；
Generally, one fan is used to ventilate and supply oxygen to a pile of materials;
- 处理量越大，通风布风系统越经济；
The larger the processing volume, the more economical the ventilation and air distribution system;



---


# 膜覆盖系统有机废物正压好氧堆肥

# Positive pressure aerobic composting of organic waste using membrane covering system

# 温度控制

- 设定控制温度，当测试值低于控制温度时，加大通风供氧，增强微生物活性，增加产热量。Set the control temperature. When the test value is lower than the control temperature, increase ventilation and oxygen supply to enhance microbial activity and increase heat production.
- 当测试值高于控制温度时，减少通风供氧，降低微生物活性。When the test value is higher than the control temperature, reduce ventilation and oxygen supply to reduce microbial activity.

# 影响堆肥化的主要因素

# The main factors affecting composting

- 对于高温二次发酵堆肥工艺来说，通风供氧、堆料含水率、温度是最主要的发酵条件；
For high-temperature secondary fermentation composting process, ventilation and oxygen supply, moisture content of pile, and temperature are the most important fermentation conditions;
- 有机质含量、粒度、C/N、C/P、pH也有影响；
Organic matter content, particle size, C/N, C/P, and pH also have an impact;



---


# 影响堆肥化的主要因素

# ① 通风 ventilation

- 目的：微生物分解有机物；带走水气，干化物料。Purpose: Microorganisms decompose organic matter; remove moisture and dry materials.
- 方式：自然通风、通风管通风、斗式装载机及翻推机、风机强制通风。Methods: Natural ventilation, ventilation pipe ventilation, bucket loaders and tippers, fan forced ventilation.
- 通风供氧和带走水气两者有关联，但两种目的、需空气量不同。有时能同时满足，有时后者需空气量更多，如污泥粪便等含水率较高物料堆肥。
Ventilation oxygen supply and removal of moisture are related, but the two purposes and air volume required are different. Sometimes both can be met at the same time, and sometimes the latter requires more air, such as composting materials with high moisture content such as sludge and feces.



---


# 影响堆肥化的主要因素

# The main factors affecting composting

# ② 含水率

- 水分是微生物生命活动的介质，影响物料孔隙度和透气性；
Water is the medium for microbial life activities and affects the porosity and air permeability of materials;
- 适宜水分45%～60%；
Suitable water content is 45% to 60%;
- 不同的有机废物的含水率相差很大，通常要把不同种类的堆肥物质混合在一起。
The water content of different organic wastes varies greatly, and different types of compost materials are usually mixed together.
- 太低时，可配以粪水或污泥调节；
When it is too low, it can be adjusted with manure or sludge;



---

影响堆肥化的主要因素

# The main factors affecting composting

# ③ 温度 Temperature

- 影响微生物活动和堆肥工艺过程重要因素 Important factors affecting microbial activity and composting process;
- 一般认为最佳温度在50～60℃之间 It is generally believed that the optimal temperature is between 50 and 60℃;
- 考虑杀灭病菌和虫卵，温度大于55℃并保持几天 Considering the killing of pathogens and insect eggs, the temperature should be greater than 55℃ and maintained for several days;
- 对于一个管理良好的堆肥系统，温度可以根据需要进行控制，例如如果废物含有致的病的病原菌，那么杀灭这些病菌将是堆肥工艺过程中的一项重要内容。对于那些不含致病菌的堆肥原料诸如牲畜的粪便等，对温度的控制只要求杀灭植物的致病微生物和杂草的种子。For a well-managed composting system, the temperature can be controlled as needed. For example, if the waste contains pathogenic bacteria that cause disease, then killing these pathogens will be an important part of the composting process. For composting materials that do not contain pathogens, such as livestock manure, temperature control is only required to kill plant pathogens and weed seeds.

-47-

---


# 影响堆肥化的主要因素

# The main factors affecting composting

# ④ 孔隙结构

- 孔隙度决定了堆体的通风效率、密度和保水性；
Porosity determines the ventilation efficiency, density and water retention of the pile;
- 孔隙过小会影响氧气在堆体中的扩散，抑制氧供给；
Porosity that is too small will affect the diffusion of oxygen in the pile and inhibit oxygen supply;
- 孔隙过大虽有利于通风供氧，但会使颗粒比表面积减小，导致总降解面积变小，延缓降解速度；
Although pores that are too large are conducive to ventilation and oxygen supply, they will reduce the specific surface area of particles, resulting in a smaller total degradation area and slowing down the degradation rate;
- 适宜的孔隙结构对堆肥腐殖化进程具有积极的贡献；
Appropriate pore structure has a positive contribution to the humification process of composting;



---


# 影响堆肥化的主要因素

# The main factors affecting composting

# ④ 其它因素

# Other factors

- 有机质含量 Organic matter content;
- 粒度、表面积大小、适宜12～60 mm; Particle size, surface area, suitable 12-60 mm;
- C/N比（4～30）:1； C/N ratio
- C/P比（75～150）:1； C/P ratio
- pH 7.5～8.5； pH 7.5～8.5;



---


# 堆肥腐熟度 Compost maturity

- 指成品堆肥的稳定程度；Refers to the stability of finished compost;
- 工程上衡量堆肥反应完成的信号；In engineering, it is a signal to evaluate the completion degree of composting reaction;
- 农业上是堆肥质量的指标；In agriculture, it is an indicator of compost quality;



---

堆肥腐熟度评价方法

# Evaluation method of compost maturity

- 直观经验法：茶褐色或黑褐色，无恶臭，质地松散，具有泥土芳香气味

Intuitive empirical method: brown or dark brown, no bad smell, loose texture, with earthy aroma; The compost should be tea-brown or dark brown in color, free from foul odor, loose in texture, and have an earthy aromatic smell.
- 淀粉测试法：该方法通过检测堆肥中淀粉残留量来判断腐熟度，淀粉含量越低，腐熟度越高。常用碘液（如碘化钾溶液）进行显色反应，未完全分解的淀粉会呈现蓝黑色。

This method evaluates compost maturity by detecting the residual starch content—the lower the starch content, the higher the degree of decomposition. A colorimetric reaction using iodine solution (e.g., potassium iodide) is commonly employed, where incompletely decomposed starch develops a blue-black color.
- 耗氧速率法：通过测定堆肥样品中微生物的氧气消耗速率来评估堆肥的腐熟度和生物稳定性。堆肥过程中，微生物分解有机物需要消耗氧气，腐熟度越高，微生物活性越低，耗氧速率也随之下降。因此，耗氧速率可作为堆肥稳定性的重要指标。

This method evaluates compost stability by measuring microbial activity through oxygen consumption rates. A mature compost exhibits low oxygen consumption rates due to diminished microbial decomposition activity.

# 施用有机肥改善土壤结构

# Applying organic fertilizers to improve soil structure

- 土壤腐殖质得到补充和更新；Soil humus is replenished and renewed;
- 改变了土壤中胶体的性质； The properties of colloids in the soil are changed;
- 土壤干燥过程中板结紧实程度降低；The compaction degree of soil is reduced during drying;
- 单位体积内的土壤重量减轻；The weight of soil per unit volume is reduced;
- 土体的孔隙度提高； The porosity of the soil is increased;
- 相应地改善了土体的通透性能； The permeability of the soil is improved accordingly;
- 调节了土体的水、肥、气、热比例；The proportion of water, fertilizer, air and heat in the soil is adjusted.
- 土壤性能变好了，作物产量也得以提高；The soil performance is improved, and the crop yield is also increased;

# Applying organic fertilizer to improve soil biological activity

有机肥副作用

# Side Effects of Organic Fertilizer

- 对于含有浓度较高的有毒有害物质，包括重金属和有机污染物的堆肥，尤其长期施用这种堆肥的土壤会积累这些有毒有害物质；

For compost containing high concentrations of toxic and hazardous substances, including heavy metals and organic pollutants, especially long-term application of such compost in the soil will accumulate these toxic and hazardous substances;
- 应严格控制堆肥产品中的有毒有害物质数量，并对施用堆肥后土地的重金属含量进行跟踪监测；

The amount of toxic and hazardous substances in compost products should be strictly controlled, and the heavy metal content of the land after the application of compost should be tracked and monitored.

固态物发酵系统 Solid matter fermentation system

# 二次发酵 Two-stage fermentation

- 圆形翻堆槽 15~17d Round compost turning trough
- ❖ 每条每天至少翻堆2次 Each windrow must be turned at least twice daily.
- ❖ 采用2套骑跨式翻堆机 Two straddle-type compost turners are employed.
- ❖ 生物菌种会自然升温约第三天温度会渐渐上升，最高可升至80度以上，将有机物体内水份转化为蒸气，在二次发酵系统中控制通气量、温度与水分 The microbial inoculum will naturally generate heat, with temperatures gradually rising around the third day and potentially reaching over 80°C. This process converts moisture within the organic matter into vapor. In the secondary fermentation system, aeration rate, temperature, and moisture content are strictly controlled.

# 固态物发酵系统 Solid matter fermentation system

# 二次发酵 Two-stage fermentation

# 静置腐熟 Standing and composting

经过7-14天静置腐熟，即可成为成品有机肥料出售 (C/N=15-20)，种子发芽率90%以上。

After 7-14 days of static maturation, the material becomes marketable organic fertilizer with a C/N ratio of 15-20 and a seed germination rate exceeding 90%.

有机肥分选包装系统
# Organic fertilizer sorting and packaging system

经过二次发酵后的熟成肥首先经过离心式筛选机，除去残留在肥料中的塑料类及颗粒较大的固体物，然后进行计量、包装、入库。

After secondary fermentation, the mature fertilizer first passes through a centrifugal screening machine to remove plastics and larger solid particles remaining in the fertilizer, and then is measured, packaged, and stored.

# 工作原理

辅料通过皮带输送机送入一体化设备进出料区，鸡粪由车辆卸入进出料区下沉料斗，外部卷帘门关闭，内部卷帘门开启，机器人到进出料区取料送入到微生物反应器层层敷设，然后进行混料匀翻发酵，同样完成其他区域的布料和翻抛。相应堆体物料发酵完成后，机器人定位到相应位置取料送至进出料区的出料位，由刮板输送机将物料送出设备外的总出料皮带，送入陈化车间。输送过程自动化。曝气过程中，机器人退回至进出料区，避免长期的高温高湿和腐蚀环境，内隔断门关闭，启动强制通风曝气充氧和引风除臭。

# 有机垃圾好氧堆肥过程中C和N保留方法

# Methods for retaining C and N during aerobic composting of organic waste

# 碳损失途径

# Carbon emission pathway

- 60%~70%：被微生物分解后以CO₂的形式损失 Lost in the form of CO₂ after being decomposed by microorganisms；
- 0.39%~1.24% ：因局部厌氧产生CH₄ 损失 CH₄ loss due to local anaerobic production；
- 0.3%~0.6%：随渗滤液流出造成的淋溶损失 Leaching loss caused by leachate outflow；

# 有机垃圾好氧堆肥过程中C和N保留方法

# Methods for retaining C and N during aerobic composting of organic waste

| C保留方法                        | 相关研究                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 调节堆肥参数                       | 提高C/N比能减小C损失率，但过高会导致堆肥对植物的毒害                                                                                                          |
| Adjust composting parameters | Increasing the C/N ratio can reduce the C loss rate, but too high a ratio can cause composting to be toxic to plants;                 |
|                              | 通风速率为 0.2 m · min⁻¹·m⁻³时，既能达到腐熟又能减少碳素损失                                                                                               |
|                              | When the ventilation rate is 0.2 m · min⁻¹·m⁻³, it can achieve both maturity and carbon loss.                                         |
| 添加调理剂                        | 在实验室条件下添加2%\~5%的木质素能促进腐殖质的生成                                                                                                          |
| Add conditioning agents      | Adding 2% to 5% lignin can promote the formation of humus under laboratory conditions;                                                |
|                              | 添加蘑菇渣可有效提高落叶与鸡粪堆肥的最终产品中腐殖质的含量                                                                                                         |
|                              | Adding mushroom residue can effectively increase the humus content in the final product of leaf litter and chicken manure composting. |
| 接种微生物菌剂                      | 链霉菌等微生物能有效促进堆肥的腐殖化进程减少碳素损失                                                                                                            |
| Inoculating microorganisms   | Microorganisms such as Streptomyces can effectively promote the humification process of compost and reduce carbon loss.               |
|                              | 加入高温放线菌能减少C的矿化损失                                                                                                                      |
|                              | Adding thermophilic actinomycetes can reduce the mineralization loss of C.                                                            |

# Methods for retaining C and N during aerobic composting of organic waste

# N emission pathways

- The main forms of N in compost: ammonium nitrogen, nitrate nitrogen, organic nitrogen
- The main forms of nitrogen loss during composting include: conversion to nitrate nitrogen (NO₃⁻) through nitrification; volatilization loss as ammonia (NH₃); immobilization as ammonium (NH₄⁺) compounds in the compost matrix.

Main pathway: Volatilization in the form of NH₃, accounting for 44%~99%.

# 堆肥化存在的问题

# Challenges of composting

- 好氧堆肥，C、N转化为二氧化碳和氮气，损失极大。应发展抑制C、N损失的好氧堆肥方法；
Aerobic composting converts C and N into carbon dioxide and nitrogen, resulting in huge losses. Aerobic composting methods that inhibit C and N losses should be developed;
- 堆肥工程处理效率低，操作过程中易产生恶臭，影响周围的环境，其工艺条件也较难控制，设备投资大、处理量小；
The composting process exhibits low treatment efficiency and is prone to generating malodors during operation, adversely impacting the surrounding environment. The process parameters are difficult to control, requiring substantial equipment investment despite limited processing capacity.
- 堆肥的生产设施一般都按照就近的原则建在市内，操作过程会产生恶臭，对环境卫生不利，必须投入较多资金装配排臭设施；
Composting production facilities are typically located in urban areas following the proximity principle, composting facilities inevitably produce foul odors during operation, posing sanitation concerns and necessitating significant investment in deodorization systems.



---


# 堆肥化存在的问题

# Challenges of composting

- 堆肥的质量和市场问题较大；
Compost quality and market viability face significant challenges
- 有效成分含量低，杂质多；
The content of effective ingredients is low and there are many impurities;
- 堆肥中N、P、K混合含量通常都很低，很少达到3%
The mixed content of N, P and K in compost is usually very low, rarely reaching 3%;
- 我国垃圾堆肥中N、P、K含量低于发展中国家平均含量，比发达国家肥效更低；
The content of N, P and K in China’s garbage compost is lower than the average content in developing countries, and the fertilizer efficiency is lower than that in developed countries;



---


# 堆肥化存在的问题

# Challenges of composting

- 由庭院修剪物、果品蔬菜加工废弃物、养殖场动物粪便和酿造厂废弃物作为原料而获得的有机肥料方可用于农业生产；
- Organic fertilizers derived from specific feedstocks—including yard trimmings, fruit/vegetable processing waste, livestock manure, and brewery byproducts—are approved for agricultural applications.
- 生活垃圾混合堆肥只能用于城市园林、沙漠、盐碱地、海边滩涂绿化和森林植被保护；
- Compost produced from mixed municipal solid waste is restricted to non-food production uses, such as: urban landscaping; desert/alkali soil reclamation; coastal tidal flat rehabilitation; forest vegetation conservation.

# 固废处置及资源化

# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 6.1 Pyrolysis Overview

# 热解概述

Yang Lei

2026-03-30

---

热解 Pyrolysis

# 定义：

利用有机物的热不稳定性，在无氧或缺氧条件下对其进行加热蒸馏，使有机物产生热裂解，经冷凝后形成各种新的气体、液体和固体，从中提取燃料油、油脂和燃料气的过程。

Definition: This process utilizes the thermal instability of organic matter, subjecting it to heating and distillation under anaerobic or oxygen-deficient conditions to induce thermal decomposition. The organic matter undergoes pyrolysis, and after condensation, various new gases, liquids, and solids are formed, from which fuel oil, greases, and fuel gas are extracted.

-3-

---

热解 Pyrolysis

# 有机固体废物

热量 无O2或缺O2 → 可燃气 + 液态油 + 固体燃料 + 炉渣

Solid organic waste     Heat  Without O2     Gas  Liquid oil  Solid fuel                Slag

- 可燃气（煤气）：包括C1～5烃类、氢和CO气体；
Combustible gas (coal gas): including C1-5 hydrocarbons, hydrogen and CO gas;
- 液态油（煤油）：包括C25烃类、乙酸、丙酮、甲醇等液态燃料；
Liquid oil (kerosene): including C25 hydrocarbons, acetic acid, acetone, methanol and other liquid fuels;
- 固体燃料（煤炭）：包括含纯碳和聚合高分子含碳物；
Solid fuel (coal): including pure carbon and polymerized high molecular carbon-containing substances;

-4-

---

# 热解过程及产物

# Pyrolysis process and products

| 裂解      | 热解  | 混合气体 |
| ------- | --- | ---- |
| 热分解     | 轻油  | 蒸馏   |
| 轻油      | 热解  |      |
| 有机固体废弃物 | 热分解 | J焦油  |
| 蒸馏      | 焦油  |      |
| 裂解      | 再聚合 | 焦类物质 |


---

热解过程及产物

随着热解温度升高，物料依次经历干燥、干馏和气体生成等不同阶段。As the pyrolysis temperature increases, the material undergoes different stages such as drying, distillation and gas generation.

200℃，水分逐渐以物理蒸发的形式析出 At 200°C, moisture is gradually released through physical evaporation.

250-500℃，物料发生干馏，经历内在水的析出、脱氧、脱硫、CO₂析出等过程，物料中纤维素、蛋白质、脂肪等大分子有机物裂解为小分子量的气体、液体和固态含碳化合物。At 250-500°C, the material undergoes dry distillation, involving processes such as: release of bound water, deoxygenation, desulfurization, CO₂ evolution. During this stage, macromolecular organic compounds (e.g., cellulose, proteins, fats) in the material thermally decompose into: low-molecular-weight gases (e.g., CH₄, CO, H₂); liquid fractions (e.g., bio-oil, tar); solid carbon-rich compounds (e.g., char, coke).

-6-

---

热解过程及产物

# Pyrolysis process and products

- 500-1200 ℃时，干馏过程产物进一步裂解，液态和固态化合物裂解为H₂、CO、CO₂、CH₄等气态产物。At 500-1200°C, the products of dry distillation undergo further cracking, with liquid and solid compounds decomposing into gaseous products such as H₂, CO, CO₂, and CH₄.
- 热解过程，中间产物存在两种变化趋势：一方面大分子变成小分子甚至气体裂解；另一方面小分子聚合成大分子。During the pyrolysis process, intermediate products exhibit two opposing transformation trends. Cracking of macromolecules: large organic compounds break down into lighter fractions through thermal decomposition, generating small molecules or gases; Polymerization of small molecules: reactive fragments recombine to form heavier compounds via secondary reactions, forming macromolecules.
- 裂解反应、脱氢反应、加氢反应、缩合反应、桥键反应。Pyrolysis reaction, dehydrogenation reaction, hydrogenation reaction, condensation reaction, bridge bond reaction.

-7-

---


# 热解特点

# Pyrolysis characteristics

- 热解过程一般在400-800℃的条件下进行，通过加热使固体物质挥发液化或分解。产物通常包括气体、液体和固体，其含量跟热解工艺和反应参数（温度、压力等）有关。The pyrolysis process is generally carried out at 400-800℃, and solid substances are volatilized, liquefied or decomposed by heating. The products usually include gas, liquid and solid, and their content is related to the pyrolysis process and reaction parameters (temperature, pressure, etc.).
- 低温会产生较多的液体产物，而高温会使气态物质增多。慢速热解（炭化）需要在低温下以较慢的反应速率进行，使固体焦类物质的产量达到最大。Low temperature will produce more liquid products, while high temperature will increase gaseous substances. Slow pyrolysis (carbonization) needs to be carried out at a slower reaction rate at low temperature to maximize the yield of solid coke substances.
- 快速或闪式热解是为了使气体和液体产物的产量最大化。同时得到的气体产物具有适中的热值（15~21MJ/m³）。Rapid or flash pyrolysis is to maximize the yield of gas and liquid products. At the same time, the gas products obtained have a moderate calorific value (15~21MJ/m³).



---


# 热解特点

# Pyrolysis characteristics

- 热解过程产物包括可燃性气体、有机液体、固体残渣三大类。可燃性气体包括H₂、CH₄、CO、C₂H₄和其他少量高分子烃类气体。The products of the pyrolysis process include three categories: combustible gas, organic liquid, and solid residue. Combustible gas includes H₂、CH₄、CO、C₂H₄ and other small amounts of high molecular hydrocarbon gases.
- 热解过程产生的可燃性气体量大，特别是在温度较高的情況下，废物有机成分的50%以上都转化为气态产物。除少部分用于维持热解过程所需热量外，剩余气体可作为燃料输出。The amount of combustible gas produced during the pyrolysis process is large, especially at high temperatures, more than 50% of the organic components of the waste are converted into gaseous products. In addition to a small amount used to maintain the heat required for the pyrolysis process, the remaining gas can be output as fuel.
- 有机液体是一类复杂的混合物 ，主要包括有机酸、芳烃、焦油和其他高分子烃类油等，可作为燃料输出。Organic liquid is a complex mixture, mainly including organic acids, aromatics, tar and other high molecular hydrocarbon oils, which can be output as fuel.



---


# 热解特点

# Pyrolysis characteristics

- 固体残渣主要包括炭黑及灰渣。废物热解后，减容量大，残余炭渣较少。

Solid residues mainly include carbon black and ash. After the waste is pyrolyzed, the volume reduction is large and the residual carbon residue is small.
- 对于不同类型的固体废物，热解过程产生的气态、液态和固态产物的成分和比例不同。从开始热解到热解结束的整个过程中，有机物都处在一个复杂的化学反应过程中，不同的温度区间所进行的反应不同，产物的组成也不同。

For different types of solid waste, the composition and proportion of gaseous, liquid and solid products produced during the pyrolysis process are different. From the beginning to the end of pyrolysis, organic matter is in a complex chemical reaction process. Different reactions are carried out in different temperature ranges, and the composition of the products is also different.



---


# 热解特点 Pyrolysis characteristics

- 在通常的反应温度下，高温热解过程以吸热反应为主，但有时也伴随着少量放热的二次反应。此外，当物料粒度较大时，由于达到热解温度所需传热时间长，扩散传质时间也长，整个过程更易发生许多二次反应，使产物组成及性能发生改变。

At normal reaction temperatures, the high-temperature pyrolysis process is mainly an endothermic reaction, but sometimes it is also accompanied by a small amount of exothermic secondary reactions. In addition, when the material particle size is large, due to the long heat transfer time required to reach the pyrolysis temperature and the long diffusion and mass transfer time, the whole process is more likely to have many secondary reactions, which will change the composition and properties of the product.
- 热解产物的产率取决于原料的化学结构、物理形态及热解的温度、速率、反应时间等参数。

The yield of pyrolysis products depends on the chemical structure and physical form of the raw materials and the parameters such as the pyrolysis temperature, rate, and reaction time.



---

热解废旧轮胎 Pyrolysis of waste tires

1 吨废轮胎通过热裂解 1 ton of waste tires undergoes thermal cracking to produce：

- • 0.35 吨炭黑 0.35 tons of carbon black;
- • 0.45 吨废橡胶油 0.45 tons of waste rubber oil;
- • 0.12 吨钢丝 0.12 tons of steel wire;
- • 部分可燃气体 partial combustible gas.

-14-

---

热解 vs 焚烧 Pyrolysis vs incineration

•  热解法和焚烧法：两个完全不同的过程。Pyrolysis and incineration: two completely different processes.

•  焚烧产物：二氧化碳和水。 Incineration products: carbon dioxide and water.

•  热解产物：可燃的低分子化合物，气态的有氢气、甲烷、一氧化碳；液态的有甲醇、丙酮、醋酸、乙醛等有机物及焦油、溶剂油等；固态的主要是焦炭或碳黑。Pyrolysis products: combustible low molecular weight compounds, gaseous ones include hydrogen, methane, carbon monoxide; liquid ones include methanol, acetone, acetic acid, acetaldehyde and other organic substances and tar, solvent oil, etc.; solid ones are mainly coke or carbon black.

•  焚烧是一个放热过程，而热解需要吸收大量热量。此外，焚烧产生的热量，量大可用于发电，量小的只可用于加热水或产生水蒸气，适于就近利用，而热解的产物是燃料油及燃料气，便于贮存和远距离运输。Incineration is an exothermic process, while pyrolysis requires the absorption of a large amount of heat. In addition, the heat generated by incineration can be used for power generation in large quantities, and small amounts can only be used to heat water or generate water vapor, which is suitable for local use, while the products of pyrolysis are fuel oil and fuel gas, which are convenient for storage and long-distance transportation.

-15-

# 固废处置及资源化

# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 6.2 Pyrolysis Process

# 热解工艺

Yang Lei

2026-03-30

---


# 温度和加热方式

| 直接加热            | Direct heating                                                     |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 间接加热            | Indirect heating                                                   |
| 低温热解（<600℃）     | Low temperature pyrolysis， biochar from agriculture waste          |
| 中温热解（600\~900℃） | Medium temperature pyrolysis, waste tire for liquid oil production |
| 高温热解（>900℃）     | High temperature pyrolysis                                         |



---


# 反应器及气化方式

# Reactor and gasification method

# 反应器

固定床、移动床、流化床和回转窑；

Reactors: fixed bed, moving bed, fluidized bed and rotary kiln;

# 热解产物聚集状态

气化方式、液化方式和碳化方式；

Aggregation state of pyrolysis products gasification, liquefaction and carbonization;

# 气化产品差异性

反应温度、升温速率、停留时间、催化剂不同，热解产品差异较大。

Gasification product differences: different reaction temperatures, heating rates, residence times and catalysts lead to large differences in pyrolysis products.



---


# 影响热解主要参数

# Main parameters affecting pyrolysis

1. # 热解温度

# Pyrolysis Temperature

- 热解温度与气体产量成正比；The pyrolysis temperature is proportional to the gas yield;
- 各种液体物质和固体残渣，随分解温度增加而减少；The various liquid substances and solid residues decrease with the increase of decomposition temperature;
- 温度较低，有机废物中大分子裂解成较多中小分子，油类含量相对较多；At lower temperatures, large molecules in organic waste are cracked into more small and medium molecules, and the oil content is relatively high;
- 温度升高，除大分子裂解外，许多中小分子发生二次裂解，C₅以下分子、CH₄及H₂成分增多，固态碳产率降低，但最终趋于一定值。As the temperature rises, in addition to the cracking of large molecules, many small and medium molecules undergo secondary cracking, and the molecules below C₅, CH₄ and H₂ components increase, and the solid carbon yield decreases, but eventually tends to a certain value.



---

# 影响热解主要参数

# Main parameters affecting pyrolysis

- 湿度 Moisture

• 水分对产气量和成分有明显影响； Moisture has a significant effect on gas production and composition;

• 水分在高温条件下，也是反应物，与物料发生反应，改变热解产物组成； Moisture is also a reactant under high temperature conditions, reacting with the material to change the composition of the pyrolysis product;

• 900℃，物料水分4-50%，气体按重量百分比计，从70%上升到86%。 At 900℃, the moisture content of the material is 4-50%, and the gas percentage by weight increases from 70% to 86%.

# 城市有机垃圾热解气的主要成分及含量

# Main composition of pyrolysis gas from municipal organic waste

| 热解气主要成分 | 组分含量%           | 热解气主要成分 | 组分含量%           |
| ------- | --------------- | ------- | --------------- |
| Gas     | Volume fraction | Gas     | Volume fraction |
| H₂      | 49\~54          | CO₂     | 14\~18          |
| CO      | 21\~24          | CH₄     | 6.5\~12         |



---


# 影响热解主要参数

# ③ 停留时间

• 反应物料完成热解时，所需要的在炉内停留时间；The time required for the reaction materials to stay in the furnace when the pyrolysis is completed;

• 物料粒度愈小，反应时间愈短；The smaller the particle size of the material, the shorter the reaction time;

• 物料分子结构愈复杂，反应时间愈长；The more complex the molecular structure of the material, the longer the reaction time;

• 反应温度愈高，反应物颗粒内外温度梯度愈大；The higher the reaction temperature, the greater the temperature gradient inside and outside the reactant particles;



---


# 影响热解主要参数

# Main parameters affecting pyrolysis

# ④ 加热速率

• 低温-低速，热解速度慢，有机物分子有足够时间在其最薄弱的接点处分解，重新结合为热稳定性固体，产物中固体产率增加；Low temperature-low speed mode, the pyrolysis speed is slow, the organic molecules have enough time to decompose at their weakest joints and recombine into thermally stable solids, and the solid yield in the product increases;

• 高温-高速，热解速度快，有机物分子结构全面裂解，生成低分子有机物，产物中气体组分增加。High temperature-high speed mode, the pyrolysis speed is fast, the organic molecular structure is completely cracked, low molecular weight organic matter is generated, and the gas component in the product increases.



---


# 影响热解主要参数

# ⑤ 反应器类型

# Types of Reactor

- 固定床热解炉 Fixed bed pyrolysis furnace：



---


# 影响热解主要参数

# ⑤ 反应器类型

# 流化床热解炉 Fluidized bed pyrolysis furnace：

- 适合含水分大、热值低、着火难的细颗粒原料；Suitable for fine raw materials with high moisture content, low calorific value and difficult to ignite;
- 原料适应性广，可大规模、高效率利用，处理量大，处理强度高；Raw materials have wide adaptability and can be used on a large scale with high efficiency, with large processing capacity and high processing intensity;
- 气固充分接触、混合均匀；Gas and solids are fully contacted and mixed evenly;
- 反应温度700-850℃；Reaction temperature is 700-850℃;
- 气化反应在床内进行，焦油也在床内裂解。Gasification reaction is carried out in the bed, and tar is also cracked in the bed.



---


# 影响热解主要参数

# Main parameters affecting pyrolysis

# ⑥ 物料特性

# Material characteristics

- 物料粒径，影响到物料之间温度传递以及气体流动，影响热解反应时间；

The particle size of the material affects the temperature transfer and gas flow between the materials, and affects the pyrolysis reaction time;
- 颗粒度越小，热解反应越容易进行；

The smaller the particle size, the easier the pyrolysis reaction is;
- 物料含水率会影响物料加热的时间，对合成气热值产生影响；含水率低，加热速率快；含水率高，合成气热值降低。

The moisture content of the material affects the heating time of the material and affects the calorific value of the syngas; the lower the moisture content, the faster the heating rate; the higher the moisture content, the lower the calorific value of the syngas.



---

---


# Effect of final pyrolysis temperature on pyrolysis of residual sludge

热解终温越高，污泥中的有机物被分解的越彻底，残余基团更少。

The higher the final pyrolysis temperature, the more thoroughly the organic matter in the sludge is decomposed, and the fewer residual groups are left.

| 试验位 | 300℃ | 450℃ | 600℃ | 750℃ | 900℃ |
| --- | ---- | ---- | ---- | ---- | ---- |
| 100 | 100  | 100  | 100  | 100  | 100  |
| 80  | 80   | 80   | 80   | 80   | 80   |
| 60  | 60   | 60   | 60   | 60   | 60   |
| 40  | 40   | 40   | 40   | 40   | 40   |
| 20  | 20   | 20   | 20   | 20   | 20   |
| 0   | 0    | 0    | 0    | 0    | 0    |

当热解终温在600℃以上时，-OH伸缩振动峰逐渐减小，C-H键几乎消失，说明高温下，污泥中有机物的一些键断裂，发生脱氢作用，转化为H₂、CH₄等物质。

When the final pyrolysis temperature is above 600 °C, the -OH stretching vibration peak gradually decreases and the C-H bond almost disappears, indicating that under high temperature, some bonds of organic matter in the sludge are broken, dehydrogenation occurs, and converted into substances such as H₂ and CH₄.

---

填埋场污泥低温碳化技术
# Low-temperature carbonization technology for landfill sludge

- 热解终温为600℃，热解时间为60 min时，可在耗费较少能量的情况下获得较高的热解气体热值及较大比表面积的热解残渣、较多的热解残渣官能团；

When the final pyrolysis temperature is 600℃ and the pyrolysis time is 60 min, a higher pyrolysis gas calorific value and a pyrolysis residue with a larger specific surface area and more pyrolysis residue functional groups can be obtained with less energy consumption;
- 填埋场现存污泥，热解残渣产率较高，约为75%，且其比表面积远高于其他种类污泥的热解残渣，在600℃热解终温下可达50 m²/g，有利于后续的资源化利用；

The existing sludge in the landfill has a high pyrolysis residue yield of about 75%, and its specific surface area is much higher than that of the pyrolysis residue of other types of sludge, which can reach 50 m²/g at a final pyrolysis temperature of 600℃, which is conducive to subsequent resource utilization;
- 热解气产率较低，但其中氢气占比约为40%，热值较高；热解油产率低于10%，质地不粘稠，利于热解设备的清洁和维护；

The pyrolysis gas yield is low, but the proportion of hydrogen is about 40%, with a high calorific value; the pyrolysis oil yield is less than 10%, and the texture is not sticky, which is conducive to the cleaning and maintenance of the pyrolysis equipment;

# 新型污泥低温碳化技术相关指标

# Related indicators of new sludge low temperature carbonization technology

- 处理80%含水率的湿污泥每公斤仅需200千卡/千克能耗；
Only 200 kcal/kg of energy is required to treat wet sludge with a moisture content of 80% per kilogram;
- 折合标煤：处理每吨污泥0.04吨；折合天然气：处理每吨污泥20立方米；
Equivalent to standard coal: 0.04 tons per ton of sludge is treated; equivalent to natural gas: 20 cubic meters per ton of sludge is treated
- 低温碳化固体颗粒物，燃值2000-3000千卡/千克（褐煤相当）
Low-temperature carbonized solid particles, calorific value 2000-3000 kcal/kg (equivalent to lignite);
- 灰、渣等可按锅炉灰渣处理，作为覆盖土、制砖材料等无机土处理
Ash, slag, etc. can be treated as boiler ash and slag, and used as inorganic soil such as covering soil and brick-making materials;

- After mechanical dehydration, the sludge with a moisture content of less than 80% is further dried by the system waste heat to reduce the moisture content;

- 污泥进入高温反应釜，在缺氧、高温条件下污泥实现热解，有机质转化为可燃气体，重金属及硅化物等灰质以固体形式存在，进一步升温后被熔融成晶体；
- The sludge enters the high-temperature reactor, where it is pyrolyzed under oxygen-deficient and high-temperature conditions, and organic matter is converted into combustible gas. Ash such as heavy metals and silicides exist in solid form and are melted into crystals after further heating;

- 系统内的燃气被收集、净化后燃烧并回收热量；
- The gas in the system is collected, purified, and then burned to recover heat;
