污泥热解气化技术

# Sludge pyrolysis gasification technology

# 热解气化工艺系统特点

# Characteristics of pyrolysis gasification process system

- 100%利用污泥热值， 在超高温条件下处理污泥， 实现 “减量化、稳定化、无害化和资源化”

100% utilization of sludge calorific value, sludge treatment under ultra-high temperature conditions, to achieve "reduction, stabilization, harmlessness and resource utilization";
- 污泥在气化媒介如空气、氧气、蒸汽或二氧化碳的存在下进行热转换，转化/氧化热量，反应热能自满足

Sludge is heat-converted in the presence of gasification media such as air, oxygen, steam or carbon dioxide, conversion/oxidation heat, reaction heat self-satisfaction.
- 由于工艺温度远高于一些有害物质的产生温度，所以限制二噁英等物质的产生，气化产物对比传统的焚烧更环保

Since the process temperature is much higher than the production temperature of some harmful substances, the production of dioxins and other substances is limited, and the gasification products are more environmentally friendly than traditional incineration;
- 受燃料、气化介质以及反应条件影响，产生的合成气主要为氢气、一氧化碳、二氧化碳、氮气、甲烷和水蒸汽

Affected by fuel, gasification medium and reaction conditions, the synthesis gas produced is mainly hydrogen, carbon monoxide, carbon dioxide, nitrogen, methane and water vapor;
- 固态产物为玻璃状熔渣，稳定性极高

The solid product is glassy slag with extremely high stability;

# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 6.4 Biomass

# 生物质

Yang Lei

2026-03-30

---

生物质 Biomass

• 蕴藏在生物质中能量，是绿色植物通过叶绿素，将太阳能转化为化学能而贮存在生物质内部的能量; The energy contained in biomass is the energy stored in biomass by green plants through chlorophyll, which converts solar energy into chemical energy;

• 构成动、植物机体材料; It constitutes the body materials of animals and plants;

• 动物由脂肪、蛋白质组成，植物由淀粉、纤维素组成; Animals are composed of fat and protein, and plants are composed of starch and cellulose;

• 煤、石油和天然气等化石能源也是由生物质能转变而来; Fossil energy such as coal, oil and natural gas are also converted from biomass energy;

-47-

---

# 生物质 Biomass

地球上只要有太阳光和植物，光合产物就不断产生，能量转化作用就不断持续下去。

As long as there is sunlight and plants on the earth, photosynthetic products will continue to be produced, and energy conversion will continue.

1. 木材料及森林工业废弃物 Wood materials and forest industry waste
2. 农业废弃物 Agricultural waste
3. 水生植物 Aquatic plants
4. 油料植物 Oil plants
5. 城市和工业有机废弃物 Urban and industrial organic waste
6. 动物粪便 Animal feces

生物质 Biomass

- 资源储量丰富，可再生；The resource reserves are abundant and renewable;
- 木质素和纤维素，每年约1640亿吨速度不断再生；Lignin and cellulose are continuously regenerated at a rate of about 164 billion tons per year;
- 相当于目前石油年产量15～20倍；It is equivalent to 15 to 20 times the current annual output of oil;
- 我国每年生产各种秸秆6亿吨；China produces 600 million tons of various straws every year;
- 生物质来源于CO₂(光合作用)，燃烧后产生CO₂，但不会增加大气中CO₂含量，因此生物质是清洁燃料。Biomass comes from CO₂ (photosynthesis) and produces CO₂ after combustion, but it does not increase the CO₂ content in the atmosphere, so biomass is a clean fuel.

生物质利用技术

# Biomass utilization

# ① 发电技术

如生活垃圾发电、秸秆燃烧发电

Power generation technology: such as household waste power generation, straw burning power generation

# ② 厌氧发酵产沼技术

家用沼气池、大中型沼气工程、城镇生活污水厌氧发酵；乙醇燃料；产氢

Anaerobic fermentation biogas production technology: household biogas tanks, large and medium-sized biogas projects, anaerobic fermentation of urban domestic sewage; ethanol fuel; hydrogen production

成型燃料，如棒状、颗粒燃料

Biomass solidification briquetting: shaped fuel, such as rods and pellets

# ④ 气化热解技术

生产“三煤”

Gasification pyrolysis technology: production of "three coals"

# ⑤ 合成技术

转化为化工原料

Synthesis technology: conversion into chemical raw materials

# ⑥ 能源植物

富含碳氢化合物植物选育、植物油提取加工、植物油酪化、燃料化

Energy plants: breeding of plants rich in hydrocarbons, plant oil extraction and processing, plant oil caseinization, fuel

-50-

---

生物质水解工艺

# Biomass hydrolysis

1. 预处理：蒸气爆破、碱水解及稀酸水解；Pretreatment: steam explosion, alkaline hydrolysis and dilute acid hydrolysis;
2. 蒸汽爆破法：蒸汽将生物质原料加热至200～240℃，维持30s～20min，高温和高压造成了木质素软化；然后迅速使原料减压，造成纤维素晶体和纤维素的爆裂，使木质素和纤维素分离；Steam explosion method: steam heats the biomass raw material to 200-240℃, maintains for 30s-20min, and the high temperature and high pressure cause the lignin to soften; then the raw material is quickly decompressed, causing the cellulose crystals and cellulose to explode, and the lignin and cellulose are separated;

-51-

---

生物质水解工艺

# ③ 酸水解：

稀硫酸或 SO₂ 浸润生物质，再以蒸汽处理；

Acid hydrolysis: dilute sulfuric acid or SO₂ soaks the biomass, then treats it with steam;

# ④ NaOH溶液处理法：

木质素可溶解于碱性溶液，用稀NaOH溶液处理生物质，使其中的木质素结构破坏，便于后续酶水解或厌氧发酵；

NaOH solution treatment: lignin can be dissolved in alkaline solution, and the biomass is treated with dilute NaOH solution to destroy the lignin structure, which is convenient for subsequent enzymatic hydrolysis or anaerobic fermentation;

# ⑤ NH₃溶液处理法：

生物质在NH₃溶液浸泡，或熏蒸，破坏纤维素结构，NH₃回收重复使用。

NH₃ solution treatment: the biomass is soaked in NH₃ solution, or fumigated, to destroy the cellulose structure, and the NH₃ is recovered and reused.

-52-

---

生物质热解生产航空煤油
# Biomass pyrolysis to produce aviation kerosene

- 木质纤维素类生物质废物向航空煤油的转化途径与原油等化石燃料不同；The conversion pathway of lignocellulosic biomass waste to aviation kerosene is different from that of fossil fuels such as crude oil;
- 原油多为碳氢化合物 ， 转化过程仅涉及 C-C 键的断裂与重组；Crude oil is mostly hydrocarbons, and the conversion process only involves the breaking and reorganization of C-C bonds;
- 木质纤维素除碳和氢外，还含有大量氧元素，所以在C-C键断裂的基础上需进一步考虑C-O-C键；In addition to carbon and hydrogen, lignocellulosic contains a large amount of oxygen, so the C-O-C bond needs to be further considered on the basis of the breaking of the C-C bond;
- C-O-C键断裂后形成的含氧自由基非常活泼，高温下易发生过度缩聚导致副产物如焦炭的形成，降低燃油品位。The oxygen-containing free radicals formed after the breaking of the C-O-C bond are very active, and are prone to excessive polycondensation at high temperatures, resulting in the formation of by-products such as coke, which reduces the quality of the fuel.

-54-

---


# 热解存在的问题

- 热解产物组分复杂，价值低 The pyrolysis products have complex components and low value;
- 多源废物共热解 ， 组分更加复杂 Co-pyrolysis of multiple source wastes has more complex components;
- 热解产物二次 、 三次热解 ， 流程很长 ， 耗能大 ， 经济性差 Secondary and tertiary pyrolysis products has a long process, high energy consumption, and poor economic efficiency;
- 高含氧废物热解，粘度大，热值低，焦油无法利用 Pyrolysis of high oxygen wastes has high viscosity, low calorific value, and tar cannot be used;
- 焚烧技术成熟 ， 工艺简单 ， 与热解存在竞争 Incineration technology is mature, the process is simple, and it competes with pyrolysis;
- 轮胎、电子废物、污泥、生活垃圾、油泥、各类农林废物，都能热解，但产物出路和价值低 Tires, electronic waste, sludge, domestic garbage, oil sludge, and various agricultural and forestry wastes can all be pyrolyzed, but the product outlet and value are low;

Yang Lei

---

# 焚烧概述 Incineration Overview

焚烧法是一种高温热处理技术，即以一定量的过剩空气与被处理的有机废物在焚烧炉内进行氧化燃烧反应，废物中的有毒有害物质在高温下氧化、热解而被破坏，是一种可同时实现废物无害化、减量化和资源化的处理技术。

Incineration is a high-temperature thermal treatment technology that involves the oxidative combustion reaction of organic waste with a certain amount of excess air in an incinerator. Toxic and harmful substances in the waste are oxidized and thermally decomposed at high temperatures, achieving destruction. This method is a treatment technology capable of simultaneously achieving waste harmless treatment, reduction, and resource recovery.

# 固体废物、液体废物和气体废物 Solid waste, liquid waste, and gaseous waste

# 生活垃圾、一般工业废物、处理危险废物 Municipal solid waste, general industrial waste, and hazardous waste

# 优点：无害化、减量化、资源化、经济性、实用性

Harmless treatment (elimination of toxins); Volume/mass reduction; Resource recovery (e.g., energy generation); Economic efficiency; Practical applicability

---

焚烧概述 Incineration Overview

# 优点 Advantages：

- 无害化 、 减量化 、 资源化 、 经济性 、 实用性 Harmless treatment (elimination of toxins);
- Volume/mass reduction; Resource recovery (e.g., energy generation); Economic efficiency;
- Practical applicability

# 缺点 Disadvantages：

- 目前焚烧炉渣的热灼减率一般为 3%-5% ， 尚有潜力挖掘；The current thermal reduction rate of incineration residues typically ranges from 3%–5%, indicating room for further optimization.
- 气相中亦残留少量CO为代表的可燃组分；Trace amounts of combustible components, such as CO, remain in the flue gas.
- 气相不完全燃烧为高毒性有机物（二噁英， Dioxin)；Incomplete combustion in the gas phase can produce highly toxic organic compounds (e.g., dioxins).
- 未燃尽的有机质和重金属的存在 ， 使灰渣中有害物质的再溶出不能完全避免；Unburned organic matter and heavy metals may persist in the ash, making the re-leaching of hazardous substances unavoidable.
- 垃圾焚烧的经济性及资源化仍有改善空间；There is still potential for improvement in the economic viability and resource recovery efficiency of waste incineration.

-4-

---

我国焚烧政策
# Incineration polices in China

- 2011年4月，国务院办公厅转发了《全国城镇生活垃圾无害化处理设施建设规划（2011-2015年）》 In April 2011, the General Office of the State Council issued the National Plan for the Construction of Municipal Solid Waste (MSW) Harmless Treatment Facilities (2011-2015).
- “十二五”期间新增城市生活垃圾无害化处理设施2251座，处理能力58.1万吨/日，到2015 年，新增焚烧设施264座，新增焚烧能力22.3万吨/日，总焚烧规模达到30.7万吨/日，占总无害化处理能力的38.4% During the 12th Five-Year Plan period, 2,251 new MSW harmless treatment facilities were added, with a total processing capacity of 581,000 tons/day. By 2015, 264 new incineration facilities were built, increasing incineration capacity by 223,000 tons/day, bringing the total incineration capacity to 307,000 tons/day—accounting for 38.38% of the total harmless treatment capacity.

-5-

---

我国焚烧政策
# Incineration polices in China

2013 年 8 月 ， 国务院发布 《 国务院关于加快发展节能环保产业的意见 》 In August 2013, the State Council released the Guidelines on Accelerating the Development of the Energy-Saving and Environmental Protection Industry, which emphasized:

- 重点发展大型垃圾焚烧设施炉排及其传动系统、循环流化床预处理工艺技术、焚烧烟气净化技术和垃圾渗滤液处理技术等。Advancing key technologies such as large-scale waste incineration grate systems and transmission mechanisms, circulating fluidized bed (CFB) pre-treatment processes, flue gas purification, and landfill leachate treatment.
- 在生活垃圾处理领域，鼓励发展环保服务总承包和环境治理特许经营模式。Encouraging environmental service contracting (EPC) and franchise operation models in the MSW treatment sector.

-6-

---

我国焚烧政策
# Incineration polices in China

2013年9月，国务院发布《国务院关于加强城市基础设施建设的意见》In September 2013, the State Council issued the Opinions on Strengthening Urban Infrastructure Construction, which stipulated:

- 到2015年，36个重点城市生活垃圾全部实现无害化处理，设市城市生活垃圾无害化处理率达到90%左右。By 2015, all municipal solid waste (MSW) in 36 key cities must undergo harmless treatment, with a harmless treatment rate of around 90% in all prefecture-level cities.
- 到2017年，设市城市生活垃圾得到有效处理，确保垃圾处理设施规范运行，防止二次污染，摆脱“垃圾围城”困境 By 2017, effective MSW treatment should be achieved in all prefecture-level cities, ensuring standardized operation of waste treatment facilities, preventing secondary pollution, and resolving the "cities besieged by waste" crisis.



---

我国焚烧政策

# Incineration polices in China

- 2013 年10月，《基础设施和公用事业特许经营法》等经十二届全国人大常委会审议同意列入立法规划；In October 2013, the Franchise Law for Infrastructure and Public Utilities was approved by the Standing Committee of the 12th National People's Congress for inclusion in the legislative agenda.
- 生活垃圾焚烧发电，政策、商业模式、财务（银行贷款）、技术、设备，都一路绿灯，特别是银行贷款，没有任何障碍，贷款额度是总投资的80%；Waste-to-energy incineration enjoyed full policy, business model, financial (bank loans), technical, and equipment support—especially bank financing, which faced no barriers, with loan quotas covering up to 80% of total investment.

---

燃烧术语 Basic Terms

•  发热量：单位质量(固体或液体燃料)或单位体积 (气体燃料)完全燃烧，且当燃烧产物冷却到燃烧前的温度时 ， 所放出的热量 。 常用的单位为 kJ/Kg 或 kJ/N m³。The amount of heat released per unit mass (for solid/liquid fuels) or unit volume (for gaseous fuels) upon complete combustion, when the combustion products are cooled to the initial temperature before burning. Common units: kJ/kg or kJ/Nm³.

•  高位发热量 Qgr：燃料完全燃烧且燃烧产物中的水蒸气全部冷凝为水时所放出的热量 。 Gross Calorific Value: The total heat released when a fuel is fully combusted, and the water vapor in the combustion products is completely condensed into liquid.

•  低位发热量 Qₙₑₜ：指燃料完全燃烧后，其燃烧产物中的水蒸气仍为气态时所放出的热量 。 Net Calorific Value: The usable heat released when a fuel is fully combusted, with the water vapor in the combustion products remaining in the gaseous state (i.e., excluding latent heat of vaporization).

-9-

---


# 燃烧术语 Basic Terms

不同种类的燃料其发热量差别很大，即使同一种燃料也会因水分、灰分的不同而有所差异，为了便于统计和评价燃料的消耗量，人们便引进了“标准燃料”的概念。The calorific value of different fuel types varies significantly. Even for the same fuel type, factors like moisture and ash content can cause differences. To standardize fuel consumption statistics and evaluation, the concept of "standard fuel" was introduced.

- 标准煤：是指低位发热量（收到基）为29270 kJ/kg（即7000 kcal/kg）的煤 Standard Coal defined as coal with a net calorific value (as received basis) of 29,270 kJ/kg (7,000 kcal/kg).
- 标准油：是指低位发热量为41820kJ/kg（即10000 kcal/kg）的油 Standard Oil, defined as oil with a net calorific value of 41,820 kJ/kg (10,000 kcal/kg).
- 标准气：是指低位发热量为41820 kJ/Nm³（即10000 kcal/Nm³）的气体燃料。 Standard Gas, Defined as gaseous fuel with a net calorific value of 41,820 kJ/Nm³(10,000 kcal/Nm³).



---


# 劣质燃料 Low-grade Fuel

- 劣质煤：高灰分 、低挥发分、低发热量或硫等有害组分高的煤 ，如：灰分≥35%，发热量
≤4500kcal/kg，挥发分≤10%，硫含量在2％～4％甚至更高。

Coal characterized by high ash content, low volatile matter, low calorific value, and/or elevated harmful components such as sulfur. Typical parameters include: Ash content ≥35%; Calorific value ≤4,500 kcal/kg; Volatile matter ≤10%; Sulfur content ranging from 2% to 4% or higher.
- 石油焦：是以炼油厂的渣油为原料制成的一种焦炭，虽然其发热量高，常达普通煤炭热值
的 1.2 ～ 1.5 倍 ， 但其挥发分只有 10% 左右 ， 尤其是有害组分硫含量常常高达 6% 以上 。

Petroleum Coke: A carbonaceous solid derived from refinery residue. Despite its high calorific value (typically 1.2–1.5 times that of standard coal), it has: low volatile matter (~10%); extremely high sulfur content (often >6%), posing environmental and operational challenges.
- 垃圾、污泥衍生燃料：这类衍生燃料是利用生活垃圾和市政污泥组成中含有较多可燃物质
的特点，通过相应的预处理技术而获得的一类燃料。一方面能够更彻底地实现垃圾和污泥
的无害化处理，达到更好的环保效果；另一方面通过将其转化为具有较高发热量的衍生燃
料 可 实 现 废 物 的 资 源化 ， 减 少 宝 贵 化 石 燃 料 的 消 耗 。

Waste/Sludge-derived fuel: Fuel produced by processing municipal solid waste (MSW) and sewage sludge to exploit their combustible components. This approach: Enhances waste harmless treatment, delivering superior environmental outcomes; Recovers energy by converting waste into high-calorific fuel, reducing reliance on fossil fuels.



---

清洁燃料 Clean Fuel

清洁燃料是指柴油、煤油、天然气、液化石油气、焦炉煤气、水煤气、发生炉冷煤气等几乎无污染的燃料。Clean Fuel refers to fuels that produce minimal pollution, such as: Diesel; Kerosene; Natural Gas; Liquefied Petroleum Gas, LPG; Coke Oven Gas; Water Gas; Producer Gas; These fuels are characterized by their low emissions and minimal environmental impact during combustion.

# 1. 柴油和煤油 Diesel and Kerosene

- 柴油是由 C15 ～ 30 的液体烃类组成的混合物 ， 一般含碳 85.5% ～ 86.5% ， 含氢 13.5% ～ 14.5%和少量（1.5%以下）的其它有机化合物和金属氧化物。Diesel is a mixture of liquid hydrocarbons (C15–C30), typically composed of: Carbon: 85.5%–86.5%; Hydrogen: 13.5%–14.5%; Trace amounts (&#x3C;1.5%) of other organic compounds and metal oxides.
- 柴油分为轻柴油和重柴油两类， 轻柴油又有普通柴油和军用柴油两种 。 Classification of diesel: Light Diesel; regular diesel; military-grade diesel; Heavy Diesel:
- 煤油分为航空煤油、拖拉机煤油、灯用煤油和炉用煤油几种。Kerosene is categorized into: Aviation kerosene; Tractor kerosene; Lamp kerosene; Stove kerosene.

-12-

---

清洁燃料 Clean Fuel

# ② 天然气（NG）和液化天然气（LNG）

- 分为伴生气和非伴生气两种。Natural gas is categorized into associated gas and non-associated gas.
- 天然气以甲烷为主要成分，伴有其它气态烃类C1～6。天然气开采后，要经过脱水、脱硫、分离等一系列工序后才能成为商品天然气。有的天然气经减压后直接送到用户，即NG；有的将其液化后，贮备使用即LNG。The primary component of natural gas is methane (CH₄), accompanied by other gaseous hydrocarbons (C1–C6). After extraction, natural gas undergoes a series of processing steps—including dehydration, desulfurization, and separation—before becoming commercial-grade natural gas. Depending on its end use: Some natural gas is directly delivered to consumers at reduced pressure (referred to as NG, or pipeline natural gas). Some is liquefied for storage and transport (referred to as LNG, or liquefied natural gas).

# ③ 液化石油气 Liquefied Petroleum Gas (LPG)

- 炼制石油的副产品，组成以丙烷和丁烷为主，另外含少量戊烷和其它烯烃类等。A byproduct of petroleum refining, primarily composed of propane and butane, with minor amounts of pentane and other olefins.

-13-

---

清洁燃料 Clean Fuel

# ④ 油制气 Oil Gas

由石油原油或其加工产品经热裂解或催化裂解或部分氧化或加氢裂解而制得的气态燃料称为油制气

Refers to gaseous fuels produced through thermal cracking, catalytic cracking, partial oxidation, or hydrocracking of crude oil or its refined products.

# ⑤ 煤制气 Coal Gas

指焦炉煤气、水煤气及发生炉煤气等由煤制成的人造气体燃料。

Denotes manufactured gaseous fuels derived from coal, including:

- 焦炉煤气是指煤在煤气炉中制焦炭时的气体产物，主要成分为：H₂、CH₄、CO、N₂和少量CₘHₙ、O₂等。
- Coke Oven Gas: byproduct gas from coking process, mainly containing H₂ (50-60%), CH₄ (20-30%), CO (5-8%), N₂ (3-5%), with trace CₘHₙ and O₂.

水煤气是将水蒸气与灼热焦炭中的碳反应制成的燃气，水煤气的理论组成（体积百分比）为CO 50%、H₂ 50%。
- Water Gas: produced by reacting steam with red-hot coke, theoretically composed of 50% CO and 50% H₂ by volume.

发生炉煤气是将气化剂（空气、水蒸气、氧等）连续通入煤气发生炉内使之与其中的煤发生反应生成的煤气。主要成分是CO，其次是H₂。

-14-

---


# 维持生活垃圾燃烧基本条件

# Basic Conditions for Maintaining Municipal Solid Waste Combustion

低位热值高于3350kJ/kg，燃烧过程无须助燃剂，否则，要添加辅助燃料才能维持燃烧。The waste must have a minimum net calorific value of 3,350 kJ/kg to sustain stable combustion without auxiliary fuels. If the calorific value is below this threshold, supplemental fuels (e.g., natural gas or diesel) must be added to maintain combustion efficiency.

LHV = 81C + 342.5(H – O/8) + 22.5S – 5.85(9H+W)

- LHV ，低位热值，kcal/kg； Lower Heating Value
- C、H、O、S—分别代表垃圾中碳、氧、氢和硫的元素组成，kg/kg；C, H, O, S, represent the elemental composition of waste, expressed in kg per kg of waste (kg/kg)
- W，废物中含水量，kg/kg；indicates the moisture content of the waste, also in kg/kg.

LHV = 340C + 1430(H – O/8) + 105S – 25(9H+W)  (Dulong方程）

LHV ，低位热值，kJ/kg



---


# 维持生活垃圾燃烧基本条件

# Basic Conditions for Maintaining Municipal Solid Waste Combustion

# 高位热值与低位热值转换

# Conversion Between Gross and Net Calorific Values

LHV = HHV – 2.442 (9 x H/100 + Mₐᵣ/100)

= HHV – 0.206H – 0.023 Mₐᵣ

- LHV，低位热值，Lower Heating Value: kJ/kg
- HHV，高位热值，Higher Heating Value: kJ/kg
- Where 2.442 kJ/g is the latent heat of water vaporization.
- H，废物中氢的质量百分率， H: Hydrogen content in waste: %
- Mₐᵣ，样品水份，收到基 Mₐᵣ: moisture content, as received basis, %



---

# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 7.2 Incineration Process and Products

# 焚烧过程及产物

Yang Lei

2026-04-08

---


# 焚烧的产物 Combustion products

+ Ê + + y − w − z · → + + u + + Ê y − w·

Cₓ HyOz Nu SvClw Á x v 4 2 ¸O² xCO₂ wHCl 2 N² vSO₂ Á 2 ¸H₂O

• 有机碳焚烧产物是CO₂气体；Organic carbon’s final product is CO₂ carbon dioxide gas

• 有机物中氢的焚烧产物是H₂O。若有氟或氯存在，也可能生成HF或HCl；hydrogen in organic compounds: primary product is H₂O (water vapor) &#x26; potential secondary products: if fluorine (F) or chlorine (Cl) is present, HF(hydrogen fluoride) or HCl(hydrogen chloride) will be formed.

• 生活垃圾中有机硫和有机磷，在焚烧过程中生成SO₂或SO₃以及P₂O₅；Sulfur forms SO₂ (sulfur dioxide) or SO₃ sulfur trioxide with excess O₂, P forms P₂O₅ (phosphorus pentoxide)



---


# 焚烧的产物 Combustion products

- 有机氮化物焚烧产物主要是气态氮，也有少量氮氧化物。

Organic Nitrogen Compounds: primary product is N₂ (gaseous nitrogen); secondary products includes NOₓ (nitrogen oxides, e.g., NO, NO₂) in trace amounts.
- 有机氟化物焚烧产物是氟化氢，若氢量不足，可能出现四氟化碳或二氟氧碳；金属元素存在时，可与氟结合生成金属氟化物。

Organic fluorine compounds: main product is HF (hydrogen fluoride); side products (if hydrogen-deficient): CF₄ (carbon tetrafluoride), COF₂ (carbonyl difluoride); with metals: forms metal fluorides (e.g., NaF, CaF₂).
- 有机氯化物的燃烧产物是氯化氢。由于氧和氯的负性相近，存在着下列可逆反应，当氢量不足时，可能产生游离氯气。

Organic Chlorine Compounds: primary product: HCl reversible reaction (due to similar electronegativity of O/Cl). Risk: free chlorine gas (Cl₂) may form if hydrogen is insufficient.

2HCl + 1/2O₂ ⇌ Cl₂ + H₂O



---


# 焚烧的产物

# Combustion products

- 有机溴化物和碘化物焚烧后生成溴化氢及少量溴气以及元素碘。
Organic bromides and iodides are burned to produce hydrogen bromide, a small amount of bromine gas and elemental iodine.
- 根据焚烧温度，金属在焚烧后可生成卤化物、硫酸盐、磷酸盐、碳酸盐、氢氧化物和氧化物等。
Depending on the incineration temperature, metals can generate halides, sulfates, phosphates, carbonates, hydroxides and oxides after incineration.



---


# 垃圾焚烧理论空气量

# Theoretical air demand for Combustion

- 废物（或燃料）完全燃烧时，所需要的最低空气量，一般以A₀来表示 The minimum amount of air required for complete combustion of waste (or fuel) is generally represented by A₀.
- 1 kg液体或固体废物中，碳、氢、氧、硫、氮、灰分、水分质量分别以C、H、O、S、N、Ash及W来表示，则理论空气量为 The mass of carbon, hydrogen, oxygen, sulfur, nitrogen, ash and water in 1 kg of liquid or solid waste is expressed as C, H, O, S, N, Ash and W respectively. The theoretical air volume is:

Volume 体积基准： A (m / k g) 2.67C + 8H - O + 0.231

质量基准： A₀ (m / k g) = 0.21 (1.867C + 5.6H - 0.7S)

Effective Hydrogen 有效氢： (H - O / 8)

- 燃料中氧是以结合水的状态存在，在燃烧中无法利用这些与氧结合成水的氢，故需要将其从全氢中减去。The oxygen in the fuel exists in the form of bound water. The hydrogen combined with oxygen to form water cannot be used during combustion, so it needs to be subtracted from the total hydrogen.



---


# 垃圾焚烧实际空气量

Actual air demand for waste incineration

- 废物（或燃料）完全燃烧时，所需要的最低空气量，一般以A₀来表，实际空气量以 A 表示。

The minimum amount of air required for complete combustion of waste (or fuel) is generally expressed as A0, and the actual amount of air is expressed as A.

A = mA0

- m，过量空气系数

Excess air coefficient



---

# 过剩空气相关参数

•  过剩空气系数 （ m）： 实际空气与理论空气比值 ， 定义为：

Excess air coefficient (m): The ratio of actual air to theoretical air, defined as:

m = A
A₀

•  过剩空气率 Excess air rate:

过剩空气率＝（m – 1）×100％

•  废气中含氧量可用于判别过剩空气状况 Oxygen content in exhaust gas can be used to determine excess air conditions

---


# 过剩空气的作用

# The role of excess air

- 实际燃烧系统中，氧气与可燃物无法完全达到理想的混合及反应状态。In the actual combustion system, oxygen and combustibles cannot completely reach the ideal mixing and reaction state.
- 仅供给理论空气量，很难使其完全燃烧，需要加上比理论空气量更多的助燃空气量，才能使废物与空气完全混合燃烧。It is difficult to achieve complete combustion by only supplying the theoretical amount of air. It is necessary to add more combustion-supporting air than the theoretical amount of air to make the waste and air completely mixed and burned.
- 过剩空气率过低，会使燃烧不完全，甚至冒黑烟，有害物质焚烧不彻底。If the excess air rate is too low, the combustion will be incomplete, even black smoke will be emitted, and the harmful substances will not be burned completely.
- 过剩空气率过高，则会使燃烧温度降低，影响燃烧效率，造成燃烧系统排气量和热损失增加。If the excess air rate is too high, the combustion temperature will be reduced, affecting the combustion efficiency, and causing the exhaust volume and heat loss of the combustion system to increase.



---


# 过剩空气经验指标 Excess Air Experience Index

- 焚烧首要目的，是完全摧毁废物中的可燃物质，过剩空气系数一般大于1.2；The primary purpose of incineration is to completely destroy the combustible substances in the waste, and the excess air coefficient is generally greater than 1.2;
- 焚烧废液 、 废气时 ， 过剩空气量一般取 1.2-1.3 的理论空气量；When incinerating waste liquid and waste gas, the excess air volume is generally 1.2-1.3 theoretical air volume;
- 焚烧固体废物时，过剩空气量一般取50％～90％，即过剩空气系数1.2～1.9；When incinerating solid waste, the excess air volume is generally 50% to 90%, that is, the excess air coefficient is 1.2 to 1.9.



---


# 影响焚烧的四大控制参数

# Four major control parameters affecting incineration

① 焚烧温度（Incineration Temperature)

固体废物的焚烧温度是指废物中有害物质在高温下氧化、分解直至破坏所需达到的温度，比废物的着火温度高得多。但过高的焚烧温度不仅需要添加辅助燃料 ，而且会增加烟气中金属的挥发及 NO数 量，引发二次污染。

The incineration temperature of solid waste refers to the temperature required for harmful substances in the waste to be oxidized, decomposed and destroyed at high temperature, which is much higher than the ignition temperature of the waste. However, too high an incineration temperature not only requires the addition of auxiliary fuel, but also increases the volatilization of metals and the amount of NO in the flue gas, causing secondary pollution.



---


# 影响焚烧的四大控制参数

# Four major control parameters affecting incineration

1. # 焚烧温度（Incineration Temperature)

- 对于废气脱臭处理，一般采用800-950 ℃；For waste gas deodorization, 800-950 ℃ is generally used;
- 废物粒径0.01~0.51微米，且供氧浓度与停留时间适当时，焚烧温度在900~100 ℃时即可避免产生黑烟；When the waste particle size is 0.01~0.51 microns, and the oxygen supply concentration and residence time are appropriate, the incineration temperature can avoid the generation of black smoke at 900~100 ℃;
- 含氯化物废物焚烧，温度在800~ 850 ℃以上时，Cl转化为HCl；低于800 ℃形成氯气，难以除去；When the waste containing chloride is incinerated, when the temperature is above 800~850 ℃, Cl is converted into HCl; below 800 ℃, chlorine gas is formed, which is difficult to remove;



---


# 影响焚烧的四大控制参数

# Four major control parameters affecting incineration

1. # 焚烧温度（Incineration Temperature)

- 含碱土金属的废物焚烧，一般在 750~800 ℃ 以下。
Waste containing alkaline earth metals is generally incinerated below 750~800 ℃.
- 含氰化物废物焚烧，温度在850~900 ℃时，氰化物几乎全部分解；
When the waste containing cyanide is incinerated at a temperature of 850~900 ℃, cyanide is almost completely decomposed;
- 可能产生NOₓ时，温度控制在1500 ℃以下，过高的温度导致NOₓ大量产生。
When NOₓ may be generated, the temperature is controlled below 1500 ℃, and excessively high temperature leads to a large amount of NOₓ.
- 高温焚烧是防止二噁英产生的最好方法，925 ℃以上。
High-temperature incineration is the best way to prevent the generation of dioxins, above 925 ℃.



---


# 影响焚烧的四大控制参数

# ② 搅拌混合程度（Turbulence)

搅拌混合程度是表征生活垃圾和空气混合程度的指标。焚烧炉采用的扰动方式有空气流扰动、机械炉排扰动、流态化扰动及旋转扰动。

Turbulence is an indicator of the degree of mixing between domestic waste and air. The disturbance methods used in the incinerator include air flow disturbance, mechanical grate disturbance, fluidization disturbance and rotation disturbance.

- 炉床下送风 Air supply under the furnace bed
- 炉床上送风 Air supply on the furnace bed



---


# 影响焚烧的四大控制参数

# ③ 气体停留时间（Time) Gas residence time

固体废物的有害组分在焚烧炉内处于焚烧条件下发生氧化、燃烧，使有害物质变成无害物质所需的时间称为焚烧停留时间。停留时间的长短直接影响焚烧完善的程度，也是决定炉体容积、尺寸的重要依据。

The time required for harmful components of solid waste to oxidize and burn under incineration conditions in the incinerator, turning harmful substances into harmless substances, is called the incineration residence time. The length of the residence time directly affects the degree of incineration perfection and is also an important basis for determining the volume and size of the furnace.



---


# 四大控制参数的关系

# The relationship between the four major control parameters

| 参数变化     | 搅拌混合程度 | 气体停留时间 | 燃烧室温度 | 燃烧室负荷 |
| -------- | ------ | ------ | ----- | ----- |
| 燃烧温度上升   | 可减小    | 可减少    | -     | 可增加   |
| 过剩空气率增大  | 会增大    | 会减少    | 会降低   | 会增加   |
| 气体停留时间增加 | 可减小    | -      | 会降低   | 会降低   |

# Equilibrium in Combustion

A solid waste contains 60% combustibles, 20% moisture, and 20% inert materials. The elemental and material composition of the waste is as follows:

- Carbon (C): 28%
- Hydrogen (H): 4%
- Oxygen (O): 23%
- Nitrogen (N): 4%
- Sulfur (S): 1%
- Moisture: 20%
- Ash: 20%

# Given Assumptions:

- The calorific value (heat value) of the waste is 11,630 kJ/kg.
- The grate residue (ash) contains 5% unburned carbon.
- The inlet air temperature to the furnace is 65°C, and the outlet grate residue temperature is 650°C.
- The specific heat capacity of the residue (ash) is 0.323 kJ/kg·C.
- The latent heat of vaporization for water is 2,420 kJ/kg.
- The radiation heat loss is 0.5% of the total furnace input heat.
- The heat value of carbon is 32,564 kJ/kg.

Task: Calculate the usable heat energy generated from the combustion of this waste.

焚烧系统构成 Incineration System Composition

# ③ 助燃空气系统 Combustion air system

- 提供适量风量和风温来烘干垃圾，为垃圾着火做准备；Provide appropriate air volume and air temperature to dry the garbage and prepare for garbage ignition;
- 提供垃圾充分燃烧和燃尽的空气量；Provide air volume for full combustion and complete burnout of the garbage;
- 促使膛内烟气的充分扰动，使炉膛出口CO含量降低；Promote full disturbance of flue gas in the furnace to reduce the CO content at the furnace outlet;
- 提供炉墙冷却风，以防炉渣在炉墙上结焦；Provide cooling air for the furnace wall to prevent slag from coking on the furnace wall;
- 冷却排炉，避免炉排过热变形；Cool the grate to prevent the grate from overheating and deformation;

# 焚烧系统构成

# Incineration System Composition

# ④ 余热利用系统

# Waste heat utilization system

- 直接热能利用：热利用效率高、设备投资省，尤其适合小规模（&#x3C;100t/d)垃圾焚烧设备和垃圾热值较低的小型垃圾焚烧厂；

Direct heat energy utilization: high heat utilization efficiency, low equipment investment, especially suitable for small-scale (&#x3C;100t/d) waste incineration equipment and small waste incineration plants with low waste calorific value;
- 预热发电（13%~22.5%）：随着垃圾量和垃圾热值的提高，直接热能利用受到设备本身和热用户需求量的限制；

Preheating power generation (13%~22.5%): With the increase of waste volume and waste calorific value, direct heat energy utilization is limited by the equipment itself and the demand of heat users;
- 热电联用（50%）

Cogeneration (50%)



---

# 焚烧系统构成

# Incineration System Composition

# ⑤ 蒸气及冷凝水系统

Steam and condensate system

• 主要用于处理外界送入的地下水或自来水，将其处理倒纯水或超纯水的品质，再送入锅炉水循环系统。处理方法为高级用水程序，包括活性炭吸附、离子交换及反渗透单元。It is mainly used to treat groundwater or tap water sent from the outside, treat it to the quality of pure water or ultrapure water, and then send it to the boiler water circulation system. The treatment method is an advanced water treatment process, including activated carbon adsorption, ion exchange and reverse osmosis units.

---


# 焚烧系统构成 Incineration System Composition

# ⑥ 烟气净化系统 Flue Gas Purification

- SNCR（选择性非催化还原法）脱硝应用案例日渐增多，SCR（选择性催化还原法）脱硝也开始得到应用；SNCR (selective non-catalytic reduction) denitrification application cases are increasing, and SCR (selective catalytic reduction) denitrification is also beginning to be used;
- 脱酸由单一“干法”或“半干法”向“半干法+干法”或“干法+湿法”组合工艺方向转变；Deacidification is changing from a single "dry process" or "semi-dry process" to a "semi-dry process + dry process" or "dry process + wet process" combined process;

# ⑦ 灰渣处理系统 Slag Management

- 固化稳定化后安全/卫生填埋 Safe landfill after solidification and stabilization
- 资源化利用 Resource utilization

# 7.4 Design of Incineration System

# 焚烧系统设计

Yang Lei

2026-04-08

---

焚烧炉设计基本原则 Basic Principles in designing Incinerator

•  炉使废物在焚烧炉膛内按规定的焚烧温度和足够的停留时间，达到完全燃烧。

The furnace allows the waste to be completely burned in the incineration furnace at the specified incineration temperature and for a sufficient residence time.

•  选择适宜的炉床，合理设计炉膛的形状和尺寸，增加废物与氧气接触的机会，

使废物在焚烧过程中水汽易于蒸发、加速燃烧，以及控制燃烧气体的流速和

流向，使得气体充分混合。

Select a suitable furnace bed, reasonably design the shape and size of the furnace, increase the chance of waste contact with oxygen, make it easy for water vapor to evaporate during the incineration process, accelerate combustion, and control the flow rate and direction of the combustion gas to ensure that the gas is fully mixed.

-45-

---

焚烧炉设计基本原则

# Basic Principles in designing Incinerator

- 炉型：燃烧形态分过氧式燃烧和控气式燃烧；Furnace type: The combustion form is divided into oxygen combustion and gas-controlled combustion;
- 送风方式 Air supply method
- 炉膛尺寸：由燃烧室允许的容积热强度和废物焚烧时在高温炉膛内所需停留时间决定。Furnace size: determined by the volumetric heat intensity allowed by the combustion chamber and the required residence time of the waste in the high-temperature furnace during incineration.
- 设计参数：垃圾性质、处理规模、处理能力、炉排机械负荷和热负荷、燃烧室热负荷、燃烧室出口温度和烟气停留时间、热酌减率；Design parameters: garbage properties, processing scale, processing capacity, grate mechanical load and heat load, combustion chamber heat load, combustion chamber outlet temperature and flue gas residence time, heat reduction rate.

-46-

---


# 焚烧炉设计和运行若干关键参数

# Several key parameters in incinerator design and operation

# 炉膛（燃烧室）容积热负荷

Furnace (combustion chamber) volume heat load

- 炉膛（燃烧室）内单位时间、单位体积热容量，是确定炉膛大小的指标; The heat capacity per unit time and per unit volume in the furnace (combustion chamber) is an indicator for determining the size of the furnace;
- 可取 can take (33.50～83.73)×10₄ kJ/m₃h;

# 炉排面积热负荷

Head load

- 单位炉排面积、单位时间内，垃圾焚烧所释放的热量 ; The heat released by garbage incineration per unit grate area and per unit time;
- 普通炉膛，可取 For ordinary furnace, it can be 277～694 kW/m₂;
- 水冷壁炉膛，可取 For water-cooled furnace, it can be 2000 kW/m₂;



---


# 焚烧炉设计和运行若干关键参数

# Several key parameters in incinerator design and operation

# 额定炉排机械负荷

Rated grate mechanical load

- 在达到规定炉渣热灼减率条件下，单位炉排面积、单位时间内最大垃圾焚烧量，也可称为额定炉排燃烧速度;
Under the condition of reaching the specified slag thermal burn-off rate, the maximum amount of garbage incineration per unit grate area and per unit time can also be called the rated grate combustion speed;

# 实际炉排机械负荷

Actual grate mechanical load

- 实际运行中单位炉排面积、单位时间内垃圾焚烧量; The amount of garbage incineration per unit grate area and per unit time in actual operation;
- 为了稳定炉温和余热锅炉蒸发量，在焚烧厂投运后前几年，可以适当增加垃圾焚烧量，如达到额定炉排机械负荷的1.2倍;
In order to stabilize the furnace temperature and the evaporation volume of the waste heat boiler, the amount of garbage incineration can be appropriately increased in the first few years after the incineration plant is put into operation, such as reaching 1.2 times the rated grate mechanical load;



---


# 焚烧炉设计和运行若干关键参数

# Several key parameters in incinerator design and operation

- 欧盟燃烧优化准则（GCP）中规定，焚化废气在燃烧室炉床上方至少须在850℃环境中停留2 s，以彻底破坏可能产生二噁英的有机物; The EU Good Combustion Guidelines (GCP) stipulate that the incineration waste gas must stay in an environment of 850℃ for at least 2 seconds above the furnace bed in the combustion chamber to completely destroy organic matter that may produce dioxins;
- 为避免废气流量过大对耐火衬产生腐蚀，一般均将燃烧室烟气流速限制在5 m/s之下，废气通过对流区的流速不得高于7 m/s; In order to avoid excessive waste gas flow causing corrosion to the refractory lining, the flue gas flow rate in the combustion chamber is generally limited to less than 5 m/s, and the flow rate of waste gas through the convection zone shall not exceed 7 m/s;
- 燃烧室内废气温度亦不可高于1050 ℃，以免飞灰因温度过高而粘着于炉壁造成软化及腐蚀，并且易于产生过量的氮氧化物; The waste gas temperature in the combustion chamber shall not be higher than 1050℃, so as to prevent the fly ash from adhering to the furnace wall due to excessive temperature, causing softening and corrosion, and easily generating excessive nitrogen oxides;



---


# 炉膛（二燃室）Secondary Combustion Chamber

- 烟气的二次燃烧，容积必须足够大; The volume of flue gas secondary combustion must be large enough;
- 焚烧烟气在850℃高温下持续2秒钟以上; The incineration flue gas is kept at a high temperature of 850℃ for more than 2 seconds;
- 早期生活垃圾热值普遍偏低，为了使炉膛内任何点的温度达到 850℃以上，炉膛炉壁不布置锅炉水冷壁管或在水冷壁管内侧覆盖耐火绝热层; The calorific value of early domestic garbage is generally low. In order to make the temperature of any point in the furnace reach above 850℃, the furnace wall is not arranged with boiler water-cooled wall tubes or the inner side of the water-cooled wall tubes is covered with a refractory insulation layer;
- 热值偏高，需要改为水冷壁管。干垃圾热值>13MJ/kg; The calorific value is too high and needs to be changed to water-cooled wall tubes. The calorific value of dry garbage is >13MJ/kg;



---


# 焚烧炉启动 Start of Incinerator

- 为了保护炉内耐火材料和其它部件，焚烧炉启动时，需要慢慢升温; In order to protect the refractory materials and other parts in the furnace, the incinerator needs to be heated up slowly when it is started;
- 一般按照规定的升温曲线提升炉膛温度; Generally, the furnace temperature is increased according to the prescribed temperature increase curve;
- 在炉膛温度升至850℃前不能进垃圾，只能靠点火燃烧器和助燃燃烧器提升炉膛温度; No garbage can be added before the furnace temperature rises to 850℃, and the furnace temperature can only be increased by ignition burners and combustion-supporting burners;



---

焚烧炉启动 Start of Incinerator

The load of the 助燃燃烧器负荷逐步升高 combustion supporting burner gradually increases, and garbage cannot be fed into the burner.

助燃燃烧器负荷逐步减小 The load of the combustion supporting burner gradually decreases, and the amount of garbage gradually increases.

焚烧炉停炉 Stop of Incinerator

为了保护炉内耐火材料和其它部件 ，焚烧炉停炉时需要慢慢降温；

In order to protect the refractory materials and other parts in the furnace, the incinerator needs to be cooled down slowly when it is shut down;

在炉排上垃圾烧完之前，炉膛温度应保持 850℃，烟气净化系统应保持运行；

Before the garbage on the grate is burned out, the furnace temperature should be maintained at 850℃ and the flue gas purification system should remain in operation;

自动燃烧控制技术 Auto Control Center

• 根据垃圾热值和特性变化，自动调节推料速度、炉排移动速度、一次风量，稳定燃烧工况、炉膛温度、锅炉蒸发量和蒸汽参数；According to the calorific value and characteristic changes of the garbage, the pushing speed, grate moving speed, and primary air volume are automatically adjusted to stabilize the combustion conditions, furnace temperature, boiler evaporation volume, and steam parameters;

• 根据锅炉出口烟气氧含量、CO含量，调节一、二次风供风量；According to the oxygen content and CO content of the flue gas at the boiler outlet, the primary and secondary air supply volumes are adjusted;

• 当炉膛温度低于850℃时，自动启动助燃燃烧器；When the furnace temperature is lower than 850℃, the combustion-supporting burner is automatically started;

# 生活垃圾焚烧水冷炉排 MSW water-cooled grate

- 水分含量不宜大于50%，灰分含量不宜大于25%，焚烧炉最大垃圾处理量点低位发热量不宜小于12000 kJ/kg，高位发热量不宜高于18000 kJ/kg 的生活垃圾；The moisture content should not be greater than 50%, the ash content should not be greater than 25%, the low calorific value of the Maximum continuous rating point should not be less than 12000 kJ/kg, and the high calorific value should not be higher than 18000 kJ/kg of domestic waste;
- 水冷系统通过加压化的方式向水冷炉排片提供冷却水，以降低炉排片的表面温度；The water cooling system provides cooling water to the water-cooled grate slices by pressurization to reduce the surface temperature of the grate slices;
- 当环境温度不高于25 ℃时，炉体外壁面温度不应高于50 ℃；当环境温度高于25 ℃时，炉体外壁面温度不应高于环境温度；When the ambient temperature is not higher than 25 ℃, the outer wall temperature of the furnace body should not be higher than 50 ℃; when the ambient temperature is higher than 25 ℃, the outer wall temperature of the furnace body should not be higher than the ambient temperature 25 ℃.



---


# 生活垃圾焚烧水冷炉排 MSW water cooled grate

- 水冷炉排片应能有效控制其表面温度，在运行过程中，其表面温度不宜超过200 ℃；The water-cooled grate plate should be able to effectively control its surface temperature. During operation, its surface temperature should not exceed 200 ℃;
- 水冷炉排水冷装置能力应能满足水冷机械炉排炉各种工况下安全稳定运行的要求，且冷却能力应考虑10% 裕量。 炉排移出热量不大于总热负荷 10%。The water-cooled grate water cooling device capacity should be able to meet the requirements of safe and stable operation of water-cooled mechanical grate furnace under various working conditions, and the cooling capacity should consider a 10% margin. The heat removed from the grate should not exceed 10% of the total heat load.

# 固体废物焚烧炉：机械炉排式

# Solid waste incinerator: Mechanical grate type

机械炉排炉是以机械式的炉排片构成炉床，可动炉排片与固定炉排片之间的相对运动，使垃圾不断翻动、搅拌并推向前进。往复焚烧炉用炉排片设计需要炉排片有规则的往复运动，从而保证垃圾进入炉内与热空气接触、升温、干燥、着火、燃烬。

The mechanical grate furnace uses mechanical grate pieces to form the furnace bed. The relative movement between the movable grate pieces and the fixed grate pieces causes the garbage to be constantly turned, stirred and pushed forward. The grate piece design for the reciprocating incinerator requires the grate piece to have regular reciprocating motion, so as to ensure that the garbage enters the furnace and contacts with the hot air, heats up, dries, ignites and burns out.

---

流化床焚烧炉 Fluidized bed incinerator

# Waste heat

- 固定层：气速较低、垃圾颗粒保持静态，气体从垃圾颗粒间通过；

Fixed layer: the gas velocity is low, the garbage particles remain static, and the gas passes between the garbage particles;
- 沸腾流动层：气速超过流动临界点的状态，从而在颗粒中产生气泡，颗粒被剧烈搅拌处于沸腾状态；

Boiling flow layer: the gas velocity exceeds the critical point of flow, so bubbles are generated in the particles, and the particles are violently stirred in a boiling state;
- 循环流动层：气速超过极限速度，气体和颗粒之间激烈碰撞混合，颗粒在气体作用下处于飞散状态；

Circulating flow layer: the gas velocity exceeds the limit speed, the gas and particles collide and mix violently, and the particles are in a scattered state under the action of the gas.

燃烧器 导流板 600~750℃

回转焚烧炉 Rotary incinerator

在现有回转窑焚烧技术的基础上，本创新工艺采用“先气化后燃烧”的流程。回转窑主要实现中温气化，从而降低其在燃烧阶段的热负荷。同时，增设了后燃炉排和气化室，废液被喷入气化室内进行气化与燃烧。

| 燃烧器    | 二次风 |
| ------ | --- |
| Burner | Air |

在回转窑-炉排的配置下，系统实现了固体燃料与气化合成气的双级燃烧。该设计可同时处理固相、液相及半固相等多相态废物。通过拓宽可处理废物种类，显著提升了初始排放性能（例如降低氮氧化物和二噁英的生成）。

| 回转窑         | 气化室                  | 二燃室                       | 燃尽室              |
| ----------- | -------------------- | ------------------------- | ---------------- |
| Rotary Kiln | Gasification Chamber | Second Combustion Chamber | Burn-out Chamber |
| 650\~800℃   | 700\~850℃            |                           |                  |

该设计可同时处理固相、液相及半固相等多相态废物。通过拓宽可处理废物种类，显著提升了初始排放性能（例如降低氮氧化物和二噁英的生成）。

烟气净化 Flue Gas Control

# 烟气浓度取样分析 Smoke concentration sampling analysis

- 半小时均值：以连续0.5h采样获取的平均值，以等时间间隔至少采取3个样品计算的平均值 ; Half-hour mean: the average value obtained by continuous sampling for 0.5 hours, and the average value calculated by taking at least 3 samples at equal time intervals;
- 小时均值：以连续1h采样获取的平均值，以等时间间隔至少采取3个样品计算的平均值; Hourly mean: the average value obtained by continuous sampling for 1 hour, and the average value calculated by taking at least 3 samples at equal time intervals;
- 日均值：24小时以等时间间隔至少采取3个样品计算的平均值; Daily mean: the average value calculated by taking at least 3 samples at equal time intervals in 24 hours;
- 测定均值：以等时间间隔至少采取 3 个样品计算的平均值 ; Measurement mean: the average value calculated by taking at least 3 samples at equal time intervals;

# 焚烧烟气排放指标

# Flue gas discharge standard worldwide

随着国家环保要求的提高和民众环保意识的增强，欧盟2010排放指标（NOx≤200mg/Nm³、SO₂≤50mg/Nm³、HCl≤10mg/Nm³、烟尘≤10mg/Nm³）已经成为各项目基本排放要求，重点地区（如浙江、天津、海南、广东、江苏、河南、河北等）提出更高的要求，不少项目、地区要达到“超低排放”指标，即NOx≤50 mg/Nm³、SO₂≤10 mg/Nm³、HCl≤5 mg/Nm³、烟尘≤5 mg/Nm³。

With the improvement of national environmental protection requirements and the enhancement of public environmental awareness, China has adopted EU 2010 emission indicators (NOₓ≤200 mg/Nm³, SO₂≤50 mg/Nm³, HCl≤10 mg/Nm³, smoke≤10 mg/Nm³) have become the basic emission requirements for various projects. Key regions (such as Zhejiang, Tianjin, Hainan, Guangdong, Jiangsu, Henan, Hebei, etc.) have put forward even higher requirements. Many projects and regions must achieve "ultra-low emission" indicators, namely NOx≤50 mg/Nm³, SO₂≤10 mg/Nm³, HCl≤5 mg/Nm³, smoke≤5 mg/Nm³.

# 半干法脱酸系统的运行

# Operation of semi-dry deacidification system

• 焚烧生产线运行第1年，通过石灰浆（粉）、尿素（或氨水）和活性炭喷射量计量和烟气污染物排放浓度检测，确定烟气排放标准限值的单位垃圾处理量和石灰浆（粉）、尿素（或氨水）和活性炭最小施用量。During the first year of operation of the incineration line, the minimum dosages of lime slurry (or powder), urea (or ammonia solution), and activated carbon per unit of waste processed - required to meet flue gas emission standard limits - shall be determined through quantitative measurement of their injection rates and monitoring of flue gas pollutant concentrations.



---


# 半干法脱酸系统的运行

# Operation of semi-dry deacidification system

- 以此作为考核基准量，用于石灰浆（粉）、尿素（或氨水）和活性炭施用量的控制。This is used as the assessment benchmark to control the application amount of lime slurry (powder), urea (or ammonia water) and activated carbon.
- 消石灰、尿素（或氨水）和活性炭是去除烟气中酸性气体、重金属和二恶英等关键材料，其质量和用量直接影响反应效率和净化效果，要对采购的消石灰、尿素（或氨水）和活性炭把好质量关，控制使用量。Slaked lime, urea (or ammonia water) and activated carbon are key materials for removing acidic gases, heavy metals and dioxins in flue gas. Their quality and dosage directly affect the reaction efficiency and purification effect. The quality of the purchased slaked lime, urea (or ammonia water) and activated carbon should be strictly controlled and the usage should be controlled.



---

消石灰品质要求 Quality requirements of slaked lime

| Density               | 900 kg/m³ ～ 1100 kg/m³ | 0.090mm       | <95％         |
| Specific surface area | 15m²/g                 | 0.063mm       | <83％         |
|                       |                        | 0.032mm       | <62％         |
|                       |                        | 0.010mm       |              |

---

半干法脱酸系统的运行
# Operation of semi-dry deacidification system

- 石灰浆的喷射量应不低于基准值量（7-10 kg/t 垃圾）；The injection amount of lime slurry should not be less than the benchmark amount (7-10 kg/t garbage);
- 石灰粉喷射应具有连续性 ， 喷射前的烟气温度要控制在 140 ～ 160℃ The lime powder injection shall be continuous, with the flue gas temperature controlled at 140-160°C prior to injection.
- 石灰浆供应系统应良好维护，防止发生堵塞现象；The lime slurry supply system should be well maintained to prevent blockage;
- 及时清洗雾化喷嘴 ，喷嘴清洗时应及时投入备用喷嘴，保证脱酸塔中和反应的连续性；The atomizing nozzle shall be cleaned promptly. During nozzle cleaning, the standby nozzle shall be activated immediately to ensure the continuity of the neutralization reaction in the acid removal tower.
- 监控石灰浆雾化效果，保证石灰浆雾滴完全雾化；Monitor the atomization effect of lime slurry to ensure that the lime slurry droplets are completely atomized;
- 监控反应塔内壁结垢（挂壁）情况，发现结垢及时清理；Monitor the scaling (hanging on the wall) of the reaction tower and clean it in time if scaling is found;

半干法脱酸系统的运行
# Operation of semi-dry deacidification system

- 活性炭粉喷射量应不小于基准量量（70-100 g/t 垃圾）；The activated carbon powder injection rate shall not be less than the baseline quantity (70–100 g/t of waste).
- 维护活性炭粉供应系统，防止输送管道堵塞现象 Maintain the activated carbon powder supply system to prevent clogging in the delivery pipelines;
- 活性炭粉喷射系统应保持连续工作 ， 备用系统应保持备用工作状态； The activated carbon powder injection system shall operate continuously, and the backup system shall remain in standby mode.
- 保证活性炭喷射均匀性，定期检查喷嘴腐蚀程度；Ensure uniform distribution of activated carbon injection, and regularly inspect the nozzle wear level.

-72-

---

布袋除尘器运行

# Bag filter operation

- 使用合格布袋产品 ， 布袋材料应选用耐高温 、 耐腐蚀产品；
Only qualified bag filter products shall be used, with materials resistant to high temperatures and corrosion.
- 定期清灰 ， 清灰时要避免损坏布袋材料；
Perform regular ash cleaning while ensuring no damage to the bag filter material during the process.
- 监控布袋损坏情况 ， 及时发现 、 及时更换；
Monitor the condition of bag filters, and replace damaged ones immediately upon detection.

-73-

---


# 脱NOₓ系统运行

# DeNOₓ system operation

# 选择性催化（Selective Catalytic Reduction, SCR）

• 使烟气中NOₓ以NO为主，且比较稳定，一般条件下氧化还原速率较慢，因此需要加入催化剂

The NOx in the flue gas is primarily composed of NO, which is relatively stable. Under normal conditions, the oxidation-reduction rate is slow, so a catalyst must be added.

# 选择性非催化（Selective Non Catalytic Reduction，SNCR）

• 使用合格尿素或氨水 ，所采购尿素或氨水品质应分别符合 《尿素标准 》（ GB2440 ） 和 《液体无水氨》（GB 536）要求；

Use qualified urea or ammonia solution, with the purchased urea and ammonia quality complying with the Urea Standard (GB 2440) and Liquid Anhydrous Ammonia (GB 536), respectively.

• 根据NOₓ排放浓度和炉膛温度变化，控制尿素溶液或氨水喷射量；

Adjust the injection rate of urea solution or ammonia based on NOₓ emission levels and furnace temperature variations.



---

在线监测系统 Online monitoring system

- 监测仪表定期检验与标定； Regular inspection and calibration of monitoring instruments;
- 定期更换传感器；Regular replacement of sensors;
- 定期检查烟气取样管路； Regular inspection of flue gas sampling pipelines;

-75-

---


# 飞灰处理系统的运行

• 飞灰密闭收集、密闭存放，不得泄漏于环境中；The fly ash shall be collected and stored in a sealed manner without leakage into the environment.

• 厂内配备飞灰稳定化处理系统的，要保证飞灰稳定化处理系统的正常运行，稳定化后各项指标要满足最终处置技术要求；For facilities equipped with an on-site fly ash stabilization treatment system, its normal operation shall be ensured, and all stabilized fly ash indicators must meet the final disposal technical requirements.

• 厂内未配备飞灰稳定化处理系统的，要建立飞灰外运、暂存和处理处置登记制度，对飞灰去向和处理处置情况进行详细记录；For facilities without an on-site stabilization system, a registration system shall be established for fly ash transportation, temporary storage, and disposal, with detailed records maintained regarding its destination and treatment/disposal status.



---



# 五大污染

# Five major pollutions

- 飞灰：浓缩二噁英、重金属，现状污染极其严重；
- Fly ash: Highly concentrated with dioxins and heavy metals, causing severe pollution;
- 烟气：活性炭、石灰质量堪忧，采用碳酸氢钠，也需严格控制流程参数；
- Flue gas: Activated carbon and lime quality issues exist - even with sodium bicarbonate usage, strict process control is required;
- 渗滤液：应该零排放；即使达标，毒性也非常大；
- Leachate: Should achieve zero discharge; remains highly toxic even when meeting standards;
- 恶臭：包括硫化氢、挥发性有机物，有机酸；
- Odors: Including hydrogen sulfide, volatile organic compounds (VOCs), and organic acids;
- 炉渣：混合了锅炉灰，做建材为主，但质量较差，市场比较小；
- Slag: Mixed with boiler ash, mainly used as construction material but of poor quality with limited market demand.



---

焚烧厂运行重点环节及技术要求
# Key links and technical requirements for incineration plant operation

- 垃圾进厂计量；Waste intake weighing;
- 垃圾焚烧炉燃烧工况；Operation conditions of waste incinerators;
- 垃圾焚烧炉的起炉和停炉；Start-up and shutdown procedures of waste incinerators;
- 烟气净化系统运行工况；Operating status of flue gas purification systems;
- 烟气净化中和剂、吸收剂和吸附剂消耗量；Consumption of neutralizing agents, absorbents and adsorbents for flue gas treatment;
- 烟气在线监测系统运行状况；Operation status of continuous emission monitoring systems;
- 烟气污染物排放浓度监测仪器的校验和标定；Calibration and verification of flue gas pollutant monitoring instruments;
- 炉渣热灼减率监测；Monitoring of thermal loss ratio of bottom ash;
- 飞灰处理；Fly ash treatment;
- 渗沥液处理；Leachate treatment;
- 厂区臭味控制；Odor control within plant area.

-78-

---

垃圾进厂车辆管理及垃圾计量
# Garbage entry vehicle management and garbage measurement

监管人员每天（运行日）检查一次垃圾进厂计量是否符合要求，符合要求的签字确认，不符合要求的签发整改指令；

Supervisors shall verify compliance with waste intake weighing requirements daily. If compliant: Sign for confirmation. If non-compliant: Issue a rectification order.

# 进厂车辆管理及垃圾计量重点内容应符合以下要求：

# Key Requirements for Vehicle Management &#x26; Waste Weighing:

- 每天的垃圾进厂计量记录资料应显示各车次车辆编号和净载量，计量记录；
- Daily waste intake records must include: Vehicle ID for each load; Net weight per vehicle; Complete weighing logs
- 资料应由现场监管人员审核、签字；
- Records must be reviewed and signed by on-site supervisors.
- 建立进场垃圾车辆登记台帐，记录每个车辆的详细信息；
- Maintain a registered vehicle ledger, documenting detailed information for each truck.
- 对新增或替换更新的日常垃圾运输车辆应及时补充至垃圾车辆登记台帐中；
- Promptly update the ledger for new/replacement waste transport vehicles.
- 对未进入登记台帐的进场垃圾车辆应重点检查、检验；
- Conduct enhanced inspections for unregistered vehicles entering the facility.

-79-

---

垃圾卸料储料环节 Garbage unloading and storage

# 焚 烧 厂 卸 料 大 厅 运 行 管 理

# Operation Management Priorities for Incineration Plant Tipping Hall:

- 臭味控制和安全操作 Odor control and safe operations shall be the focal points.
- 地面应及时冲洗 Floor surfaces must be promptly washed down.
- 卸料大厅内的安全设施应保持完好 Safety facilities within the tipping hall shall be maintained in sound condition.
- 通风除臭系统应保持良好的工作状态 Ventilation &#x26; deodorization systems shall operate at optimal performance.

-80-

---


# 垃圾卸料储料环节 Garbage unloading and storage

- 卸料门应保持密封良好，卸料完毕应及时关闭；The discharge gates shall maintain proper sealing and shall be closed immediately after unloading.
- 部分焚烧炉停运后垃圾池排风除臭系统应及时投入使用，并应根据停运焚烧炉台数调节排风机风量，保证垃圾池臭味不外逸；When partial incinerators are shut down, the waste pit ventilation and deodorization system shall be activated promptly. The exhaust fan capacity shall be adjusted based on the number of idled incinerators to prevent odor escape from the waste pit.
- 全部焚烧炉停运前宜清空垃圾池，无法清空时，焚烧炉停运后应关闭所有卸料门，并及时启动垃圾池排风除臭系统；Prior to complete shutdown of all incinerators, the waste pit should ideally be emptied. If complete emptying is unachievable, all discharge gates shall be closed after incinerator shutdown, and the pit ventilation/deodorization system shall be immediately activated.
- 垃圾池排风除臭系统应保持良好工作状态，并定期检查除臭药剂或材料是否失效，若失效应及时更换；The waste pit ventilation/deodorization system shall be maintained in optimal working condition. Regular inspections shall verify the efficacy of deodorizing agents/materials, with immediate replacement if deactivation is detected.
- 应保持垃圾池底部渗沥液导排畅通；Leachate drainage channels at the waste pit base shall be kept unobstructed at all times.



---

焚烧炉进料

# Operation Requirements for Incinerator Feeding System:

- 下料喉管内的垃圾，不发生闷烧现象；Prevent smoldering of waste in the feed chute. Ensure smooth and consistent movement of the pusher mechanism.
- 推料器运动要均匀；The pusher should move evenly;
- 根据垃圾特点、余热锅炉负荷、炉渣热灼减率等情况，调节料层高度，保证炉排料层高度均匀合理；According to the characteristics of the garbage, the load of the waste heat boiler, the thermal loss rate of the slag, etc., adjust the material layer height to ensure that the height of the grate material layer is uniform and reasonable;
- 按焚烧炉设计小时处理能力，向炉内给料，不宜长期过度超负荷和过度低负荷运行；Feed the furnace according to the designed hourly processing capacity of the incinerator, and it is not suitable to operate it under excessive overload or excessive underload for a long time;

-82-

---


# 炉排燃烧工况控制

# Grate combustion condition control

焚烧炉运行期间应做好炉排燃烧工况的控制，其中包括一次风、料层高度、推料速度、炉排移动速度等控制；

During the operation of the incinerator, the grate combustion conditions should be well controlled, including the primary air, material layer height, pushing speed, grate moving speed, etc.

# 炉排上垃圾燃烧工况应做到以下几点

# The following points should be achieved for the garbage combustion conditions on the grate：

- 炉排上干燥段、燃烧段和燃烬段(以下简称“三段”)的长度控制应合理，确保垃圾燃烧完全，炉渣热灼减率应小于 5%；
- The length control of the drying section, combustion section and burnout section (hereinafter referred to as the "three sections") on the grate should be reasonable to ensure complete combustion of the garbage, and the slag thermal burn-off rate should be less than 5%;
- 炉排料层不得出现局部漏风现象；
- There should be no local air leakage in the grate material layer;
- 应结合推料速度、炉排移动速度等的调节，控制料层高度和“三段”长度
- The material layer height and the length of the "three sections" should be controlled in combination with the adjustment of the pushing speed and grate moving speed.
- “三段”的一次风风量、风压应根据各段的需要合理分配和控制；
- The primary air volume and air pressure of the "three sections" should be reasonably distributed and controlled according to the needs of each section;



---

炉排燃烧工况控制

# 炉膛燃烧工况应符合下列要求

- 炉膛应保持微负压
- 炉膛温度应均匀、稳定，并控制在850℃以上
- 炉膛内烟气应形成扰动，延长烟气在炉膛内的停留时间
- 助燃系统应保持良好备用状态，保证炉膛温度低于850℃时能立即启动助燃

-84-

---


# 炉排燃烧工况控制

# 炉膛温度监测

1. 炉膛上、中、下三个断面的温度需实时监测，并在控制电脑里形成温度变化曲线；The temperatures of the upper, middle and lower sections of the furnace need to be monitored in real time, and a temperature change curve is formed in the control computer;
2. 测温元件及仪表定期校核；The temperature measuring components and instruments are calibrated regularly;
3. 对低于850℃的时间段进行记录分析，并采取措施稳定炉温；The time period below 850℃ is recorded and analyzed, and measures are taken to stabilize the furnace temperature;



---


# 炉排燃烧工况控制

# Grate combustion condition control

# 🞑 焚烧炉停炉

# Incinerator shutdown

- 停炉前，将炉排上的垃圾燃尽，燃尽前炉膛温度应保持850℃以上，随后应按设计的降温曲线逐渐降低炉膛温度；

Before shutdown, burn out all the garbage on the grate. The furnace temperature should be kept above 850℃ before burning out, and then the furnace temperature should be gradually reduced according to the designed cooling curve;
- 助燃燃烧器；

Combustion-supporting burner;
- 定期启动助燃燃烧器，并检查助燃燃烧器能否自动运行；

Start the combustion-supporting burner regularly and check whether the combustion-supporting burner can operate automatically;



---

焚烧炉重要运行工况参数
# Important operating parameters of incinerator

🞑 炉膛温度 、 锅炉出口氧含量 、 烟 气 CO 浓 度 、 炉膛负压

Furnace temperature, boiler outlet oxygen content, flue gas CO concentration, furnace negative pressure

# ２号　ＡＣＣ運転状況

| --------- | -------- | ------------- |
| 22.5      | 1000     | 1100          |
| 20        | 900      |               |
| 17.5      | 800      |               |

-87-

---

炉渣热灼减率日常检测
# Daily detection of slag thermal loss rate

- 焚烧厂运行方对炉渣热灼减率日常检测频次，不得小于每周 2次；
The frequency of daily testing of the thermal loss rate of slag by the operator of the incineration plant shall not be less than twice a week;
- 应对每台焚烧炉炉渣分别取样和检测；
The slag of each incinerator shall be sampled and tested separately;
- 炉渣热灼减率检测所用炉渣样品，应在一天的焚烧炉渣中均匀获取，取样应在24小时内间歇进行，间隔时间不大于3小时，每次所取炉渣量不少于5kg，总取样量不少于40kg；
The slag samples used for the thermal loss rate test of slag shall be evenly obtained from the incineration slag of one day. Sampling shall be intermittent within 24 hours, with an interval of no more than 3 hours. The amount of slag taken each time shall not be less than 5kg, and the total sampling amount shall not be less than 40kg;
- 对取得的炉渣样品应先进行破碎和搅拌匀化，破碎粒度不应大于5mm；
The obtained slag samples shall be crushed and stirred for homogenization, and the crushed particle size shall not be greater than 5mm;
- 破碎、匀化后的炉渣样品，可用四分法进行缩分 ，缩分后的样品重量不得小于 2kg；
The crushed and homogenized slag samples can be divided into four parts by the quartering method, and the weight of the divided samples shall not be less than 2kg;

生活垃圾堆场 Domestic Waste Dump Site

- 无防渗 No impermeable layer;
- 无渗滤液导排与处理 No leachate collection and treatment;
- 无沼气导排与处理 No landfill gas collection and treatment;
- 无日覆盖 No daily cover;

-6-

---


# 垃圾卫生填埋

# Sanitary Landfill

•  卫生填埋是利用工程手段 ，采取有效技术措施，防止渗滤液及有害气体对水体、土壤和大气的污染，最大幅度地压实减容，随时采用膜或土覆盖，使整个过程对公共卫生安全及环境均无害的一种垃圾土地处理方法。Sanitary landfill is an engineered waste disposal method that employs effective technical measures to prevent leachate and harmful gases from polluting water bodies, soil and the atmosphere. It maximizes compaction and volume reduction, regularly applies soil or membrane cover, and ensures the entire process is safe for public health and the environment.

| 生活垃圾随意堆放                         | 随机填埋              |
| -------------------------------- | ----------------- |
| Random dumping of domestic waste | Sanitary Landfill |

# 重大变革

# Major transformation



---

垃圾卫生填埋 Sanitary Landfill

# 1. 填埋场稳定化进程与选址 Stabilization and site sitting

# 2. 填埋场防渗与作业工艺 Lining and operation

# 3. 高维填埋 High-rise landfill

# 4. 填埋气收集净化发电 Landfill gas

# 5. 矿化垃圾利用与可持续填埋 Aged refuse

# 6. 蚊蝇与恶臭控制 Flies

# 7. 渗滤液处理 Leachate

# 8. 封场与土地利用 Closure and reuse of land

-8-

---

填埋场类型 Types of Landfill Sites

# 1. 平地型填埋 Plain Landfills

在平原地形建设的填埋场 Landfills constructed on flat terrain exhibit the following characteristics:

- 填埋区外汇水面积小，易于防洪和雨水导排；Small external catchment area, facilitating flood control and stormwater drainage;
- 通常需要下挖或利用现有坑 ， 场底易出现地下水渗出；Typically require excavation or utilization of existing pits, with groundwater seepage likely at the base;
- 填埋区内单元划分较容易 ， 填埋期间易于进行雨污水分流；Relatively easy waste cell division, enabling effective separation of rainwater and leachate during operation;
- 垃圾堆高后对周围影响较明显；More noticeable environmental impact on surrounding areas when waste reaches higher elevations.

-9-

---

填埋场类型 Types of Landfill Sites

# 1. 山谷型填埋 Valley Landfills

利用山谷作为填埋库容的填埋场 utilizing natural gullies for waste containment, typically demonstrate below operational characteristics:

- 一般填埋区外汇水面积较大 ， 需在填埋区外围做好截洪和雨水导排；Large upstream catchment areas, requiring perimeter interception channels and comprehensive stormwater diversion systems;
- 可利用山谷的库容 ， 不需下挖；Natural topographic advantage, enabling direct waste deposition without base excavation (though groundwater monitoring remains essential);
- 填埋区单元划分不易，填埋期间雨污分流较为复杂；Operational complexity in waste cell delineation and challenging rainwater-leachate separation during active filling phases;
- 会对山区自然环境造成破坏；Inevitable ecosystem disruption, including vegetation loss and altered hydrological patterns in mountainous environments.

-10-

---

填埋场类型 Types of Landfill Sites

# 1. 坡地型填埋 Slope Landfills

在具有一定坡度的地形上建设的填埋场 Landfills constructed on sloped terrain and exhibit the following characteristics:

- 填埋区外上游有一定的汇水面积；An upstream catchment area exists outside the landfill zone;
- 场底有天然的坡度 ， 易于渗沥液的导排收集； The natural slope of the base facilitates leachate collection and drainage;
- 填埋区内单元划分较容易 ， 填埋期间易于进行雨污水分流； Relatively straightforward waste cell division, enabling effective rainwater-leachate separation during operation.

-11-

---

填埋场类型 Types of Landfill Sites

# 🞑 滩涂型填埋 Sea shore Landfills

在海滩或河滩上建设的填埋场 Landfills Constructed on Beaches or River Flats

- 一般场底地基较软，需要进行加固；The soft foundation typically requires ground reinforcement;
- 其余与平原填埋场类似；Other characteristics are similar to plain-type landfills.

-12-

---

卫生填埋场选址

# Sanitary Landfill Site Selection

- 制定场址预选标准，对每一个预选场址的情况对照预选标准进行评价；

Establish Preliminary Site Selection Criteria – Evaluate each potential site against the predefined criteria.
- 采用否定法剔除不符合预选标准的场址(例如地下水保护区、居民区、自然保护区等)；

Exclusion Screening (Negative Screening Method) – Eliminate sites that fail to meet mandatory requirements (e.g., groundwater protection zones, residential areas, nature reserves).
- 对预选出的场址采用肯定法对场址的环境条件（如道路交通、地域大小、地形、距居民区距离等）进行评价，选出几个初选场址；

Positive Evaluation of Candidate Sites – Assess remaining sites based on environmental conditions (e.g., road access, land area, terrain, distance from residential areas) to identify a shortlist of preliminary options.
- 对初选场址进行进一步的地形地貌调查，然后比较选出最佳场址。

Detailed Site Investigation &#x26; Final Selection – Conduct in-depth topographic and geomorphic surveys on the shortlisted sites, then compare and determine the optimal location.

-13-

---

卫生填埋场选址

# 生态保护或特别地区

• 填埋场场址不应选在国务院和国务院有关主管部门及省、自治区、直辖市人民政府划定的生态保护红线区域、永久基本农田和其他需要特别保护的区域内。Landfills shall not be located within ecological protection redlines, permanent basic farmland, or other specially protected zones designated by the State Council, relevant central authorities, or provincial-level governments.

# 洪泛区和水体缓冲区

• 填埋场不得选在江河、湖泊、运河、渠道、水库最高水位线以下的滩地和岸坡，以及长远规划中的水库等人工蓄水设施的淹没区和保护区之内。Sites are prohibited in: Tidal flats or banks below the highest water level of rivers, lakes, canals, channels, or reservoirs; Planned inundation or protection zones of future artificial water storage facilities (e.g., reservoirs).



---

# 卫生填埋场选址

# 地质灾害处

填埋场场址的选择应避开下列区域：破坏性地震及活动构造区；活动中的坍塌、滑坡和隆起地带；活动中的断裂带；废弃矿区的活动塌陷区；活动沙丘区；海啸及涌浪影响区；湿地；尚未稳定的冲积扇及冲沟地区；泥炭以及其他可能危及填埋场安全的区域。

Seismic/active tectonic zones (e.g., destructive earthquake zones, active faults); Unstable slopes (landslides, collapses, uplift areas); Abandoned mining subsidence areas; Active dunes; Tsunami/surge-affected zones; Wetlands; Unstable alluvial fans/gullies; Peatlands or other high-risk zones.

---

# 卫生填埋场选址

# 喀斯特和火成岩带

填埋场场址的选择应避开石灰岩溶洞发育带、岩浆岩区域。如果无法避开，经地质详勘确认该地区不存在所列地质结构，并经专题论证后，在设计施工阶段加强技术处理和工程措施以保证地基承载力及填埋场结构安全的前提下，可作为填埋场备选场址。

Avoid limestone karst caves and magmatic rock regions. Exceptions may apply only if: Detailed geological surveys confirm no hazardous structures exist; Technical feasibility studies demonstrate safety; Enhanced engineering measures (e.g., foundation reinforcement) ensure structural integrity.

选址考察标准 Site Selection Evaluation Criteria

# 1. 卫生防护距离 Distance to the Villages

# 2. 工程地质 、 水文地质 、 水文地理 Engineering Geology, Hydrogeology, and Hydrography

# 3. 天然水体保护 Protection of Natural Water Bodies

# 4. 空气、噪音及土壤 Air Quality, Noise, and Soil Impacts

# 5. 交通和居住区情况 Transportation Accessibility and Proximity to Residential Areas

# 6. 对其它行业的影响 Impacts on Other Industries

# 7. 其它因素 Other Relevant Factors

-17-

---


# 填埋场应根据当地自然条件合理设置以下设施

Landfills shall be equipped with the following facilities based on local natural conditions:

- 计量设施、垃圾坝、防渗系统、渗滤液收集和导排系统、渗滤液处理系统、
防洪系统、雨污分流系统、地下水收集导排系统。

Measurement facilities; Waste dam; Impermeable system; Leachate collection and drainage system;
Leachate treatment system; Flood control system; Rainwater and sewage diversion system;
Groundwater collection and drainage system
- 填埋气体导排及处理系统、覆盖和封场系统、环境监测设施、应急设施及其他公用工程和配套设施。

Landfill gas extraction and treatment system; Cover and closure system; Environmental monitoring facilities;
Emergency facilities; Other public utilities and auxiliary facilities.
- 填埋场应建设围墙或栅栏等隔离设施，并在填埋区边界周围设置防飞散设施、安全防护设施、
防火隔离带以及绿化隔离带。

Landfills shall be enclosed with walls, fences, or other isolation barriers. Additionally, the following measures
shall be implemented along the landfill boundary: Anti-scattering facilities; Safety protection facilities;
Firebreaks; Green buffer zones.

# 防渗层构造

# Structure of lining system

适应于生活垃圾卫生填埋场高浓度、高污染、高不稳定沉降状况：

确保稳定寿命大于50年。Designed for high-load, high-contaminant, and differential settlement conditions in municipal solid waste sanitary landfills, guaranteeing a minimum 50-year structural integrity.



---

垂直防渗 Vertical Barrier Systems

• 垂直防渗是对于填埋区地下有不透水层的填埋场而言的，垂直防渗是指在这种填埋场的填埋区四周建垂直防渗幕墙，幕墙深入至不透水层，使填埋区内的地下水与填埋区外的地下水隔离开，防止场外地下水受到污染；

Vertical Barrier Systems are applicable to landfills where an impermeable layer exists beneath the site. This technique involves constructing vertical cutoff walls around the landfill perimeter, extending down to the impermeable stratum, thereby isolating groundwater inside the landfill from external aquifers to prevent off-site contamination.

• 垂直防渗主要用于山谷型填埋场，前提条件是山谷必须是独立的水文地质单元；

Most suitable for valley-type landfills (requiring the valley to function as an independent hydrogeological unit).

• 垂直防渗的优点就是投资小（山谷型填埋场）；

Lower capital investment (especially for valley-type landfills).

• 缺点是防渗幕墙的效果不保证；

Limitations: Unreliable containment performance (no guaranteed long-term sealing efficacy).

垂直防渗案例 Vertical Cutoff Wall Construction Specifications

• 垂直防渗采用直径650mm水泥膨润土搅拌桩，在垃圾堆场四周设置垂直防渗墙；The vertical barrier system employs 650mm-diameter cement-bentonite mixing piles installed around the perimeter of the waste disposal area to form an impermeable cutoff wall.

• 水泥土搅拌桩深度平均约为15m，总长度 920.8m，搅拌桩中水泥掺量 20%； Key Parameters: Average pile depth: 15m; Total wall length: 920.8m; Cement content: 20% by weight.

# 搅拌桩技术要求如下 Technical Requirements:

- • 三轴水泥土搅拌桩直径D=650mm，桩间搭接长度200 mm；Triple-axis mixing piles: Diameter (D): 650mm; Overlap between adjacent piles: 200mm.
- • 水泥浆液质量标准应符合 “水工混凝土施工规范”(SDJ207-82)标准要求；Grout quality: Cement slurry shall comply with "Specifications for Hydraulic Concrete Construction" (SDJ207-82) standards.
- • 土体加固后，搅拌土体28天抗压强度不小于设计强度；Soil-cement strength: 28-day unconfined compressive strength of stabilized soil must meet or exceed design requirements.

---

水泥搅拌桩 Soil-Cement Mixing Piles

- 水泥搅拌桩渗透系数能达到10⁻⁶ cm/s 级 The permeability coefficient of soil-cement piles shall achieve 10⁻⁶ cm/s level；
- 搭接施工的相邻桩的施工间歇时间应不超过 10～16小时；Time interval between adjacent overlapping piles must be 10-16 hours maximum to ensure proper bonding.
- 孔位放样误差小于2cm，钻孔深度误差小于5cm，桩深垂直度按设计要求，误差不大于 1/200 桩长； Surveying tolerance: &#x3C;2cm for borehole positioning; Drilling accuracy: &#x3C;5cm depth deviation; Verticality: ≤1/200 of pile length (as designed)
- 垂直防渗水泥土搅拌桩深度平均约为15m，库区四周打设垂直防渗帷幕深入相对不透水层不小于 2m，与场底的相对不透水层形成相对封闭系统； Barrier System Configuration: Average pile depth: 15m; Cutoff curtain extends ≥2m into the confining layer on all sides; Forms integrated containment system with basal impermeable stratum.
- 可有效防止垃圾渗滤液向非填埋区的水平渗透，降低垃圾渗滤液对周围地下水的风险影响 。 This engineered barrier can effectively block lateral migration of landfill leachate; Significantly reduces groundwater contamination risks in peripheral areas.

水平防渗 Horizontal Impermeable Liner System

水平防渗是在填埋场的场底及侧边铺设人工防渗材料或天然防渗材料，以防填埋场内的污水渗入地下。 The horizontal impermeable liner system involves laying artificial or natural barrier materials at the base and side slopes of landfills to prevent leachate infiltration into subsurface environments.

- 应用最多的人工防渗材料是高密度聚乙烯膜：渗透系数小、抗拉强度大、抗穿刺能力强、耐老化、耐紫外线等优点；Most widely applied artificial liner material is High-Density Polyethylene (HDPE) Geomembrane: Ultra-low permeability coefficient (&#x3C;10⁻¹³ cm/s); High tensile strength; Superior puncture resistance; UV/aging stability
- 水平防渗材料还可采用一些天然材料，如黏土、膨润土及沥青混凝土等；Natural Barrier Materials, Compacted clay liners (CCL): Minimum 0.6m thickness with k≤1×10⁻⁷ cm/s; Bentonite-amended soil: Hydraulic conductivity ≤5×10⁻⁹ cm/s (per GRI-GCL3); Asphalt concrete: 8-12cm layers with k≤1×10⁻⁸ cm/s.

-29-

---

水平防渗 Horizontal Impermeable Liner System

• 确定HDPE膜作为库区水平防渗关键材料。Primary Liner Material Selection: HDPE geomembrane confirmed as key horizontal barrier material for the containment area.

• 选择粘土保护层与土工膜的人工水平（复合）防渗结构, 提出人工水平（复合）防渗基本结构：单层≥1.5 mm厚HDPE土工膜; 粘土保护层0.6 m厚，k≤5×10⁻7cm/s. Adopted engineered composite liner system consisting of: 1.5mm-thick HDPE geomembrane (minimum single-layer thickness); 0.6m compacted clay protective layer with permeability coefficient k≤5×10⁻⁷ cm/s.

• 解决渗滤液对地下水二次污染问题 。 The above design effectively addresses secondary groundwater contamination from leachate infiltration.

• 填埋库区基础层底部应与地下水年最高水位保持 1 m及以上的距离。当填埋区基础层底部与地下水年最高水位距离不足 1 m时应建设地下水收集导排系统。Groundwater Protection Requirements: Base elevation criteria: Landfill base must maintain ≥1m clearance above annual highest groundwater table; Mitigation measures: When clearance &#x3C;1m, mandatory installation of groundwater collection and drainage system.

# 水平防渗 Horizontal Lining System

-34-

---

水平防渗基本要求
# Basic requirements for horizontal anti-seepage

填埋场应根据填埋区天然基础层的地质情况选择单人工复合衬层或双人工复合衬层。Landfills shall adopt either a single composite liner or a double composite liner based on the geological conditions of the natural foundation layer in the landfill area.

如果天然基础层饱和渗透系数不大于 1.0×10⁻⁵ cm/s，且厚度不小于2m，可采用单人工复合衬层 。 并应满足以下条件： If the saturated permeability coefficient of the natural foundation layer is ≤ 1.0×10⁻⁵ cm/s and its thickness is ≥ 2 m, a single composite liner may be used, provided the following conditions are met:

1. 采用高密度聚乙烯膜作为人工合成材料衬层的 ，其厚度不小于 2.0 mm；If high-density polyethylene (HDPE) geomembrane is used as the synthetic liner, its thickness must be ≥ 2.0 mm;
2. 人工合成材料衬层下应具有厚度不小于0.75 m，且其压实后饱和渗透系数不大于1.0×10⁻⁷ cm/s 的天然或改性粘土防渗衬层； Beneath the synthetic liner, there must be a natural or modified clay anti-seepage layer with a thickness of ≥ 0.75 m and a compacted saturated permeability coefficient of ≤ 1.0×10⁻⁷ cm/s.

-35-

---

水平防渗基本要求
# Basic requirements for horizontal anti-seepage

➢ 如果天然基础层饱和渗透系数大于1.0×10⁻⁵ cm/s，或天然基础层厚度小于2m，应采用双人工复合衬层，并应满足以下条件：

If the saturated permeability coefficient of the natural foundation layer exceeds 1.0×10⁻⁵ cm/s, or if its thickness is less than 2 m, a double composite liner system must be adopted, meeting the following requirements:

1. 采用高密度聚乙烯膜作为人工合成材料衬层的，主防渗衬层厚度不小于2.0 mm，次防渗衬层厚度不小于1.5 mm；
If high-density polyethylene (HDPE) geomembranes are used as synthetic liners: The primary liner thickness must be ≥ 2.0 mm; The secondary liner thickness must be ≥ 1.5 mm;
2. 人工合成材料衬层下应具有厚度不小于0.75 m，且其被压实后的饱和渗透系数不大于1.0×10⁻⁷ cm/s的天然或改性粘土防渗衬层；
Beneath each synthetic liner, there must be a natural or modified clay anti-seepage layer with: A thickness of ≥ 0.75 m; A compacted saturated permeability coefficient of ≤ 1.0×10⁻⁷ cm/s;
3. 双人工复合衬层之间应布设液体导排层作为渗漏检测层；
A leachate collection and detection layer must be installed between the two composite liners to monitor leaks.

-36-

---

生活垃圾卫生填埋场防渗技术

# Technical Requirements for Anti-Seepage Systems in Municipal Solid Waste (MSW) Sanitary Landfills

至少单层HDPE膜，膜厚度1.5 mm；Minimum Requirement: Single-layer HDPE geomembrane with thickness ≥1.5mm

推荐双层 HDPE 膜 ， 上层 2 mm ， 下层 1.5 mm ， 中间采用 0.6 m 粘土隔开 Recommended Standard: Double-layer HDPE system: Upper layer: 2.0mm; Lower layer: 1.5mm; Separated by 0.6m compacted clay interlayer.

最佳防渗是双层HDPE+垂直防渗；Optimal Configuration: Composite system combining: Double HDPE liners (2.0mm+1.5mm); Vertical cutoff wall (anti-seepage curtain).

HDPE上下必须采用粘土、土工布保护；Both surfaces of HDPE must be protected with: Clay layers (minimum 0.75m compacted) Geotextile cushion layers.

---

渗滤液导排 Leachate Drainage

• 及时有效地将垃圾填埋场内的渗滤液从填埋场内导排出来，尽量降低填埋场内的水位，减小渗滤液在填埋场内的停留时间，以防渗滤液渗透至地下； Implement timely and effective leachate drainage to maintain low hydraulic head levels; minimize leachate retention time and prevent groundwater contamination.

• 渗滤液导排工程主要由场底水平导流盲沟和竖向导流井组成； The leachate drainage project mainly consists of horizontal drainage blind ditches at the bottom of the field and vertical drainage wells;

渗滤液导排 Leachate Drainage

# 1. First layer leachate collection pipe

第一层（主）淋滤液收集管

高维填埋 High-dimensional landfill

🞑 传统填埋，填埋高度偏低，土地利用率较差。

Traditional landfill has a low landfill height and poor land utilization rate.

🞑 基于生活垃圾抗弯曲、矿化垃圾抗压、水平防渗与垂直防渗抗拉、堆体文丘里泵排水的高维填埋。

High-dimensional landfill based on the bending resistance of domestic waste, compression resistance of mineralized waste, horizontal and vertical anti-seepage and tensile resistance, and Venturi pump drainage of the pile.

生活垃圾填埋高度提高2-3倍以上

The height of domestic waste landfill has increased by more than 2-3 times.

---

地下水监测要求

# Groundwater Monitoring Requirements

本底井1眼，设在填埋场地下水流向上游30-50 m 处；

Background Well, Quantity: 1 well; Location: 30-50 m upstream of the landfill along the groundwater flow direction.

污染扩散井不少于4眼，分别设在垂直填埋场地下水走向的两侧各30-100 m处；

Contaminant Diffusion Wells, Quantity: ≥4 wells; Location: positioned 30-100 m on both sides perpendicular to the groundwater flow path; Evenly distributed along landfill boundaries.

污染监视井不少于3眼，分别设在填埋场地下水流向下游30、50、100m处；

Contaminant Monitoring Wells, Quantity: ≥3 wells; Location: Downstream of landfill along groundwater flow; Specific distances: 30 m, 50 m, and 100 m from landfill edge.

-47-

---

地下水监测要求

# Groundwater Monitoring Requirements

- 设置地下水收集导排系统的应在导排管出口处设置1处污染监测井，无地下水收集导排系统时无需设置；

Monitoring Well Configuration, With groundwater collection/drainage system: Install 1 contaminant monitoring well at the drainage pipe outlet; Without groundwater collection/drainage system: No monitoring well required.
- 地下水监测指标为pH值、总硬度、溶解性总固体、高锰酸盐指数、氨氮、硝酸盐、亚硝酸盐、硫酸盐、氯化物、挥发性酚类、氰化物、砷、汞、六价铬、铅、氟、镉、铁、锰、铜、锌、总大肠菌群。

pH, Total Hardness, Total Dissolved Solids (TDS), Permanganate Index (CODMn); Ammonia Nitrogen, Nitrate, Nitrite; Sulfate, Chloride, Volatile Phenols, Cyanide; Arsenic (As), Mercury (Hg), Hexavalent Chromium (Cr⁶⁺), Lead (Pb), Cadmium (Cd); Total Coliforms.

-48-

---

卫生防疫与环境监测
# Sanitation, Epidemic Prevention, and Environmental Monitoring

# 环境监测

# 大气监测

监测点不少于4个，1月监测1次，测颗粒物、甲烷、硫化氢、氨等；

Atmospheric Monitoring, Monitoring Points: ≥4 locations; Frequency: Monthly; Parameters: Particulate matter (PM10/PM2.5); Methane (CH₄); Hydrogen sulfide (H₂S); Ammonia (NH₃).

# 填埋气体监测

测点选在填埋气体排放口和甲烷易聚集的地方；监测频率根据情况确定；

Landfill Gas Monitoring, Monitoring Points: Landfill gas venting outlets; Methane accumulation-prone areas (e.g., subsurface utilities, enclosed spaces); Frequency: Adjusted based on gas production rates and risk assessment.

# 地下水监测

不少于5点（本地井1个、污染扩散井2个、污染监测井2个）；每年不少于3次；监测项目按标准要求23项（重点是浊度、色度、化学需氧量、总悬浮物、大肠菌值、细菌总数等）；

Groundwater Monitoring, Monitoring Wells: ≥5 wells; Background well: 1 (upstream); Contaminant diffusion wells: 2 (lateral); Contaminant monitoring wells: 2 (downstream); Frequency: ≥3 times annually.

-49-

---

卫生防疫与环境监测
# Sanitation, Epidemic Prevention, and Environmental Monitoring

# 环境监测

# 排水监测

监测点在排水口；连续排放口要每天监测或在线监测；监测项目 12 项；

Drainage Water Monitoring; Monitoring Point: Discharge outlet; Continuous discharge outlets: Daily monitoring or real-time online monitoring; 12 key indicators (including pH, COD, SS, etc.)

# 渗滤液监测

监测点在调节池进水口；每月不少于 1 次；监测项目 18 项

Leachate Monitoring, Monitoring Point: Inlet of the equalization tank; Frequency: ≥1 time per month; Parameters: 18 key indicators (including BOD₅, NH₃-N, heavy metals, etc.)

# 苍蝇密度监测

监测点不少于 10 个；夏季每月 2 次 ;

Fly Density Monitoring; Monitoring Points: ≥10 locations (covering working area, leachate collection points, etc.); Frequency: Summer season: 2 times per month; Other seasons: Adjust based on actual conditions.
