# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 8.3 Construction of domestic garbage landfill

# 生活垃圾填埋场施工技术

Yang Lei

2026-04-20

---

填埋场库区土基清理、碾压方法及要求
# Landfill Cell Base Preparation Technical Specifications

1. 填埋库区底先用平地机刮平、水准仪找平、人工配合清理平整，并夯实紧密，场地内杂草、石头、杂物及表层虚土应彻底清除, 清理完的场底若见植物深根应人工拔除。
First, the bottom of the landfill area should be scraped flat with a grader, leveled with a leveler, cleaned and leveled by manual labor, and compacted tightly. Weeds, stones, debris and loose soil on the surface of the site should be completely removed. If deep roots of plants are seen at the bottom of the site after cleaning, they should be removed manually.
2. 填方用土应尽量采用粉质粘土，并层层夯实，压实密度不低于0.95 g/cm³；回填土的颗粒大小、均匀程度、以及化学兼容性都应得到监理工程师认可，钙质过高不能作为回填土使用;
The backfill soil should be made of silty clay as much as possible and compacted layer by layer, with a compaction density of no less than 0.95 g/cm³; the particle size, uniformity, and chemical compatibility of the backfill soil should be approved by the supervision engineer, and soil with too high calcium content cannot be used as backfill soil;
3. 对棱角较大的岩石区域，土工膜铺设前应先将岩石区域处理平整，然后用水泥砂浆抹平;
For rock areas with large edges and corners, the rock area should be leveled before laying the geomembrane, and then smoothed with cement mortar.

-52-

---

填埋场库区土基清理、碾压方法及要求

在槽底先在场底用手锤钉小方木(小方木长5cm，2㎝×2㎝见方)，小方木间距为5m，梅花式，用水准仪将槽底设计标高+20㎝高程线，刻画在小方木上，工长亲自检查槽底标高，高处用人工铲平，低处用挖槽的种植土筛完后垫至设计标高。

At the bottom of the trench, first nail small square wood (5cm long, 2cm×2cm square) with a hand hammer, the spacing of the small square wood is 5m, plum blossom style, and the design elevation of the trench bottom + 20cm elevation line is engraved on the small square wood with a level. The foreman personally checks the elevation of the trench bottom, and the high places are manually leveled, and the low places are sieved with the planting soil from the trench to the design elevation.

-53-

---

# 填埋场库区土基清理、碾压方法及要求

⑤ 用12t碾压机排压后，通过取样试验达到设计要求，基面必须平整，无开裂，无明显的尖突、凹陷，其平整度在允许的范围内平缓变化，坡度均匀，坡向一致，符合图纸要求。

After the pressure is discharged by a 12t roller, the design requirements are met through sampling tests. The base surface must be flat, without cracks, obvious protrusions, and depressions. Its flatness changes gently within the allowable range, the slope is uniform, and the slope direction is consistent, meeting the requirements of the drawings.

⑥ 对于基坑的边坡按图纸的平面尺寸，在坑底的下端及上端钉小方木挂线，每10～15m挂一趟线，按线将边坡修平整，基面上的阴阳角处做到圆滑过渡半径&#x3C;10cm，边坡表面无砖石块，无颗粒状硬杂质，无树枝，无杂草等，整个基坑表面通过验收合格再进行下一道工序施工。

For the slope of the foundation pit, according to the plane dimensions of the drawings, small square wood hanging lines are nailed at the lower and upper ends of the pit bottom. The lines are hung every 10 to 15 meters, and the slope is leveled according to the lines. The corners on the base surface have a smooth transition radius of &#x3C;10cm. There are no bricks or stones, no granular hard impurities, no branches, no weeds, etc. on the surface of the slope. The entire foundation pit surface has been accepted before the next construction process can be carried out.

---


# 膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求

# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

① 膨润土毯(GCL) 及HDPE土工膜铺设前应请监理工程师对现场条件进行全面确认，保证填埋库区的基础表面平整，没有凹凸不平现象，裂缝、无尖刺颗粒、硬杂物等存在，无可能破坏土工膜的各种异物，并对防渗材料的质量(性能指标：表面是否有气泡、孔洞、皱纹、破损等)进行严格检查，确认无误后方可进行铺设。

Before laying bentonite blanket (GCL) and HDPE geomembrane, the supervision engineer should be invited to fully confirm the site conditions to ensure that the foundation surface of the landfill area is flat, without unevenness, cracks, sharp particles, hard debris, etc., and there are no foreign objects that may damage the geomembrane. The quality of the anti-seepage material (performance indicators: whether there are bubbles, holes, wrinkles, damage, etc. on the surface) should be strictly checked. Only after confirmation can it be laid.



---


# 膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求

# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

膨润土毯及HDPE土工膜验收检验的取样应按连续生产同一牌号原料、同一配方、同一规格、同一工艺的产品，检验项目按照膨润土毯及HDPE土工膜性能内容执行，配套的颗粒膨润土粉应使用生产商推荐的、与膨润土毯中相同的钠基膨润土，检查在运输过程中有无破损，断裂等现象，须验明产品标识。

The sampling of bentonite blanket and HDPE geomembrane acceptance inspection should be based on the continuous production of products with the same brand of raw materials, the same formula, the same specifications, and the same process. The inspection items are carried out according to the performance content of bentonite blanket and HDPE geomembrane. The matching granular bentonite powder should use the sodium-based bentonite recommended by the manufacturer and the same as that in the bentonite blanket. Check whether there is damage or breakage during transportation, and the product identification must be verified.



---


# 膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求

# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

1. 铺设每卷材料应进行编号，并按顺序进行铺设。土工膜编号后由监理工程师存档，以便检测。
Each roll of material should be numbered and laid in sequence. The geomembrane will be numbered and filed by the supervising engineer for inspection.
2. 现场施工的土工膜材料不得长时间暴露，并远离火源，膨润土防水毯的储存及配套的膨润土粉都必须用塑料布或防渗膜遮盖，铺设的膨润土防水毯不得无遮盖过夜，避免过早水化。
The geomembrane materials used in on-site construction shall not be exposed for a long time and shall be kept away from fire sources. The storage of bentonite waterproof blankets and the matching bentonite powder must be covered with plastic cloth or anti-seepage membrane. The laid bentonite waterproof blankets shall not be left uncovered overnight to avoid premature hydration.
3. 在铺设过程中，工作人员不得穿对膨润土防水毯、土工膜有损伤的鞋子，不得在铺设现场吸烟和进行其它能损坏膨润土防水毯，土工膜的活动。
During the laying process, workers shall not wear shoes that may damage the bentonite waterproof blankets and geomembranes, and shall not smoke or engage in other activities that may damage the bentonite waterproof blankets and geomembranes at the laying site.



---


# 膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求

# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

1. 铺设膨润土毯前应注意铺设的正反方向，铺设时应尽量减少膨润土毯在地基上的拖拉，以免膨润土毯受到损坏，如有需要，可以在地面上加放一层临时的土工织物，以减少摩擦。
Before laying the bentonite blanket, attention should be paid to the positive and negative directions of laying. During laying, the dragging of the bentonite blanket on the foundation should be minimized to avoid damage to the bentonite blanket. If necessary, a temporary layer of geotextile can be placed on the ground to reduce friction.
2. 对铺设好的防渗材料应及时压放土袋，以防被风刮走。
The laid anti-seepage materials should be pressed and released in time with earth bags to prevent them from being blown away by the wind.
3. 土工膜室外施工应在气温5℃以上，风力四级以下，并无雨、无雪的天气进行；
Outdoor construction of geomembrane should be carried out in weather with a temperature above 5℃, wind force below level 4, and no rain or snow;
4. 不允许任何车辆直接在膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜及无纺布上行驶；
No vehicle is allowed to drive directly on bentonite blanket, HDPE geomembrane and non-woven fabric;



---

膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求

# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

1. 为避免由于温度变化导致材料的收缩、皱纹现象或使材料产生应力，因此在征得监理工程师的同意后，应对铺设完的HDPE土工膜及时进行焊接，经检验合格后，铺设保护材料；
2. In order to avoid shrinkage, wrinkles or stress of materials due to temperature changes, the laid HDPE geomembrane should be welded in time after obtaining the consent of the supervising engineer, and protective materials should be laid after passing the inspection;
3. HDPE土工膜在焊接施工过程中，应及时请监理工程师对操作程序、铺设和焊接质量进行检查，发现有质量问题应及时修补；
4. During the welding construction of HDPE geomembrane, the supervising engineer should be asked to check the operating procedures, laying and welding quality in time, and any quality problems should be repaired in time;
5. 土工膜中间平台锚固沟及坡顶锚固沟不应该有树根、石块及较大颗粒杂物，应整理平整夯实；
6. The anchor trench of the middle platform of the geomembrane and the anchor trench on the top of the slope should not have tree roots, stones and larger particles, and should be leveled and compacted.

-59-

---

膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求

# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

⑬ 膨润土毯纵向的拱接长度不能小于150mm，如果膨润土毯的末端的无纺布不是槽状切割， 就需要在拱接区使用膨润土补强。膨润土毯的横向末端搭接至少需要600mm长。所有针织加劲的横向末端搭接处都需要使用膨润土补强。补强的膨润土用量不应小于0.4kg/m。

The longitudinal arch length of the bentonite blanket shall not be less than 150mm. If the non-woven fabric at the end of the bentonite blanket is not cut in a groove shape, bentonite reinforcement is required in the arch area. The lateral end overlap of the bentonite blanket needs to be at least 600 mm long. All lateral end overlaps of knitted reinforcements need to be reinforced with bentonite. The amount of bentonite used for reinforcement should not be less than 0.4kg/m.

⑭ 当膨润土毯受到损坏时，可在新的膨润土毯上切割一块进行补丁，补丁的四边距离破损的地方长度不能小300mm，铺放“补丁”前应在破损周围撒一些颗粒状膨润土或膨润土浆，如有必要也可以使一些粘合剂以防止 “补丁” 移位。

When the bentonite blanket is damaged, a patch can be cut on the new bentonite blanket for patching. The length of the four sides of the patch should not be less than 300mm from the damaged area. Before laying the "patch", some granular bentonite or bentonite slurry should be sprinkled around the damage. If necessary, some adhesive can also be used to prevent the "patch" from shifting.

-60-

---

膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求
# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

# ⑮ HDPE土工膜焊接位置及焊接技术的要求

# Requirements for welding position and welding technology of HDPE geomembrane：

- 边坡上土工膜焊接布置时应使搭接缝平行于边坡的方向，边坡横向搭接缝位置应离开坡面底的边线1.0米以上；
When arranging the welding of geomembrane on the slope, the lap joint should be parallel to the direction of the slope, and the position of the horizontal lap joint of the slope should be more than 1.0 meter away from the edge line of the bottom of the slope;
- 在焊接设备焊接的试样未通过试焊检查或监理工程师确认之前，不得开始正式焊接；
Formal welding shall not be started before the sample welded by the welding equipment passes the test welding inspection or is confirmed by the supervision engineer;
- 焊接前必须将土工膜表面灰土、污物等异物清洁干净；
Before welding, the dust, dirt and other foreign matter on the surface of the geomembrane must be cleaned;
- 两焊接土工膜的重叠部分不得少于10㎝；
The overlapping part of the two welded geomembranes shall not be less than 10cm;

-61-

---

膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求
# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

# ⑮ HDPE土工膜焊接位置及焊接技术的要求

# Requirements for welding position and welding technology of HDPE geomembrane：

- 温度低于5℃或高于40℃时，施工单位应提供与规定温度范围内同等焊接质量的书面说明，并请监理工程师确认后，方可进行施工；
When the temperature is lower than 5℃ or higher than 40℃, the construction unit shall provide a written description of the same welding quality as within the specified temperature range, and ask the supervision engineer to confirm before construction;
- 土工膜焊接形式采用双焊缝搭接，挤压式焊接使用在修复(如修补孔洞)，且熔焊设备达不到的地方；
The geomembrane welding form adopts double weld lap, and extrusion welding is used for repair (such as repairing holes) and in places where fusion welding equipment cannot reach;
- 土工膜焊缝联结强度应大于母材料强度；
The joint strength of the geomembrane weld should be greater than the strength of the parent material;

-62-

---

膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求
# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

⑯ 施工现场应具备足够焊接设备 ， 确保焊接施工连续进行 。 The construction site should be equipped with sufficient welding equipment to ensure continuous welding construction.

⑰ 施工现场应设有检测实验室，并配备有必要的检测设备，且现场检测应对 HDPE土工膜焊缝进行非破坏性试验(如充气和真空试验等 )和破坏性试验 ( 如剪切剥离试验等 ) 。 The construction site should have a testing laboratory equipped with necessary testing equipment, and the on-site inspection should conduct non-destructive tests (such as inflation and vacuum tests, etc.) and destructive tests (such as shear peeling tests, etc.) on the HDPE geomembrane welds.

⑱ 土工膜焊接现场检测完毕，应立即对检测时所做的充气和真空试验造成的穿孔，采用挤压焊缝法补堵。After the on-site inspection of geomembrane welding is completed, the perforations caused by the inflation and vacuum tests during the inspection should be immediately repaired by the extrusion welding method.

-63-

---


# 膨润土毯、HDPE土工膜和无纺布铺设施工要求

# Construction requirements for laying bentonite blankets, HDPE geomembranes, and non-woven fabrics

1. 对于虚焊、漏焊的接缝应及时补焊，并应对补焊部位进行真空检测。
For joints with false welding or leaking welding, repair welding should be done in time, and vacuum detection should be carried out on the repaired welding parts.
2. 施工单位必须有完善的施工组织计划，根据施工现场、GCL和HDPE土工膜的尺寸进行合理布置材料，减少土工膜的损耗。
The construction unit must have a complete construction organization plan, arrange materials reasonably according to the construction site, the size of GCL and HDPE geomembrane, and reduce the loss of geomembrane.
3. 防渗系统施工需有 GCL 及 HDPE 土工膜供货商指导施工或安装。
The construction of the anti-seepage system requires the guidance of GCL and HDPE geomembrane suppliers for construction or installation.



---

粘土保护防渗层施工要求
# Construction requirements for clay protection and anti-seepage layer

1. 施工前对粘土进行精心挑选，彻底清除粘土中的石子，树根、树枝、玻璃渣、钢筋头、及其它尖锐物体等杂物，防止破坏HDPE土工膜和无纺土工布。

Carefully select the clay before construction, and thoroughly remove stones, tree roots, branches, glass slag, steel bar heads, and other sharp objects in the clay to prevent damage to the HDPE geomembrane and non-woven geotextile.
2. 对于干硬的粘土块，设专人清除，防止在夯实或用轻型压路机碾压时破坏HDPE土工膜和无纺土工布。

For dry and hard clay blocks, assign a dedicated person to remove them to prevent damage to the HDPE geomembrane and non-woven geotextile during compaction or rolling with a light roller.

-65-

---

粘土保护防渗层施工要求
# Construction requirements for clay protection and anti-seepage layer

③ 对粘土的含水量严格控制，进场后即请专业技术人员对现场的粘土最佳含水量进行检测，以保证碾压夯实的密度达到设计要求。对含水量大的粘土进行晾晒，含水量小于最佳含水量的要洒水，由自卸车将粘土运至上下坡道上，然后用手推车及人工回填。

The moisture content of the clay is strictly controlled. After entering the site, professional technicians are invited to test the optimal moisture content of the clay on site to ensure that the density of rolling and compaction meets the design requirements. Clay with high moisture content is aired, and clay with a moisture content lower than the optimal moisture content is sprinkled with water. The clay is transported to the up and down ramps by dump trucks, and then backfilled with wheelbarrows and manual work.

④ 施工方向顺无纺土工布施工方向施工，主要用人力车运土，并在土上铺跳板或钢模板，在上面用手推车运土。沿施工方向展开，以防止破坏防渗层和无纺土工布。

The construction direction is along the construction direction of the non-woven geotextile. The soil is mainly transported by rickshaw, and a springboard or steel formwork is laid on the soil, and the soil is transported by wheelbarrow on it. Expand along the construction direction to prevent damage to the anti-seepage layer and non-woven geotextile.



---

沙砾石层与碎石层铺设工艺
# Sand and gravel layer and crushed stone layer laying process

1. 渗滤液管为 HDPE φ250、φ350 塑料管；

The leachate pipe is a HDPE φ250, φ350 plastic pipe;
2. 施工顺序为：防渗膜保护层验收 → 清扫施工面 → 安装 → 检查 → 验收；

The construction sequence is: acceptance of the anti-seepage membrane protective layer → cleaning the construction surface → installation → inspection → acceptance;
3. 施工工艺为：填埋场主次渗滤液管按平面图位置放线，不得碰坏防渗膜，若有损坏必须及时修补好，砾石及粗砂保护层必须均匀铺设，碾压密实，其厚度及坡度满足设计要求，沟槽处回填土必须分层夯实，回填密实达90%以上；

The construction process is: the primary and secondary leachate pipes of the landfill are laid out according to the plan position, and the anti-seepage membrane must not be damaged. If damaged, it must be repaired in time. The graded gravel and coarse sand protective layer must be evenly laid and compacted. Its thickness and slope meet the design requirements. The backfill soil in the trench must be compacted layer by layer, and the backfill density must reach more than 90%;

---

沙砾石层与碎石层铺设工艺
# Sand and gravel layer and crushed stone layer laying process

# ④ 主要施工质量措施

- 管道放线依据填埋坑位置及环场道路中心线、规划桩位及坐标值定位后再行施工；

The pipeline layout is based on the location of the landfill pit and the center line of the ring road, the planned pile position and the coordinate value before construction;
- 施工前必须对填埋坑底高程进行复测，并检查防渗层及防渗层以上垫土是否符合要求，无误后再行施工；

Before construction, the elevation of the bottom of the landfill pit must be re-measured, and the anti-seepage layer and the cushion soil above the anti-seepage layer must be checked to see if they meet the requirements. Construction can only be carried out after they are correct;
- HDPE渗沥管网的施工操作必须符合设计要求，施工前必须对前项工程进行验收，合格后方可进行管网的安装施工，并在施工过程中根据工程顺序进行质量验收；

The construction operation of the HDPE seepage pipe network must meet the design requirements. Before construction, the previous project must be inspected and accepted. Only after passing the inspection can the installation and construction of the pipe network be carried out, and quality inspection shall be carried out according to the project sequence during the construction process;

-68-

---

沙砾石层与碎石层铺设工艺
# Sand and gravel layer and crushed stone layer laying process

# ④ 主要施工质量措施 Main construction quality measures

- 所用材料必须符合设计要求 ，且具有质量证明文件，HDPE管材必须使用HDPE防渗膜相同的原料及配方制造，使用前必须进行检查，质量合格，并有证明文件，不合格或无证明文件的不得使用；

The materials used must meet the design requirements and have quality certification documents. HDPE pipes must be made of the same raw materials and formula as HDPE anti-seepage membranes. They must be inspected before use and have qualified quality and certification documents. Unqualified or no certification documents shall not be used;
- 基础必须符合设计要求，穿膜管根部防渗元件施工前，必须经监理试水，检验观察24h无渗漏，签字后方可施工。

The foundation must meet the design requirements. Before the construction of the anti-seepage element at the root of the membrane pipe, it must be tested by the supervisor, and there must be no leakage for 24 hours. Construction can only be carried out after signing.



---

沙砾石层与碎石层铺设工艺
# Sand and gravel layer and crushed stone layer laying process

# ④ 主要施工质量措施

✓ 安装要在5～40℃之间的无雨天气下进行。HDPE管网的安装施工按图纸设计要求确定，管基础做好后开始管网安装施工顺序由低到高，全部安装完毕经检查合格，撤下管腹下所有托管小车、木砖、使整个管网落实在管基础上，重新检查，符合设计要求后方可进行下道工序施工。

The installation must be carried out in rainless weather between 5 and 40℃. The installation and construction of the HDPE pipe network is determined according to the design requirements of the drawings. After the pipe foundation is completed, the pipeline network installation construction sequence starts from low to high. After all installations are completed and qualified, all the trolleys and wooden bricks under the pipe belly are removed, so that the entire pipe network is implemented on the pipe foundation, and re-inspected. Only after meeting the design requirements can the next process be carried out.



---

沙砾石层与碎石层铺设工艺
# Sand and gravel layer and crushed stone layer laying process

# ④ 主要施工质量措施

- 管材的截断，管口垂直于轴线，管材的长度比设计长度长10mm；
When cutting the pipe, the pipe mouth is perpendicular to the axis, and the length of the pipe is 10mm longer than the designed length;
- HDPE管安装完毕须符合设计要求，管口对正偏差不得大于管壁厚的15%，否则必须断开重焊，重焊合格后方可验收；
After the HDPE pipe is installed, it must meet the design requirements, and the pipe mouth alignment deviation shall not be greater than 15% of the pipe wall thickness, otherwise it must be disconnected and re-welded, and acceptance can only be carried out after the re-welding is qualified;
- 拉断力不小于管材拉断力的70%。焊缝强度只在安装前试验室进行，已焊好焊缝不得进行破坏性实验；
The tensile force shall not be less than 70% of the tensile force of the pipe. The weld strength is only tested in the laboratory before installation, and destructive tests shall not be carried out on welded welds;
- 现场人员不得踏越已焊好的管材，安装完毕后进入下道工序时不得损伤管道，若有损伤及时修复；
On-site personnel shall not step over welded pipes, and shall not damage the pipeline when entering the next process after installation. If there is any damage, it shall be repaired in time;

边坡保护层（粘土编织袋）施工方法
# Slope protection layer (clay woven bag) construction method

粘土编织袋是为了保护HDPE土工膜的，因而在施工中，采取以下措施：

Clay woven bags are used to protect HDPE geomembrane. Therefore, the following measures are taken during construction:

- 施工人员必须穿平底胶鞋或平底布鞋，以防止踩坏 HDPE 土工膜；
Construction workers must wear flat rubber shoes or flat cloth shoes to prevent the HDPE geomembrane from being damaged;
- 原则上严禁踩踏HDPE土工膜，当必须踩踏时要在脚下扩垫编织袋，编织袋上保证无砂石等杂物，防止踩坏 HDPE土工膜；
In principle, it is strictly forbidden to step on the HDPE geomembrane. When it is necessary to step on it, a woven bag should be placed under the feet to ensure that there is no sand, gravel or other debris on the woven bag to prevent the HDPE geomembrane from being damaged;
- 设专人负责检查粘土，确保编织袋中的粘土无砂石，树枝、玻璃等硬物；
A dedicated person is responsible for checking the clay to ensure that there is no sand, gravel, branches, glass or other hard objects in the woven bag;

-72-

---

边坡保护层（粘土编织袋）施工方法
# Slope protection layer (clay woven bag) construction method

- 施工顺序由下至上进行人工铺设，为防止编织袋中的粘土散落到砾石上，编织袋口用手提式缝合机缝合；The construction sequence is manual paving from bottom to top. To prevent the clay in the woven bags from falling onto the gravel, the woven bag mouth is sewn with a portable sewing machine;
- 编织袋中的粘土不能装得过满，达到70~80%即可，以防止铺设不平和不整齐；The clay in the woven bags cannot be filled too full, 70~80% is enough to prevent uneven and irregular paving;
- 水平方向，袋与袋之间叠压宽度5~10cm，垂直方向叠压长度不小于20cm，上下缝错开；In the horizontal direction, the overlapping width between bags is 5~10cm, and the overlapping length in the vertical direction is not less than 20cm, and the upper and lower seams are staggered.
- 编织袋在坑上装好，用人工运至坑下进行铺设；The woven bags are installed on the pit and transported to the bottom of the pit by hand for paving;

填埋场气体导排系统施工方法
# Construction method of landfill gas drainage system

1. 填埋气导排采用导气中空管，管长为2米，用U-PVC管连接；The landfill gas is discharged through a hollow gas pipe with a length of 2 meters and connected with a U-PVC pipe;
2. 中空导气管周围用铁丝石笼围住 ，石笼要高于垃圾作业面1米；The hollow gas pipe is surrounded by a wire gabion, which should be 1 meter higher than the garbage working surface;
3. 导气排出管采用钢管；The gas discharge pipe is made of steel pipe;
4. 导气管的安装之前，先放置直径D1300，L=2500mm的钢套管，在其中设入导气管、铁丝石，在笼内充填卵石。当垃圾填埋到压实高度后，将钢套管提拔到新高度；Before installing the gas pipe, a steel casing with a diameter of D1300 and L=2500mm is placed, in which the gas pipe and wire gabion are installed, and pebbles are filled in the cage. When the garbage is landfilled to the compaction height, the steel casing is lifted to a new height;

填埋场场内地下水导排及雨污分流
# Groundwater drainage and rainwater and sewage diversion within landfills

# 🞑 场内地下水导排 Groundwater drainage within the site

对于山谷型填埋场或地下水水位较高的平原填埋场 , 需要考虑防渗层下地下水的导排； For valley-type landfills or plain landfills with high groundwater levels, it is necessary to consider the drainage of groundwater under the impermeable layer;

# 🞑 雨污分流系统 Rainwater and sewage diversion system

雨水不能进入垃圾堆体；作业面的雨水尽可能排除；Rainwater cannot enter the garbage pile; rainwater on the working surface should be removed as much as possible;

填埋气体的导排及综合利用
# Drainage and comprehensive utilization of landfill gas

# 产气量确定 Gas production determination

# 理论计算法 Theoretical calculation method：

有机物（固体）+ H₂O →可生物降解有机物 + CH₄ + CO₂ + 其它气体

CₐHbOcNd + [(4a-b-2c+3d)/4] H₂O → [(4a+b-2c-3d)/4] CH₄ + [(4a-b+2c+3d)/8]CO₂ + d NH₃

# 经验公式计算法和实测法 Empirical formula method and actual measurement method

实际产生的沼气量约为化学计算公式产生的沼气量1/2，实际可回收的沼气量约为理论量1/4；

The actual amount of biogas produced is about 1/2 of the amount produced by the chemical calculation formula, and the actual amount of biogas that can be recovered is about 1/4 of the theoretical amount;

填埋气体的导排及综合利用
# Drainage and comprehensive utilization of landfill gas

# 1. 填埋气体的主动导排方式及系统组成

# Active drainage methods and system composition of landfill gas

填埋气体收集和导排系统作用是减少填埋气体向大气的排放和在地下的横向迁移，并回收利用甲烷气体。The function of the landfill gas collection and drainage system is to reduce the emission of landfill gas to the atmosphere and the lateral migration underground, and to recycle and utilize methane gas.

# 气体主动导排

- 接至抽气：在填埋场内铺设一些垂直的或水平的导气井和盲沟，用管道将这些导气井和盲沟连接设备, 利用抽气设备对导气井和盲沟抽气, 将填埋场内的填埋气体抽出来 Active gas drainage: Lay some vertical or horizontal gas wells and blind ditches in the landfill, connect these gas wells and blind ditches with pipelines to the exhaust equipment, use the exhaust equipment to exhaust the gas wells and blind ditches, and extract the landfill gas in the landfill

# 被动导排

- 不用机械抽气设备, 填埋气体依靠自身的压力沿导排井和盲沟排向填埋场外 Passive drainage: Without mechanical exhaust equipment, the landfill gas relies on its own pressure to be discharged outside the landfill along the drainage wells and blind ditches
- 垂直气体导排井 Vertical gas drainage wells
- 水平气体导排盲沟 Horizontal gas drainage blind ditches

-80-

主动导排：抽气井、气体收集管、冷凝水收集井和泵站、抽风机、气体处理站（回收或焚烧）以及气体监测设备等组成。

Active drainage: It consists of a gas extraction well, a gas collection pipe, a condensate collection well and a pump station, an exhaust fan, a gas treatment station (recovery or incineration) and gas monitoring equipment.

填埋气体的导排及综合利用
# Drainage and comprehensive utilization of landfill gas

# 填埋气高效收集与深度净化技术

# Landfill gas efficient collection and deep purification technology

# 硅氧烷组分净化

# Siloxane component purification

- 国内填埋气中硅氧烷组分以D4(八甲基环四硅氧烷)、D5 (十甲基环五硅氧烷)为主，总硅氧烷含量低于10 mg/m³；The siloxane components in domestic landfill gas are mainly D4 (octamethylcyclotetrasiloxane) and D5 (decamethylcyclopentasiloxane), and the total siloxane content is less than 10 mg/m³;
- 活性炭中的1.7-3.0 nm微孔区具有显著的吸附D4能力；活性炭的比表面、孔容与吸附量呈现正相关性；The 1.7-3.0 nm micropore area in activated carbon has a significant ability to adsorb D4; the specific surface area, pore volume and adsorption amount of activated carbon are positively correlated;
- 碱性官能团越多，活性炭D4吸附效果越好；羧基、内酯基等酸性官能团越多，活性炭D4吸附效果越差；The more basic functional groups, the better the D4 adsorption effect of activated carbon; the more acidic functional groups such as carboxyl and lactone groups, the worse the D4 adsorption effect of activated carbon;

卫生填埋场封场及封场后维护

# Sanitary landfill closure and post-closure maintenance

# 填埋场封场 Closure

- 局部封场：某单元或区域已填埋至设计终场标高，对该单元或区域实施终场覆盖和其它环境治理工程；
Partial closure: a unit or area has been landfilled to the designed final elevation, and the unit or area has been covered and other environmental management projects have been implemented;
- 整场封场：全场填埋至终场标高，对全场进行的终场覆盖和其它场区改造和环境治理工程；
Entire closure: the entire landfill has been landfilled to the final elevation, and the entire landfill has been covered and other landfill renovations and environmental management projects have been carried out;

# 填埋场封场后的维护 Maintenance after closure

- 渗沥液收集处理 Leachate collection and treatment
- 填埋气体收集处理 Landfill gas collection and treatment
- 防洪排水设施的维护 Maintenance of flood control and drainage facilities
- 绿化的维护 Greening maintenance

垃圾堆场封场整治
# Closure and remediation of garbage dump

# 垃圾堆场特点 Characteristics of garbage dumps

- 点多分散，主要分布在市郊农村；The sites are scattered, mainly in suburban rural areas;
- 规模大小不一 The sizes vary;
- 堆放方式不同，有的堆高，有的推平；The stacking methods are different, some are high, some are flat;
- 进行简易堆放，技术水平普遍较低，基本未采取压实、防渗、污水收集处理及沼气导排等措施；The stacking is simple, the technical level is generally low, and no measures such as compaction, anti-seepage, sewage collection and treatment, and biogas drainage are taken;
- 堆场周围环境差，污染严重；The environment around the dump is poor and the pollution is serious;

-92-

---


# 垃圾堆场封场整治分类

# Garbage dump site closure and remediation classification

- 封场&#x3C;3年，异地搬迁修复；
If the site is closed for less than 3 years, the site will be relocated and repaired;
- 封场3～10年，垂直防渗，并辅以植被种植修复；
If the site is closed for 3 to 10 years, vertical anti-seepage measures will be implemented, supplemented by vegetation planting and repair;
- 封场>10年，就地封场修复，矿化垃圾开采利用，实现土地再利用
If the site is closed for more than 10 years, the site will be closed and repaired on site, and mineralized waste will be mined and utilized to achieve land reuse.
- 封场32年后可终止现场监管及渗滤液处理。
After 32 years of closure, on-site supervision and leachate treatment can be terminated.

填埋矿化垃圾综合利用

Yang Lei

可持续生活垃圾填埋
# Sustainable landfill of domestic waste

# 填埋场稳定化与矿化垃圾开采利用

- 填埋场是一座巨大的生物反应器；The landfill is a huge bioreactor;
- 填埋是一种生活垃圾资源化的处理技术；Landfill is a treatment technology for the resource utilization of domestic waste;
- 填埋技术是可持续的；Landfill technology is sustainable;

南方填埋场垃圾在8-10年基本上达到稳定化，此时的垃圾被称为矿化垃圾，可以开采和利用。The waste in the southern landfill is basically stabilized in 8-10 years. At this time, the waste is called mineralized waste and can be mined and utilized.

-96-

---

矿化垃圾定量化指标
# Quantitative indicators of mineralized waste

- 填埋场有机质 9－15％；Landfill organic matter 9-15%;
- 填埋场填埋时间 8－10年以上；Landfill time 8-10 years or more;
- 微生物 >10 exp6-8/g 干垃圾； Microorganisms >10 exp6-8/g dry garbage;
- 粒径 &#x3C; 40 mm；Particle size &#x3C; 40 mm;
- 原生垃圾中粉煤灰含量 &#x3C;10%；Fly ash content in primary garbage &#x3C;10%;
- 阳离子交换容量 1.20-1.49 mmol/1g 干垃圾； Cation exchange capacity 1.20-1.49 mmol/1g dry garbage;

-97-

---

# 矿化垃圾填料介质

# Mineralized garbage fill media

矿化垃圾完全适合作为一种优良的生物反应器填料或介质；

Mineralized garbage is completely suitable as an excellent bioreactor filler or medium;

- 较大的比表面积； Large specific surface area;
- 松散的结构； Loose structure;
- 较好的水力传导和渗透性能； Good hydraulic conductivity and permeability;
- 较好的阳离子交换能力； Good cation exchange capacity;
- 优良的生物种群和较高的细菌总数； Excellent biological population and high total bacterial count;

# 填埋垃圾的基本组成

# The basic composition of landfill waste

# 填埋垃圾开采和分选程序

# Landfill waste mining and sorting procedures

- 在选定的填埋单元内随机选取 5个开挖点，每个开挖点采集500 kg填埋垃圾，共采样2500 kg；
Randomly select 5 excavation points in the selected landfill unit, collect 500 kg of landfill waste at each excavation point, and sample 2500 kg in total;
- 在开挖当日摊铺通风 ，次日手工分选 ，分类为砖石 、细料、塑料、橡胶、玻璃、布、纸、木竹；
Spread and ventilate on the day of excavation, and manually sort the next day, and classify them into bricks and stones, fine materials, plastics, rubber, glass, cloth, paper, wood and bamboo;

渗滤液综合处理利用系统设想方案
# Conceptual plan for comprehensive treatment and utilization system of leachate

| Fresh Leachate | Treatment                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Dilution | Irrigation |
| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ---------- |
| Aged Leachate  | * 用于填埋场生态修复植被绿化 、市政绿化、苗木等方面，避免造成大面积污染；Used for landfill ecological restoration, vegetation greening, municipal greening, seedlings, etc. to avoid causing large-scale pollution;
* 避免用于农牧业生产，防止潜在的有害物质进入食物链；Avoid use in agricultural and animal husbandry production to prevent potential harmful substances from entering the food chain; |          |            |

-103-

---


# 渗滤液处理方法

# Leachate treatment methods

渗 滤 液 处 理 技 术 ， 按 原 理 主 要 可 分 为

Leachate treatment technology can be divided into the following categories according to the principle:

- 物理化学处理技术 Physical chemical treatment
- 生物化学处理技术 Biochemical treatment
- 自然处理技术 Natural treatment
- 组合处理工艺 Combined treatment process



---

物理化学法 Physical chemical Methods

# • 吹脱、吸附、混凝沉淀

Stripping, adsorption, coagulation and precipitation

# • 高级氧化法

（Fenton法、光激发氧化法、活性炭－H₂O₂催化氧化法、光催化氧化法 、 湿式氧化 、 电解氧化法 、 臭氧氧化法）

Advanced oxidation methods (Fenton method, photo-induced oxidation method, activated carbon-H₂O₂ catalytic oxidation method, photocatalytic oxidation method, wet oxidation, electrolytic oxidation method, ozone oxidation method)

# • 蒸发和膜分离

（微滤、超滤、纳滤和反渗透）、辐射和超声波、高压脉冲放电等离子体等；

Evaporation and membrane separation (microfiltration, ultrafiltration, nanofiltration and reverse osmosis), radiation and ultrasound, high-voltage pulse discharge plasma, etc.;

# • 主要作用：

用于去除渗滤液中的COD、氨氮、重金属、SS以及色度、浊度等

Main function: used to remove COD, ammonia nitrogen, heavy metals, SS, color, turbidity, etc. in leachate

# • 应用范围：

可作为渗滤液预处理和深度处理。

Application range: can be used as leachate pretreatment and deep treatment.

-105-

---

物理化学法优缺点

# Draws and merits of physical chemical methods

# 吹脱法

先将渗滤液的pH值调至碱性，然后再以曝气的方式使游离氨从水中逸出，以降低渗滤液中的NH₃-N；

Stripping method: first adjust the pH value of the leachate to alkaline, and then use aeration to allow free ammonia to escape from the water to reduce NH₃-N in the leachate;

# 优点

对氨氮具有较高的去除效率；

Advantages: high removal efficiency for ammonia nitrogen;

# 缺点

但投资运行成本高，脱氨尾气难以治理；

Disadvantages: high investment and operation costs, and difficult to treat de-ammonification tail gas;

# 混凝法

混凝剂多为Al₂(SO₄)₃、AlCl₃、FeSO₄、FeCl₃及聚铁、聚铝等；

Coagulation method: coagulants are mostly Al₂(SO₄)₃, AlCl₃, FeSO₄, FeCl₃, polyiron, polyaluminum, etc.;

# 优点

可有效地去除渗滤液的浊度、色度和重金属离子，对COD也有一定的去除效果（去除率30%～60%）；

Advantages: can effectively remove turbidity, color and heavy metal ions of the leachate, and also has a certain removal effect on COD (removal rate 30% to 60%);

# 缺点

伴有大量化学污泥产生，出水的pH值较低，含盐量高；

Disadvantages: accompanied by a large amount of chemical sludge, low pH value of the effluent, and high salt content;

-106-

---

物理化学法优缺点

# Draws and merits of physical chemical methods

# 反渗透技术

Reverse osmosis technology: The main process is medium temperature anaerobic + membrane bioreactor (MBR) + nanofiltration (NF) / reverse osmosis (RO)

# 缺点

Disadvantages: There is contamination, scaling and damage on the membrane surface, and the cost is high; RO浓缩液处理目前没解决（回灌、高级氧化、蒸发、膜蒸馏等）

RO concentrate treatment has not been solved yet (reinjection, advanced oxidation, evaporation, membrane distillation, etc.)

-107-

---

# 膜生物反应器（Membrane Bio-reactor, MBR）

污水在反应器中 ，经生物处理完成对有机污染物质的分解与转化后 ，利用微滤膜（MF）或超滤膜（UF）的高效分离，完成污水的固液分离，从而达到污水的最终净化效果；

After the sewage is treated biologically in the reactor to decompose and transform the organic pollutants, the solid-liquid separation of the sewage is completed by the efficient separation of microfiltration membrane (MF) or ultrafiltration membrane (UF), thereby achieving the final purification effect of the sewage;

# 工艺组成

进水井、格栅、调节池、毛发聚集器 、MBR反应池、消毒装置、计量装置、电控装置等；

Process composition: water inlet well, screen, regulating tank, hair collector, MBR reaction tank, disinfection device, metering device, electronic control device, etc.;

目前已进行大量研究并投入大规模实际应用的只有膜分离生物反应器 。

Currently, only membrane separation bioreactor has been extensively studied and put into large-scale practical application.

---


# 渗滤液的组合处理工艺 Combined process

- 物化预处理 (混凝沉淀、氨氮吹脱、化学氧化等)＋生物处理(厌氧、缺氧、好氧等)＋物化深度处理(吸附、反渗透、催化氧化等 )组合工艺，出水直接排放或并入污水管网。Combined process of physical and chemical pretreatment (coagulation and sedimentation, ammonia nitrogen stripping, chemical oxidation, etc.) + biological treatment (anaerobic, anoxic, aerobic, etc.) + physical and chemical deep treatment (adsorption, reverse osmosis, catalytic oxidation, etc.), the effluent is directly discharged or incorporated into the sewage network.
- 选用“预处理＋生物处理＋深度处理”、“生物处理+深度处理”或“预处理+深度处理”等组合工艺。其中代表性工艺为：厌氧生物处理＋ MBR＋纳滤+反渗透。Select the combined process of "pretreatment + biological treatment + deep treatment", "biological treatment + deep treatment" or "pretreatment + deep treatment". The representative process is: anaerobic biological treatment + MBR + nanofiltration + reverse osmosis.



---


# 渗滤液处理达标率低

# Low compliance rate of leachate treatment

- 生活垃圾处理，政府补贴少，不满足达标排放技术处理费用要求；
There is little government subsidy for domestic waste treatment, which does not meet the requirements for the treatment cost of the standard emission technology;
- 目前达标处理技术主要为含RO的组合技术，但其建设成本高达 4~10万元/吨水，处理费用为40~70元/吨（不含设备折旧、利息），如果第三方运营，还需考虑设施折旧、利息等，加上后续浓缩液、污泥处理成本，其运营管理费用超过100元/吨。
Currently, the standard treatment technology is mainly a combination technology containing RO, but its construction cost is as high as 40,000 to 100,000 yuan/ton of water, and the treatment cost is 40 to 70 yuan/ton (excluding equipment depreciation and interest). If it is operated by a third party, it is also necessary to consider the depreciation of facilities, interest, etc., plus the subsequent concentrate and sludge treatment costs, its operating and management costs exceed 100 yuan/ton.
- 各地生活垃圾政府补贴没有统一标准，差距较大，大城市为80~250元/吨，绝大多数地区为20~50元/吨，而真正用于渗滤液处理的费用仅为5~20元/吨，远不能满足渗滤液达标排放技术处理费用。
There is no unified standard for government subsidies for domestic waste in various places, and the gap is large. It is 80 to 250 yuan/ton in large cities and 20 to 50 yuan/ton in most areas. The actual cost of leachate treatment is only 5 to 20 yuan/ton, which is far from meeting the technical treatment cost of leachate emission standards.
- RO浓缩液妥善处理难。
It is difficult to properly handle RO concentrate.



---

生活垃圾填埋场技术挑战
# Technical Challenges of Municipal Waste Landfill

- 防渗层破损预警与修补 Early warning and repair of impermeable layer damage；
- 加速有机废物生物降解和稳定化 Accelerate the biodegradation and stabilization of organic waste; Suppress the emission of methane and carbon dioxide and the release of odor in landfills；
- 抑制填埋场甲烷和二氧化碳排放、恶臭释放；Suppress the emission of methane and carbon dioxide and the release of odor in landfills;
- 存量垃圾挖采-深度脱水-高效筛分-废塑料洁净利用-橡胶皮革利用-腐殖土提纯和高值化利用（污水处理生物填料、种植桉树、杨树等经济林木） Existing garbage excavation - deep dehydration - efficient screening - clean utilization of waste plastics - utilization of rubber and leather - purification and high-value utilization of humus soil (biological fillers for sewage treatment, planting of economic trees such as eucalyptus and poplar)；
- 土地修复和利用与可持续填埋 Land restoration and utilization and sustainable landfill；

---

危险废物 Hazardous Waste

# 地壳中含量最丰富的元素 The most abundant elements in the Earth's crust:

氧（O, 46.6%）、硅（Si, 27.7%）、铝（Al, 8.1%）、铁（Fe, 5.0%）、钙（Ca, 3.6%）、钠（Na, 2.8%）、钾（K, 2.6%）、镁（Mg, 2.1%）。

# 不友好的物质 Unfriendly substances:

- 重金属类：铅及其化合物；汞及其化合物；镉及其化合物；六价铬化合物等

Heavy metals: Lead and its compounds; mercury and its compounds; cadmium and its compounds; hexavalent chromium compounds, etc.
- 持久性有机化合物：二噁英类、多氯联苯（PCB）、多溴联苯（PBB）、多溴二苯醚（PBDE）等。

Persistent organic compounds: Dioxins, polychlorinated biphenyls (PCBs), polybrominated biphenyls (PBBs), polybrominated diphenyl ethers (PBDEs), etc.
- 其他：砷及其化合物、CN等。

Others: Arsenic and its compounds, cyanide (CN⁻), etc.

危险废物 Definition of Hazardous Waste

# 危险废物 Definition of Hazardous Waste

•  危险废物是指列入《国家危险废物名录》或根据国家规定的危险废物鉴别标准和鉴别方法认定的具有腐蚀性、毒性、易燃性、反应性和感染性等中一种或一种以上危险特性，以及不排除具有以上危险特性 的固体、液体或其它形态的废物。 Hazardous waste refers to wastes that are either listed in the National Catalog of Hazardous Wastes or identified as possessing one or more hazardous characteristics—such as corrosivity, toxicity, ignitability, reactivity, or infectivity—based on national hazardous waste identification standards and methodologies. This includes solid, liquid, or other forms of waste that cannot be ruled out as exhibiting such hazardous properties.

•  对于危险废物应采取源头减量措施，尽可能在产生的源头作为原料加以利用，或进行有效分离和去毒，实现无害化。 Management Requirements for Hazardous Waste: Source Reduction: Priority should be given to minimizing hazardous waste generation at the source. Where feasible, such waste should be reused as raw material or subjected to effective separation and detoxification to achieve harmless treatment.

•  对于无法资源化利用或无法去毒的危险废物，应进行末端安全处置，包括固化/稳定化、安全填埋和焚烧等。Safe Disposal: For hazardous waste that cannot be recycled or detoxified, proper end-of-life treatment must be implemented, including methods such as solidification/stabilization, secure landfill disposal, and incineration.

# 危害特性鉴别法 Hazard Characterization

所谓危害性鉴别法，就是按照一定的标准通过测试废物的性质来判别该废物是否属于危险废物。由于危害特性种类繁多，从实用的角度，通常主要鉴别废物的腐蚀性、易燃性、反应性和毒性这四类性质。

The so-called hazard identification method is to determine whether the waste is hazardous waste by testing the properties of the waste according to certain standards. Due to the wide variety of hazard characteristics, from a practical point of view, the four main properties of waste are usually identified: corrosiveness, flammability, reactivity and toxicity.



---

易燃性鉴别标准 Flammability identification standard

# 1. 液态易燃性危险废物 Liquid flammable hazardous wastes

闪点温度低于60℃（闭杯实验）的液体、液体混合物或含有固体物质的液体。Liquids, liquid mixtures or liquids containing solid substances with a flash point temperature below 60℃ (closed cup test).

# 2. 固态易燃性危险废物 Solid flammable hazardous wastes

在标准温度和压力（25℃，101.3 kPa）下因摩擦或自发性燃烧而起火，经点燃后能剧烈而持续地燃烧并产生危害的固体废物 。 Solid wastes that ignite due to friction or spontaneous combustion at standard temperature and pressure (25℃, 101.3 kPa), and can burn violently and continuously after ignition and produce hazards.

# 3. 气态易燃性危险废物 Gaseous flammable hazardous wastes

在20℃，101.3 kPa状态下，在与空气的混合物中体积百分比≤13%时可点燃的气体，或者在该状态下，不论易燃下限如何，与空气混合，易燃范围的易燃上限与易燃下限之差大于或等于12%的气体。Gases that can be ignited when the volume percentage in the mixture with air is ≤13% at 20℃, 101.3 kPa, or gases that, in this state, are mixed with air and the difference between the upper flammable limit and the lower flammable limit of the flammable range is greater than or equal to 12% regardless of the lower flammable limit.

-13-

---

腐蚀性鉴别标准 Corrosiveness identification standard

# 1. 标准要求

① 按照 GB/T 15555.12-1995 制备的浸出液， pH 值 ≥12.5，或者 ≤2.0。The pH value of the leaching solution prepared in accordance with GB/T 15555.12-1995 is ≥12.5, or ≤2.0.

② 在 55 ℃ 条件下，对 GB/T 669 中规定的 20 号钢材的腐蚀效率 ≥6.35 mm/a。At 55°C, the corrosion efficiency of No. 20 steel specified in GB/T 669 is ≥6.35 mm/a.

-14-

---

反应性鉴别标准

# Reactivity Identification Criteria

# ① 具有爆炸性质

- 常温常压下不稳定，在无引爆条件下，易发生剧烈变化
Unstable at room temperature and pressure, prone to drastic changes without detonation
- 标准温度和压力下（25℃，101.3 kPa），易发生爆轰或爆炸性分解反应
Prone to detonation or explosive decomposition reaction at standard temperature and pressure (25℃, 101.3 kPa)
- 受强起爆剂作用或在封闭条件下加热，能发生爆轰或爆炸反应
Can detonate or explode when subjected to strong detonators or heated under closed conditions

-15-

---

反应性鉴别标准 Reactivity Identification Criteria

# 1. 与水或酸接触产生易燃气体或有毒气体

- 与水混合发生剧烈化学反应，并放出大量易燃气体和热量 Produces violent chemical reaction when mixed with water, and releases a large amount of flammable gas and heat
- 与水混合能产生足以危害人体健康或环境的有毒气体、蒸气或烟雾 Produces toxic gas, vapor or smoke that is harmful to human health or the environment when mixed with water
- 在酸性条件下，每千克含氰化物废物分解产生≥250 mg氰化氢气体，或者每千克含硫化物废物分解产生≥500 mg硫化氢气体 Under acidic conditions, decomposition of cyanide waste produces ≥250 mg hydrogen cyanide gas per kilogram, or decomposition of sulfide waste produces ≥500 mg hydrogen sulfide gas per kilogram

# 2. 废弃氧化剂或有机过氧化物

- 极易引起燃烧或爆炸的废弃氧化剂 Waste oxidants that are extremely prone to combustion or explosion
- 对热、震动或摩擦极为敏感的含过氧基的废弃有机过氧化物 sensitive to heat, vibration or friction

-16-

---

浸出毒性鉴别标准 Leaching toxicity identification standard

• 按照HJ/T 299制备的固体废物浸出液中，若有任何一种危害成分含量超过国家规定的危险废物鉴别标准表中所列的浓度限值（分析方法具体见《GB 5083.2-2007危险废物鉴别标准浸出毒性鉴别》），则判定该固体废物是具有浸出毒性特征的危险废物。

If any hazardous component in the solid waste leachate prepared in accordance with HJ/T 299 exceeds the concentration limit listed in the national hazardous waste identification standard table (for specific analysis methods, see "GB 5083.2-2007 Hazardous Waste Identification Standard Leaching Toxicity Identification"), the solid waste is judged to be a hazardous waste with leaching toxicity characteristics.

• HJ/T 299：浸提液 2:1 浓硫酸和浓硝酸混合液，滴入水中，使 pH3.2。

HJ/T 299: The leachate is a 2:1 mixture of concentrated sulfuric acid and concentrated nitric acid, which is dripped into water to make the pH 3.2.

-17-

---


# 浸出方法 Leaching methods

- 废物浸出毒性浸出方法标准—水平振荡法浸出程序（HVEP）

Waste leaching toxicity leaching method standard - horizontal oscillation leaching procedure (HVEP)
- 取100.0g样品放入2L的具盖广口聚乙烯瓶中，加入1L pH3.2硫酸-硝酸混合浸提液（液固比为10:1），盖紧瓶盖，将瓶子垂直固定在水平往复式振荡机上，调节振荡频率为110±10次/min，振幅40mm，在室温下振荡8h，取下静置16h。用中速定量滤纸过滤，收集全部滤液即浸出液，供分析用。

Take 100.0g of sample and put it into a 2L wide-mouth polyethylene bottle with a lid, add 1L pH3.2 sulfuric acid-nitric acid mixed leaching solution (liquid-to-solid ratio is 10:1), cover the bottle cap tightly, fix the bottle vertically on a horizontal reciprocating oscillator, adjust the oscillation frequency to 110±10 times/min, amplitude 40mm, oscillate at room temperature for 8h, remove and let stand for 16h. Filter with medium-speed quantitative filter paper, collect all the filtrate, i.e., the leaching liquid, for analysis.
- 振荡浸出过程是一种强化的浸出过程，与固体废物在水作用下的浸出过程相比，其浸出速率明显大于后者，具有一定的不真实性，但它可以反映在最不利的情况下，固体废弃物中有害物质的最大浸出速率和浸出浓度。

The oscillation leaching process is an enhanced leaching process. Compared with the leaching process of solid waste under the action of water, its leaching rate is significantly greater than the latter, and it has a certain degree of unreality, but it can reflect the maximum leaching rate and leaching concentration of harmful substances in solid waste under the most unfavorable conditions.

急性毒性初筛鉴别标准
# Acute Toxicity Preliminary Screening and Identification Criteria

1. 经口摄取：固体LD₅₀≤200 mg/kg，液体LD₅₀≤500 mg/kg，白鼠口服后14天内死亡一半的剂量

Oral ingestion: Solid LD50 ≤ 200 mg/kg, liquid LD50 ≤ 500 mg/kg (The dose at which half of the test rats die within 14 days after oral administration)
2. 经皮肤接触：LD₅₀≤1000 mg/kg，白鼠裸露1天内死亡一半的剂量

Dermal contact: LD50 ≤ 1000 mg/kg (The dose at which half of the test rats die within 1 day after skin exposure)
3. 蒸气、烟雾或粉尘吸入：LD₅₀≤10 mg/L，白鼠吸入1小时后，14天内死亡一半的剂量

Vapor, fume, or dust inhalation: LD50 ≤ 10 mg/L (The concentration at which half of the test rats die within 14 days after 1 hour of inhalation)

-23-

---

危险废物混合后判定规则

# Rules for determining hazardous waste after mixing

- 具有毒性和感染性等危险特性的危险废物与其他固体废物混合时，混合后的废物属于危险废物。When hazardous wastes with hazardous characteristics such as toxicity and infectivity are mixed with other solid wastes, the mixed wastes are hazardous wastes.
- 仅具有腐蚀性、易燃性或反应性的危险废物与其他固体废物混合，混合后的废物经鉴别不再具有危险特性的，不属于危险废物。Hazardous wastes with only corrosive, flammable or reactive properties are mixed with other solid wastes, and the mixed wastes are no longer hazardous after identification.

-24-

---

危险废物混合后判定规则

# Rules for determining hazardous waste after mixing

- 具有毒性和感染性危险特性的危险废物处理后的废物仍属于危险废物，国家有关法规、标准另有规定的除外。Wastes after treatment of hazardous wastes with toxic and infectious hazardous characteristics are still hazardous wastes, except where otherwise provided by relevant national laws and regulations and standards.
- 仅具有腐蚀性、易燃性或反应性的危险废物处理后，经鉴别不再具有危险特性的，不属于危险废物。Hazardous wastes with only corrosive, flammable or reactive properties are no longer hazardous after identification.

# 危险废物鉴别工作程序 Hazardous Waste Identification Procedures

# 涉及环境司法

# 待鉴别固体废物 Solid waste to be identified

# Environmental Justice

# 危险废物

通过刑事侦查、溯源分析可确定固体废物来源 The source of solid waste can be determined through criminal investigation and traceability analysis.

委托鉴别机构开展鉴别工作 Entrust an identification agency to carry out identification work.

# 鉴别的一般性程序 General Procedural Requirements for Hazardous Waste Identification

是否列入《国家危险废物名录》 Is it included in the National List of Hazardous Wastes?

| 是 | 直接出具鉴别报告 Directly issue identification report                                            |
| - | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 否 | 根据工艺分析是否可能具有危险特性 Based on process analysis whether it may have hazardous characteristics |

# 无法确定固体废物来源 Unable to identify the source of solid waste

| 是 | 编制、论证鉴别方案，采样、检测 Prepare and verify identification plans, sampling and testing |
| - | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 否 | 报请环境保护部认定 Submit to the Ministry of Environmental Protection for approval     |

# 危险特性检测结果是否可以得出鉴别结论 Can the results of hazardous characteristic testing lead to identification conclusions?

| 是 | 是否超过危险废物鉴别标准 Whether it exceeds the hazardous waste identification standard |
| - | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 否 | 不属于危险废物 Not hazardous waste                                                 |

属于危险废物 Hazardous waste

鉴别机构出具鉴别结论 The identification agency issues an identification conclusion.

危险废物豁免管理清单
# Hazardous waste exemption management list

• 列入豁免管理清单的废物共16种/类，在所列的豁免环节，且满足相应的豁免条件时，可以按照豁免内容的规定实行豁免管理，不仅可以减少危险废物管理过程中的总体环境风险，而且可以提高危险废物环境管理效率。A total of 16 types/categories of waste are included in the Exemption Management List. When these wastes meet the specified exemption conditions in the designated exemption processes, they may be managed in accordance with the exemption provisions. This approach not only reduces the overall environmental risks in hazardous waste management but also enhances the efficiency of environmental regulation.

• 如生活垃圾焚烧厂飞灰，固化稳定化后可以在生活垃圾卫生填埋场单独分区填埋（不得与生活垃圾混合填埋）。For example, fly ash from municipal solid waste incineration plants, after undergoing solidification/stabilization treatment, may be disposed of in a dedicated section of a sanitary landfill site for municipal solid waste (it must not be co-disposed with untreated municipal solid waste).

# Legal interpretation on illegal transportation and illegal disposal of hazardous waste

# 中华人民共和国固体废物污染环境防治法

Law of the People's Republic of China on the Prevention and Control of Environmental Pollution by Solid Wastes

# 最高人民法院最高人民检察院关于办理环境污染刑事案件适用法律若干问题的解释：

Interpretation of the Supreme People's Court and the Supreme People's Procuratorate on Several Issues Concerning the Application of Law in Handling Environmental Pollution Criminal Cases:

# 第一条

实施刑法第三百三十八条规定的行为，具有下列情形之一的，应当认定为“严重污染环境”：

Article 1 Any act falling under the provisions of Article 338 of the Criminal Law that involves any of the following circumstances shall be identified as "causing severe environmental pollution":

---

危险废物非法转运和非法处理法律解释
# Legal interpretation on illegal transportation and illegal disposal of hazardous waste

- 在饮用水水源一级保护区 、自然保护区核心区排放、倾倒、处置有放射性的废物、含传染病病原体的废物、有毒物质的；
Discharging, dumping, or disposing of radioactive waste, waste containing pathogens of infectious diseases, or toxic substances in the primary protection zones of drinking water sources or the core areas of nature reserves;
- 非法排放、倾倒、处置危险废物三吨以上的；
Illegally discharging, dumping, or disposing of more than three metric tons of hazardous waste;
- 非法排放含重金属、持久性有机污染物等严重危害环境、损害人体健康的污染物超过国家污染物排放标准或者省、自治区、直辖市人民政府根据法律授权制定的污染物排放标准三倍以上的；
Illegally discharging pollutants containing heavy metals, persistent organic pollutants, or other substances that seriously endanger the environment or harm human health, exceeding three times the limits set by national pollutant discharge standards or the standards established by the people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, or municipalities directly under the central government under legally authorized authority;
- 私设暗管或者利用渗井、渗坑、裂隙、溶洞等排放、倾倒、处置有放射性的废物、含传染病病原体的废物、有毒物质的；
Discharging, dumping, or disposing of radioactive waste, waste containing pathogens of infectious diseases, or toxic substances through concealed pipes, seepage wells, seepage pits, fissures, karst caves, or other similar means.

危险废物非法转运和非法处理法律解释
# Legal interpretation on illegal transportation and illegal disposal of hazardous waste

✓中华人民共和国固体废物污染环境防治法 Law of the People's Republic of China on the Prevention and Control of Environmental Pollution by Solid Wastes

- 两年内曾因违反国家规定，排放、倾倒、处置有放射性的废物、含传染病病原体的废物、有毒物质受过两次以上行政处罚，又实施前列行为的；
- Having been subjected to two or more administrative penalties within the past two years for illegally discharging, dumping, or disposing of radioactive waste, waste containing pathogens of infectious diseases, or toxic substances, and committing the same acts again;
- 致使乡镇以上集中式饮用水水源取水中断十二小时以上的；
- Causing the interruption of centralized drinking water supply from township-level or above sources for over 12 hours;
- 致使基本农田、防护林地、特种用途林地五亩以上，其他农用地十亩以上，其他土地二十亩以上基本功能丧失或者遭受永久性破坏的；
- Causing the loss of basic functions or permanent damage to more than five mu (≈0.33 hectares) of basic farmland, shelter forest land, special-purpose forest land, more than ten mu of other agricultural land, or more than twenty mu of other land;

-34-

---

危险废物非法转运和非法处理法律解释

# Legal interpretation on illegal transportation and illegal disposal of hazardous waste

- 致使森林或者其他林木死亡五十立方米以上，或者幼树死亡二千五百株以上的；
Causing the death of more than 50 cubic meters of forest or other trees, or the death of more than 2,500 saplings;
- 致使公私财产损失三十万元以上的；
Causing the loss of public or private property of more than 300,000 yuan;
- 致使疏散、转移群众五千人以上的；
Causing the evacuation or relocation of more than 5,000 people;
- 致使三十人以上中毒的；
Causing more than 30 people to be poisoned;
- 致使三人以上轻伤、轻度残疾或者器官组织损伤导致一般功能障碍的；
Causing more than three people to suffer minor injuries, minor disabilities, or general functional disorders caused by organ tissue damage;
- 致使一人以上重伤、中度残疾或者器官组织损伤导致严重功能障碍的；
Causing more than one person to suffer serious injuries, moderate disabilities, or serious functional disorders caused by organ tissue damage;
- 其他严重污染环境的情形。
Other serious environmental pollution situations.

---

危险废物污染特性-污染形式多样

# Hazardous waste pollution characteristics-pollution in various forms

不恰当的处理处置（对人体和生态环境的直接暴露）

Improper treatment and disposal (direct exposure to human body and ecological environment)

- 排入江河湖海
- Discharge into rivers, lakes and seas
- 地面堆置、倾倒
- Pile-up and dumping on the ground

收集、运输、处理处置和再生过程中产生的各种污染（二次污染）

Various pollutions (secondary pollution) generated during collection, transportation, treatment and disposal and recycling

- 填埋：渗滤液渗漏或者渗滤液不达标排放
- Landfill: leakage of leachate or discharge of leachate that does not meet the standards
- 焚烧：尾气排放污染大气
- Incineration: exhaust gas emissions pollute the atmosphere
- 再生利用：再生利用产品生产过程中产生的污染物排放 ;以及再生利用产品中污染物的释放
- Recycling: emission of pollutants generated during the production of recycled products; and release of pollutants in recycled products

危险废物污染特性-污染形式多样

Hazardous waste pollution characteristics-pollution in various forms

废物去向不确定，即污染物释放途径（暴露场景）的不确定，导致危险废物的污染不确定；

Uncertainty of waste destination, i.e. uncertainty of pollutant release pathways (exposure scenarios), leads to uncertainty of hazardous waste pollution;

# 飞灰的可能去向

Example: Possible destination of fly ash

- 填埋：混合填埋；专用填埋；其他；
Landfill: mixed landfill; dedicated landfill; others;
- 建材：水泥；砖瓦；骨料；路基材料；其他。
Building materials: cement; bricks and tiles; aggregates; roadbed materials; others.

# 废线路板的可能去向

Example: Possible destination of waste circuit boards

- 有价金属（金、银、铂、铑、钯以及铜等）回收：火法；湿法；
Recycling of valuable metals (gold, silver, platinum, rhodium, palladium and copper, etc.): pyrometallurgy; hydrometallurgy;
- 建材：建筑用塑料
Building materials: plastics for construction
- 填埋：混合填埋；专用填埋；其他；
Landfill: mixed landfill; dedicated landfill; others;
- 焚烧：露天焚烧；专用焚烧；其他。
Incineration: open-air incineration; dedicated incineration;

# 固废处置及资源化

# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 9.2 Technology for Safe Landfilling and Resource Utilization of Hazardous Waste

# 危险废物安全填埋与资源利用技术

Yang Lei

2026-05-06

---

安全填埋作业 Safe landfill operation

- 应进行固化稳定化 It should be solidified and stabilized.
- 必须成型、块状化填埋 It must be shaped and landfilled in blocks.
- 不能散装填埋 It cannot be landfilled in bulk.
- 应该有屋盖，雨水不能进入 It should have a roof to prevent rainwater from entering.
- 不得与生活垃圾和城市污泥混合填埋 It must not be mixed with domestic garbage and urban sludge for landfill.

-39-

---

危险废物安全填埋入场要求
# Requirements for safe landfill of hazardous waste

# GB5086.1-1997 固体废物浸出毒性 、 浸出方法 （ 翻转法 ）

Solid waste leaching toxicity, leaching method (inversion method)

• 取70g 样品放入1L的具盖广口聚乙烯瓶中，加入700 mL蒸馏水（液固比为10:1），盖紧瓶盖，将瓶子垂直固定在水平往复式振荡机上，调节振荡频率为30±2次/min，在室温下振荡18 h，取下静置30 min。用中速定量滤纸过滤，收集全部滤液即浸出液，供分析用。

Take 70g of sample and put it into a 1L wide-mouth polyethylene bottle with a lid, add 700mL of distilled water (liquid-solid ratio is 10:1), cover the bottle cap tightly, fix the bottle vertically on a horizontal reciprocating oscillator, adjust the oscillation frequency to 30±2 times/min, oscillate at room temperature for 18 hours, remove and let stand for 30 minutes. Filter with medium-speed quantitative filter paper, collect all the filtrate, i.e. the leaching liquid, for analysis.

| 9  | 铍及其化合物（以总铍计）Be    | 0.20          |
| 10 | 钡及其化合物（以总钡计）Ba    | 85            |
| 11 | 镍及其化合物（以总镍计）Ni    | 2             |
| 12 | 砷及其化合物（以总砷计）As    | 1.2           |

柔性安全填埋 Flexible safe landfill

•  采用双人工复合衬层作为防渗层的填埋处置设施。以有机合成材料（常用HDPE）和黏土配合作为防渗构造，目前是危险废物填埋场的主要构造。A landfill disposal facility that uses a double artificial composite liner as an impermeable layer. It uses a combination of organic synthetic materials (usually HDPE) and clay as an impermeable structure, which is currently the main structure of hazardous waste landfills.

---

刚性安全填埋 Rigid safe landfill

采用钢筋混凝土作为防渗阻隔填埋单元

- 结构的填埋处置设施。以钢筋混凝土作为框架和基础防渗结构，配合有机合成材料作为防渗构造。

A landfill disposal facility that uses reinforced concrete as an anti-seepage barrier structure. Reinforced concrete is used as the frame and foundation anti-seepage structure, and organic synthetic materials are used as the anti-seepage structure.

-43-

---

刚性安全填埋设计

# Design of rigid safe landfill

刚性填埋场设计应符合以下规定

The design of rigid landfills shall comply with the following provisions：

- 刚性填埋场钢筋混凝土的设计应符合GB50010的相关规定，防水等级应符合GB50108 一级防水标准;

The design of reinforced concrete in rigid landfills shall comply with the relevant provisions of GB50010, and the waterproof grade shall comply with the first-level waterproof standard of GB50108;
- 钢筋混凝土与废物接触的面上应覆有防渗 、防腐材料；钢筋混凝土抗压强度不低于25 N/mm²，厚度不小于35cm。

The surface of reinforced concrete in contact with waste shall be covered with anti-seepage and anti-corrosion materials; the compressive strength of reinforced concrete shall not be less than 25 N/mm², and the thickness shall not be less than 35cm.
- 应设计成若干独立对称的填埋单元，每个填埋单元面积不得超过50 m²且容积不得超过 250 m³。

It shall be designed into several independent and symmetrical landfill units, and the area of each landfill unit shall not exceed 50 m² and the volume shall not exceed 250 m³.
- 填埋结构应设置雨棚，杜绝雨水进入；在人工目视条件下能观察到填埋单元的破损和渗漏情况，并能及时进行修补。

The landfill structure shall be equipped with a canopy to prevent rainwater from entering; the damage and leakage of the landfill unit can be observed under artificial visual conditions, and can be repaired in time.

-44-

---

刚性安全填埋设计
# Design of rigid safe landfill

刚性填埋场设计应符合以下规定

The design of rigid landfills shall comply with the following provisions：

- 刚性结构填埋场的钢筋混凝土箱体侧墙和底板应按抗渗结构进行设计，其渗透系数应≤1.0×10⁻⁶ cm/s;

The side walls and bottom plates of the reinforced concrete box of the rigid structure landfill should be designed as impermeable structures, and their permeability coefficient should be ≤1.0×10-6 cm/s;
- 刚性填埋场底部以及侧面的人工衬层的渗透系数应 ≤ 1.0×10⁻¹² cm/s ， 厚度应 ≥ 2.0 mm 。

The permeability coefficient of the artificial lining at the bottom and sides of the rigid landfill should be ≤1.0×10-12 cm/s, and the thickness should be ≥2.0 mm.
- 填埋场系统中应包括接收与贮存设施、分析与鉴别系统、预处理设施、填埋处置设施、环境检测系统、封场覆盖系统、应急设施及其他公用工程和配套设施，填埋场应建设封闭性的围墙或栅栏等隔离设施，专人管理的大门，安全防护和监控设施，并且在入口处标识填埋场的主要建设内容和环境管理制度，填埋场处置不相容的废物应设置不同的填埋区。

The landfill system should include receiving and storage facilities, analysis and identification systems, pretreatment facilities, landfill disposal facilities, environmental testing systems, closure and covering systems, emergency facilities and other public works and supporting facilities. The landfill should have closed walls or fences and other isolation facilities, gates managed by dedicated personnel, safety protection and monitoring facilities, and the main construction content and environmental management system of the landfill should be marked at the entrance. Different landfill areas should be set up for the disposal of incompatible wastes.

-45-

---

刚性安全填埋设计

# 刚性填埋场设计应符合以下规定

The design of rigid landfills shall comply with the following provisions：

- 刚性填埋场设计应设计成若干独立对称的填埋单元，每个填埋单元面积不得超过 50m²且容积不得超过250m³; The rigid landfill shall be designed into several independent and symmetrical landfill units, each of which shall not exceed 50m² in area and 250m³ in volume;

刚性安全填埋设计

# Design of rigid safe landfill

刚性填埋场设计应符合以下规定

The design of rigid landfills shall comply with the following provisions：

# 1）基底防渗设计

# Base anti-seepage design

初始填埋层：危险废物

Initial landfill layer: hazardous waste；

导流层：HDPE 排水滤垫

Diversion layer: HDPE drainage filter pad；

保护层：800 g/m² 长丝无妨土工布

Protective layer: 800g/m² filament non-blocking geotextile

防渗层：2.0 mm 厚光面 HDPE 土工膜

Anti-seepage layer: 2.0 mm thick smooth HDPE geomembrane

基层：抗渗混凝土底板

Base layer: impermeable concrete base plate

# 2）挡墙防渗设计

# Retaining wall anti-seepage design

反滤层：800 g/m² 长丝无妨土工布

Filter layer: 800g/m² filament non-blocking geotextile

防渗层： 2.0 mm 厚光面 HDPE 土工膜

Anti-seepage layer: 2.0 mm thick smooth HDPE geomembrane

基础层：抗渗钢筋砼挡墙

Base layer: impermeable reinforced concrete retaining wall

刚性安全填埋设计 Design of rigid safe landfill

• 医疗废物、与衬层具有不相容性反应的废物、液态废物和具有反应性、易燃性的废物不得进入刚性填埋场；Medical waste, waste with incompatible reactions with the liner, liquid waste, and reactive and flammable waste shall not enter rigid landfills;

• 砷含量大于5%的废物，应进入刚性填埋场。Waste with arsenic content greater than 5% shall enter rigid landfills.

# 刚性填埋 vs 柔性填埋 Flexible vs Rigid

# 柔性填埋场 Flexible Landfill

- 优点：造价低，技术相对成熟，操作简便。Low construction costs, relatively mature technology, and simple operation.
- 缺点：对地质条件要求较高，入场废物受限，且大部分废物需作预处理，渗漏污染控制极其困难，对建设质量和运行管理要求极高，发现渗漏困难，修复难度较大，回取利用困难，后期费用（包括维护费用、修复费用、退役后管理费用等）极高。High requirements for geological conditions; Restricted types of acceptable waste; Most waste requires pretreatment; Extremely difficult to control leakage contamination; Demands exceptionally high construction quality and operational management; Leak detection is challenging; Difficult and costly to repair; Retrieval and reuse are complicated; Extremely high long-term costs (including maintenance, remediation, and post-closure management expenses)

# 刚性填埋场 Rigid Landfill

- 优点：地质条件的限制较小，渗漏污染控制难度较小，入场废物指标要求较小，后期管理难度较小，有利于回取利用，且预处理要求低，建设难度和运行管理要求低。Less restrictive geological requirements; Easier leakage contamination control; Broader acceptance criteria for waste types; Lower long-term management complexity; More conducive to waste retrieval and reuse; Minimal pretreatment requirements; Lower construction and operational management difficulty
- 缺点：建设成本较高，规范不成熟。Higher construction costs; Lack of mature technical standards



---

上海嘉定一期危险废物填埋场
# Shanghai Jiading Phase I Hazardous Waste Landfill

- 底部是 2 米厚度的钢筋混凝土；
The bottom is 2 meters thick reinforced concrete;
- 混凝土上面是 2.5 mm HDPE ；
The top of the concrete is 2.5 mm HDPE;
- 危险废物必须是药剂和水泥混合固化稳定化；
Hazardous waste must be solidified and stabilized by mixing chemicals and cement;
- 设计了屋盖，希望不进入雨水；
The roof is designed to prevent rainwater from entering;
- 专用安全填埋场，不得与生活垃圾和城市污泥混合填埋；
A dedicated safe landfill that must not be mixed with domestic waste and urban sludge;

危险废物固化/稳定化技术
# Hazardous waste solidification/stabilization technology

- 目的：是使危险废物中的所有污染组分呈现化学惰性或被包容起来，以便贮存、运输、利用和处置。
Purpose: To make all polluting components in hazardous waste chemically inert or contained for storage, transportation, utilization and disposal.
- 稳定化：一种将污染物全部或部分固定于支持介质、黏结剂上的方法。
Stabilization: A method of fixing all or part of the pollutants on a supporting medium or binder.
- 固化：是一种利用添加剂改变废物的工程特性（例如渗透性、可压缩性和强度等）的过程。固化可当作是一种特定的稳定化过程。
Solidification: A process of using additives to change the engineering properties of waste (such as permeability, compressibility and strength, etc.). Solidification can be regarded as a specific stabilization process.

-56-

---

水泥固化技术 Cement curing technology

适用处理无机类型废物，特别是重金属废物。Suitable for treating inorganic waste, especially heavy metal waste.

- 水泥高pH值，形成不溶性的氢氧化物或碳酸盐形式； Cement has a high pH value and forms insoluble hydroxide or carbonate forms;
- 某些重金属也可固定在水泥基体的晶格中 Some heavy metals can also be fixed in the lattice of the cement matrix;
- 铅、铬、铜、锌、锡、镉固定效果良好 Lead, chromium, copper, zinc, tin, and cadmium have good fixation effects;
- 可以添加一些药剂，如吸附剂：活性氧化铝、粘土、蛭石等；缓凝剂：酒石酸、柠檬酸、硼酸盐等；促凝剂：水玻璃、铝酸钠、碳酸钠等；减水剂：表面活性剂等。Some agents can be added, such as adsorbents: activated alumina, clay, vermiculite, etc.; retarders: tartaric acid, citric acid, borates, etc.; accelerators: water glass, sodium aluminate, sodium carbonate, etc.; water reducers: surfactants, etc.

缺点：体积增加；固化体长期稳定性较差；成本偏高。Disadvantages: increased volume; poor long-term stability of the solidified body; high cost.

-57-

---

石灰固化技术 Lime curing technology

• 加入氢氧化钙（熟石灰）使污泥得到稳定。石灰中的钙与废物中的硅铝酸根会产生硅酸钙、铝酸钙的水化物或者硅铝酸钙。Calcium hydroxide (slaked lime) is added to stabilize the sludge. The calcium in the lime reacts with the aluminosilicates in the waste to produce calcium silicate, calcium aluminate hydrate or calcium aluminosilicate.

• 需要补充少量添加剂，以获得额外的稳定效果。A small amount of additives is required to obtain additional stabilization.

• 主要用于处理含重金属等无机污染物的污泥。Mainly used to treat sludge containing inorganic pollutants such as heavy metals.

-58-

---

# 熔融固化技术 Melt solidification technology

• 亦称玻璃化技术。Also known as vitrification technology.

• 将待处理的危险废物与细小的玻璃质如玻璃屑、玻璃粉混合，经混合造粒成型后，在1500℃高温下熔融成玻璃化固体，借助玻璃体的致密结构，确保固化体的永久稳定。The hazardous waste to be treated is mixed with fine glass such as glass chips and glass powder, and after mixed granulation, it is melted into a vitrified solid at a high temperature of 1500℃. With the help of the dense structure of the glass body, the permanent stability of the solidified body is ensured.

• 用于高放射性废物处理，还需要加入稳定剂。For the treatment of high-level radioactive waste, stabilizers need to be added.

---


# 药剂稳定化 chemical stabilization

# ① pH值控制技术 pH control

- 重金属在高pH下形成难溶氢氧化物，如Cu；Heavy metals form insoluble hydroxides at high pH, such as Cu;
- 常用药剂：石灰、苏打、氢氧化钠；普通水泥、石灰窑灰渣、硅酸钠等。Common reagents: lime, soda, sodium hydroxide; ordinary cement, lime kiln ash, sodium silicate, etc.

# ② 氧化/还原电势控制技术 Oxidation/reduction potential control technology

- 6价铬(Cr⁶⁺)还原为3价铬(Cr³⁺)、5价砷(As⁵⁺)还原为3价砷(As³⁺)，使之形成难溶化合物；Hexavalent chromium (Cr⁶⁺) is reduced to trivalent chromium (Cr³⁺), and pentavalent arsenic (As⁵⁺) is reduced to trivalent arsenic (As³⁺), forming insoluble compounds;
- 还原剂包括硫酸亚铁、硫代硫酸钠、亚硫酸氢钠、二氧化硫等。Reducing agents include ferrous sulfate, sodium thiosulfate, sodium bisulfite, sulfur dioxide, etc.



---


# 药剂稳定化 chemical stabilization

# ③ 沉淀技术 Precipitation technology

- 硫化物沉淀：大多数重金属硫化物溶解度很小，但不能在酸性介质中使用
Sulfide precipitation: Most heavy metal sulfides have low solubility, but cannot be used in acidic media。
- 硅酸盐沉淀：硅酸盐沉淀在较宽 pH值范围（2～11）有较低溶解度。
Silicate precipitation: Silicate precipitation has low solubility in a wide pH range (2-11).
- 碳酸盐沉淀：重金属（Ba、Cd、Pb）碳酸盐溶解度很小。
Carbonate precipitation: Heavy metal (Ba, Cd, Pb) carbonates have low solubility.
- 共沉淀：二价重金属离子与Fe²⁺共存的溶液中，投加等当量碱，则有：
Coprecipitation: In a solution where divalent heavy metal ions and Fe2+ coexist, adding an equivalent amount of alkali, there is:
xM²⁺+(3-x)Fe²⁺+60H⁻→MₓFe₃₋ₓ(OH)₆
再用空气氧化使之再溶解，经络合
Then use air to oxidize it and dissolve it again.
MₓFe₃₋ₓ(OH)₆+O₂→MₓFe₃₋ₓO₄
- 在铁氧体中：三价铁离子和二价金属离子(也包括二价铁离子)之比是2：1，适用于Mn²⁺、Zn²⁺、Ni²⁺、Mg²⁺、Cu²⁺ 的共沉淀。
In ferrite, the ratio of trivalent iron ions to divalent metal ions (including divalent iron ions) is 2:1, which is suitable for the co-precipitation of Mn²⁺, Zn²⁺, Ni²⁺, Mg²⁺, and Cu²⁺.



---

危险废物主要处理方法
# Main methods of treating hazardous waste

# ① 湿法工艺 Wet process

- 以获得其中的有价金属为目的，通过酸浸、氨浸等湿法手段实现金属的富集和分离。The purpose is to obtain the valuable metals, and the metals are enriched and separated by wet methods such as acid leaching and ammonia leaching.
- 优点是浸出效果好，浸出液离子浓度高，工艺相对成熟。The advantages are good leaching effect, high ion concentration of leachate, and relatively mature technology.
- 缺点是浸出、沉淀、过滤、萃取、还原等工序多，工艺流程长，浸出液后续处理过程复杂，除杂剂消耗量大，除了产生处理成本很高的大量废水外，也将同时产生大量的酸性、氨性废气和二次污泥，达到无害化处置所需的环保处理成本过高，不适合环境容量小的区域使用。The disadvantages are that there are many processes such as leaching, precipitation, filtration, extraction, and reduction, the process is long, the subsequent treatment process of the leachate is complicated, and the consumption of impurity removal agents is large. In addition to generating a large amount of wastewater with high treatment costs, a large amount of acidic and ammonia waste gas and secondary sludge will also be generated at the same time. The environmental protection treatment cost required for harmless disposal is too high, and it is not suitable for use in areas with small environmental capacity.

-62-

---


# 危险废物主要处理方法

# Main methods of treating hazardous waste

# ② 焙烧-浸出工艺 Roasting-leaching process

- 采用传统的火法富集、湿法分离技术，火法与湿法工艺联合提取其中的有价金属。Using traditional pyrometallurgical enrichment and wet separation technology, the pyrometallurgical and wet separation processes are combined to extract the valuable metals.
- 优点是可以对单纯湿法工艺中难分离的金属进行提取分离，污泥减容率高 The advantage is that it can extract and separate metals that are difficult to separate in the simple wet process, and the sludge volume reduction rate is high.
- 缺点是能耗高、工艺流程长、设备投入大，且没有完全解决单纯湿法工艺所带来的废水、废渣等二次污染问题，实际生产中较难推广。The disadvantages are high energy consumption, long process flow, large equipment investment, and it does not completely solve the secondary pollution problems such as wastewater and waste residue caused by the simple wet process, which is difficult to promote in actual production.



---


# 危险废物主要处理方法

# Main methods of treating hazardous waste

# ③ 微生物法 Biological process

- 采用经过特殊培养的菌种对危险废物中特定种类的有色金属进行选择性结合、吸附或溶解，然后通过其他技术手段实现金属与基底材料的分离。Specially cultured strains are used to selectively bind, adsorb or dissolve specific types of non-ferrous metals in hazardous wastes, and then other technical means are used to separate metals from base materials.
- 优点是经过特殊选择和培养的菌种对金属的选择性强，相较传统方法提取有色金属所消耗的酸碱等材料少，具有提取金属工艺过程清洁、环境友好、持续性强。The advantage is that the specially selected and cultured strains have strong selectivity for metals, and consume less acid, alkali and other materials than traditional methods to extract non-ferrous metals. The metal extraction process is clean, environmentally friendly and sustainable.
- 缺点是处理周期长，处理效率低，菌种培养难、菌种耐受性和处理效率低，提取有色金属后剩余的大量危废仍需填埋或进一步无害化处置。The disadvantages are long treatment cycles, low treatment efficiency, difficult strain cultivation, low strain tolerance and treatment efficiency, and a large amount of hazardous waste remaining after the extraction of non-ferrous metals still needs to be landfilled or further harmlessly disposed.



---


# 危险废物主要处理方法

# Main methods of treating hazardous waste

# ④ 一体化熔炼工艺 Integrated smelting process

- 将烘干或焙烧后的危废与硅石、铁矿石、石灰石、铜矿石等辅助材料配料入炉，以煤炭、焦炭作为燃料和还原物质进行还原，提炼金属。Hazardous waste after drying or roasting is mixed with auxiliary materials such as silica, iron ore, limestone, copper ore, etc. and put into the furnace, and coal and coke are used as fuel and reducing materials for reduction to extract metals.
- 优点是处理规模大，可以有效将金属与非金属分离，得到金属含量在90%-99% 合金块，直接作为产品出售给下游冶炼企业。The advantage is that the processing scale is large, metals and non-metals can be effectively separated, and alloy blocks with a metal content of 90%-99% can be obtained, which are directly sold as products to downstream smelting companies.
- 缺点是前端危废干化环节能耗高，渣型控制困难，熔渣一次性玻璃态效率低，非玻璃态的熔炼渣仍需作为危废进一步处置。同时，末端废气治理难度较大。The disadvantages are that the front-end hazardous waste drying link has high energy consumption, slag shape control is difficult, the one-time glass state efficiency of slag is low, and non-glass smelting slag still needs to be further disposed as hazardous waste. At the same time, the end-of-line waste gas treatment is difficult.



---

危险废物主要处理方法

# Main methods of treating hazardous waste

# ⑤ 固化安全填埋 Solidification and safe landfill

- 采用石灰固化、水泥固化、熔融固化和热塑性固化等手段将含金属污泥固化后进行危废填埋场。The metal-containing sludge is solidified by lime solidification, cement solidification, melt solidification and thermoplastic solidification and then placed in a hazardous waste landfill.
- 处理工艺简单，直接处理成本低，但将造成填埋土地和有色金属资源的浪费。The treatment process is simple and the direct treatment cost is low, but it will cause a waste of landfill land and non-ferrous metal resources.

-66-

---


# 固废处置及资源化

# Solid Waste Disposal and Resource Utilization

# 9.3 Alkaline hydrometallurgy of zinc and lead hazardous waste

# 锌铅危险废物碱介质湿法冶金

Yang Lei

2026-05-06



---


# 可分离的组合 Separable combination

- 锌铅，NaOH溶液碱法浸取，再通过硫化钠分离，效果极佳。Zinc and lead, NaOH solution alkaline leaching, and then separated by sodium sulfide, the effect is excellent.
- 锌铁，NaOH溶液碱法浸取，再通过硫化钠分离，效果极佳。Zinc and iron, NaOH solution alkaline leaching, and then separated by sodium sulfide, the effect is excellent.
- 锌铜，NaOH溶液碱法浸取，再通过硫化钠分离，效果极佳。Zinc and copper, NaOH solution alkaline leaching, and then separated by sodium sulfide, the effect is excellent.
- 铅铜 ， NaOH 溶液碱法浸取 ， 再通过硫化钠分离 ， 效果极佳 。 Lead and copper, NaOH solution alkaline leaching, and then separated by sodium sulfide, the effect is excellent.
- 铅铁，NaOH溶液碱法浸取，再通过硫化钠分离，效果极佳。Lead and iron, NaOH solution alkaline leaching, and then separated by sodium sulfide, the effect is excellent.



---

碱法适宜的危险废物

# Alkali Process Suitable Hazardous Waste

- 锌锭熔铸时的含氯浮渣 Chlorine-containing slag during zinc ingot casting;
- 锌危险废物、镀锌废物 Zinc hazardous waste, galvanizing waste;
- 铜锌铅危险废物 Copper, zinc and lead hazardous waste;
- 金属锌或锌氧化物废物 Metallic zinc or zinc oxide waste;
- 锌灰 Zinc ash;
- 次氧化锌废物 Zinc secondary oxide waste;

主要成分：碳酸锌 、 氧化锌 、 硅酸锌及其铅化合物 Main components: zinc carbonate, zinc oxide, zinc silicate and lead compounds

品位：3-55% Zn，0.1-10%Pb Grade: 3-55% Zn, 0.1-10% Pb

原料中碳酸锌 、 氧化锌 、 硅酸锌及其铅化合物 ， 溶于强碱性溶液中。 Zinc carbonate, zinc oxide, zinc silicate and lead compounds in the raw materials are dissolved in a strong alkaline solution.

-69-

碱法浸出效果 Alkaline leaching effect

• NaOH溶液浸出 ZnO、ZnCO₃和Zn₂SiO₄；和相应的铅化合物；无法浸出 ZnS、PbS。NaOH solution leaches ZnO, ZnCO₃ and Zn₂SiO₄; and the corresponding lead compounds; it cannot leach ZnS and PbS.

• NaOH浓度4-5 mol/L。NaOH concentration 4-5 mol/L.

# 碱法最佳工艺条件

# Optimal process conditions for alkaline process

- 含锌烟灰：NaOH 6 mol/L、温度90℃、液固比10:1、浸出时间90 min，浸出率>90％。

Zinc-containing smoke ash: NaOH 6 mol/L, temperature 90℃, liquid-solid ratio 10:1, leaching time 90 min, leaching rate>90%.
- 碳酸锌矿：NaOH 6 mol/L、温度90℃、液固比5：1、浸出时间60 min、粒径过100目筛，浸出率>90％。

Zinc carbonate ore: NaOH 6mol/L, temperature 90℃, liquid-solid ratio 5:1, leaching time 60 min, particle size passes 100 mesh sieve, leaching rate>90%.
- 硅酸锌矿：NaOH 8 mol/L、温度90℃、液固比5:1、浸出时间60 min、粒径过100目筛，浸出率73.8％。

Zinc silicate ore: NaOH 8mol/L, temperature 90℃, liquid-solid ratio 5:1, leaching time 60 min, particle size passes 100 mesh sieve, leaching rate 73.8%.

硫化锌可通过以下反应浸出

Zinc sulfide can be leached by the following reaction:

PbCO₃(s) + ZnS(s) + 4NaOH(aq.) = Na₂Zn(OH)₄(aq.) + Na₂CO₃(aq.) + PbS(s)

铅可通过以下反应循环利用

Lead can be recycled through the following reactions:

PbS(s) + Na₂CO₃(aq.) + 2O₂(g) = PbCO₃(aq.) + Na₂SO₄(aq.)

危险废物处置难题
# Challenges in Hazardous waste disposal

- 危险废物管理复杂，管理成本极高。Hazardous waste management is complex and the management cost is extremely high.
- 危险废物处理成本很高，处理设施严格。Hazardous waste treatment costs are very high and treatment facilities are strict.
- 危险废物固化稳定化药剂消耗大，安全填埋成本非常高。Hazardous waste solidification and stabilization agents consume a lot of money and the cost of safe landfill is very high.
- 必须采用清洁生产，减少危险废物产生量。Clean production must be adopted to reduce the amount of hazardous waste generated.
- 可采用火法冶金处理危险废物，有价物质挥发回收，高温残渣进入水泥窑。Hazardous waste can be treated by pyrometallurgy, valuable substances are volatilized and recovered, and high-temperature residues enter cement kilns.
